# Rashi on Jeremiah

רש"י על ירמיהו

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 1392, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 1356)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:1:1)


**דִּבְרֵי יִרְמְיָהוּ בֶּן חִלְקִיָּהוּ וְגוֹ׳.** לֵיתֵי בַּר קַלְקַלְתָּא דְּתַקְנָן עוֹבְדוֹהִי – יִרְמְיָה בָּא מֵרָחָב הַזּוֹנָה (יהושע ב:א), וְלוֹכַח בַּר מְתַקְּנְתָּא דִּמְקַלְקְלָן עוֹבְדוֹהִי – אֵלּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁקִּלְקְלוּ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶן, שֶׁבָּאוּ מִזֶּרַע כָּשֵׁר (פסיקתא רבתי יג,ד):


**The words of Jeremiah son of Hilkiah** Let the son of the corrupt woman, whose deeds are proper Jeremiah was descended from Rahab the harlot and let him reprove the son of the righteous woman whose deeds are corrupt these are Israel who corrupted their deeds who are descended from legitimate seed.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:2:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הָיָה דְבַר ה׳ אֵלָיו וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר הִתְחִילָה שְׁכִינָה לִשְׁרוֹת עָלָיו בְּאוֹתוֹ הַזְּמַן:


**To whom the word of the Lord came** Upon whom the Shechinah commenced to rest at that time.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:3:1)


**וַיְהִי בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים.** וַיְהִי נָבִיא כָּל **יְמֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ** הַנּוֹתָרִים, וִימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים בְּנוֹ וִימֵי **צִדְקִיָּהוּ** בְּנוֹ, **עַד תֹּם עַשְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה**, הִיא הַשָּׁנָה **עַד גְּלוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִַם בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַחֲמִישִׁי**:


**And he was in the days of Jehoiakim** And he was a prophet all the remaining days of Josiah, the days of his son Jehoiakim, and the days of his son Zedekiah, until the end of the eleventh year that is the year until the exile of Jerusalem in the fifth month.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:1)


**בְּטֶרֶם אֶצָּרְךָ בַבֶּטֶן וְגוֹ׳.** מִימֵי אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן; הֶרְאָהוּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְאָדָם דּוֹר וְדוֹר וּנְבִיאָיו (סדר עולם רבה ל):


**When I had not yet formed you in the womb, etc.** Since the days of the first man. The Holy One, blessed be He, showed Adam each generation and its prophets.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:2)


**אֶצָּרְךָ.** לְשׁוֹן צוּרָה:


**I...formed you** Heb. אצרך, an expression of צורה, a form.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:3)


**יְדַעְתִּיךָ.** קְנִיּוֹשְׁטְ״וִי בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא נוֹדַעְתִּי לָהֶם״ (שמות ו:ג):


**I knew you** connois toi in O.F. Comp. (Exodus 6:3), “I was not known (נודעתּי) to them.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:4)


**הִקְדַּשְׁתִּיךָ.** זִמַּנְתִּיךָ לְכָךְ:


**I appointed you** I appointed you for this.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:5:5)


**נָבִיא לַגּוֹיִם.** לְיִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים עַצְמָם כְּעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי: ״נָבִיא מִקִּרְבְּךָ וְגוֹ׳ יָקִים לְךָ״ (דברים יח:טו) וְלֹא לִמְכַחִישֵׁי תּוֹרָה. הָא מָה אֲנִי מְקַיֵּם **נָבִיא לַגּוֹיִם נְתַתִּיךָ**? בִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים כְּעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בְּטֶרֶם תֵּצֵא מֵרֶחֶם הִקְדַּשְׁתִּיךָ**, עָלֶיךָ אָמַרְתִּי לְמֹשֶׁה: ״נָבִיא אָקִים לָהֶם כָּמוֹךָ״ (דברים יח:יח). זֶה הוֹכִיחָם וְזֶה הוֹכִיחָם, זֶה נִתְנַבֵּא אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה וְזֶה נִתְנַבֵּא אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה:


**a prophet to the nations** To Israel, who behave like the nations. In this manner it is expounded in Sifrei on the verse: “A prophet from your midst, etc.” (Deut. 18:15), will set up for you and not for those who deny the Torah. How then do I fulfill “A prophet to the nations I made you”? To the children of Israel who deport themselves with the customs of the nations. It can further be interpreted: “A prophet for the nations,” like “About the nations,” to give them to drink the cup of poison, to prophesy retribution upon them, as it is said: “Take this cup of the wine of wrath from My hand, you shall give all the nations to drink of it” (infra 25:15). Another explanation of “When you had not yet emerged from the womb I appointed you” is: Concerning you I said to Moses: “I will set up a prophet...like you” (Deut. 18:18). This one reproved them, and this one reproved them. This one prophesied for forty years and this one prophesied for forty years.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:6:1)


**אֲהָהּ.** לְשׁוֹן יְלָל, קוֹנְפְּלֵיינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Alas** This is an expression of wailing (konpljjnt in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:6:2)


**כִּי נַעַר אָנֹכִי.** אֵינִי כְדַאי לְהוֹכִיחָן. מֹשֶׁה הוֹכִיחָן סָמוּךְ לְמִיתָתוֹ (דברים א), כְּבָר נֶחְשַׁב בְּעֵינֵיהֶם בְּכַמָּה נִסִּים שֶׁעָשָׂה לָהֶם: הוֹצִיאָם מִמִּצְרַיִם (שמות יב:לא-מג), וְקָרַע לָהֶם אֶת הַיָּם (שמות יד:טו-לא), הוֹרִיד אֶת הַמָּן, הֵגִיז אֶת הַשְּׂלָיו (במדבר יא), נָתַן לָהֶם אֶת הַתּוֹרָה (שמות לד), הֶעֱלָה אֶת הַבְּאֵר (במדבר כא:טז-יח); אֲנִי, בִּתְחִלַּת שְׁלִיחוּתִי אֲנִי בָּא לְהוֹכִיחָם?!


**for I am a youth** I am not worthy to reprove them. Moses reproved them shortly before his death, when he was already esteemed in their eyes through the many miracles that he had performed for them. He had taken them out of Egypt, split the Reed Sea for them, brought down the manna, caused the quails to fly, given them the Torah, brought up the well. I come to reprove them at the beginning of my mission.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:7:1)


**עַל כָּל אֲשֶׁר אֶשְׁלָחֲךָ.** אֶל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**wherever I send you** to the heathens.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:7:2)


**וְאֵת כָּל אֲשֶׁר אֲצַוְּךָ.** לְיִשְׂרָאֵל **תְּדַבֵּר**:


**and whatever I command you** to Israel, you shall speak.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:9:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח ה׳ אֶת יָדוֹ.** כָּל שְׁלִיחוּת שֶׁהוּא אֵצֶל יָד – לְשׁוֹן הוֹשָׁטָה. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: כַּתַּרְגּוּם: ״וְשָׁלַח ה׳ יָת פִּתְגָמֵי נְבִיאוּתֵיהּ״:


**And the Lord stretched forth His hand** Every sending mentioned concerning a hand is an expression of stretching forth. Another explanation is like the Targum: And the Lord sent the words of His prophecy.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:10:1)


**הִפְקַדְתִּיךָ.** מִנִּיתִיךָ **עַל הַגּוֹיִם**:


**I have appointed you** I have appointed you over the heathens.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:10:2)


**לנתוש ולנתוץ.** דיספציר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**to uproot and to crush** (depayser in French, to uproot) and over Israel to build and to plant if they heed. So did Jonathan paraphrase it.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:11:1)


**מַקֵּל שָׁקֵד.** מַנְדוֹלִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **מַקֵּל שָׁקֵד** ״מֶלֶךְ דְּמוֹחִי לְאַבְאָשָׁא״:


**a rod of an almond tree** (amendleer in O.F.) Jonathan, however, renders: A King who hastens to do evil.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:12:1)


**הֵיטַבְתָּ לִרְאוֹת.** הַשָּׁקֵד הַזֶּה הוּא מְמַהֵר לְהוֹצִיא פֶרַח קֹדֶם לְכָל הָאִילָנוֹת, אַף אֲנִי מְמַהֵר לַעֲשׂוֹת **דְּבָרִי**. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (ירושלמי תענית ד, ה): הַשָּׁקֵד הַזֶּה הוּא מִשְּׁעַת חֲנִיטָתוֹ עַד גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּלוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאֶחָד יוֹם, כְּמִנְיַן יָמִים שֶׁבֵּין שִׁבְעָה עָשָׂר בְּתַמּוּז שֶׁבּוֹ הָבְקְעָה הָעִיר, לְתִשְׁעָה בְּאָב שֶׁבּוֹ נִשְׂרַף הַבַּיִת:


**You have seen well** This almond tree hastens to blossom before all other trees. I, too, hasten to perform My word. And the Midrash Aggadah (Ecc. Rabbah 12:8) explains: An almond tree takes twenty-one days from its blossoming until it is completely ripe, as the number of days between the seventeenth of Tammuz, when the city was broken into, until the ninth of Ab, when the Temple was burnt.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:13:1)


**סִיר נָפוּחַ.** רוֹתֵחַ בּוֹיִילְנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a bubbling pot** [lit. blown up,] seething (boillant in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:13:2)


**וּפָנָיו.** רְתִיחוֹתָיו, אֵשִׁישׁאוֹנְדִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**whose foam** [lit. and its face,] its seething (et ses ondes in O.F.) [and its waves].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:14:1)


**מִצָּפוֹן תִּפָּתַח הָרָעָה.** בָּבֶל לִצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא (גיטין ו.):


**From the north the misfortune will break forth** Babylon is on the north of Eretz Israel.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:16:1)


**וְדִבַּרְתִּי מִשְׁפָּטַי אוֹתָם.** אֶתְוַכַּח עִמָּם, עִם יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִָם:


**And I will utter My judgments against them** I will debate with them, with Judah and Jerusalem.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:17:1)


**תְּאַזֹּר מָתְנֶיךָ.** לְשׁוֹן זֵרוּז, כְּאִישׁ חַיִל:


**And you shall gird your loins** This is an expression of quickening like a man of valor.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:18:1)


**לְמַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה.** כְּנֶגֶד מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה:


**against the Kings of Judah** lit. to the Kings of Judah.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 1:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/1:19:1)


**וְנִלְחֲמוּ אֵלֶיךָ.** ״וִיהוֹן דָּיְנִין וּמְגִיחִין לְקָבְלָךְ לְמִסְתַּר יָת פִּתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָתָךְ״:


**And they shall fight against you** They shall quarrel and fight against you to refute the words of your prophecy.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:2:1)


**זָכַרְתִּי לָךְ.** אִם הֱיִיתֶם שָׁבִים אֵלַי, תַּאֲוָתִי לְרַחֵם עֲלֵיכֶם, כִּי **זָכַרְתִּי חֶסֶד נְעוּרַיִךְ** וְ**אַהֲבַת** כְּלִילַת חֻפָּתֵךְ, שֶׁהִכְלַלְתִּיךְ לְחֻפָּה; וְלָשׁוֹן הַכְנָסָה הוּא. **כְּלוּלֹתָיִךְ** נוֹצִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. וּמַה הוּא **חֶסֶד נְעוּרַיִךְ**? **לֶכְתֵּךְ** אַחַר שְׁלוּחַי, מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן; מֵאֶרֶץ נוֹשָׁבֶת (שמות טז:לה) יְצָאתֶם לַ**מִּדְבָּר**, וְאֵין צֵדָה לַדֶּרֶךְ, כִּי הֶאֱמַנְתֶּם בִּי (מכילתא בא פסחא יד):


**I remember to you** Were you to return to Me, I would desire to have mercy on you for I remember the loving kindness of your youth and the love of the nuptials of your wedding canopy, when I brought you into the wedding canopy, and this (כלולתיך) is an expression of bringing in. Your nuptials (Noces in O.F.). Now what was the loving kindness of your youth? Your following My messengers, Moses and Aaron, from an inhabited land to the desert without provisions for the way since you believed in Me.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:3:1)


**קֹדֶשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כִּתְרוּמָה:


**Israel is holy** like terumah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:3:2)


**רֵאשִׁית תְּבוּאָתֹה.** כְּרֵאשִׁית קָצִיר לִפְנֵי הָעֹמֶר, שֶׁאָסוּר בַּאֲכִילָה וְהָאוֹכְלוֹ מִתְחַיֵּב, כֵּן **כָּל אֹכְלָיו יֶאְשָׁמוּ**. כֵּן תַּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**the first of His grain** Like the first of the harvest before the Omer, which it is forbidden to eat, and whoever eats it is liable, so will all those who eat him be guilty. So did Jonathan render it.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:1)


**וְלֹא אָמְרוּ אַיֵּה ה׳.** כִּי נֵלֵךְ אַחֲרֵי אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים:


**And they did not say, Where is the Lord** that we should follow other gods?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:2)


**עֲרָבָה.** פְּלַנוּרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**plains** Heb. ערבה (planure in O.F.), related to pianoro in Old Italian, meaning ‘a plateau.’


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:3)


**וְשׁוּחָה.** נְטוּיָה, אִינְפוּשִׁיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and pits** Heb. ושוחה (enfosses in O.F.), pitted.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:4)


**צִיָּה.** דִּישְׁגַאטָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**waste** Heb. ציה (degat in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:6:5)


**וְצַלְמָוֶת.** לְשׁוֹן חֹשֶׁךְ:


**and darkness** Heb. וצלמות. An expression of darkness.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:7:1)


**אֶל אֶרֶץ הַכַּרְמֶל.** ״לְאַרְעָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דַּהֲוַת מְנַצְּבָא כְּכַרְמְלָא״ (תרגום יונתן; פסיקתא רבתי טו, א), פֵּירֵשׁ נְטוּעָה כְּיַעַר:


**to a forest land** To the land of Israel which is planted like כרמל, meaning: planted like a forest.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:1)


**תּוֹפְשֵׂי הַתּוֹרָה.** סַנְהֶדְרִין:


**and those who hold onto the Torah** The Sanhedrin.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:2)


**וְהָרֹעִים.** הַמְּלָכִים:


**and the rulers** the kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:8:3)


**נִבְּאוּ בַבַּעַל.** בְּשֵׁם הַבַּעַל:


**prophesied by Baal** In the name of Baal.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:9:1)


**עוֹד אָרִיב.** טֶרֶם אָבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם רָעָה, אֶתְוַכַּח עִמָּכֶם עוֹד עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרַבְתִּי עִמָּכֶם כְּבָר יָמִים רַבִּים:


**Therefore, I will still contend** Before I bring misfortune upon you, I will still contend with you through My prophets, although I have already contended with you many days.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:1)


**אִיֵּי כִתִּיִּים.** לְאִיֵּי כִתִּיִּים:


**the isles of the Kittites** To the isles of the Kittites.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:2)


**אִיֵּי.** אִישְׁלַ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


איי is (isles in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:3)


**וְקֵדָר שִׁלְחוּ.** וְלִקְדָר שִׁלְחוּ לִרְאוֹת מִנְהָגָם:


**and send to Kedar** And send to Kedar to see their custom.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:4)


**וְהִתְבּוֹנְנוּ מְאֹד.** וְשִׂימוּ לֵב לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בַּדָּבָר יָפֶה:


**and consider diligently** And put your heart to it to consider the matter diligently.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:10:5)


**הֵן.** כְּמוֹ אִם, אִם **הֵמִיר גּוֹי** מֵהֶם אֶת אֱלֹהָיו וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁ**הֵמָּה לֹא אֱלֹהִים, וְעַמִּי הֵמִיר** אֶת **כְּבוֹדוֹ** אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ הָיָה נִכְבָּד. וְ**כִתִּיִּים** וְקֵדָרִיִּים יוֹשְׁבֵי אֹהָלִים וְרוֹעֵי מִקְנֶה, וְנוֹסְעִים וְהוֹלְכִים וּמִטַּלְטְלִין מִמִּרְעֶה לְמִרְעֶה וּמִמִּדְבָּר לְמִדְבָּר, וְנוֹשְׂאִים אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם עִמָּהֶם לְמָקוֹם שֶׁחוֹנִים שָׁם, וַאֲנִי נָשָׂאתִי אֶתְכֶם עַד אֲשֶׁר קָבַעְתִּי אֶתְכֶם, וְהִנַּחְתֶּם אוֹתִי, כָּךְ תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (תענית ה:): כִּתִּיִּים עוֹבְדִים לַמַּיִם, וְקֵדָרִיִּים עוֹבְדִים לָאֵשׁ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיּוֹדְעִים שֶׁהַמַּיִם מְכַבִּין אֶת הָאֵשׁ, לֹא הִנִּיחוּ אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם:


**whether** Heb. הן, like אם, if. Whether either of those nations exchanged its god although they are no gods, yet My nation exchanged their glory, with which they were honored. The Kittites and the Kedarites were tent dwellers and cattle herders, who would travel, go, and wander from pasture to pasture and from desert to desert, and they carry their gods with them to the place where they encamp. But I carried you until I established you, yet you forsook Me. This is how Jonathan paraphrased it. Our Sages said however: The Kittites worshipped water and the Kedarites worshipped fire. And although they know that water quenches fire, they did not forsake their god.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:11:1)


**בְּלֹא יוֹעִיל.** בַּעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ תּוֹעֶלֶת:


**for what does not avail** For an idol that does not avail.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:1)


**שֹׁמּוּ שָׁמַיִם.** לְשׁוֹן תִּמָּהוֹן, כְּמוֹ הִשְׁתּוֹמְמוּ; וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן צִוּוּי, כְּמוֹ ״אִם כֹּה יֹאמְרוּ אֵלֵינוּ דֹּמּוּ״ (שמואל א יד:ט):


**O heavens, be astonished** Heb. שמו, an expression of astonishment, like השתוממוּ. It is the imperative form, with the same vowel points as (I Sam. 14:9): “If they say thus to us, “Wait (דּמּוּ) .”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:2)


**וְשַׂעֲרוּ.** לְשׁוֹן סַעַר:


**and storm** Heb. ושערו, an expression of סער, a storm.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:12:3)


**חָרְבוּ מְאֹד.** כְּאִלּוּ אַתֶּם נֶחֱרָבִים עַל בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁעָתִיד לֵיחָרֵב:


**become very desolate** As though you are becoming desolate because of the Temple that is destined to be destroyed.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:1)


**שְׁתַּיִם רָעוֹת.** אִם הֵמִירוּ יִרְאָתָם בְּכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ, יֵשׁ כָּאן רָעָה אַחַת; וְעַכְשָׁיו שֶׁ**אוֹתִי עָזְבוּ**, שֶׁאֲנִי **מְקוֹר מַיִם חַיִּים**, לָלֶכֶת אַחֲרֵי הָעֲבוֹדָה הַזָּרָה, שֶׁהֵם כְּ**בֹארוֹת** שֶׁל מַיִם מְכֻנָּסִין וְנִשְׁבָּרִין וְנִסְדָּקִין, וּמֵימֵיהֶם נִבְלָעִים בִּסְדָקֵיהֶם, הֲרֵי **שְׁתַּיִם רָעוֹת**:


**two evils** Had they exchanged their Deity [lit. their fear] for one His equal, it would be one evil, and now that they have forsaken Me, that I am a spring of living waters, to follow idols, which are like cisterns of stored up water, and they are broken and cracked, and their water is absorbed in their cracks, these are two evils.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:2)


**לַחְצֹב.** לַחְפֹּר:


**to dig** Heb. לחצב, lit. to hew.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:13:3)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא יָכִלוּ.** טִינְדְרוֹנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז אֶת מֵימֵיהֶם; שֶׁהַמַּיִם מְטַשְׁטְשִׁין אֶת שְׂפָתָם וְכוֹתְלֵיהֶם וְהֵם נוֹפְלִין:


**that do not hold** (Tendront in O.F.) their water, for the water will make their edge and their walls muddy, and they cave in.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:14:1)


**יְלִיד בַּיִת.** בֶּן הָאָמָה:


**Is he a home born slave?** The son of a maidservant.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:1)


**יִשְׁאֲגוּ.** שׁוֹאֲגִים, לְשׁוֹן הֹוֶה:


**roar** roar, a present tense.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:2)


**כְּפִירִים.** מְלָכִים:


**young lions** Symbolic of kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:15:3)


**נִצְּתוּ.** נִצְּתוּ בָּאֵשׁ:


**were burnt** were burnt with fire.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:16:1)


**גַּם בְּנֵי נֹף וְתַחְפַּנְחֵס.** הֵם מִצְרַיִם שֶׁאַתֶּם בּוֹטְחִים עֲלֵיהֶם לְעֶזְרָה:


**Also the children of Noph and Tahpanhes** They are the Egyptians upon whom you trust for aid.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:16:2)


**יִרְעוּךְ קָדְקֹד.** לְשׁוֹן רְצִיצָה, כְּדִמְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״וְרָצוּץ״ (דברים כח:לג): ״וּרְעִיעַ״:


**will break your crown** They will break your skull. ירעוּך is an expression of breaking (רציצה), as we translate ורצוץ, “and crushed” (Deut. 28:33), וּרעיע.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:17:1)


**הֲלֹא זֹאת תַּעֲשֶׂה לָּךְ.** הֲלֹא אֶת הָרָעָה וְאֶת הַפּוּרְעָנוּת הַזֹּאת תִּגְרֹם לָךְ הָאַשְׁמָה וְהֶעָוֹן אֲשֶׁר עָזַבְתְּ **אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהַיִךְ**:


**Is not this caused to you** Is not this misfortune and this retribution caused to you by the guilt and the iniquity that you have forsaken the Lord your God?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:17:2)


**בְּעֵת מוֹלִיכֵךְ בַּדָּרֶךְ.** שֶׁהָיָה מְלַמֵּד אוֹתְךָ דֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹבָה וְהַיְשָׁרָה:


**at the time He leads you by the way** For He would teach you the good and the straight way.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:1)


**מַה לָּךְ לְדֶרֶךְ מִצְרַיִם.** לָמָּה תַּעַזְבִי אוֹתִי וְתִבְטְחִי עַל מִצְרַיִם?


**what have you to do in the way of Egypt** Why do you leave Me and trust in Egypt?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:2)


**לִשְׁתּוֹת מֵי שִׁיחוֹר.** שֶׁטִּבְּעוּ זְכוֹרֵיכֶם בַּנִּילוּס; **שִׁיחוֹר** הוּא נִילוּס, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן הַשִּׁיחוֹר אֲשֶׁר עַל פְּנֵי מִצְרָיִם״ בְּסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע יג:ג):


**to drink the water of the Shihor** For they drowned your male children in the Nile. Shihor is the Nile, as it is said: “From the Shihor which is before Egypt,” in the Book of Joshua (13:3).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:18:3)


**וּמַה לָּךְ.** לִמְרֹד בִּי לְמַעַן תִּגְלִי **לְדֶרֶךְ אַשּׁוּר**, לְמֵעֵבֶר נְהַר פְּרָת:


**and what have you to do** to rebel against Me so that you should be exiled to the way of Assyria, to the other side of the Euphrates River?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:1)


**תְּיַסְּרֵךְ רָעָתֵךְ.** סוֹף שֶׁרָעָתֵךְ תָּבִיא עָלֶיךָ יִסּוּרִין:


**Your evil will chastise you** Eventually, your evil will bring suffering upon you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:2)


**וּמְשׁוּבוֹתַיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן ״בָּנִים שׁוֹבָבִים״ (ירמיה ג:יד):


**and your backslidings**, Heb. ומשבותיך, an expression related to “backsliding children (שובבים)” (infra 3:22).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:3)


**תּוֹכִיחוּךְ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מוּסָר״ (ירמיה ל:יד):


**will reprove you** Heb. תוכחך, an expression of reproof.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:19:4)


**וְלֹא פַחְדָּתִי.** פַּחַד שֶׁלִּי לֹא הָיָה בְּלִבֵּךְ לִירָא אוֹתִי:


**and the fear of Me was not** My fear was not in your heart that you should fear Me.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:1)


**שָׁבַרְתִּי עֻלֵּךְ.** הָעוֹל שֶׁל עֵץ נוֹפֵל בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן שְׁבִירָה, וְהַמּוֹסְרוֹת, שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל עוֹר, נוֹפֵל בָּהֶן לְשׁוֹן **נְתִיקָה**:


**I broke your yoke** To the wooden yoke an expression of breaking applies, and to the yoke-bands which are of leather an expression of tearing open applies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:2)


**מוֹסְרוֹתַיִךְ.** הֵם רְצוּעוֹת שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין בָּהֶם הָעוֹל:


**yoke-bands** [מוסרותיך are the] ropes used to shackle the yoke [to the animal].


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:3)


**וַתֹּאמְרִי לֹא אֶעֱבוֹר.** עַל דְּבָרֶיךָ:


**and you said, “I will not transgress.”** your words.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:20:4)


**כִּי עַל כָּל גִּבְעָה.** אַךְ אַתָּה לֹא שָׁמַרְתָּ הַבְטָחָתְךָ, וְ**עַל כָּל גִּבְעָה אַתְּ צֹעָה** – לְשׁוֹן מִשְׁכָּב וּמַצָּע. **כִּי** מְשַׁמֵּשׁ לְשׁוֹן אֶלָּא:


**but on every lofty hill** Put you did not keep your promise, for on every lofty hill you recline (צעה). This is an expression of a bed and a sheet (מצע). [The word] כי serves as an expression of ‘but.’



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:1)


**נְטַעְתִּיךְ שׂוֹרֵק.** שׂוֹרֵק הוּא זְמוֹרוֹת גֶּפֶן טוֹב, כְּלוֹמַר בְּנֵי אָבוֹת כְּשֵׁרִים וְצַדִּיקִים. וּמִדְרָשׁוֹ: **נְטַעְתִּיךְ שׂוֹרֵק**, הוֹסַפְתִּי לְךָ עַל שֶׁבַע מִצְווֹת שֶׁל בְּנֵי נֹחַ שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת וָשֵׁשׁ, כְּמִנְיַן שׂוֹרֵ״ק:


**I planted you a noble vine stock** Heb. שורק is the branches of a good vine, that is to say the children of pious and righteous fathers. Its midrashic interpretation is: I planted you שורק. I added for you to the seven commandments of the children of Noah, six hundred and six, as is the numerical value of שורק.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:2)


**סוּרֵי.** דִישְׁטוֹרְלָטוֹ״רְשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**degenerate** Heb. סוּרי (Destoultours in O.F).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:21:3)


**גֶּפֶן נָכְרִיָּה.** הַצּוֹמַחַת בַּיְּעָרִים:


**wild vine** that grows in the forests.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:1)


**בַּנֶּתֶר.** מִין אֲדָמָה שֶׁחָפִין וְשָׁפִין בָּהּ אֶת הַבְּגָדִים:


**with natron** A type of earth with which garments are cleansed and rubbed.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:2)


**בּוֹרִית.** נִקָּיוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״וּבַר לֵבָב״ (תהלים כד:ד). וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִים **בּוֹרִית** – שָׁאבוֹ״ן בְּלַעַז:


**soap** Heb. בֹּרִית, cleanliness. Comp. “And pure (וּבַר) of heart” (Ps. 24:4). Some explain בֹּרִית as savon in French, soap.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:3)


**נִכְתָּם עֲוֹנֵךְ.** ״הָא כְּרוֹשֶׁם כִּתְמָא דְּלָא דָכֵי, כֵּן סַגִּיאוּ חוֹבֵיךְ קֳדָמַי״ (תרגום יונתן):


**your iniquity is stained** Jon. renders: Like the mark of a stain that is unclean, so have your sins increased before Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:4)


**כֶּתֶם.** טָאקָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


כתם is tka in O.F.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:22:5)


**עֲוֹנֵךְ.** עַל עֲוֹן הָעֵגֶל נֶאֱמַר, שֶׁהוּא קַיָּם לְעוֹלָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּבְיוֹם פָּקְדִי וּפָקַדְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם״ (שמות לב:לד) – כָּל פְּקִידוֹת הַבָּאוֹת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, יִהְיֶה מִקְצָת עֲוֹן הָעֵגֶל בָּהֶם (סנהדרין קב.):


**your iniquity** This is said concerning the iniquity of the ‘Golden Calf,’ which remains in existence forever, as it is stated: “And on the day of My visitation, I will visit upon them their sin” (Exodus 32:34). All visitations that come upon Israel have part of the iniquity of the Golden Calf in them.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:1)


**רְאִי דַרְכֵּךְ בַּגַּיְא.** מָה עָשִׂית מוּל בֵּית פְּעוֹר (דברים ג:כט)! וְעוֹדֵ עַתָּה אַתְּ מַחֲזֶקֶת בְּאוֹתוֹ הַדֶּרֶךְ כִּבְכָרָה קַלָּה הַמְשָׂרֶכֶת דְּרָכֶיהָ:


**See your way in the valley** See what you have done opposite Beth-Peor, and until now you adhere to that way like a swift she camel clinging to her ways.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:2)


**בִּכְרָה.** גְּמַלָּה נְקֵבָה בְּחוּרָה, שֶׁהִיא אוֹהֶבֶת לָנוּעַ. ״בִּכְרֵי מִדְיָן״ (ישעיהו ס:ו) מְתֻרְגָּם: ״הוֹגְנֵי מִדְיָן״, וְהֵם גְּמַלִּים בְּחוּרִים; כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין נב.): נְפִישֵׁי גְּמַלֵּי סָבֵי דְּטָעֲינִי מַשְׁכֵּי דְּהוֹגְנֵי:


**young she-camel** Heb. בכרה, a young female camel, that loves to wander. “The young camels (בכרי) of Midian” (Isa. 60:6) is translated “and they are young camels,” as we find in Sanhedrin 52a: There are many old camels laden with the skins of young camels (הוגני).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:23:3)


**מְשָׂרֶכֶת.** מַחֲזֶקֶת בְּדַרְכֵי נְעוּרֶיהָ; לְשׁוֹן מַסְרִיךְ (בבא מציעא ו.), סָרִיךְ (גיטין סח:). וְיֵשׁ לְחַבְּרוֹ עִם ״שְׂרוֹךְ נַעַל״ (בראשית יד:כג) – קוֹשֶׁרֶת דַּרְכֵי נַעֲרוּתָהּ בְּלִבָּהּ:


**clinging** Adhering to the ways of her youth, an expression similar to: “it would have clung (מסריך סריך)” (Chullin 51a). This may be associated with “a shoe thong (שרוך נעל)” (Gen. 14:23). She binds the ways of her youth in her heart.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:1)


**פֶּרֶה.** שַׁלְוָואטִיקְ״וּ בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִין קוּלִיי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**A wild donkey** (salvatico in O.F.) wild, and some interpret it as poulain in O.F., a foal.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:2)


**לִמּוּד מִדְבָּר.** הַלָּמוּד לִהְיוֹת בַּמִּדְבָּרוֹת; כֵּן אָהַבְתְּ לָנוּעַ:


**accustomed to the desert** Accustomed to be in the deserts, so she loves to wander.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:3)


**שָׁאֲפָה רוּחַ.** פּוֹתַחַת אֶת פִּיהָ וְשׁוֹאֶפֶת רוּחַ, וּלְעוֹלָם הוּא חוֹזֵר לִמְקוֹמוֹ (קהלת א:ו):


**that snuffs up the wind** She opens her mouth and snuffs up the wind, and he always returns to his place.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:4)


**תַּאֲנָתָהּ מִי יְשִׁיבֶנָּה.** אוֹתָהּ מִדַּת תַּנִּינִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ, כִּי גַּם הַתַּנִּין שׁוֹאֵף רוּחַ, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״שָׁאֲפוּ רוּחַ כַּתַּנִּים״ (ירמיה יד:ו). מִי יְשִׁיבֶנָּה מֵאוֹתָהּ מִדָּה? כֵּן אַתֶּם, מִי יוּכַל לַהֲשִׁיבְכֶם מִדַּרְכְּכֶם הָרָעָה?


**her tendency like the sea creatures, who can hinder her?** That trait of the sea creatures that she has, for also the sea creature snuffs up the wind, as it is said: “They snuff up the wind like sea creatures” (infra 14:6). Who can hinder her from that trait? So it is with you who can return you from your evil way? **her tendency like a sea creature** Son dagronemant in O.F., dragon nature. Jonathan rendered it in this manner, כערודה. Another explanation: It is an expression of wailing, comp. “moaning and wailing”.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:24:5)


**כָּל מְבַקְשֶׁיהָ לֹא יִיעָפוּ.** כִּי עַל חִנָּם יִיעָפוּ, שֶׁלֹּא יוּכְלוּ לְהַשִּׂיגָהּ. וּמַה סוֹפָהּ? **בְּחָדְשָׁהּ יִמְצָאוּנְהָ.** חֹדֶשׁ אֶחָד יֵשׁ בַּשָּׁנָה שֶׁהִיא יְשֵׁנָה כָּל הַחֹדֶשׁ, וְאָז הִיא נִלְכֶּדֶת; אַף אַתֶּם, חֹדֶשׁ אֶחָד (הוּא אָב) הוּכַן לָכֶם כְּבָר מִימוֹת הַמְרַגְּלִים, שֶׁקָּבְעוּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם בְּכִיַּת חִנָּם, בּוֹ תִּלָּכְדוּ (סוטה לה.). **תַּאֲנָתָהּ.** שׁוֹ״ן דְּגְרוֹנְמִינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כִּירוּדָא״. דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן יְלָלָה, כְּמוֹ ״תַּאֲנִיָּה וַאֲנִיָּה״ (יחזקאל כט:ב). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: שַׁאקוֹנְטְרִיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״תַּאֲנַת שִׁילֹה״ (יהושע טז:ו):


**all who seek her will not weary** For they will weary needlessly, for they will be unable to overtake her. What will her end be? In her month, they will find her. There is one month in the year that she sleeps for the whole month, and then she is captured. You, too, - one month (viz. Ab) was already prepared for you from the days of the spies, when your forefathers established it as a time of vain weeping, therein, you will be captured. (תּאניה ואניה) (Lam. 2:5). Another explanation: (sa contree in French,) her country, comp. Taanath Shiloh (Josh. 16:6).



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:25:1)


**מִנְעִי רַגְלֵךְ מִיָּחֵף.** זוֹ הִיא מִדָּתֵךְ, כְּפֶרֶא הָאוֹהֶבֶת לָנוּעַ. נְבִיאַי אוֹמְרִים לִיךְ: מִנְעִי עַצְמֵךְ מֵעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁלֹּא תֵּלְכִי **יָחֵף** בַּגּוֹלָה, וּמִנְעִי **גְּרוֹנֵךְ** מִלָּמוּת בַּצָּמָא; **וַתֹּאמְרִי** עַל דִּבְרֵי הַנְּבִיאִים **נוֹאָשׁ**. אֵין מִיחוּשׁ, נוֹאַשְׁתִּי בְּדִבְרֵיכֶם. **נוֹאָשׁ** נוֹקְלִיי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Withhold your foot from going barefoot** This your habit, like the wild donkey that loves to wander. My prophets say to you, “Withhold your foot from idolatry lest you go barefoot into exile and withhold your throat from dying of thirst.” **but you said** concerning the words of the prophets. **I despair** It is of no concern. I despair of your words. נואש is (nonkalajjr in O.F.) nonchalair in modern French.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:26:1)


**כִּי יִמָּצֵא.** בִּתְחִלָּה כְּשֶׁהוּא נִמְצָא **גַּנָּב**, וְהוּא הָיָה מֻחְזָק נֶאֱמָן; כָּךְ תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**when he is found out** At the beginning, when he is found to be a thief, and he was presumed to be faithful. In this manner, Jonathan rendered it.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:27:1)


**וּבְעֵת רָעָתָם יֹאמְרוּ.** אֵלַי **קוּמָה וְהוֹשִׁיעֵנוּ**. וְכֵן תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּבְעִידָּן דְּבִישְׁתָּא אַתְיָא עֲלֵיהוֹן כַּפְרִין בְּטָעוּתְהוֹן וּמוֹדָן קֳדָמַי וְאָמְרִין רַחֵם עֲלָנָא וּפְרוּקְנָא״:


**and at the time of their misfortune they say** i.e., they say to Me, “Arise and save us.” Jonathan, too, translates in this manner: And at the time that misfortune befalls them, they deny their idols and confess before Me, and say. “Have mercy upon us and save us.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:28:1)


**מִסְפַּר עָרֶיךָ הָיוּ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** בְּכָל עִיר וָעִיר אֱלוֹהַּ אַחֵר:


**as many as your cities were your gods** In every city was another god.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:29:1)


**לָמָּה תָרִיבוּ.** אֵיךְ תָּבֹאוּ לְהִתְוַכֵּחַ בְּרִיב וְלוֹמַר: לֹא חָטָאנוּ?!


**Why do you contend** How do you come to contend with the quarrel and say, “We do not sin”?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:29:2)


**כֻּלְּכֶם פְּשַׁעְתֶּם בִּי.** אַף הַצַּדִּיקִים בְּמַשְׁמָע; הַיְינוּ דְּאָמְרֵי אִינְשֵׁי: בַּהֲדֵי הוּצָא, לָקִיא כַּרְבָּא (בבא קמא צב.):


**All of you have rebelled against Me** Even the righteous are meant. This is what people say, “With the thorn the cabbage suffers.”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:30:1)


**לַשָּׁוְא הִכֵּיתִי.** עַל חִנָּם, כִּי לֹא הוֹעִיל שֶׁיִּקְחוּ **מוּסָר**:


**In vain have I smitten** For no purpose, for it did not avail that they should receive correction.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:30:2)


**אָכְלָה חַרְבְּכֶם.** אֶת נְבִיאֵיכֶם, הֲרַגְתֶּם אֶת זְכַרְיָה (דברי הימים ב כד:כא) וִישַׁעְיָה (יבמות מט:):


**Your sword devoured** your prophets, Zechariah and Isaiah.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:1)


**רְאוּ דְבַר ה׳.** הוֹצִיא לָהֶם צִנְצֶנֶת הַמָּן, אָמַר לָהֶם: רְאוּ כָּאן מַה דְּבַר ה׳ מוֹעִיל (מכילתא בשלח ויסע ה)! **הֲמִדְבָּר הָיִיתִי לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימָה הוּא, לְכָךְ נְקוּדה מ״ם רָפִי וְה״א בַּחֲטַף פַּתָּח: כְּלוּם נִדְמֶה לַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶם שֶׁהוּא מִדְבָּר כְּשֶׁהָלְכוּ בּוֹ, שֶׁהוֹרַדְתִּי לָהֶם לֶחֶם זֶה דְּבַר יוֹם בְּיוֹמוֹ (שמות טז:ד)?


**See the word of the Lord** He took out to them the jar of manna. He said to them, “See here how helpful the word of the Lord is.” **was I a desert to Israel** Heb. המדבר. This is the interrogative form. Therefore, the ‘mem’ is punctuated ‘weak,’ i.e., without a ‘dagesh’ and the ‘heh’ with a ‘hataph- pattah.’ Did it seem to your forefathers that it was a desert when they traversed it? For I brought down for them this bread, the necessity of each day in its day.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:2)


**מַאְפֵּלְיָה.** לְשׁוֹן חֹשֶׁךְ:


**thick darkness** מאפליה, an expression of darkness.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:31:3)


**מַדּוּעַ אָמְרוּ עַמִּי רַדְנוּ.** נִבְדַּלְנוּ מִמְּךָ, כְּמוֹ הָרוֹדֶה פַּת מִן הַתַּנּוּר (תרומות י,ג). כָּךְ מִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, במדבר ב). וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ **רַדְנוּ** לְשׁוֹן יְרִידָה, וְעוֹד פָּנִים אֲחֵרִים:


**Why have My people said, “We have been separated...”?** Heb. רדנו, we have been separated from You. Like one who separates (רודֶה) bread from the oven. So is the midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (Bamidbar 2). Menahem, however, explains רדנוּ as an expression of descending (ירידה) and also in other manners (Machbereth Menahem, p. 162).



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:1)


**תֵּיטִיבִי.** תְּקַשְּׁטִי עַצְמֵךְ לִקְרַאת נוֹאֲפַיִךְ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתֵּיטֶב רֹאשָׁהּ״ דְּאִיזֶבֶל, בְּפָרָשַׁת יֵהוּא (מלכים ב ט:ל):


**How you adorn** Heb. תיטבי. You adorn yourself to greet your adulterers. Comp. “And she adorned her head” (I Kings 9:30) concerning Jezebel in the section dealing with Jehu.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:2)


**גַּם אֶת הָרָעוֹת לִמַּדְתְּ.** חָזַרְתְּ עַל כָּל רוֹעַ דֶּרֶךְ, לִלְמוֹד אֶת הַמְקֻלְקֶלֶת שֶׁבְּכֻלָּם:


**even to the most evil you accustomed** You searched throughout all the evil ways to learn the most corrupt of them all. גם is an expression of ‘even.’


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:3)


**גַּם.** לְשׁוֹן אֲפִילוּ. **לָכֵן.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעַת אֱמֶת, אַךְ **אֶת** הַדֶּרֶךְ הָרָעָה שֶׁבְּכָל הַדְּרָכִים **לִמַּדְתְּ** לְעַצְמֵךְ:


**verily** Heb. לכן, an expression of a true oath. Verily, to the most evil way of all the ways you accustomed yourself.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:33:4)


**לִמַּדְתְּ.** הִנְהַגְתְּ:


**you accustomed** Heb. למדת, lit. you taught.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:1)


**בַּמַּחְתֶּרֶת מְצָאתִים.** לֹא מָצָאתָ אוֹתָם בַּמַּחְתֶּרֶת כְּשֶׁהָרַגְתָּ אוֹתָם:


**you did not find them breaking in** You did not find them breaking in when you slew them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:2)


**מְצָאתִים.** כֹּה יֹאמַר לִנְקֵבָה לְשׁוֹן פָּעַלְתְּ, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בְּסֵפֶר (יחזקאל טז:נח) אֶת נְשָׂאתִים:


**you did not find them** So does Scripture say for a feminine second person singular, and there are many such examples in the Book of Ezekiel (16:58), “You bore them (נשאתים).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:34:3)


**כִּי עַל כָּל אֵלֶּה.** שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹכִיחִים אוֹתְךָ בַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**but for all these** i.e., because they were reproving you for all these things.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:1)


**וַתֹּאמְרִי.** בְּלִבֵּךְ׃


**And you said** i.e., you said in your heart.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:2)


**כִּי נִקֵּיתִי.** אֲשֶׁר זַכָּאָה אֲנִי נַפְשִׁי:


**But I am innocent** That I have purified my soul.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:35:3)


**נִשְׁפָּט אוֹתָךְ.** בָּא עִמָּךְ בְּמִשְׁפָּט:


**I contend with you** I will come with you in judgment.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:1)


**תֵּזְלִי.** לְשׁוֹן זִלְזוּל:


**degrade yourself** Heb. תזלי, an expression of degradation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:2)


**לְשַׁנּוֹת אֶת דַּרְכֵּךְ.** לַעֲזֹב אוֹתִי וְלִסְמֹךְ עַל מִצְרַיִם לְעֶזְרָה, בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים (מלכים ב כד:ז) וְצִדְקִיָּהוּ (ירמיה לז):


**to change your way** To leave Me and to rely on Egypt for aid in the days of Jehoiakim and Zedekiah (infra 37).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:3)


**לְשַׁנּוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן שִׁנּוּי:


**to change** Heb. לשנות, an expression of change.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 2:36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/2:36:4)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר בֹּשְׁתְּ מֵאַשּׁוּר.** שֶׁסָּמַךְ אָחָז עָלָיו, ״וַיָּצַר לוֹ וְלֹא חִזְּקוֹ״ כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (דברי הימים ב כח:כ):


**as you were ashamed through Assyria** Upon whom Ahaz relied and “he oppressed him and did not strengthen him” (II Chron. 28:20).



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:1)


**לֵאמֹר הֵן יְשַׁלַּח וְגוֹ׳.** יֵשׁ לִי לוֹמַר, שֶׁאֵין עוֹד אַתְּ רְאוּיָה לִי, כִּי כֵן דֶּרֶךְ הַמְּשַׁלֵּחַ **אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וְהָלְכָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**to say** I have to say that you are no longer fit for me, for so is the way of one who sends away his wife and she goes away, etc.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:2)


**וְאַתְּ זָנִית רֵעִים רַבִּים.** עִם אוֹהֲבִים רַבִּים; וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן, שׁוּבִי **אֵלַי**:


**But you have played the harlot [with] many lovers** With many lovers. Nevertheless, return to Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:1:3)


**וְשׁוּב אֵלַי.** כְּמוֹ ״עֲשׂוֹת״, וְלָשׁוּב אֵלַי אֲנִי עוֹסֵק בָּךְ:


**yet return to me** Heb. ושוב, lit. and to return to me, like עשות, to do, and to return to Me, I long for you.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:1)


**שְׁפָיִים.** יִבְלֵי מַיִם:


**rivulets** rivulets of water.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:2)


**לֹא שֻׁגַּלְתְּ.** לְשׁוֹן פִּילֶגֶשׁ:


**you were not lain with** Heb. שלגת, an expression of a פילגש a concubine.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:3)


**יָשַׁבְתְּ לָהֶם.** לִהְיוֹת נְכוֹנָה לִקְרָאתָם:


**you sat for them** to be prepared to greet them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:2:4)


**כַּעֲרָבִי.** יוֹשֵׁב אֹהָלִים, שֶׁהוּא תָּמִיד מָצוּי בַּחוּץ, בַּמִּדְבָּרוֹת; וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ הוּא קָרוּי עֲרָבִי, שֶׁשּׁוֹכֵן בָּעֲרָבָה:


**like an Arab** A tent dweller, who is always found outside in the deserts, and because of this, he is called ערבי since he dwells in the ערבה, the plain.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:3:1)


**וּמַלְקוֹשׁ.** כְּמָטָר הַיּוֹרֵד בְּנִיסָן (יואל ב:כג) עַל הַמְּלִילוֹת וְעַל הַקַּשִּׁין. **וּמַלְקוֹשׁ** טְרַדִיוֹ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and the latter rain** The rain that falls in Nissan on the ears (מלילות) and the straws (קשים). מלקוש is (tardif in O.F.).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:4:1)


**הֲלוֹא מֵעַתָּה.** הַלְוַאי שַׁבְתְּ מֵרָעָתֵךְ וְ**קָרָאת לִי אָבִי**, וְאִם תַּעֲשִׂי כָּךְ, **הֲיִנְטֹר** לָךְ אֲדוֹנַיִךְ **לְעוֹלָם** אֵת אֲשֶׁר חָטָאת לוֹ, **אִם יִשְׁמֹר לָנֶצַח**? לֹא יִשְׁמֹר!


**Will you not from now** If only you repent of your evil and call me “My Father.” If you do so, will your Lord bear a grudge forever for what you have sinned? Will He keep it to eternity? He will not keep it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:5:1)


**הִנֵּה דִבַּרְתְּ.** בְּפִיךְ: ״לֹא נָבוֹא עוֹד אֵלֶיךָ״ (ירמיה ב:לא), וְאוֹתָהּ הָרָעָה תַּעֲשִׂי וְלֹא תִתְּנִי לֵב לָשׁוּב:


**Behold, you have spoken** with your mouth, “We will no longer come to You,” and that evil you will do, and you will not give any thought of returning.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:5:2)


**וַתּוּכָל.** וְנִצַּחְתְּ לִמְרוֹד:


**and you were able** And you prevailed in rebelling with a victorious and irrefutable reply.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:1)


**מְשׁוּבָה.** נִצַּחַת (ירמיה ח:ה) וּמוּכַחַת **בִּימֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ הַמֶּלֶךְ** כְּשֶׁצִּוַּנִי לְהַחֲזִיר עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים (מגלה יד:). **הֲרָאִיתָ אֲשֶׁר עָשְׂתָה מְשֻׁבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל** ״הַשּׁוֹבֵבָה״ (ירמיה לא:כא), אַנְבּוֹזִייָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**In the days of King Josiah** when He commanded me to bring back the Ten Tribes, “Have you seen what the backsliding Israel has done? משבה is the same as שובבה, (envejjsede in O.F).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:2)


**יִשְׂרָאֵל.** עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**Israel** The Ten Tribes.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:3)


**הוֹלְכָה הִיא.** בְּעוֹדָהּ עַל אַדְמָתָהּ, קוֹדֶם שֶׁגָּלְתָה בִּימֵי חִזְקִיָּהוּ עִם הוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה (מלכים ב יז):


**she goes** i.e., when she was still on her land before she was exiled in the days of Hezekiah with Hoshea son of Elah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:4)


**הוֹלְכָה.** הָיְתָה הוֹלֶכֶת **עַל כָּל הַר גָּבֹהַּ**:


הלכה means, she would go on every lofty mountain.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:6:5)


**וַתִּזְנִי שָׁם.** לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה. **וַתִּזְנִי** כְּמוֹ **וַתִּזֶן**: יוֹ״ד יְתֵרָה:


**and she played the harlot there** i.e., she played the harlot by worshipping idols. וַתִּזְנִי is the equivalent of וַתִּזֶן, the ‘yod’ being superfluous.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:7:1)


**וָאֹמַר.** עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי עָמוֹס וְהוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן בְּאֵרִי וּשְׁאָר נְבִיאִים: **אֵלַי תָּשׁוּב**:


**And I said** through My prophets, Amos and Hosea son of Beeri and the other prophets, “You shall return to Me.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:7:2)


**וַתֵּרֶא בָּגוֹדָה אֲחוֹתָהּ יְהוּדָה.** כָּל זֶה. **בֹּגֵדָה** קָשָׁה מִ**מְּשׁוּבָה**: יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהֵם רִאשׁוֹנִים לְקִלְקוּל וְלֹא רָאוּ פֻּרְעָנוּת לִלְמֹד מִמֶּנּוּ וְלָשׁוּב, קְרוּיִין **מְשׁוּבָה**; יְהוּדָה, שֶׁרָאוּ שֶׁגָּלוּ אֵלּוּ וְלֹא לָקְחוּ מוּסָר, קְרוּיִין **בֹּגֵדָה**:


**And her treacherous sister Judah saw it** i.e., saw all this. The appellation “treacherous” is harsher than “backsliding.” Israel, since they were the first to sin, and they did not see any retribution from which to learn and to repent, are called “backsliding.” Judah, who saw that these went in exile and did not receive correction, are called “treacherous.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:8:1)


**וָאֵרֶא כִּי עַל כָּל אוֹדוֹת וגו׳.** נְקֻדּוֹת טַעֲמֵי מִקְרָא זֶה מְלַמְּדִין עַל פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: **יִשְׂרָאֵל** נָקוּד זָקֵף, מֻבְדָּל מִשִּׁלַּחְתִּיהָ; **שִׁלַּחְתִּיהָ** נָקוּד זָקֵף גָּדוֹל לְעַצְמוֹ. וְזֶה פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: **וָאֵרֶא** נִסְתַּכַּלְתִּי בָּהּ לִיפָּרַע מִמֶּנָּה, וְלָמָּה? **כִּי עַל כָּל אוֹדוֹת אֲשֶׁר נִאֲפָה מְשׁוּבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל**, וּמָה הָיְתָה נִקְמָתִי? **שִׁלַּחְתִּיהָ** מֵעַל פָּנַי. **וְלֹא יָרְאָה בֹּגֵדָה יְהוּדָה** שֶׁרָאֲתָה בְּפוּרְעָנוּת שֶׁל שׁוֹמְרוֹן, לָתֵת לִבָּהּ לָשׁוּב:


**And I saw, because of all that backsliding Israel had committed adultery** The signs of the intonation of this verse teach us its explanation. ישראל is punctuated with a ‘zakef-katan,’ separated from שלחתיה, I sent her away. שלחתיה, I sent her away, is punctuated with a ‘zakef-gadol’ by itself. And this is its explanation: I saw; i.e., I contemplated her to exact punishment upon her. And why? Because of all that backsliding Israel had committed adultery. And what was My vengeance? I sent her away from before Me. And treacherous Judah did not fear when she saw the retribution of Samaria, to set her heart to repent.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:9:1)


**וַתִּנְאַף אֶת הָאֶבֶן.** ״וְטָעַת עִם פָּלְחֵי אַבְנַיָּא וְאָעֵיָא״, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **מִקֹּל זְנוּתָהּ** לְשׁוֹן קַלּוּת: ״מִדְּקַלִּילָא בְּעֵינָהָא טָעֲוָותָהָא״:


**and she committed adultery with the stones** Jonathan paraphrases: And she strayed with the worshippers of stones and wood. And Jonathan renders מִקֹל as an expression of lightness, since her idols were light in her eyes.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:10:1)


**וְגַם בְּכָל זֹאת.** שֶׁרָאוּ בְּקִלְקוּלַת פֻּרְעָנוּת חֲבֵרֵיהֶם:


**And yet for all this** that they saw the misfortune of the retribution of their companions.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:10:2)


**כִּי אִם בְּשֶׁקֶר.** דּוֹרוֹ שֶׁל יֹאשִׁיָּה מַרְאִין עַצְמָם שֶׁהֵם צַדִּיקִים, וְהֵם רְשָׁעִים הָיוּ. צָרִים צוּרוֹת שֶׁל עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה עַל דַּלְתוֹתֵיהֶם מִבִּפְנִים, חֶצְיָהּ עַל זוֹ וְחֶצְיָהּ עַל זוֹ, וּכְשֶׁהָיוּ מְבַעֲרֵי עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה בּוֹדְקִים, הָיָה הַדֶּלֶת פָּתוּחַ וְלֹא הָיוּ מַכִּירִין בָּהּ:


**but falsely** Josiah’s generation would show themselves as righteous although they were wicked. They would make forms of pagan deities on the inside of their doors, half on this door and half on this one, and when the destroyers of idols would inspect, the door would be open, and they would not notice it.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:11:1)


**צִדְּקָה נַפְשָׁהּ מְשֻׁבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** נִקְּתָה וּפָטְרָה עַצְמָהּ מִן הַדִּין, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לָהּ מִמִּי לִלְמוֹד:


**has proved herself more righteous** She has been cleared and she has freed herself from an unfavorable verdict, for she had no one to learn from.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:1)


**הָלוֹךְ וְקָרָאתָ וְגוֹ׳.** הָלוֹךְ כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אֲזִיל״, לְשׁוֹן צִוּוּי; כָּאן צִוָּהוּ לֵילֵךְ וּלְהַחֲזִיר עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים בִּימֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ (מגלה יד:), כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה (פסוק ו), שֶׁבִּימֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ נֶאֶמְרָה לוֹ נְבוּאָה זוֹ; וְחָזְרוּ מִקְצָת מֵהֶן בִּשְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ (דברי הימים ב לה,יז-יט):


**Go and proclaim, etc.** Go, as the Targum renders, אזיל, the imperative form. Here he commanded him to go and bring back the Ten Tribes during the days of Josiah, as he states above that during the days of Josiah this prophecy was said to him and some of them returned in the eighteenth year of Josiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:2)


**צָפוֹנָה.** אֶל הַמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁגָּלוּ שָׁם לְאַשּׁוּר:


**to the north** To the places where they were exiled there to Assyria.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:12:3)


**פָּנַי.** ״רוּגְזִי״, כֵּן תַּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**My anger** My anger, so did Jonathan render it.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:13:1)


**פָּשָׁעְתְּ.** מָרַדְתְּ:


**you have rebelled** Heb. פשעת.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:13:2)


**וַתְּפַזְּרִי אֶת דְּרָכַיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן זְנוּת; פִּסּוּק רַגְלַיִם לְמִשְׁכְּבֵי אִשָּׁה:


**and you have scattered your ways** This is an expression of harlotry, spreading the legs to the woman’s private parts.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:14:1)


**שׁוֹבָבִים.** אַנְוִוישִׁי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, שֶׁשּׁוֹבַבְתֶּם דַּרְכְּכֶם מֵרוֹב כֹּל (דברים כח:מז), שֶׁהֱיִיתֶם בְּשַׁלְוָה וְעֹנֶג, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר ״הַפּוֹרְטִים עַל פִּי הַנָּבֶל״ (עמוס ו:ה), ״הַשֹּׁתִים בְּמִזְרְקֵי יַיִן״ (עמוס ו:ו):


**backsliding children** (Envasec in O.F.) That you perverted your way because of much good, that you enjoyed tranquility and pleasure, as Scripture states: They hum snatches of song to the tune of the lute (Amos 6:5), those who drink from bowls of wine (v. 6).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:14:2)


**כִּי אָנֹכִי בָּעַלְתִּי בָכֶם.** וְנִקְרֵאתֶם בִּשְׁמִי, שֶׁאֲנִי אָדוֹן לָכֶם, וְאֵין כְּבוֹדִי לְהַנִּיחַ אֶתְכֶם בְּיַד אוֹיְבִי:


**for I possessed you** and you are called by My name, that I am your Master, and it is not honorable for Me to leave you in the hands of My enemies.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:1)


**לֹא יֹאמְרוּ עוֹד אֲרוֹן.** כִּי כָל כְּנִיסַתְכֶם תְּהֵא קְדוֹשָׁה, וְאֶשְׁכּוֹן בָּהּ כְּאִלּוּ הוּא אֲרוֹן:


**they will no longer say, “The ark of the Lord’s covenant”** For your entire assembly will be holy, and I will dwell therein as though it were an ark.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:2)


**יִפְקֹדוּ.** כְּמוֹ **יִזְכְּרוּ**:


**nor shall they remember it** Heb. יפקדו, like יזכרוּ, remember or mention.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:16:3)


**וְלֹא יֵעָשֶׂה עוֹד.** מַה שֶּׁנַּעֲשָׂה בּוֹ כְּבָר בְּשִׁילֹה, שֶׁהֱבִיאוּהוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָה עִם פְּלִשְׁתִּים, בִּימֵי עֵלִי (שמואל א ד):


**nor shall it be done anymore** i.e., it shall not be done what was done with it already in Shiloh, that they brought it into the battle with the Philistines in the days of Eli.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:17:1)


**וְנִקְווּ.** וְנִקְבְּצוּ, לְשׁוֹן ״מִקְוֵה מַיִם״ (ויקרא יא:לו):


**shall gather** Heb. ונקוו, an expression of: מקוה מים, a gathering of water.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:17:2)


**שְׁרִירוּת.** לְשׁוֹן רְאִיָּה, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ״ (במדבר כד, יז):


**the idea of** an expression of seeing. Comp. אשורנו “I see him” (Num. 24:17).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:18:1)


**יֵלְכוּ בֵית יְהוּדָה עַל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל.** עִמָּם יִתְחַבְּרוּ וְיִתְוַסְּפוּ עֲלֵיהֶם לִהְיוֹת מַמְלָכָה אַחַת:


**the house of Judah will go with the house of Israel** They will join them and they will be added to them to be one kingdom.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:1)


**וְאָנֹכִי אָמַרְתִּי.** בְּלִבִּי, וְלָכֵן הִנְחַלְתִּים לַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶם:


**And I said** to Myself, etc. and therefore, I caused your forefathers to inherit them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:2)


**אֵיךְ אֲשִׁיתֵךְ בַּבָּנִים.** אֶת, עֲדָתִי וְאֻמָּתִי, בְּתוֹךְ שְׁאָר בָּנִים, לְפִיכָךְ בֵּרַרְתִּי מָנָה יָפָה **וְאֶתֶּן לָךְ אֶרֶץ חֶמְדָּה**:


**How shall I place you among the sons?** i.e., how shall I place you, my congregation and My nation, among the other sons, mingled with the heathens. I, therefore, selected a handsome portion, and I gave you a desirable land.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:19:3)


**צְבִי צִבְאוֹת גּוֹיִם.** צִבְיוֹן כָּל צִבְאוֹת גּוֹיִם (תנחומא, משפטים יז):


**the beauty of hosts of nations** The beauty of all the hosts of nations.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:20:1)


**אָכֵן.** אֲבָל אַתֶּם לֹא עֲשִׂיתֶם מַחֲשַׁבְתִּי, כִּי אִם כְּמוֹ אִשָּׁה שֶׁבּוֹגְדָה מֵרֵעָהּ עַל שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַסְפִּיק מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ, **כֵּן בְּגַדְתֶּם בִּי** – הַמַּסְפִּיק לָכֶם כָּל טוֹב; כָּךְ מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, משפטים יז):


**Truly** But you did not live up to My expectations [lit., do My thought], but like a woman who betrays her beloved because he cannot provide her sustenance, so did you betray Me, Who provides you with all good. So is the Midrash Aggadah (Tanhuma Mishpatim 17).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:21:1)


**עַל שְׁפָיִים נִשְׁמָע.** קָרוֹב הוּא לְהִשָּׁמַע:


**A voice on the rivulets is heard** It is likely to be heard.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:22:1)


**הִנְנוּ אָתָנוּ לָךְ.** הַנָּבִיא מְלַמְּדָם לְהִתְוַדּוֹת וְלוֹמַר כֵּן: **הִנְנוּ אָתָנוּ לָךְ**, **אָכֵן לַשֶּׁקֶר וְגוֹ**, **וְהַבֹּשֶׁת אָכְלָה וְגוֹ׳ נִשְׁכְּבָה בְבָשְׁתֵּנוּ**. כָּל זֶה לְשׁוֹן תְּפִלָּה שֶׁהַנָּבִיא מְלַמְּדָם לוֹמַר:


**Here we are, we have come to You** The prophet teaches them to confess and to say as follows, “Here we are, we have come to You... Truly, in vain... But the shameful thing has consumed, etc., Let us lie in our shame.” All this is the language of the prayer that the prophet teaches them to say.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:22:2)


**אָתָנוּ.** בָּאנוּ:


We have come אתנו.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:23:1)


**אָכֵן לַשֶּׁקֶר.** שָׁמַרְנוּ מִגְּבָעוֹת וְהָמוֹן הָרִים שֶׁעָבַדְנוּ עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שָׁם (על פי שמואל א כה:כא):


**Truly, in vain** we awaited from the hills and the multitude of mountains where we worshipped idols.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:23:2)


**אָכֵן.** בֶּאֱמֶת:


**Truly** in truth.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:24:1)


**וְהַבֹּשֶׁת.** עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁעָסַקְנוּ בָּהּ; כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמֵר ״וַיִּנָּזְרוּ לַבֹּשֶׁת״ (הושע ט:י):


**But the shameful thing** The idolatry with which we occupied ourselves, as Scripture states. “And they turned aside to the shameful thing” (Hosea 9:10).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 3:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/3:24:2)


**אָכְלָה אֶת יְגִיעַ אֲבוֹתֵינוּ.** בַּעֲוֹן עֲבוֹדָתָהּ הָיָה יְגִיעֵנוּ לָבַז:


**consumed the toil of our fathers** Because of the iniquity of its worship, our toil was pillaged.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:1:1)


**אִם תָּשׁוּב יִשְׂרָאֵל.** בִּתְשׁוּבָה זוֹ **אֵלַי תָּשׁוּב** לִכְבוֹדְךָ וּגְדֻלָּתְךָ הָרִאשׁוֹנָה:


**If you return, O Israel** with this repentance, to Me, you will return to your original glory and greatness.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:1:2)


**וְאִם תָּסִיר שִׁקּוּצֶיךָ מִפָּנַי.** אָז **לֹא תָנוּד** – לָצֵאת בַּגּוֹלָה:


**and if you remove your detestable things from My Presence** Then you shall not wander to go out in exile.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:1)


**וְנִשְׁבַּעְתָּ חַי ה׳ בֶּאֱמֶת.** כְּשֶׁתִּשָּׁבַע בִּשְׁמִי תִּשָּׁבַע **בֶּאֱמֶת**, וְלֹא כַּאֲשֶׁר עַתָּה, שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּכֶם ״אִם חַי ה׳ יֹאמְרוּ אָכֵן לְשֶׁקֶר יִשָּׁבֵעוּ״ (ירמיה ה:ב):


**And you will swear, “As the Lord lives,” in truth** When you swear by My name, you will swear in truth, not as now, that it is written concerning you, “And if they say, ‘As the Lord lives,’ surely they swear falsely” (5:2).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:2)


**וְהִתְבָּרְכוּ בוֹ גוֹיִם.** אִם תַּעֲשׂוּ כֵּן, אָז יִתְבָּרְכוּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל הַגּוֹיִם; כָּל גּוֹי יֹאמַר לִבְנוֹ: תְּהֵא כִּפְלוֹנִי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**nations will bless themselves with him** If you do so, then nations will bless themselves with Israel. Every non-Jew will say to his son, “You shall be like So-and-so the Jew.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:2:3)


**יִתְהַלָּלוּ.** יַגִּידוּ שִׁבְחֲכֶם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **יִתְהַלָּלוּ** – מִי שֶׁיּוּכַל לְהִדָּבֵק בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, יִתְפָּאֵר בַּדָּבָר; פורוונטי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**boast** Heb. יתהללו, they will recite your praise. Another explanation of is יתהללו: Whoever is able to cleave to Israel will boast about the matter (porvanter in French).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:3:1)


**כִּי כֹה אָמַר ה׳ וגו׳ נִירוּ לָכֶם נִיר.** – הֱווּ לְמֵדִין מֵעוֹבְדֵי אֲדָמָה, שֶׁנָּרִים אוֹתָהּ בִּימֵי הַקַּיִץ לְהָמִית שָׁרְשֵׁי הָעֲשָׂבִים, שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא מַעֲלָה **קוֹצִים** בְּעֵת הַזֶּרַע בַּחֹרֶף; אַף אַתֶּם הֵטִיבוּ מַעֲשֵׂיכֶם בְּטֶרֶם תָּבֹא עֲלֵיכֶם הָרָעָה, שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא תְּפִלַּתְכֶם נִמְאֶסֶת כְּשֶׁתִּצְעֲקוּ:


**For so said the Lord, etc. Plow for yourself a furrow** Learn from the tillers of the soil, who plow it in the summer to kill the roots of the grasses, that it should not grow thorns at the time of sowing in the winter. You, too, improve your deeds before the evil befalls you, so that your prayer should not be rejected when you cry out.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:3:2)


**וְאַל תִּזְרְעוּ אֶל קוֹצִים.** וְאַל תְּהִי צַעֲקַתְכֶם לְפָנַי מִתּוֹךְ הָרֶשַׁע כִּי אִם מִתּוֹךְ תְּשׁוּבָה, שֶׁלֹּא תִהְיוּ דּוֹמִים לְזוֹרֵעַ בְּלֹא נִיר, וְהִיא נֶהְפֶּכֶת לְקוֹצִים; קַרְדוֹנְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and do not sow upon thorns** And let not your cry before Me be from amidst wickedness, but from amidst repentance, that you shall not resemble one sowing without plowing, and that turns into thorns (kardons in O.F., chardons in modern French).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:4:1)


**הִמּוֹלוּ לַה׳.** בְּטֶרֶם **תֵּצֵא חֲמָתִי כָאֵשׁ**; זֶהוּ כְּנִיר לִפְנֵי הַזֶּרַע (ירמיה ג:ג):


**Circumcise yourselves to the Lord** Before My anger comes forth like fire. This is like plowing before the sowing.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:5:1)


**וְאִמְרוּ תִּקְעוּ שׁוֹפָר.** הַזְהִירוּ אֶת יוֹשְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ, שֶׁיִּתְקְעוּ בְּשׁוֹפָר לִבְרוֹחַ:


**and say, “Sound a shophar...”** Warn the inhabitants of the land that they sound the shophar to flee.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:5:2)


**מַלְאוּ.** לְשׁוֹן אֲסִיפָה, וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בַּמִּקְרָא: ״אֲשֶׁר יִקָּרֵא עָלָיו מְלֹא רֹעִים״ (ישעיהו לא:ד); ״קָרְאוּ אַחֲרֶיךָ מָלֵא״ (ירמיהו יב:ו); ״יַחַד עָלַי יִתְמַלָּאוּן״ (איוב טז:י):


**assemble** Heb. מלאו. An expression of gathering, and there are many in the Scriptures: (Isaiah 31: 4) “Although a band of (מְלֹא) shepherds gather against him,” Also (infra 12:6) “They called after you a band (מָלֵא).” Also (Job 16:10) “Together they gather (יתמלאוּן) against me.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:6:1)


**שְׂאוּ נֵס.** לִבְרוֹחַ לְצִיּוֹן:


**Raise a standard** to flee to Zion.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:6:2)


**הָעִיזוּ.** הֵאָסְפוּ, כְּמוֹ ״שְׁלַח הָעֵז״ (שמות ט:יט):


**assemble** Heb. העיזו. Comp. “Send, gather (העז)” (Exodus 9:19).



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:1)


**עָלָה אַרְיֵה.** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (מגלה יא.):


**A lion has come up** That is Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:2)


**מִסֻּבְּכוֹ.** דֶּרֶךְ אַרְיֵה לֵישֵׁב בְּמַאֲרַב סִבְכֵי הַיַּעַר (ישעיה י:לד); אשפיישי״ש בְּלַעַז:


**from his thicket** It is customary for a lion to lie in wait in the thickets of the forest (espaises in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:7:3)


**תִּצֶּינָה.** לְשׁוֹן ״צִיָּה״ (ירמיה ב:ו):


**will be desolate** Heb. תצינה, an expression of desolation.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:8:1)


**עַל זֹאת.** מַה הוּא זֹאת? **כִּי לֹא שָׁב וְגוֹ׳**. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדִּבֵּר לְמַעְלָה בִּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר עַל הַחֻרְבָּן, כָּאן חָזַר לְדַבֵּר בְּמִיתַת יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ:


**Because of this** What is “this?” “For the fierce anger etc.” Although he spoke above concerning Nebuchadnezzar in regards to the destruction, here he returns to speak of the death of Josiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:8:2)


**כִּי לֹא שָׁב חֲרוֹן אַף וְגוֹ׳.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשָּׁב יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ בְּכָל לֵב, נֶאֱמַר בּוֹ (מלכים ב כג:כו) ״לֹא שָׁב ה׳ מֵחֲרוֹן אַפּוֹ עַל כָּל הַכְּעָסִים אֲשֶׁר הִכְעִיסוֹ מְנַשֶּׁה״:


**for the fierce anger of the Lord etc.** Although Josiah repented wholeheartedly, it is stated concerning him. (II Kings 23:26)... “the Lord did not turn back from his great wrath...because of all the provocations that Manasseh had provoked Him.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:9:1)


**וְהָיָה בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** בְּיוֹמוֹ שֶׁל יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ יִתְמְהוּ: לָמָּה עָלְתָה בּוֹ כָּל כָּךְ? וְהֵם אֵין יוֹדְעִין שֶׁאֵין דּוֹרוֹ חוֹזֵר יָפֶה; כְּשֶׁבָּא לְבַעֵר אֶת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה מֶה הָיוּ לֵיצָנֵי הַדּוֹר עוֹשִׂין? חוֹקְקִין צוּרַת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה עַל דַּלְתוֹתֵיהֶן, חֶצְיָהּ בְּדֶלֶת זֶה וְחֶצְיָהּ בְּדֶלֶת זֶה; כְּשֶׁהָיָה פּוֹתְחוֹ לֹא הָיְתָה נִכֶּרֶת, וּכְשֶׁהָלַךְ מִשָּׁם סוֹגְרָהּ וְהִיא מִתְחַבֶּרֶת:


**And it shall be on that day** On the day of Josiah they will wonder why so much befell him, for they do not know that his generation did not repent properly. When he came to abolish idolatry, what did the scorners of the generation do? They would engrave an image of a pagan deity on their doors, half of it on this door and half of it on this door. When he would open it, it was not discernible, and when he went away from there, he would close it, and it would join together.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:10:1)


**הַשֵּׁא הִשֵּׁאתָ.** אַטִינְטַא״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן ״הַנָּחָשׁ הִשִּׁיאַנִי״ (בראשית ג:יג); שֶׁנְּבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר נִבָּאִים לָכֶם: **שָׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לָכֶם**:


**Surely You have misled** Heb. השא השאת (fa)tenter in O.F.), tempting, an expression like (Gen. 3: 13) “The serpent misled me (השיאני),” for the false prophets prophesy for them, You will have peace.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:11:1
