[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:1)


**לָעָם הַזֶּה.** עַל הָעָם הַזֶּה, כְּשֶׁיִּגְלוּ כֻּלָּם:


**of this people** Concerning this people when they are all exiled.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:2)


**צַח שְׁפָיִים.** מְכַבֵּד וּמְטַאטֵא אַף אֶת שְׂדוֹת יִבְלֵי הַמַּיִם, לְשִׁיתָם מִדְבָּר. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **צַח** לְשׁוֹן ״צָמָא״, דִּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״צְחוּתָא״:


**has swept the rivulets** It sweeps even the fields with rivulets of water to make them into a desert. Another explanation: צח is an expression of thirst, which is translated צחותא.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:3)


**דֶּרֶךְ בַּת עַמִּי.** אוֹתוֹ **רוּחַ** בָּא. וְגַם **לוֹא לִזְרוֹת** מוֹץ מִגֹּרֶן, אהבוניי״ר בְּלַעַז, כְּמוֹ ״וְאֶזְרֵם בְּמִזְרֶה״ (ירמיה טו:ז):


**[on] the way of the daughter of My people** That wind will come, and also, not to winnow the chaff from the threshing floor ([a]vanner in O.F.). Comp. (infra 15:7) “And I winnowed them with a sieve (ואזרם במזרה).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:11:4)


**וְלוֹא לְהָבַר.** וְלִנְקוֹת תְּבוּאָה הוּא בָּא, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא לְטוֹבָה כִּי אִם לְרָעָה, לְהַגְלוֹת אֶת אֲנָשִׁים (וְנָשִׁים) טַף וּבְהֵמָה:


**and not to cleanse** And not to cleanse grain does it come. That is to say, not for good but for evil, to exile men [women], and children, and livestock.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:12:1)


**רוּחַ מָלֵא.** מֵאֵלֶּה הַפֻּרְעָנוּת **יָבוֹא לִי** רוּחַ מָלֵא, רוּחַ שָׁלֵם, שֶׁלֹּא אֶחְזוֹר בּוֹ וְלֹא אֶנָּחֵם. רוּחַ טַלְנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**A full spirit** of these retributions shall come to me. **A full spirit** A full spirit, that I will not retract and not repent. **spirit** (talant in O.F.)


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:12:2)


**מִשְׁפָּטִים.** לְשׁוֹן וִכּוּחַ:


**debate** Heb. משפטים, an expression of debating.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:13:1)


**וְכַסּוּפָה.** אִיקוֹמָאטְרוֹבִילוֹ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and like the whirlwind** (comme tourbillon in O.F.)



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:14:1)


**אוֹנֵךְ.** חֲמָסֵךְ:


**your iniquitous thoughts** Your injustice.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:15:1)


**כִּי קוֹל מַגִּיד מִדָּן.** ״אָרֵי קַל נְבִיָּא מִתְנְבָן עֲלֵיהוֹן דְּגָלוֹן עַל דְּפָלְחוּ לָעֲגָלָה דִּבְדָן וּמְבַשְּׂרָן בְּשׁוֹרָן בַּיְּשָׁן יָתוּן עֲלֵיהוֹן עַל דְּאִשְׁתַּעֲבְדוּ לְצַלְמָא דָּקִים מִיכָה בְּטוּרָא דְּבֵית אֶפְרַיִם״. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכְּבָר גָּלוּ הָעֲגָלִים מִיְּמוֹת סַנְחֵרִב, עֲדַיִן עֲווֹנָם קַיָּם. גְּדוֹלָה הִיא עֲבֵרָה שֶׁשּׁוֹבֶרֶת לְפָנֶיהָ וּלְאַחֲרֶיהָ: לְפָנֶיהָ מִיְּמוֹת אַבְרָהָם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְיִרְדֹּף עַד דָּן״ (בראשית יד:יד), כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְדָן תָּשַׁשׁ כֹּחוֹ שֶׁעֲתִידִין בָּנָיו לַעֲבֹד שָׁם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה; וּלְאַחֲרֶיהָ בְּחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: כִּי קוֹל מַגִּיד מִדָּן:


**For a voice proclaims from Dan** Jonathan paraphrases: “Behold the voice of the prophets prophesy about them that they will be exiled because they worshipped the calf that was in Dan, and people bringing bad tidings will come upon them because they subordinated themselves to the image that Micah erected on the mountain of the house of Ephraim.” Although the calves had already been exiled since the days of Sennacherib, their iniquity was still existing. Sin is harsh, for it destroys [lit. breaks] before it and after it; before it, since the days of Abraham, as it is stated (Gen. 14:14): “And he pursued until Dan.” As soon as he reached Dan, he grew faint [lit. his strength was weakened], for his children would, in the future, worship idols there, and after it, in the destruction of the Temple, as it is stated: “A voice proclaims from Dan.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:15:2)


**וּמַשְׁמִיעַ אָוֶן. אָוֶן** שֶׁבֶר:


**and announces calamity** Heb. און, a breach.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:16:1)


**נוֹצְרִים.** נוֹתְנֵי מָצוֹר עֲלֵיהֶם:


**besiegers** Heb. נצרים. Those who will make a siege upon them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:17:1)


**שָׂדַי.** כְּמוֹ שָׂדֶה:


**a field** like שדה, a field.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:17:2)


**אוֹתִי מָרָתָה.** הִקְנִיטָה; כְּמוֹ ״מַמְרִים הֱיִיתֶם״ (דברים ט:כד) – מַרְגְּזִין:


**for she has rebelled against me** She has provoked Me. Comp. (Deut. 9: 24) “You have been rebellious (ממרים),” provoking.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:18:1)


**עָשׂוּ אֵלֶּה.** לַעֲשׂוֹת אֵלֶּה לָךְ:


**have done these to you** To do these to you.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:1)


**אוֹחִילָה.** לְשׁוֹן חִיל וְרֶתֶת:


**I writhe in pain** Heb. אוחילה. An expression of writhing in pain (חיל) and shuddering.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:2)


**קִירוֹת.** אַנְטִינָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the walls of my heart!** (ettancon in O.F.), blocking wall, perhaps from late Latin, intimus, innermost.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:19:3)


**שָׁמְעַת נַפְשִׁי.** מֵאֲחוֹרֵי הַפַּרְגּוֹד (חגיגה טו.) בָּאַתְנִי בְּשׂוֹרַת קוֹל מִ**תְּרוּעַת מִלְחָמָה**:


**you, my soul, have heard** From behind the curtain of heaven the ridings of the sound of the alarm of war reached me.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:20:1)


**שֶׁבֶר עַל שֶׁבֶר.** חָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַל גָּלוּת יְכָנְיָה:


**Destruction upon destruction** The destruction of Jerusalem upon the exile of Jeconiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:20:2)


**רֶגַע יְרִיעוֹתָי.** רֶגַע שֻׁדְּדוּ יְרִיעוֹתָי:


**in a moment my curtains** In a moment my curtains were plundered.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:21:1)


**עַד מָתַי אֶרְאֶה.** הָאֲסִיפוֹת הָאֵלֶּה הַנּוֹשְׂאִים **נֵס**, לְהִקָּבֵץ לָבֹא לְהִלָּחֵם:


**How long will I see** these groups carrying the standard to come to wage war.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:24:1)


**הִתְקַלְקָלוּ.** נִתְּקוּ וְנִזְרְקוּ מִמְּקוֹמָן; וְעִם ״קַלְקַל בַּחִצִּים״ (יחזקאל כא:כו) חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**moved to and fro** Heb. התקלקלו, they were uprooted and cast out of their place. Menahem associates it with: “He shakes the arrows to and fro (קלקל)” (Ezek. 21:26).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:26:1)


**וְהִנֵּה הַכַּרְמֶל.** אֶרֶץ הַנְּטוּעַ כָּל טוֹב כַּכַּרְמֶל נִהְיָה **מִדְבָּר**. מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: רָאִיתִי הֶהָרִים אֵלּוּ הָאָבוֹת, וְכָל הַגְּבָעוֹת אֵלּוּ אִמָּהוֹת (ראש השנה יא.), כְּלוֹמַר זְכוּתָן תַּמָּה, אֵין הָאָדָם, זְכוּת מֹשֶׁה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ עָנִיו מִכָּל הָאָדָם (במדבר יב,ג), **וְהִנֵּה הַכַּרְמֶל** אֵלִיָּהוּ:


**and behold, the fruitful field** The land, planted with all good like a forest, has become like a desert. The Midrash Aggadah states: “I saw the mountains”These are the Patriarchs. “And all the hills”These are the Matriarchs. That is to say that their merit was depleted. “There were no people”This refers to Moses, about whom it is said: “humbler than all the people” (Num. 12:3). “And behold the Karmel”This is Elijah.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:27:1)


**וְכָלָה לֹא אֶעֱשֶׂה.** בְּיוֹשְׁבֶיהָ, כִּי אוֹתִיר פְּלִיטִים שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ בַּגּוֹלָה:


**but I will not make an end** of its inhabitants, for I will leave over refugees who will go into exile.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:1)


**תֶּאֱבַל.** תֶּחֱרַב:


**shall mourn** Shall be destroyed.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:2)


**זַמּוֹתִי.** לְשׁוֹן מַחֲשָׁבָה:


**I planned** Heb. זמתי, an expression of thought.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:3)


**עַל כִּי דִבַּרְתִּי.** הַפּוּרְעָנוּת הַזֶּה, וַאֲקַיְּמֶנָּה:


**for that which I spoke** i.e., this retribution, and I will fulfill it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:28:4)


**וְלֹא אָשׁוּב.** וְלֹא אֶנָּחֵם, לַחְשֹׁב מַחְשָׁבוֹת טוֹבוֹת עֲלֵיהֶם:


**and I will not turn back** And I will not repent to think good thoughts about them.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:1)


**וְרוֹמֵה.** כְּמוֹ ״רָמָה בַיָּם״ (שמות טו:א):


**archers** Heb. רמה קשת, lit. bow shooters. Comp. “Cast (רמה) into the sea” (Exodus 15:1).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:2)


**בָּאוּ בֶעָבִים.** בַּיְּעָרוֹת עָבִים, מְקוֹם סִיכּוּךְ קוֹצִים לְהִטָּמֵן:


**they came into the thickets** Into the thick forests, a place intertwined with thorns, to hide.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:29:3)


**וּבַכֵּפִים עָלוּ.** ״סֶלַע״ (ירמיה כג:כט) מְתֻרְגָּם ״בְּכִיפַיָּא״:


**and they ascended among the boulders** Heb. ובכפים. סלע, a rock, is translated into Aramaic as בכפיא.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:1)


**כִּי תִלְבְּשִׁי שָׁנִי.** לְהִתְנָאוֹת וְלִמְצֹא רַחֲמִים לִפְנֵי הַשּׁוֹדֵד:


**that you wear crimson** to beautify yourself and to gain clemency before the plunderer.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:2)


**תִּקְרְעִי בַפּוּךְ.** לְשׁוֹן קְרִיעָה, שֶׁהַפּוּךְ נִרְאֶה כְּמַרְחִיב קֶרַע הָעַיִן; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. וְגַם מְנַחֵם לֹא חָלַק, בְּמַחְבֶּרֶת קֶרַע:


**that you enlarge...with paint** Heb. תקרעי. An expression of tearing, for the paint appears as widening the opening of the eye. So have I heard, and Menahem did not differentiate in the group of קרע.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:3)


**פּוּךְ.** לְשׁוֹן כָּחוֹל (שבת ח,ג):


**paint** Heb. בפוך, an expression of a blue paint.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:4)


**עוֹגְבִים.** הַמְּשַׂחֲקִים עִם הַנָּשִׁים בְּצִדֵּי מִינֵי נִיאוּף; כְּמוֹ ״וַתַּעְגְּבָה עַל פִּלַגְשֵׁיהֶם״ (יחזקאל כג:כ):


**lovers** Heb. עגבים, those who jest with the women with various types of adultery. Comp. (Ezekiel 23:20) “She lusted (ותעגבה) for concubinage with them.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:30:5)


**נַפְשֵׁךְ יְבַקֵּשׁוּ.** מְבַקְּשִׁים לְהָרְגֵךְ:


**they seek your life** They seek to kill you.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:1)


**כְּמַבְכִּירָה.** כְּאִשָּׁה הַמְּבַכֶּרֶת תְּחִלַּת לֵידָתָהּ:


**like a woman bearing her first child** Heb. כמבכירה, like a woman bearing her first child, the beginning of her birth.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:2)


**תִּתְיַפֵּחַ.** תְּיַלֵּל וְתִצְעַק גְּנִיחוֹת; קונפליינ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּמוֹ ״וִיפֵחַ חָמָס״ (תהלים כז:יב) – לְשׁוֹן דִּבּוּר:


**who will wail** Heb. תתיפח, she will wail and cry out with sighs (konpljjnt in O. F:). Comp. “who speak (ויפח) violence” (Psalms 27:12), an expression of speech.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 4:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/4:31:3)


**אוֹי נָא לִי.** כָּךְ אוֹמֶרֶת:


**“Woe is to me...”** This is what she says.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:1)


**ה׳ עֵינֶיךָ הֲלוֹא.** הֵם בַּעֲלֵי אֱמוּנָה לְהֵיטִיב, וְלָמָּה בָּגְדוּ אֵלֶּה בָּךְ וְאֵין מְבַקְּשִׁין אֱמוּנָה?


**O Lord, Your eyes** are they not on people with faith, to do good to them? Now why have these betrayed You, and they do not seek faith?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:2)


**הִכִּיתָה אוֹתָם.** וְלֹא נַעֲשׂוּ חוֹלִים עַל הַמַּכָּה, לָשׂוּם עַל לִבָּם לָשׁוּב מֵרָעָתָם:


**You smote them** and they did not become ill because of the blow to lay it to their heart to repent of their evil.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:3:3)


**קַחַת.** כְּמוֹ לָקַחַת:


**to receive** Heb. קחת, equivalent to (לקחת), to receive.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:4:1)


**וַאֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי.** הַנָּבִיא אוֹמֵר כָּךְ. **אַךְ דַּלִּים הֵם** מֵחָכְמָה, אֵלּוּ שֶׁאֲנִי מְדַבֵּר עִמָּהֶם:


**And I said** The prophet says so, “They are but impoverished of wisdom,” these with whom I speak.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:4:2)


**נוֹאֲלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״אֱוִיל״ (ירמיה ד:כב), כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אִיטְפַּשׁוּ״:


**they have become foolish** Heb. נואלו, an expression of אויל, a fool, as the Targum renders: אטפשו.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:5:1)


**אֵלְכָה לִי אֶל הַגְּדוֹלִים.** הֵם יִשְׁמְעוּ; **אַךְ** לֹא הוֹעַלְתִּי, וְגַם **הֵמָּה יַחְדָּו שָׁבְרוּ עֹל** שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵעֲלֵיהֶם:


**I will go to the great ones** They will hearken. But I did not avail, and they too together broke the yoke of the Holy One, blessed be He, off them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:1)


**אַרְיֵה.** מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל:


**a lion** The kingdom of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:2)


**זְאֵב.** מַלְכוּת מָדַי:


**a wolf** The kingdom of Media.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:3)


**נָמֵר.** מַלְכוּת אַשּׁוּר:


**a leopard** The kingdom of Greece.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:4)


**יִטָּרֵף.** בֶּאֱדוֹם (אסתר רבה, פתיחתא ה):


**shall be torn to pieces** by Edom.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:5)


**זְאֵב עֲרָבוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן ״עֲרָבָה וְשׁוּחָה״ (ירמיה ב:ו): זְאֵב הַמִּדְבָּר:


**a wolf of the deserts** Heb. ערבות, an expression of (supra 2:6) ערבה ושוחה “plains and pits,” a wolf of the desert.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:6:6)


**שׁוֹקֵד.** מְמַהֵר, וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם לְשׁוֹן שְׁמִירָה, כְּמוֹ ״לִשְׁקֹד עַל דַּלְתוֹתַי״ (משלי ח:לד). וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם לְשׁוֹן אֲרִיבָה:


**hastens** Heb. שקד. Menahem (p. 180) associates it with an expression of watching. Comp. (Prov. 8:34) “To watch (לשקוד) My doors daily.” And Jonathan rendered it as an expression of lying in wait.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:7:1)


**אֵי לָזֹאת אֶסְלַח לָךְ.** בִּתְמִיהָּ: שֶׁמָּא לָזֹאת אֶסְלַח לָךְ?! וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ אֵי כְּמוֹ אַיִן. אֲשֶׁר **בָּנַיִךְ עֲזָבוּנִי וָאַשְׂבִּעַ אוֹתָם** כָּל טוֹב:


**Shall I forgive you for this?** This is the rhetorical question, Shall I forgive you for this? And Menahem (p. 22) explains אי like אין, not. For this I will not forgive you, because your children have forsaken Me, when I sated them with all good.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:7:2)


**יִתְגּוֹדָדוּ.** יִתְקַבְּצוּ גְדוּדִים:


**they banded together** They gather in troops.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:8:1)


**סוּסִים מְיֻזָּנִים מַשְׁכִּים הָיוּ.** בְּכָל בֹּקֶר הָיוּ. **מַשְׁכִּים** – לְאוֹר בֹּקֶר בְּהַשְׁכָּמָה:


**Like armed stallions they would arise early** Like these stallions who are armed with erect membra, every morning they would arise at daybreak.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:8:2)


**יִצְהָלוּ.** לָשׁוֹן זֶה נוֹפֵל עַל הַסּוּסִים: ״מִקּוֹל מִצְהֲלוֹת אַבִּירָיו״ (ירמיה ח:טז). לְשׁוֹן צַהֲלָה – אֵישְׁיוּדְמִינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**they would neigh** Heb. יצהלו. This too is an expression appropriate for horses. Comp. (8:16) “From the sound of the neighing (מצהלות) of his steeds,” an expression of shouting (esjodement in O.F.).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:10:1)


**בְשָׁרוֹתֶיהָ.** לְשׁוֹן מִישׁוֹר, וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן חוֹמָה עִם ״אֲדַלֵּג שׁוּר״ (תהלים יח:ל), אַךְ לֹא יִתָּכְנוּ **נְטִישׁוֹתֶיהָ** צִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, שֶׁהֵם שָׁרְשֵׁי כֶּרֶם, לִהְיוֹת נֶחְשָׁבִים עִם הַחוֹמוֹת וְלֹא עִם הַמִּישׁוֹר. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר **שָׁרוֹתֶיהָ** לְשׁוֹן שׁוּרוֹת הַכֶּרֶם. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **בְּשָׁרוֹתֶיהָ** ״בְּקִירוֹהָא״, וּ**נְטִישׁוֹתֶיהָ** ״בְּרָנִיתָהָא״:


**Ascend upon its rows** Heb. בשרותיה, an expression of a plain. Menahem (p. 181) associates it as an expression of a wall with (II Sam. 22:30) “I scale a wall (שור),” but נטישותיה (cep in Prov.) do not fit for they are the roots of the vineyard to be associated either with the walls or with the plain. I say, therefore, that בשרותיה is an expression of the rows of the vineyard. Jonathan, however, rendered בשרותיה, in her cities, and נטישותיה, her palaces.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:12:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ לֹא הוּא.** ״לָא מִן קֳדָמוֹהִי אַתְיָא עֲלָנָא טָבְתָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And [they] said, “It is not He”** [The Targum renders:] Our good fortune does not come before Him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:13:1)


**וְהַנְּבִיאִים יִהְיוּ לְרוּחַ.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וְנָבִיֵּי שִׁקְרָא יְהוֹן לְלָמָא״; כְּלוֹמַר: הַנְּבוּאָה שֶׁנִּיבָּאִים לָנוּ נְבִיאֵי שֶׁקֶר בִּשְׁמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, דִּבְרֵיהֶם יֵהָפְכוּ לְהֶבֶל, **וְהַדִּבֵּר** שֶׁל קְדֻשָּׁה, שֶׁהֵם אוֹמְרִים לָנוּ בְּשֵׁם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, אֵינָן **בָּהֶם**:


**And the prophets shall become wind** As the Targum renders: And the false prophets shall become naught. That is to say, the prophecy that the false prophets prophesy for us in the name of the Holy One, blessed be He, their words shall turn to futility, and the speech of holiness that they say to us in the name of the Holy One, blessed be He, is not in them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:13:2)


**כֹּה יֵעָשֶׂה לָהֶם.** הַנָּבִיא אוֹמֵר לָאוֹמְרִים זֹאת, כֹּה יֵעָשֶׂה לָהֶם, כַּאֲשֶׁר אָמַר ה׳ לִי. וּמָה אָמַר ה׳ לִי? **יַעַן דַּבֶּרְכֶם אֶת הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה וְגוֹ׳**:


**So shall be done to them** The prophet says to those who say this, “So shall be done to them as the Lord said to me, and what did the Lord say to me? “Because you have spoken this word, etc.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:14:1)


**וַאֲכָלָתַם.** וְתֹאכַל אוֹתָם:


**and it shall consume them** Heb. ואכלתם.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:15:1)


**אֵיתָן.** תַּקִּיף (מכילתא בשלח ויהי ו):


**mighty** Heb. איתן, strong.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:15:2)


**גּוֹי מֵעוֹלָם הוּא.** מִימֵי קֶדֶם הִתְחִיל לִמְרוֹד בִּי, בְּדוֹר הַפְּלַגָּה בְּאֶרֶץ שִׁנְעָר (בראשית יא:א-ט; פסחים צד.-צד:):


**It is an ancient nation** From days of yore it began to rebel against Me, in the generation of separation in the land of Shinar.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:16:1)


**אַשְׁפָּתוֹ.** מְקוֹם נַרְתֵּק הַחִצִּים:


**His quiver** Heb. אשפתו. The place of the arrow case.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:1)


**יְרוֹשֵׁשׁ עָרֵי מִבְצָרֶיךָ וְגוֹ׳.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס: **יְרוֹשֵׁשׁ בֶּחָרֶב** אֶת **עָרֵי מִבְצָרֶיךָ אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה בּוֹטֵחַ בָּהֵנָּה**:


**He will impoverish your fortified cities [upon which you rely, with the sword]** This verse is in the wrong order [and should be arranged as follows:] He will impoverish with the sword your fortified cities upon which you rely.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:2)


**יְרוֹשֵׁשׁ.** יְמַסְכֵּן:


**impoverish** Heb. ירשש, he will impoverish.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:17:3)


**בַּחָרֶב.** עַל יְדֵי גְּיָסוֹת, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״וְחֶרֶב לֹא תַעֲבֹר בְּאַרְצְכֶם״ (ויקרא כו:ו):


**with the sword** With troops. Comp. “And a sword will not pass in your land” (Lev. 26:6).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:1)


**הַאוֹתִי לֹא תִירָאוּ.** הֲלֹא הַיָּם, שֶׁאֵינוֹ דּוֹאֵג מִפְּנֵי הַפּוּרְעָנוּת וְאֵינוֹ מְצַפֶּה לְקִבּוּל שָׂכָר, יָרֵא מִפָּנַי, וְאֵינוֹ עוֹבֵר הַ**גְּבוּל אֲשֶׁר שַׂמְתִּי** סְבִיבָיו:


**Will you not fear Me?** Does not the sea, which is not concerned about retribution and does not look forward to receiving reward, fear Me and not cross the boundary that I set around it?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:2)


**תָּחִילוּ.** לְשׁוֹן פַּחַד:


**do you not quake** Heb. תחילו, an expression of fear.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:3)


**וַיִּתְגָּעֲשׁוּ.** גַּלָּיו, וּמְרוֹמֵם לַעֲבוֹר הַגְּבוּל **וְלֹא יוּכָלוּ** לַעֲבוֹר:


**and toss themselves** i.e., its waves, and they lift themselves to cross the border but cannot cross.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:4)


**וְהָמוּ גַלָּיו.** שָׁאוֹן קוֹל גָּדוֹל (ישעיהו יג:ד):


**and its waves roar** A loud sound of roaring.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:22:5)


**וְלֹא יַעַבְרֶנְהוּ.** הַגַּלִּים לֹא יַעַבְרוּ הַגְּבוּל:


**but they do not pass it** The waves do not pass the border.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:1)


**יוֹרֶה וּמַלְקוֹשׁ.** כַּתַּרְגּוּם: ״בְּכִיר וְלָקִישׁ״:


**the early rain and the latter rain** Heb. יורה ומלקוש, as the Targum renders: בכיר ולקיש, the early rain and the latter rain.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:2)


**שְׁבֻעוֹת חֻקּוֹת קָצִיר.** שִׁבְעָה שָׁבוּעוֹת, שֶׁחָקַק לָנוּ בָּהֶן שְׁתֵּי חֻקּוֹת קָצִיר: חֹק הָעֹמֶר וְחֹק שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם (ויקרא רבה כח, ג):


**the weeks of the laws of harvest** Seven weeks in which He ordained two ordinances of harvest, the ordinance of the omer, and the ordinance of the two breads.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:24:3)


**יִשְׁמָר לָנוּ.** שֶׁיְּהֵא הַקָּצִיר בִּזְמַנּוֹ:


**He keeps for us** that the harvest should be in its time.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:25:1)


**הִטּוּ אֵלֶּה.** הִטּוּ הַחֻקּוֹת הָאֵלֶּה מֵעֲלֵיכֶם; שֶׁאֵינָם שׁוֹמְרִים לָכֶם, וְאַתֶּם כָּלִים בָּרָעָב:


**have brought about these** Have brought about these laws. Have cast aside these laws from you, so they don’t guard you and you will be consumed from hunger.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:1)


**יָשׁוּר.** יֶאֱרֹב, וְכֵן ״לְמַעַן שׁוֹרְרָי״ (תהילים ה:ט); לְשׁוֹן ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ וְלֹא קָרוֹב״ (במדבר כד:יז):


**they lie in wait** Heb. ישור. Comp. למען שוררי because of those who lie in wait for me (Psalms 27: 11), an expression of “I see him (אשורנו) but not near” (Num. 24:17).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:2)


**כְּשַׁךְ יְקוּשִׁים.** כַּאֲשֶׁר יִשּׁוֹךְ הַיָּקוֹשׁ שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לִלְכּוֹד צְבָיִים בַּיַּעַר, כְּשֶׁרַגְלוֹ בָּא לְתוֹכוֹ, וְהוּא נוֹשְׁכוֹ וּמַחֲזִיקוֹ בְּרַגְלוֹ, וְנִלְכָּד; וְקוֹרִין לוֹ פִּיי״ד בְּלַעַז:


**as a trap bites** As a trap that they make to catch deer in the forest, bites when his foot enters it and it bites him and holds onto his foot and he is caught, and the call this (piege in O. F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:3)


**הִצִּיבוּ מַשְׁחִית.** צִינְבִּי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז; כֵּן עוֹשִׂין אוֹרְבִים עַל עִיר אוֹ עַל דֶּרֶךְ:


**they stationed an ambush** (Canbel in O.F.) So do those who lie in wait make for a city or on the road.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:26:4)


**הִצִּיבוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״מַצַּב פְּלִשְׁתִּים״ (שמואל א יג:כג) (סא״א):


**they station** Heb. יציבו, like “the garrison (מצב) of the Philistines” (I Sam. 14:4).



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:27:1)


**כִּכְלוּב.** הוּא מָקוֹם שֶׁמְּפַטְּמִין בּוֹ עוֹפוֹת; וְכֵן תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּבֵית פִּיטְמָא״; וּבְלַעַ״ז פְרַנְ״ק:


**As a cage** That is a place where they fatten fowl, and so does Jonathan render it, like a fattening place, and in O.F. (frank).



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:1)


**עָשְׁתוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״עֶשֶׁת שֵׁן״ (שיר השירים ה:יד), גּוּף עָב; וּבְלַעַז אִישְׁפִּישׁוֹרְ״ט:


**they have become thick** Heb. עשתו, an expression of “a block (עֶשֶת) of ivory” (Song 5:14), a thick body, and in O.F. enboeront.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:2)


**עָבְרוּ דִבְרֵי רָע.** אִם בָּאת רָעָה אוֹ עֲבֵירָה לְיָדָם לֹא נָטוּ מִמֶּנָּה, אֶלָּא עָבְרוּ בָהּ וַעֲשָׂאוּהָ:


**they transgressed in deeds of wickedness** If evil or a sin came into their hand, they did not turn away from it, but transgressed it and committed it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:28:3)


**לֹא דָנוּ דִּין יָתוֹם.** לְמַעַן **יַצְלִיחוּ**:


**they did not plead the cause of the orphan** in order that they prosper.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:30:1)


**שַׁמָּה.** תִּמָּהוֹן:


**An appalling...thing** Heb. שמה, a wonder.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 5:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/5:30:2)


**וְשַׁעֲרוּרָה.** דָּבָר מְגֻנֶּה לַכֹּל. וּמַהוּ דְּבַר הַתִּמָּהוֹן? הַנְּבִיאִים נִבְּאִים שֶׁקֶר וְאוֹמְרִים: הֱווּ גּוֹזְלִין וְאוֹנְסִין, וְהַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁהֵם שׁוֹפְטֵי הָעָם וְנוֹגְשֵׂיהֶם יִרְדּוּ אֶת הָעָם עַל יְדֵי הַנְּבִיאִים, כְּלוֹמַר: לְאַחֲרֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ ״וְהַמֶּלֶךְ עוֹבֵר וְכָל הָעָם עוֹבְרִים עַל יָדוֹ״ (שמואל ב טו:יז-כג) (רוֹדֵף) (ס״א דְּדָוִד) אַחֲרָיו, אַף כָּאן עַל יְדֵיהֶם – אַחֲרֵיהֶם, לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּדִבְרֵי הַנְּבִיאִים:


**and horrible thing** Heb. ושערורה, a disgraceful thing to all, and what is the appalling thing? The prophets prophesy falsely and say, “Rob and plunder,” and the priests, who are the judges of the people and their overseers, govern the people on the hands of the prophets, that is to say, after them. Comp. (II Sam. 15) “And the king was crossing and all the people were crossing on his hand,” stated concerning David, meaning ‘after him.’ Here too, “on their hands” means ‘after them,’ to do according to the words of the prophets.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:1)


**הָעִזוּ בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִן.** לָלֶכֶת לְעָרֵיכֶם, לִשְׁמֹר מִבְצָרֵיכֶם:


**Gather the sons of Benjamin** to go to their cities to guard their fortifications.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:2)


**בֵּית הַכֶּרֶם.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם הוּא:


**Beth-hakerem** That is a place name.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:1:3)


**שְׂאוּ מַשְׂאֵת.** נֵס, לְהִקָּבֵץ וּלְהִלָּחֵם עַל נַפְשְׁכֶם:


**raise a standard** a standard to gather and fight for your lives.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:2:1)


**הַנָּוֶה וְהַמְּעֻנָּגָה דָּמִיתִי.** הֶחֱרַבְתִּי תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן; לְשׁוֹן ״וְנָדַמּוּ נְאוֹת הַשָּׁלוֹם״ (ירמיה כה:לז); ״נִדְמְתָה אַשְׁקְלוֹן״ (ירמיה מז:ה):


**The comely and the delicate one have I destroyed** Heb. דמיתי. Jonathan renders it as an expression related to (25:37) “And the dwellings of peace shall be destroyed (ונדמוּ).” Also (47:5), “Ashkelon has been destroyed (נדמתה).”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:1)


**רוֹעִים וְעֶדְרֵיהֶם.** מָשָׁל הוּא: מְלָכִים וְחֵילוֹתֵיהֶם:


**Shepherds and their flocks** Figurative of kings and their armies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:2)


**רָעוּ.** אָכְלוּ:


**they feed bare** Heb. רעו, they eat.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:3:3)


**אֶת יָדוֹ.** אֶת מְקוֹמוֹ; סְבִיבָיו:


**his place** Heb. ידו [lit. his hand.] His place around him.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:1)


**קַדְּשׁוּ.** זַמְּנוּ:


**Prepare** Heb. קדשו.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:2)


**אוֹי לָנוּ.** לְעֵת פְּנוֹת הַיּוֹם:


**woe is to us** at the time the day declines.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:4:3)


**כִּי פָנָה הַיּוֹם.** כַּאֲשֶׁר פָּנָה לְעֵת עֶרֶב הִצִּיתוּ הָאוּר בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (תענית כט.):


**for the day has declined** When it declined to eventide, they ignited the Temple.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:6:1)


**כִּרְתוּ עֵצָה.** לֹא מַפִּיק הֵ״א, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן ׳עֵץ׳, שֶׁאֵין לְשׁוֹן ׳כְּרִיתָה׳ נוֹפֵל עַל הָ״עֵצָה״ (שמואל ב טז:כ). וְעֵצָה הָאָמוּר כָּאן – לְשׁוֹן הַרְבֵּה עֵצִים, כְּמוֹ ״דָּגָה״ (שמות ז:יח) – לְשׁוֹן דָּגִים הַרְבֵּה:


**Cut the trees** Heb. עצה. This is not a ‘mappiq heh,’ yet it is an expression of a tree (עץ), for the expression of cutting is not appropriate for advice, (עצה), and עצה mentioned here is an expression of many trees, as דגה is an expression of many fish.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:6:2)


**הָעִיר הָפְקַד.** שֶׁבָּאָה פְּקֻדָּתָהּ:


**the city whose sins have been visited upon her** Heb. הפקד, whose visitation has come.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:1)


**כְּהָקִיר.** לְשׁוֹן מָקוֹר:


**As...lets...flow** Heb. כהקיר, an expression of a spring.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:2)


**בַּיִר.** בְּאֵר:


**a well** Heb. בַּיִר, equivalent to בְּאֵר.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:3)


**כֵּן הֵקֵרָה רָעָתָהּ.** כֵּן נוֹבַעַת רָעָתָם; תָּמִיד מוֹסִיפִים וּמְחַדְּשִׁים:


**so has she let her evil flow** So does their evil flow, constantly increasing and renewing.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:7:4)


**חֳלִי וּמַכָּה.** ״עַל כֵּן אַיְתֵיתִי עֲלָהּ מְרַע וּמְחָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**sickness and wounds** Therefore, I brought upon her sickness and wounds.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:8:1)


**הִוָּסְרִי.** קַבְּלִי מוּסָר:


**Be corrected** Heb. הוסרי. Accept correction.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:8:2)


**פֶּן תֵּקַע.** פֶּן תִּנָּתֵק. הוֹקַע לְשׁוֹן חִבּוּר: ״וְהוֹקַע אוֹתָם״ (במדבר כה:ד), וְיֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן הֲסָרַת הַחִבּוּר, וְהַרְבֵּה תֵּיבוֹת מְשַׁמְּשׁוֹת כֵּן:


**lest...be alienated** Heb. תקע, lest it be pulled away. יקע is an expression of joining. Comp. “and hang them up (והוקע)” (Num. 25:4), and it is sometimes an expression of detaching. There are many words that are used in such a manner.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:1)


**עוֹלֵל.** גְּרַפָּאִי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז. לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּצְרוּ הַכֶּרֶם וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ עוֹלֵלוֹת, וְהָעֲנִיִּים בָּאִים וְעוֹלְלִים אוֹתָם, כֵּן יָבוֹזּוּ וְיַחְזְרוּ וְיָבוֹזּוּ:


**thoroughly glean** (grappiller in French). After the vineyard has been gathered, and gleanings are left, and the poor come and glean them, so will they spoil and return and spoil.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:2)


**הָשֵׁב יָדְךָ.** אַתָּה, הַשּׂוֹנֵא, הָשֵׁב יָדְךָ לִשְׁלוֹל שֵׁנִית, **כְּבוֹצֵר** הַמֵּשִׁיב יָדוֹ **עַל סַלְסִלּוֹת** – הַמְּסַלְסֵל וּמְחַפֵּשׂ הָעוֹלֵלוֹת. וְכֵן הָיְתָה: מִשֶּׁהִגְלָה אֶת יְהוֹיָקִים וַהֲרָגוֹ וְהִמְלִיךְ אֶת יְכָנְיָה, חָזַר לְסוֹף שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים וְהִגְלָהוּ, חָזַר לְסוֹף עַשְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְהִגְלָה אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כד-כה):


**return your hand** You, the enemy, return your hand to spoil a second time, like a vintager who returns his hand for the search, who looks and searches for the gleanings. And so it was when he exiled Jehoiakim and slew him, and crowned Jeconiah, he returned three months later and exiled him. He returned eleven years later and exiled Zedekiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:9:3)


**סַלְסִלּוֹת.** רְבִירְקְדוּרְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״סַלְסְלֶהָ וּתְרוֹמְמֶךָּ״ (משלי ד:ח) – כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוֹ דּוּנַשׁ. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן ״סַל״ (שמות כט:ג); פַּנִירְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the branches** Heb. סלסלות (reverchedures in O.F). Comp. (Prov. 4: 8) “Search for it (סלסלה) and it will exalt you.” So did Dunash (Teshuvoth Dunash p. 21) explain it. Menahem (Machbereth p. 126), however, explains it as an expression of a basket (סל) (paniers in O. F. baskets).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:1)


**וְאָעִידָה.** לְשׁוֹן הַתְרָאָה:


**and warn** Heb. ואעידה, an expression of warning.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:2)


**עֲרֵלָה.** אֲטוּמָה, וְכֵן כָּל לְשׁוֹן עֲרֵלָה:


**clogged** Heb. ערלה, clogged, and so is every expression of ערלה.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:10:3)


**לְחֶרְפָּה.** מְחָרְפִין אוֹתוֹ:


**a disgrace** They deride it.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:1)


**וְאֶת חֲמַת ה׳ מָלֵאתִי.** לִבִּי מָלֵא נְבוּאָה לָבוֹא חֲמַת ה׳ עֲלֵיהֶם:


**Therefore, I am full of the fury of the Lord** My heart is full of the prophecy of the coming of the fury of the Lord upon them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:2)


**הָכִיל.** כְּמוֹ ״לְהָכִיל״ (יחזקאל כא:לג), אשופרי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**containing** Heb. הכיל, like להכיל, to contain ([a]souffrir in O.F.), to tolerate.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:3)


**שְׁפֹךְ עַל עוֹלָל.** לִשְׁפֹּךְ עַל הַיּוֹנְקִים:


**to pour it out upon the babes** to pour it upon the sucklings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:4)


**בַּחוּץ.** בַּשּׁוּק:


**in the street** in the market.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:11:5)


**מְלֵא יָמִים.** קָרוֹב לַמָּוֶת, וְהוּא יֶתֶר עַל **זָקֵן**:


**one full of days** i.e., one close to death, older than ‘an old man.’



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:12:1)


**שָׂדוֹת וְנָשִׁים יַחְדָּו.** יוּסַבּוּ **לַאֲחֵרִים**:


**fields and wives together** shall be turned over to others.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:13:1)


**בּוֹצֵעַ בָּצַע.** אוֹנֵס מָמוֹן:


**commit robbery** rob money.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:14:1)


**עַל נְקַלָּה.** קַל הוּא לִנְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר לְהוֹצִיא מִפִּיהֶם וְלוֹמַר שָׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לָכֶם (ירמיה ד:י), וּבַדָּבָר הַזֶּה מְרַפְּאִין בְּדִבּוּרָם אֶת הַ**שֶּׁבֶר** הֶעָתִיד לָבֹא עֲלֵיהֶן:


**easily** It is easy for the false prophets to pronounce with their mouths and to say, “You shall have peace,” and with this thing they heal with their speech the breach destined to come upon them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:1)


**הוֹבִישׁוּ.** ״עֲלֵיהוֹן לְמִיבְהַת״ (תרגום יונתן):


**They should be ashamed** Heb. הובישו, they should be ashamed [from Jonathan].


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:2)


**הַכְלִים.** כְּמוֹ לְהַכְלִים:


**to feel disgrace** Heb. הכלים, like להכלים.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:15:3)


**גַּם בּוֹשׁ לֹא יֵבוֹשׁוּ.** אֵינָם מִתְבַּיְּשִׁין מִמַּעֲשֵׂיהֶם הָרָעִים:


**Neither are they ashamed** They are not ashamed of their evil deeds.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:16:1)


**מַרְגּוֹעַ.** מְנוּחָה:


**rest** Heb. מרגוע.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:17:1)


**וַהֲקִימֹתִי עֲלֵיכֶם צוֹפִים.** הוּא שֶׁמַּעֲמִידִין אֲנָשִׁים עַל הַמִּגְדָּלִים עֵת הַחֵרוּם אִישׁ צוֹפֶה, וְרוֹאֶה: אִם גַּיִס בָּא לָעִיר, תּוֹקֵעַ בַּשּׁוֹפָר וּמַזְהִיר אֶת הָעָם לְהֵחָלֵץ וְלָבֹא עַל הַחוֹמָה; אַף אֲנִי הֶעֱמַדְתִּי נְבִיאִים הַמַּשְׁמִיעִים אֶתְכֶם אֶת הַפֻּרְעָנוּת הַבָּאָה, לְהִזְדָּרֵז וּלְתַקֵּן דַּרְכֵיכֶם שֶׁתְּנַצְּלוּ, וְהוּא כְּשׁוֹפָר תְּקִיעוֹת הַצּוֹפֶה, הַקְשִׁיבוּ לְקוֹל הַשּׁוֹפָר הַזֶּה וְהֵחָלְצוּ:


**And I have stationed lookouts over you** That is that they station men on the towers at the time of strife. A man looks and sees. If a troop is approaching the city he sounds a shophar and warns the people to arm and to come on the wall. I too appointed prophets who inform you about the impending retribution to alert yourselves and to mend your ways so that you be saved and that is like the shophar sounded by the lookout. Hearken to the sound of this shophar and arm yourselves.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:17:2)


**וְהַקִימֹתִי.** פֵּירוּשׁ: הֶעֱמַדְתִּי לָהֶם כְּבָר; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וַאֲקִימִית״, **צוֹפִים** גִּיט״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And I have stationed** Heb. והקמותי. This means: I have already stationed for them. And so did Jonathan render: ואקימית, and I stationed. **lookouts** (Badetes in O.F.).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:18:1)


**אֶת אֲשֶׁר בָּם.** אֶת הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדָם:


**what is within them** i.e., the evil that is in their hands.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:19:1)


**פְּרִי מַחְשְׁבוֹתָם.** שְׂכַר מַחְשְׁבוֹתָם:


**the fruit of their thoughts** the retribution for their thoughts.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:19:2)


**וְתוֹרָתִי וַיִּמְאֲסוּ בָהּ.** הֲרֵי זֶה דּוּגְמַת ״עָזִּי וְזִמְרָת יָהּ וַיְהִי לִי לִישׁוּעָה״ (שמות טו:ב), שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר הָיָה לִי לִישׁוּעָה; אַף כָּאן הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר וְתוֹרָתִי מָאֲסוּ בָהּ. וְדֶרֶךְ הַמִּקְרָאוֹת לְדַבֵּר כֵּן:


**and they rejected My instruction** Heb. ותורתי וימאסו בה. This follows the pattern of (Exodus 15:2) “The strength and destruction of God was my salvation (ויהי לי לישועה),” for he should have said: והיה לי לישועה. So too, here it should have said, ותורתי מאסו בה, but it is the style of Scripture to speak in this manner.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:20:1)


**לָמָּה זֶּה לִי לְבוֹנָה.** אֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם מַקְרִיבִים לְפָנַי, אֲשֶׁר **מִשְּׁבָא** בָּאָה:


**Why do I need frankincense** that you offer up to Me, that comes from Sheba?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:20:2)


**לֹא עָרְבוּ.** לֹא נָעֲמוּ:


**are not pleasant** Heb. ערבוּ.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:23:1)


**יַחֲזִיקוּ.** יִקְחוּ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּשְׁלַח יָדוֹ וַיַּחֲזֶק בּוֹ״ (שמות ד:ד), וְכֵן ״צָרָה הֶחֱזִיקַתְנוּ״ (ירמיה ו:כד) – אֲחָזַתְנוּ:


**They will grasp** Heb. יחזיקו, they will take. Comp. (Exodus 4:4) “And he stretched out his hand and took it (ויחזק).” Comp. also (infra verse 24) “trouble grasped us (החזיקתנוּ) held us.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:25:1)


**מָגוֹר.** יִרְאָה. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן אֲסִיפָה, כְּמוֹ ״מְגוּרֵי אֶל חֶרֶב״ (יחזקאל כא:יז); וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**there is fear** Heb. מגור. Another explanation: An expression of gathering. Comp. (Ezekiel 21: 17) “Gathered (מגורי) to the sword.” And in this manner Jonathan renders it.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:1)


**וְהִתְפַּלְּשִׁי.** הִתְגַּלְגְּלִי:


**and roll** Heb. והתפלשי.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:2)


**אֵבֶל יָחִיד.** אֵבֶל שֶׁל בֵּן יָחִיד, מִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּן יָחִיד וּמֵת:


**a mourning [as] for an only child** A mourning for an only son; one who has only one son and he dies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:3)


**מִסְפַּד תַּמְרוּרִים.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא דָּבוּק נָקוּד מִסְפַּד בְּפַתָּח, וְאֵינוֹ נָקוּד מִסְפֵּד (ירמיה מח:לח) בְּצֵירֵי:


**a bitter lamentation** Heb. מספד תמרורים. Since it is the construct state, it is vowelized מספד with a ‘pattah,’ and it is not vowelized מספד with a ‘tzere.’


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:26:4)


**עָלֵינוּ.** כִּבְיָכוֹל, עָלַי וְעָלֶיךָ:


**upon us** So to speak, upon Me and upon you.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:27:1)


**בָּחוֹן נְתַתִּיךָ.** לְיִרְמְיָה נֶאֱמַר: חָזָק נְתַתִּיךָ בְּתוֹכָם. **וְתֵדַע וּבָחַנְתָּ אֶת דַּרְכָּם.** שֶׁתּוֹכִיחֵם וְהֵם לֹא יִשְׁמְעוּ. **בָּחוֹן.** לְשׁוֹן חוֹזֶק, כְּמוֹ ״עֹפֶל וָבַחַן״ (ישעיהו לב:יד); ״הֵקִימוּ בְחוּנָיו״ (ישעיהו כג:יג):


**I have made you the strong one** This was said to Jeremiah. I have made you the strong one in their midst, and you shall know and test their way, that he tested them and they will not heed. בחון is an expression of strength. Comp. (Isa. 32:14) “rampart and tower (בחן),” also (ibid. 23:13) “they erected its towers (בחוניו).”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:28:1)


**סָרֵי סוֹרְרִים.** סָרִים מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ:


**extremely perverse** Turning away from the road.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:28:2)


**הוֹלְכֵי רָכִיל.** בֵּין הַנְּחֹשֶׁת וְהַבַּרְזֶל: מַטִּילֵי אֵיבָה בֵּין שְׁנֵי שָׂרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן כֹּחַ לְהִתְגָּרוֹת יַחַד; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: **הוֹלְכֵי רָכִיל** הֵם, וַחֲזָקִים כַּ**נְּחֹשֶׁת וּבַרְזֶל** לְהַחֲזִיק כְּזָבֵיהֶם. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם רָכִיל זֶה לְשׁוֹן נוֹכֵל: ״מְהַלְּכִין בִּנְכִילוּ כְּמָא דְמְעָרֵב נְחָשׁ עִם פַּרְזְלָא״. וְנוֹכֵל הוּא לְשׁוֹן מַחֲשָׁבָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּתְנַכְּלוּ אֹתוֹ לַהֲמִיתוֹ״ (בראשית לז:יח):


**going tale bearing** between copper and iron. They introduce hatred between two princes who have the power to provoke one another. That is what I heard. But I say, they are tale bearers, and they are as strong as copper and iron to maintain their falsehood. Jonathan, however, renders this word רכיל as an expression of guile (נוכל). They go with guile as one mixes copper with iron. נוכל is an expression of plot. Comp. (Gen. 37:18) “And they plotted (ויתנכלו) against him to slay him.”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:1)


**נָחַר מַפּוּחַ.** מָשָׁל הוּא: כְּשֶׁצּוֹרְפִים כֶּסֶף נוֹפְחִין בַּמַּפּוּחַ, וְנוֹתְנִין תָּמִיד עוֹפֶרֶת בְּתוֹךְ הַכּוּר וּמוֹעִיל לִצְרוֹף; אֲבָל אַתֶּם, בָּאתִי לִצְרוֹף אֶתְכֶם – הַמַּפּוּחַ **נָחַר** וְנֶחְרַךְ מִכֹּחַ **הָאֵשׁ**, וְתַם הָעוֹפֶרֶת כֻּלּוֹ. **נָחַר** לְשׁוֹן ״חָרָה אַפּוֹ״ (איוב לב:ב) ״וְעַצְמִי חָרָה מִנִּי חֹרֶב״ (איוב ל:ל) אישר״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The bellows is heated** This is an allegory. When they refine silver, they blow with a bellows, and they always put lead into the crucible, which helps for the refining. But you—I have come to refine you. The bellows is heated and parched because of the fire, and the lead is completely consumed. נחר is an expression related to (Job 42:7) “My wrath has been kindled (חרה)”; (ibid. 30:30) “And my bones are burned (חרה) with heat,” (esrast in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:2)


**לַשָּׁוְא.** עַל חִנָּם **צָרַף** הַצּוֹרֵף כִּי לֹא הוֹעִיל:


**in vain** In vain does the founder refine, for it is of no avail


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:3)


**וְרָעִים לֹא נִתָּקוּ.** מִדַּרְכָּם הָרָעָה; כְּלוֹמַר: יָגְעוּ הַנְּבִיאִים לְהוֹכִיחַ וְלֹא הוֹעִילוּ:


**and the wicked ones have not been separated** from their evil way. That is to say that the prophets toiled to reprove, but it did not avail them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:29:4)


**נִתָּקוּ.** פּוֹרְצְטְרַאקִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, (נוסח אחר **וְרָעִים** כְּמוֹ רְשָׁעִים סא״א):


**Separated** Heb. נתקו, (furent de... in O.F.). (Another version:) and the wicked ones. This is like רשעים.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 6:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/6:30:1)


**כֶּסֶף נִמְאָס.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִצְרָף בְּשׁוּם יְגִיעָה:


**Refuse silver** which cannot be refined with any toil.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:4:1)


**הֵיכַל ה׳ הֵיכַל ה׳ הֵיכַל ה׳.** ״תְּלָת זִמְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא אַתּוּן מִתְחֲזִין קֳדָמוֹהִי״:


**The Temple of the Lord, the Temple of the Lord, etc.** Three times a year you appear before Me.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:9:1)


**הֲגָנֹב רָצֹחַ וְגוֹ׳.** בִּתְמִיהָּ, וְכִי אֵלֶּה תַּעֲשׂוּ וְיוֹעִיל הַהֵיכָל לָכֶם?


**Will you steal, murder, etc.** This is a question. Will you do all these, and should the Temple of the Lord avail for you?



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:11:1)


**הַמְעָרַת פָּרִיצִים הָיָה.** בְּעֵינֵיכֶם **הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה**? בִּתְמִיהָּ:


**Has...a cave of profligate men?** Was this house in your eyes? This is a question.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:11:2)


**הִנֵּה רָאִיתִי.** שֶׁכָּךְ הוּא בְּעֵינֵיכֶם:


**behold I have seen** that it is so in your eyes.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:14:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי לְשִׁילוֹ.** בִּימֵי עֵלִי (שמואל א ד:יא-יח):


**as I did to Shiloh** in the days of Eli.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:15:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר הִשְׁלַכְתִּי וְגוֹ׳.** עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**as I cast, etc.** the ten tribes.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:18:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת כַּוָּנִים.** דְּפוּס הַכּוֹכָב:


**star-shaped cakes** Heb. כַּוָנִים, the shape of a star.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:18:2)


**לִמְלֶכֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם.** כּוֹכָב הַגָּדוֹל הָיוּ קוֹרִין מְלֶכֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם, לְשׁוֹן מַלְכָּה; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לְכוֹכֶבֶת שְׁמַיָּא״:


**to the queen of heaven** The largest star they would call מלכת השמים, an expression of a queen, (מלכּה), and so did Jonathan render it: לכוכבת שמיא, to the female star of heaven.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:20:1)


**נִתֶּכֶת.** יוֹרֶדֶת וּמַגַּעַת; כְּמוֹ ״וּמָטָר לֹא נִתַּךְ אָרְצָה״ (שמות ט:לג) ״לָא מְטָא״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**reaches** Heb. נתכת, falls and reaches. Comp. (Exodus 9:33) “and rain did not reach (נתּך) the earth”; did not reach.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:21:1)


**עוֹלוֹתֵיכֶם.** שֶׁאַתֶּם מַקְרִיבִים כָּלִיל (ויקרא א:ח-ט), טוֹב לָכֶם שֶׁתּוֹסִיפוּ אוֹתָם עַל שַׁלְמֵיכֶם וְתַקְרִיבוּ אוֹתָם שְׁלָמִים וְתֹאכְלוּ הַבָּשָׂר, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָם מְקֻבָּלוֹת לְפָנַי, וְלָמָּה תַפְסִידוּם?


**your burnt offerings** that you offer up completely it would be better for you to add them to your peace-offerings and sacrifice them as peace-offerings and eat the flesh, for they are not acceptable before Me. Why, then, should you lose them?



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:22:1)


**בְּיוֹם הוֹצִיאִי אוֹתָם.** תְּחִלַּת תְּנַאי לֹא הָיְתָה אֶלָּא ״אִם שָׁמוֹעַ תִּשְׁמְעוּ בְּקוֹלִי וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם אֶת בְּרִיתִי וִהְיִיתֶם לִי סְגֻלָּה״ (שמות יט:ה).


**on the day I brought them forth** The beginning of the condition was only (Exodus 19:5): “If you hearken to My voice and keep My covenant, you shall be a peculiar treasure to Me.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:24:1)


**בִּשְׁרִירוּת לִבָּם.** בְּמַרְאֵה לִבָּם; לָשׁוֹן ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ״ (במדבר כד:יז):


**in the view** Heb. בשררות, in the view of their heart, an expression related to (Num. 24:17) “I see him אשורנו.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:25:1)


**יוֹם הַשְׁכֵּם וְשָׁלוֹחַ.** מִדֵּי יוֹם בְּיוֹם הַשְׁכֵּם וְשָׁלוֹחַ:


**sending them day [after day] every fresh morn** Every day sending them early.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:29:1)


**גָּזִּי נִזְרֵךְ.** תַּלְשִׁי שְׂעָרֵךְ; כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּגָז שַׂלְוִים״ (במדבר יא:לא); ״וַיָּגָז אֶת רֹאשׁוֹ״ (איוב א:כ). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **נִזְרֵךְ** כִּתְרֵךְ, לְשׁוֹן גְּדֻלָּה; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״רַבְרְבִיךְ״, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**So tear off your diadem** Heb. גזי נזרך, tear out your hair. Comp. (Num. 11:31) “And He caused quails to fly (ויגז)”; also (Job 1:20), “And he shaved (ויגז) his head.” Another explanation is that נזרך means ‘your diadem,’ an expression of greatness, and so did Jonathan render: רברביך your great ones, and so did Menahem classify it (p. 83).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:29:2)


**וַיִּטֹּשׁ.** וַיַּעֲזֹב:


**and abandoned** Heb. ויטש.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:1)


**בָּמוֹת הַתֹּפֶת.** הוּא הַמֹּלֶךְ (מלכים ב כג:י), שֶׁהָיָה שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת, וּמַסִּיקִין אוֹתוֹ מִתַּחְתָּיו וְיָדָיו פְּשׁוּטוֹת וְנִסָּקוֹת, וְנוֹתְנִין אֶת הַיֶּלֶד עַל יָדָיו וְהוּא נִכְוֶה וְנוֹהֵם, וְהַכּוֹמְרִים הָיוּ מַקִּישִׁין בְּתוּפִּים שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁמַע הָאָב קוֹל הַבֵּן וְיִכָּמְרוּ רַחֲמָיו (בראשית מג:ל). **תֹּפֶת** עַל שֵׁם הַתּוֹף:


**the high places of Topheth** That is Molech, which was of copper, and they would heat it up from underneath it with its hands spread out and heated. And they would place the child on his hands, and he would be burnt and moan, and the priests would beat drums so that the father should not hear his son’s voice and take pity. It is called Topheth because of the drum (תּוף), Hinnom because of the child’s moaning (נהמת).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:2)


**הִנֹּם.** עַל שֵׁם נַהֲמַת הַבֵּן (תנחומא בובר, ואתחנן הוספה ב):


**Hinnom** Because of the child’s moaning.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:31:3)


**לֹא צִוִּיתִי.** בְּמִצְווֹת שֶׁיַּקְרִיבוּ בְּנֵיהֶם לְקָרְבָּן, וְלֹא דִבַּרְתִּי לְאֶחָד מִן הַנְּבִיאִים. וּכְשֶׁדִּבַּרְתִּי לְאַבְרָהָם לִשְׁחוֹט אֶת בְּנוֹ (בראשית כב:ב), **לֹא עָלְתָה עַל לִבִּי** שֶׁיִּשְׁחוֹט, אֶלָּא לְהוֹדִיעַ צִדְקוֹ:


**which I did not ordain** with commandments that they offer up their children as sacrifices, and I did not speak to any of the prophets, and when I did speak to Abraham to slaughter his son, it did not enter My mind that he slaughter, but only to make his righteousness known.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 7:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/7:33:1)


**וְאֵין מַחֲרִיד.** אֶת הָעוֹפוֹת מֵעַל הַפְּגָרִים:


**and no one will frighten them** i.e., no one will frighten the fowl from the carcasses.



## Chapter 8


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:2:1)


**וּשְׁטָחוּם לַשֶּׁמֶשׁ.** וְהַכַּשְׂדִּים יִשְׁכְּנוּ לָצוּר עַל הָעִיר בְּקִבְרֵי הַשָּׂרִים, שֶׁהָיוּ נָאִים כְּפַלָּטִין (סנהדרין צו:):


**And spread them before the sun** And the Chaldeans shall dwell when they besiege the city, in the graves of the princes, that were as beautiful as palaces.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:2:2)


**לְדֹמֶן.** לְזֶבֶל:


**for dung** For fertilizer.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:3:1)


**וְנִבְחַר מָוֶת מֵחַיִּים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵם רוֹאִים אֶת הַמֵּתִים נְתוּנִין בְּבִזָּיוֹן, צָרַת הַחַיִּים גְּדוֹלָה הֵימֶנָּה, וְיִבְחֲרוּ אֶת הַמָּוֶת:


**And death shall be preferable to life** Although they see the dead being subjected to disgrace, the distress of the living is worse than that, and they will choose death.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:4:1)


**הֲיִפְּלוּ וְלֹא יָקוּמוּ.** הֲטוֹב לָהֶם שֶׁיַּפִּילוּ אֶת עַצְמָם לִנְפִילָה שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם תְּקוּמָה:


**Shall they fall and not rise** Is it good for them to cast themselves into a fall from which there is no rising?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:4:2)


**אִם יָשׁוּב וְלֹא יָשׁוּב.** לִכְשֶׁיִּרְצוּ לָשׁוּב מֵרָעָתָם, לֹא יָשׁוּב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִגְּזֵרָתוֹ (ספר במדבר מב). דָּבָר אַחֵר: אַף אִם יָשׁוּבוּ בִּתְשׁוּבָה, לֹא תִתְקַיֵּם בְּיָדָם, כִּי יְמַהֲרוּ לָשׁוּב לְרִשְׁעָתָם (ספר במדבר מב); וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אִם לְמֵיתַב, גְּלֵי קֳדָמַי דְּלָא יְתוּבוּן״:


**if he repents, will He not return?** If they wish to repent of their wickedness, will the Holy One, blessed be He, not return from His decree? Another explanation: If they repent, the repentance will not be permanent, for they will quickly return to their wickedness, and in this manner Jonathan rendered it: If to repent, it is revealed before Me that they will not repent.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:1)


**מְשׁוּבָה נִצַּחַת.** מְשׁוּבַת עוֹלָם; כְּמוֹ ״נֶצַח״ (ירמיה טו:יח):


**a perpetual backsliding** Heb. משבה נצחת, a perpetual backsliding, like נצח, perpetual.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:2)


**הֶחֱזִיקוּ.** אָחֲזוּ:


**They have held** Heb. החזיקו.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:5:3)


**בַּתַּרְמִית.** בְּמִרְמָה:


**to deceit** Heb. בתרמית.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:1)


**לֹא כֵן.** לֹא בַּדִּין, כְּמוֹ ״כֵּן בְּנוֹת צְלָפְחָד״ (במדבר כז:ז):


**not...properly** Heb. לא כן, not right, cf. (Num. 27:7) “The daughters of Zelaphehad speak justly (כּן).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:2)


**כֻּלֹּה שָׁב בִּמְרֻצוֹתָם.** בַּדֶּרֶךְ הָרִאשׁוֹן חוֹטְאִים, וְחוֹזְרִים וְחוֹטְאִים. **מְרוּצָתָם** – קוֹרְשִׁ״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Each one turns away in his course** [lit. in their course.] In the first way. They sin and repeatedly sin. מרוצתם is korse in O.F.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:6:3)


**שׁוֹטֵף.** אֵישְׁפְּרִידִי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**like a frightening** effaye in French.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:1)


**חֲסִידָה.** צִיגוֹנִיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Even the stork** (Cigogne in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:2)


**מוֹעֲדֶיהָ.** עֵת עָבְרָם אֶל אִיֵּי הַיָּם מִפְּנֵי הַצִּנָּה, וְעֵת שׁוּבָם:


**her seasons** The time they pass to the islands of the sea because of the cold, and the time of their return.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:3)


**שָׁמְרוּ.** הִמְתִּינוּ:


**await** Heb. שמרו.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:7:4)


**וְתוֹר וְסִיס וְעָגוּר.** ״וְשַׁפְנִינָא וְכוּרְכְיָא וּסְנוּנִיתָא״ (תרגום יונתן). כּוּרְכְיָא – גְּרוּאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז, סְנוּנִית – אֲרוּנְדִּילָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and the turtledove and the crane and the swallow** Jonathan renders: ושפנינא וכורכיא וּסנוּניתא. כּורכיא is (grue in French), a crane. סנוּניתא is (hirondelle in French), a swallow.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:8:1)


**אָכֵן הִנֵּה לַשֶּׁקֶר.** הִנֵּה חָכְמַתְכֶם לַשֶּׁקֶר בָּכֶם, לְרַפְּאוֹתְכֶם **עַל נְקַלָּה**:


**Verily, behold it is in vain** Behold your wisdom is in vain within you to heal you lightly.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:8:2)


**עָשָׂה עֵט שֶׁקֶר סוֹפְרִים.** נְבִיאֲכֶם:


**he made a false scribes’ pen** i.e., your prophet.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:9:1)


**הוֹבִישׁוּ חֲכָמִים.** כְּמוֹתְכֶם. וּמַהוּ הַבֹּשֶׁת? – **הִנֵּה בִדְבַר ה׳ מָאֲסוּ וְחָכְמַת מֶה לָהֶם**:


**Wise men were ashamed** i.e., wise men like you. And what is the shame? Behold, they rejected the word of the Lord, now what wisdom have they?



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:11:1)


**וַיְרַפּוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיְרַפְּאוּ״ (ירמיה ו:יד):


**And they healed** Heb. וירפו, similar to וירפאו.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:12:1)


**הוֹבִישׁוּ.** בֹּשֶׁת וּכְלִימָּה תָּבֹא לָהֶם:


**They shall be put to shame** Shame and embarrassment shall come upon them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:12:2)


**גַּם בּוֹשׁ לֹא יֵבוֹשׁוּ.** אֵינָם מִתְבַּיְּשִׁים בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם הָרָעִים, לָתֵת לֵב לָשׁוּב:


**Neither are they ashamed** They are not ashamed of their evil deeds to take heart to repent.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:1)


**אָסֹף אֲסִיפֵם.** לְשׁוֹן כְּלָיָה, כְּמוֹ ״אָסֹף אָסֵף כֹּל מֵעַל פְּנֵי הָאֲדָמָה״ (צפניה א:ב):


**I will utterly consume them** This is an expression of destruction. Comp. (Zeph. 1:2) “I will utterly consume (אסף אסף) everything from upon the face of the earth.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:2)


**אֵין עֲנָבִים בַּגֶּפֶן.** לֹא יִוָּתֵר דָּבָר:


**There shall be no grapes on the vine** Nothing will be left over.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:3)


**וְהֶעָלֶה נָבֵל.** לְשׁוֹן כָּמוֹשׁ, פְּלֵיישְׁטִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּמוֹ ״וּכְנֹבֶלֶת מִתְּאֵנָה״ (ישעיהו לד:ד), ״כָּאֵלָה נוֹבֶלֶת עָלֶיהָ״ (ישעיהו א:ל):


**even the leaves will be withered** Heb. נבל, an expression of withering (fleirit in French). Comp. (Isa. 34:4) “and as a withered fig (וּכנבלת) from a fig tree.” Also (ibid. 1:30), “like an elm whose leaves are wilting (נבלת).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:13:4)


**וָאֶתֵּן לָהֶם יַעַבְרוּם.** כָּל זֹאת תִּהְיֶה לָהֶם עַל כִּי נָתַתִּי לָהֶם חֻקִּים וְעָבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם:


**for what I gave them they transgressed** All this shall befall them because I gave them statutes and they transgressed them.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:14:1)


**וְנִדְּמָה שָׁם.** וְנִדּוֹם שָׁם, נֵשֵׁב שָׁם דּוֹמִים וַאֲבֵלִים:


**and there let us sit in silence** And let us be silent there. Let us sit there silent and mournful.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:14:2)


**מֵי רֹאשׁ.** ״כָּס דִּלְוָוט בִּישׁ כְּרֵישֵׁי חִיוָן״ (תרגום יונתן); ״רֹאשׁ פְּתָנִים״ (דברים לב:לג) הוּא אֶרֶס שֶׁבְּשִׁנָּיו:


**poisoned water** Jonathan renders: the cup of the curse, like the venom of serpents. The venom of the serpents is the poison that is in his teeth.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:1)


**מִדָּן נִשְׁמַע.** ״עַל דְּפַלְחוּ לְעֶגְלָא דִּבְדָן״ (תרגום יונתן; סנהדרין צו.):


**From Dan is heard** Because they worshipped the calf that was in Dan.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:2)


**נַחְרַת סוּסָיו.** עֲטִישׁוֹת נְחִירָיו:


**the snorting of their horses** The sneezing of his nostrils.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:16:3)


**מִצְהֲלוֹת אַבִּירָיו.** צַהֲלַת מִשְׂחַק סוּסָיו; וּמָצִינוּ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר סוּסִים נִקְרָאִים אַבִּירִים: ״מִדַּהֲרוֹת דַּהֲרוֹת אַבִּירָיו״ (שופטים ה:כב), לְשׁוֹן ״סוּס דּוֹהֵר״ (נחום ג:ב). וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״מִקָּל פְּסִיעוֹת גַּבְרוֹהִי״:


**the neighing of their stallions** The neighing of joy of his horses, and we find elsewhere that horses are called אבּירים (Jud. 5:22) by reason of the prancings, “the prancings of their mighty ones (אבּיריו),” an expression of (Nahum 3:2) “a prancing horse.” And Jonathan renders: from the sound of the steps of their mighty ones.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:17:1)


**נְחָשִׁים צִפְעֹנִים.** לְסוֹף שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה נַעֲשֶׂה צֶפַע, וְאוֹטֵם אָזְנוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁמַע לְקוֹל חֶבֶר הַמְלַחֲשׁוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כְּמוֹ פֶתֶן חֵרֵשׁ יַאְטֵם אָזְנוֹ...״ (תהלים נח:ה-ו); הוּא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: **אֲשֶׁר אֵין לָהֶם לָחַשׁ** (ראה שוחר טוב נח:ב):


**serpents, adders** After seventy years, the snake becomes a צפע, which shuts its ear so that it will not hear the voice of the charmer who charms him, as it is stated (Psalms 58:5), “Like a deaf asp that shuts up its ear.” That is the meaning of “that cannot be charmed.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:18:1)


**מַבְלִיגִיתִי עֲלֵי יָגוֹן.** כְּמוֹ הִבְלַגְתִּי, וְשֵׁם דָּבָר הוּא מישאיטינמנ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּלוֹמַר אִם אָמַרְתִּי אַבְלִיגָה וְאֶתְאַפֵּק עֲלֵי יָגוֹנִי:


**Were I to suppress my grief** Heb. מבליגיתי, my suppression, and it is a noun, (mes estenimenc in O.F.). That is to say, if I said, I will suppress and keep in my grief.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:18:2)


**עָלַי לִבִּי דַוָּי.** מִקּוֹל נְבוּאוֹת הַנִּגְלוֹת אֵלַי בְּפֻרְעָנִיּוֹת הַבָּאוֹת:


**my heart is sick within me** From the voice of the prophecies that are revealed to me of the coming retributions.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:19:1)


**הִנֵּה קוֹל שַׁוְעַת בַּת עַמִּי.** אֲנִי שׁוֹמֵעַ שֶׁסּוֹפָן לִצְעֹק בְּ**אֶרֶץ מַרְחַקִּים**. וְלָמָּה כָּל זֹאת? הֲלֹא **ה׳ בְּצִיּוֹן**, וְאִם יָשׁוּבוּ אֵלָיו יִמָּצֵא לָהֶם!


**Hark the voice...the cry of the daughter of My people** I hear that eventually they will cry from a distant land, but why is all this necessary? Is not the Lord in Zion? If they return to Him, He will allow Himself to be found by them.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:20:1)


**עָבַר קָצִיר כָּלָה קָיִץ.** צִפִּינוּ לְעֶזְרַת מִצְרַיִם וְלֹא בָאָה; אָמַרְנוּ: יַעֲבֹר הַקָּצִיר וְיִהְיוּ פְנוּיִין וְיָבֹאוּ, וְהִנֵּה עָבַר זְמַן הַקָּצִיר בְּאִיָּר, וּזְמַן הַקָּיִץ בְּתַמּוּז, וְלֹא בָאוּ (בבא מציעא קו:):
