**The harvest is past, the summer is ended** We looked forward to the aid of Egypt, but it did not come. We said, “Let the harvest pass, and they will be free, and they will come.” Now behold, the time of harvest has passed in Iyar, and the summer season in Tammuz, and they have not come.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:1)


**קָדַרְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן שַׁחֲרוּת וְאֹפֶל:


**I mourn** lit. I am darkened, an expression of blackness and darkness.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:2)


**שַׁמָּה.** תִּמָּהוֹן:


**dismay** Heb. שמה, wonder.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:21:3)


**הֶחֱזִיקַתְנִי.** אֲחָזַתְנִי:


**has taken hold of me** Has held me.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:22:1)


**הַצֳּרִי אֵין בְּגִלְעָד.** שֶׁהַצֳּרִי בָּא מִשָּׁם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״עֲלִי גִלְעָד וּקְחִי צֳרִי״ (ירמיה מו:יא); כְּלוֹמַר: וְכִי לֹא הָיוּ לָהֶם אֲנָשִׁים צַדִּיקִים מִמִּי לִלְמֹד, וְיֵיטִיבוּ דַרְכֵיהֶם?


**Is there no balm in Gilead** For balm comes from there, as it is said (infra 46:11) “Go up to Gilead and take balm.” That is to say, Did they not have any righteous men from whom to learn so that they should improve their ways?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 8:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/8:22:2)


**אֲרֻכַת.** רְפוּאַת אִינְפְּלַשְׁטְרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the health of** lit. the cure of, (emplatre in O.F.), plaster, dressing.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:1:1)


**עֲצֶרֶת בֹּגְדִים.** כְּנֵסִיּוֹת בּוֹגְדִים:


**a company of traitors** Companies of traitors.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:2:1)


**וַיַּדְרְכוּ.** לָשׁוֹן זֶה נוֹפֵל בְּהַזְמָנַת הַקֶּשֶׁת לִירוֹת חֵץ; וְקֶשֶׁת הִיא אַרְבַּלִישְׁטְ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, וּבָרַגְלַיִם דּוֹרְכִין אוֹתָהּ, לְכָךְ קוֹרֵא אוֹתָהּ דְּרִיכָה. וְהַנָּבִיא הִמְשִׁיל לְשׁוֹנָם לְקֶשֶׁת שֶׁקֶר, שֶׁדָּרְכוּ **לְשׁוֹנָם** קֶשֶׁת בְּ**שֶׁקֶר**:


**They have directed** Lit. trodden. This expression is appropriate for the preparation of the bow to shoot an arrow, and a bow (קשת) is (arbalete in French), and they tread it with their feet. Therefore, he calls it treading, and the prophet compared their tongue to a treacherous bow, for they directed their tongue like a treacherous bow.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:2:2)


**מֵרָעָה אֶל רָעָה.** מֵעֲבֵירָה אֶל עֲבֵירָה:


**from evil to evil** From sin to sin.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:3:1)


**עָקוֹב יַעֲקֹב.** אָרוֹב יֶאֱרֹב:


**forges plans** He lays traps.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:4:1)


**הַעֲוֵה נִלְאוּ.** יָגְעוּ וְטָרְחוּ לְעַוֵּת אֶת דַּרְכָּם:


**they commit iniquity [until] they are weary** They wearied themselves and toiled to pervert their way.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:4:2)


**הַעֲוֵה.** כְּמוֹ ״הַכְבֵּד אֶת לִבּוֹ״ (שמות ח:יא): לְהַעֲווֹת:


העוה is like (Exod. 8:11) “and making heavy (והכבּד) his heart,” to commit iniquity.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:5:1)


**שִׁבְתְּךָ בְּתוֹךְ מִרְמָה.** כְּשֶׁהֵן יוֹשְׁבִים, חוֹשְׁבִין מַחְשְׁבוֹת מִרְמָה; וּ**בְמִרְמָה** הֶחְלִיפוּ יִרְאָתִי וּ**מֵאֲנוּ דַעַת אוֹתִי**:


**Your habitation is in the midst of deceit** When they sit, they devise plans of deceit, and with deceit they have exchanged fear of Me and refused to know Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:5:2)


**בְּמִרְמָה.** בִּשְׁבִיל הַמִּרְמָה:


**because of deceit** Heb. בּמרמה.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:6:1)


**הִנְנִי צוֹרְפָם.** בְּיִסּוּרִין:


**I will refine them** with torments.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:6:2)


**מִפְּנֵי בַת עַמִּי.** ״מִן קֳדָם חוֹבֵי כְּנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל״ (תרגום יונתן):


**because of the daughter of My people** Because of the sins of the congregation of Israel.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:7:1)


**חֵץ שָׁחוּט לְשׁוֹנָם.** מָשׁוּךְ; כְּמוֹ ״זָהָב שָׁחוּט״ (מלכים א י:טז), וְכֵן ״וְשַׁחֲטָה שִׁטִּים״ (הושע ה:ב) טְרֵיי״ט בְּלַעַ״ז. לִישְׁנָא אַחֲרִינָא: לְשׁוֹן שְׁחִיטָה מַמָּשׁ, טְרַאנְקָא״ט בְּלַעַ״ז; וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּגִיר דִּי מַחְרִיף״:


**Their tongue is a deadly arrow** Heb. שחוט, drawn. Comp. (I Kings 10:16) “malleable gold” (שחוט). Also, (Hos. 5:2) “And the extent of their turning away (ושחטה),” (tirer in French), pulling. Another explanation is that it is actually an expression of slaughter (tranchant in French, cutting). In this manner Menahem classified it (Machbereth Menahem p. 172), and in this manner Jonathan rendered it: כּגיר די מחרף, like a sharpened arrow.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:7:2)


**וּבְקִרְבּוֹ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּבְמָעוֹהִי״:


**but in his heart** lit. in his midst. Jonathan renders: in his innards.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:1)


**עַל הֶהָרִים.** עַל חֻרְבַּן הֶהָרִים:


**for the mountains** i.e., for the destruction of the mountains.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:2)


**וְעַל נְאוֹת מִדְבָּר.** נְוֵה רוֹעִים שֶׁבַּמִּדְבָּרוֹת. **נְאוֹת** לְשׁוֹן ״נָוֶה״, וְכֵן ״נְאוֹת אֱלֹהִים״ (תהלים פג:יג), וְכֵן ״לְבֵיתְךָ נָאֲוָה קֹדֶשׁ״ (תהלים צג:ה):


**for the dwellings of the wilderness** The dwelling of the shepherds in the wilderness. נאות is an expression of a dwelling. Comp. (Psalms 83: 13) “the dwellings (נאות) of God,” also (ibid. 93:5) “to Your house, the dwelling (נאוה) of sanctity.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:3)


**נִצְּתוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״צִיָּה״ (ירמיה ב:ו), וְכֵן ״נִצְּתָה״ (ירמיה ט:יא):


**they are withered** Heb. נצתו. An expression of aridness. Comp. (v. 11) “withered (נצתה) .”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:9:4)


**נָדְדוּ הָלָכוּ.** סוֹפָן לֵילֵךְ. חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה לֹא עָבַר אִישׁ בִּיהוּדָה; מִגָּלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ עַד פְּקִידַת כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנָה הָיוּ, וְרֶמֶז לַדָּבָר: **עַד בְּהֵמָה**. חֶשְׁבּוֹנָהּ בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם; וְהַחֶשְׁבּוֹן מְכֻוָּן בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְגִלָּה (ראה יומא נד.):


**have fled and are gone** i.e., they are destined to go. For fifty-two years no one passed through Judah; from the exile of Zedekiah until the order of Cyrus, king of Persia, were fifty-two years. An allusion to the matter [is that the numerical value for] “and the beast” (בּהמה) amounts to fifty-two, and the computation is exact, in Tractate Megillah [should read: Yoma 54a].



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:10:1)


**לְגַלִּים.** ״דְּגוֹרִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**heaps** Heb. דגורין heaps.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:11:1)


**מִי הָאִישׁ הֶחָכָם.** שֶׁיָּבִין **זֹאת**, וּמִי הַנָּבִיא **אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר פִּי ה׳ אֵלָיו**:


**Who is the man so wise** Who will understand this, and who is the prophet to whom the mouth of the Lord has spoken,


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:11:2)


**וְיַגִּידָהּ.** וּמִי יַגִּיד **עַל מָה אָבְדָה הָאָרֶץ** וּבָא כָּל הַפֻּרְעָנוּת הַזֶּה:


**that he may declare it?** And who will declare why the land is ruined and all this retribution has come?



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:14:1)


**לַעֲנָה.** עֵשֶׂב מַר:


**with wormwood** Bitter grass.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:14:2)


**מֵי רֹאשׁ.** אֶרֶס נָחָשׁ:


**poisonous water** Snake venom.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:18:1)


**כִּי הִשְׁלִיכוּ מִשְׁכְּנוֹתֵינוּ.** אֶת שׁוֹכְנֵיהֶם. וְיֵשׁ עוֹד לוֹמַר: כִּי הִשְׁלִיכוּ הָאוֹיְבִים אֶת מִשְׁכְּנוֹתֵינוּ לָאָרֶץ:


**they have cast down our dwellings** Heb. השליכו משכנותינו, [our dwellings] have cast out their inhabitants. We can also explain: For the enemies have cast our dwellings to the ground.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:20:1)


**לְהַכְרִית עוֹלָל מִחוּץ.** מִן הַשּׁוּק; כְּמוֹ ״וְחוּצוֹת תָּשִׂים לְךָ בְדַמֶּשֶׂק״ (מלכים א כ:לד):


**from the streets** Heb. מחוץ, from the street. Comp. (I Kings 20:34) “And you shall make yourself streets in Damascus.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:22:1)


**אַל יִתְהַלֵּל חָכָם.** אַל תִּתְהַלְּלוּ לוֹמַר: ״חֲכָמִים אֲנַחְנוּ״ (ירמיה ח:ח); הִנֵּה בִּדְבָרַי מְאַסְתֶּם וְחָכְמַת מַה לָכֶם (ירמיה ח:ט); אִם הִשְׂכַּלְתֶּם וִידַעְתֶּם אוֹתִי, אָז תִּתְהַלְּלוּ בְּחָכְמַתְכֶם:


**Let not the wise man boast** Do not boast, saying, “We are wise.” Behold, you have rejected My words, so what wisdom do you have? If you understand and know Me, then you can boast of your wisdom.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:23:1)


**השכל.** כמו ״הַכְבֵּד״ (שמות ח:יא) (אגטליינ״ט בלע״ז):


**that he understands** Heb. השכיל, like (Exod. 8:11) “making heavy (הכבד)” (Enteliant in O.F.).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:24:1)


**עַל כָּל מוּל בְּעָרְלָה.** עַל כָּל מוּל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עָרְלָה, אֲפִלּוּ עָרְלַת לֵב (בראשית רבה מו, ה):


**every circumcised one for his uncircumcision** Every circumcised one who has a foreskin, even an uncircumcised heart, and surely I will punish the heathens.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:25:1)


**עַל מִצְרַיִם וְגוֹ׳.** שְׁכֵינֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ, וְלָקוּ אַחֲרֶיהָ בְּסָמוּךְ לָהּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בִּיחֶזְקֵאל (יחזקאל כט:ב-יב); וּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו):


**Egypt etc.** These were the neighbors of Eretz Israel, and they were punished shortly after her, as it is written in Ezekiel (29), and explained in Seder Olam (ch. 26).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 9:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/9:25:2)


**קְצוּצֵי פֵאָה.** לְשׁוֹן קָצֶה: הַמּוּקְצִין בִּפְאַת מִדְבָּר:


**cast off to the corners** Heb. פאה, an expression of an end, those cast off to the corner of the desert.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:2:1)


**אֶל דֶּרֶךְ הַגּוֹיִם אַל תִּלְמָדוּ.** וְאָז **מֵאוֹתוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם אַל תֵּחָתּוּ** – מֵחַמָּה לוֹקָה וּמִמְּאוֹרוֹת לוֹקִין:


**Of the way of the nations you shall not learn** Of the way of the nations you shall not learnand then, from the signs of the heavens you will not be dismayed, from the eclipse of the sun or the eclipse of the luminaries.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:3:1)


**חָרָשׁ.** אֻמָּן:


**a carpenter** Heb. חרש, a craftsman.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:3:2)


**מַעֲצָד.** דּוֹלְדוֹיִירָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a small axe** Dolodojjre in O.F.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:1)


**מַסְמְרוֹת.** קְלוֹיִ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with nails** clous in French.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:2)


**וּמַקָּבוֹת.** קוֹרְנָס, מַרְטִי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and with sledge hammers** A hammer, marteau in French.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:4:3)


**יָפִיק.** יִכְרַע בִּרְכָּיו, לְשׁוֹן ״פּוּקָה״ (שמואל א כה:לא) ״וּפִיק בִּרְכַּיִם״ (נחום ב:יא):


**it does not bend** Heb. יפיק, it does not kneel with its knees. Comp. (I Sam. 25:31) “a stumbling block (פוקה)”; (Nahum 2:11) “and tottering (ופיק) of the knees.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:1)


**כְּתֹמֶר מִקְשָׁה.** מַקִּישׁ אוֹתוֹ בְּקוּרְנָס עַד שֶׁזּוֹקֵף לוֹ קוֹמָה כְּדֶקֶל:


**Like a palm tree they are beaten** He hammers them with a hammer until it has an upright stature like a palm tree.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:2)


**אַל תִּירְאוּ מֵהֶם.** אִם תִּפְרְשׁוּ מֵהֶם, **כִּי לֹא יָרֵעוּ** לָכֶם:


**fear them not** if you separate from them for they will not harm you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:5:3)


**וְגַם הֵיטִיב אֵין אוֹתָם.** אִם תַּעַבְדוּם. **הֵיטִיב** כְּמוֹ ״לְהֵיטִיב״ (ירמיה לב:מא):


**neither is it in them to do good** If you worship them. היטיב is like להיטיב to do good.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:7:1)


**לְךָ יָאָתָה.** לְךָ נָאֶה:


**for it befits You** It is proper for you.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:8:1)


**וּבְאַחַת יִבְעֲרוּ.** הַגּוֹיִים. וּמָה הִיא הָאַחַת **מוּסַר הֲבָלִים** שֶׁל **עֵץ** שֶׁלָּהֶם וְיִתְיַסְּרוּ עָלָיו. **יִבְעֲרוּ** לְשׁוֹן שְׁטוּת, וְכֵן **וְיִכְסָלוּ**:


**But with one thing they are brutish** i.e., the heathens, and what is that one thing? Their vanities of wood for which they will be punished, and they will be punished for it. יבערו is an expression of foolishness, so is ויכסלו.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:9:1)


**מְרֻקָּע.** מְרֻדָּד:


**Silver beaten into plates** Heb. מרקע.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:10:1)


**וַה׳ אֱלֹהִים אֱמֶת.** לָמָּה? כִּי **הוּא אֱלֹהִים חַיִּים וּמֶלֶךְ עוֹלָם**, לְכָךְ יָכוֹל לְאַמֵּת דְּבָרָיו. אֲבָל בָּשָׂר וָדָם – אוֹמֵר לַעֲשׂוֹת, וּמֵת אוֹ תָּשַׁשׁ כֹּחוֹ אוֹ יוֹרֵד מִנְּכָסָיו, וְאֵין בְּיָדוֹ לְקַיֵּם (פסיקתא רבתי יד):


**But the Lord God is true** Why? Because He is a living God and the King of the world. Therefore, He can make His words come true. A mortal person, however, plans to do something and dies or becomes weak or loses his property, and has no ability to execute his plans.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:11:1)


**כִּדְנָה תֵאמְרוּן.** אִגֶּרֶת הִיא שֶׁשָּׁלַח יִרְמְיָה לִיכָנְיָה וְגָלוּתוֹ בַּגּוֹלָה, לְהָשִׁיב לַכַּשְׂדִּים אֲרָמִית תְּשׁוּבָה, אִם אוֹמְרִין לָהֶם לַעֲבוֹד לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה (תנחומא, שופטים יב):


**So shall you say to them** This is a letter that Jeremiah sent to Jeconiah and those exiled with him in exile, to reply to the Chaldees in Aramaic, a reply if they tell them to worship idols.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:12:1)


**נָטָה.** טינדי״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**He stretched out** Tendre in French.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:1)


**לְקוֹל תִּתּוֹ.** בְּעֵת בֹּא קוֹל שֶׁהוּא נוֹתֵן **הֲמוֹן מַיִם בַּשָּׁמַיִם**:


**At the sound of His giving** At the time the sound comes that He is giving a multitude of water in the heavens.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:2)


**נְשִׂיאִים.** עֲנָנִים:


**clouds** Heb. נשאים, clouds.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:13:3)


**בְּרָקִים.** פְלַנְדוֹרִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**lightning** plaindres in old French.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:14:1)


**נִבְעַר.** לְשׁוֹן ״אִישׁ בַּעַר״ (תהילים צב:ז), וְכֵן **נִבְעֲרוּ הָרוֹעִים**:


**brutish** Heb. נבער. Comp. (Psalms 92:7) “a brutish (בער) man”; also here (infra 10:21), “the shepherds have become brutish (נבערו).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:14:2)


**נִסְכּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מַסֵּכָה״ (שמות לב:ד):


**his molten images** Heb. נסכו. An expression of מסכה, a molten image.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:15:1)


**בְּעֵת פְּקֻדָּתָם.** כְּשֶׁאֶפְקֹד עֲלֵיהֶם:


**at the time of their visitation** When I will visit retribution upon them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:17:1)


**אִסְפִּי מֵאֶרֶץ כִּנְעָתֵךְ.** הַחְבִּיאוּ, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּנוֹשִׁי מֵאַרְעָא סְחוֹרְתִּיךְ״, כְּמוֹ ״כְּנַעַן בְּיָדוֹ מֹאזְנֵי מִרְמָה״ (הושע יב:ח); וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ **כִּנְעָתֵךְ** – לְשׁוֹן הַכְנָעָה וְשִׁפְלוּת, וְ**אִסְפִּי** – לְשׁוֹן שְׁחִיָּה: שְׁחִי וְהַשְׁפִּילִי יוֹתֵר מִשִּׁפְלוּת אֶרֶץ אֶת הַכְנָעָתֵךְ, אַתְּ, יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, הַ**יּוֹשֶׁבֶת** עַתָּה **בַּמָּצוֹר**:


**Gather in from the land your merchandise** Heb. כנעתך. Hide, gather in from the land your merchandise. Comp. (Hos. 12:8) “The trafficker (כּנען) in his hand are deceitful scales.” Menahem explains כנעתך as an expression of humility (הכנעה) and lowliness. And אספי is an expression of bending down. Bend down and humble yourself more than the lowliness of the earth, you Jerusalem, that dwells now in a fortified city (Machbereth p. 107).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:18:1)


**קוֹלֵעַ.** כְּזֶה שֶׁהוּא זוֹרֵק אֶבֶן בַּקֶּלַע:


**sling** as one slings a stone with a slingshot.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:18:2)


**לְמַעַן.** אֲשֶׁר יִמְצְאוּ שְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּתָם:


**that** they may find their just deserts.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:19:1)


**נַחְלָה.** לְשׁוֹן חוֹלִי:


**grievous** Heb. נחלה, an expression of sickness (חלי).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:19:2)


**וְאֶשָּׂאֶנּוּ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וַאֲסוֹבְרַנִיהּ״; אֲשׁוֹפְרִיר״לוֹי בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and I will bear it** Heb. ואשאנו. Jonathan renders. And I will bear it ([a]souffrir lui in O.F.).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:20:1)


**יְצָאוּנִי.** יָצְאוּ מִמֶּנִּי; יְרוּשָׁלַיִם אוֹמֶרֶת כֵּן:


**have left me** Heb. יצאוני, have left me, Jerusalem says this.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:1)


**הָרֹעִים.** הַמְּלָכִים:


**For the shepherds** The kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:2)


**לֹא הִשְׂכִּילוּ.** לֹא הִצְלִיחוּ בְּמַלְכוּתָם; כְּמוֹ ״בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִפְנֶה יַשְׂכִּיל״ (משלי יז:ח, שמואל א יד:מז):


**they have not prospered** They have not prospered in their kingdom. Comp. (Prov. 17:8) “To all that he turns, he will prosper (ישכיל).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:21:3)


**מַרְעִיתָם.** צֹאן מַרְעִיתָם:


**their flocks** Heb. מרעיתם, lit. their pasture, the flocks of their pasture.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:23:1)


**לֹא לָאָדָם דַּרְכּוֹ.** כִּי יֵצֶר הָרַע שֶׁבָּרָאתָ בּוֹ מַטֵּהוּ מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **יָדַעְתִּי ה׳ כִּי לֹא לָאָדָם דַּרְכּוֹ**, אֵין בְּיַד כָּל אָדָם כֹּחַ לְהַצְלִיחַ דַּרְכּוֹ, וְאִם לֹא עַל פִּיךָ, אֵין כֹּחַ בְּיַד אוֹיֵב לְהַחֲרִיב בֵּיתֶךָ:


**man’s way is not his** For the evil inclination that You created in him leads him astray from the way. Another explanation is: I know, O Lord, that man’s way is not his; not every man has the power to make his way prosper, and if it were not for Your command, the enemy would have no power to destroy Your Temple.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:1)


**אַךְ בְּמִשְׁפָּט.** בְּיִסּוּרִין:


**but with justice** With chastisements.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:2)


**בְּמִשְׁפָּט.** יוּשְׁטִיצָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with justice** (justice in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:3)


**אַךְ בְּמִשְׁפָּט.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בְּרַם בְּדִין חֲשׁוּךְ״:


**but with justice** Jonathan renders: but with mild judgment.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:24:4)


**אַל בְּאַפְּךָ.** פֶּן תְּהֵא כְלָיָה:


**not with Your wrath** lest there be complete destruction.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 10:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/10:25:1)


**אָכְלוּ.** דְוֹורִירוֹ״נְט בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן **וַאֲכָלֻהוּ**:


**they have devoured** (devorerent in French). The same applies to “and consumed him” (ואכלהוּ).



## Chapter 11


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:5:1)


**אָמֵן ה׳.** שֶׁיְּהֵא אָרוּר:


**“Amen, O Lord”** that he be cursed.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:7:1)


**הָעֵד הַעִדֹתִי.** לְשׁוֹן הַתְרָאָה:


**For I warned** Heb. העידתי, an expression of warning.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:8:1)


**וָאָבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת כָּל דִּבְרֵי הַבְּרִית.** הָאָלוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בַּבְּרִית עַל הַדְּבָרִים אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי לַעֲשׂוֹת וְלֹא עָשׂוּ:


**And I brought upon them all the words of this covenant** The curses stated in the covenant for the things that I commanded to do and they did not do.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:12:1)


**עָרֵי יְהוּדָה.** מֵעָרֵי יְהוּדָה:


**And they shall go the cities of Judah** From the cities of Judah.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:14:1)


**רִנָּה וּתְפִלָּה.** ״בְּבָעוּ וּבְצָלוּ״ (תרגום יונתן):


**cry nor prayer** This follows the Targum.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:1)


**מָה לִידִידִי בְּבֵיתִי.** מָה לְעַם סְגֻלָּתִי לָבֹא עוֹד בְּבֵיתִי:


**What has my beloved to do in My house** What reason has My treasured people to come anymore into My house?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:2)


**עֲשׂוֹתָהּ הַמְזִמָּתָה.** לַעֲשׂוֹת הִיא מְזִמַּת עֲצַת חֲטָאִים שֶׁלָּהּ:


**[seeing that]...perform evil design** That she does the evil design of her sinful plans.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:3)


**הָרַבִּים.** שֶׁבָּהֶם:


**the many** of them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:4)


**וּבְשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ יַעַבְרוּ מֵעָלֶיךָ.** אַף הַמִּילָה בִּטְּלוּ מִבְּשָׂרָם, שֶׁעַל יָדָהּ הָיוּ מְקֻדָּשִׁים לִי (מנחות נג:):


**and the holy flesh they remove from you** Even the circumcision through which they were hallowed to Me, they have abolished from their flesh.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:15:5)


**כִּי רָעָתֵכִי אָז תַּעֲלֹזִי.** כְּשֶׁאַתֶּם עוֹבְרִים עֲבֵרָה אַתֶּם שְׂמֵחִים:


**for [with] your evil, then you rejoice** When you commit a transgression, you rejoice.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:16:1)


**לְקוֹל הֲמוּלָּה.** לְשׁוֹן שָׁאוֹן עַם רַב שֶׁיָּבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם:


**to the sound of a great tumult** Heb. המולה, an expression of the tumult of a large multitude that will come upon them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:16:2)


**וְרָעוּ דָּלִיּוֹתָיו.** וְרִצְּצוּ אֶת עֲנָפָיו:


**And its branches were broken.** Heb. ורעו דליותיו. And its branches were shattered.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:18:1)


**וַה׳ הוֹדִיעַנִי.** כָּךְ אָמַר הַנָּבִיא: הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הוֹדִיעַנִי אֶת פּוּרְעָנוּתָם:


**And the Lord let me know** So said the prophet, The Holy One, blessed be He, let me know their retribution.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:1)


**וַאֲנִי כְּכֶבֶשׂ אַלּוּף.** אֲשֶׁר **יוּבַל לִטְבוֹחַ**:


**And I was like a lamb [and] a bull** that will be brought to the slaughter.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:2)


**יוּבַל.** לְפִי שֶׁאֲנִי מוֹדִיעָם הָרָעָה, הֵם אוֹמְרִים לְהָרְגֵנִי:


**brought** Since I let them know of the evil, they plan to kill me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:3)


**כְּכֶבֶשׂ אַלּוּף.** דּוּנָשׁ וִיהוּדָה בֶּן קוּרִישׁ פֵּתְרוּהוּ: **כְּכֶבֶשׂ** וּפָר אֲשֶׁר **יוּבַל לִטְבוֹחַ**. וְ**אַלּוּף** כְּמוֹ ״שְׁגַר אֲלָפֶיךָ״ (דברים כח:ד). וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר וְאַלּוּף, יֵשׁ לָנוּ כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ: ״שֶׁמֶשׁ יָרֵחַ עָמַד זְבֻלָה״ (חבקוק ג:יא); ״כְּסוּס עָגוּר כֵּן אֲצַפְצֵף״ (ישעיהו לח:יד). וּמְנַחֵם פֵּתֵר **כֶּבֶשׂ אַלּוּף** – כֶּבֶשׂ גָּדוֹל (עיין בתהלים נח בפירוש רש״י); וְיוֹנָתָן מְסַיְּעוֹ, שֶׁתִּרְגֵּם: ״כְּאִימַר בְּחִיר״:


**like a lamb [and] a bull** Heb. ככבש אלוף. Dunash and Judah son of Kudish (Karish) interpreted it as a lamb and a bull, brought to the slaughter. And אלוף is like (Deut. 28:4) “the offspring of your cattle (אלפיך).” Although it was not stated ואלוף, we find similar cases, e.g., (Habukkuk 3:11) “The sun [and] the moon (ירח) stood on high”; (Isaiah 38:14) “Like a crane [and] a swallow (עגור) so I chirp.” And Menahem interpreted ככבש אלוף, a large lamb (see Psalms 58:9, Rashi ad loc.), and Jonathan supports him, since he renders like a choice lamb.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:19:4)


**נַשְׁחִיתָהּ עֵץ בְּלַחְמוֹ.** ״נִרְמֵי סְמָא דְּמָוְתָא בְּמִכְלֵיהּ״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Let us destroy his food with wood** Let us put poison into his food.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:20:1)


**אֶרְאֶה נִקְמָתְךָ מֵהֶם.** אֶת **אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת** הָיָה מְקַלֵּל:


**Let me see Your vengeance against them** He cursed the people of Anathoth.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/11:23:1)


**אֶל אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת.** כְּמוֹ עַל אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת:


**upon the men of Anathoth** Heb. אל-אנשי ענתות, like על-אנשי ענתות, upon the men of Anathoth.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:1)


**צַדִּיק אַתָּה ה׳ כִּי אָרִיב אֵלֶיךָ.** כְּשֶׁאֶתְוַכֵּחַ עִמְּךָ, יָדַעְתִּי כִּי תִצְדַּק בִּדְבָרֶיךָ, אַךְ חָפֵץ אֲנִי לְדַבֵּר **מִשְׁפָּטִים אוֹתָךְ**, לְהוֹדִיעֵנִי דַּרְכֶּךָ (שמות לג:יג):


**You are right, O Lord, when I contend with You** When I debate with You, I know that you will be right in Your words, but I wish to argue with You so that You will let me know Your way.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:2)


**מַדּוּעַ דֶּרֶךְ רְשָׁעִים צָלֵחָה.** שֶׁנָּתַתָּ גְּדֻלָּה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הָרָשָׁע וְתַצְלִיחֵהוּ לְהַחֲרִיב בֵּיתֶךָ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: עַל אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת הָיָה צוֹעֵק וְקוֹרֵא תִּגָּר (ירמיה יא:כא):


**Why has the way of the wicked prospered?** That You have given greatness to Nebuchadnezzar the wicked, and you have made him succeed in destroying Your house? Another explanation is that he was crying and complaining about the men of Anathoth.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:1:3)


**שָׁלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״שַׁלְוָה״ (תהלים קכב:ז):


**have peace** Heb. שלו, an expression of שלוה, peace.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:1)


**וְאַתָּה ה׳ יְדַעְתָּנִי.** גַּם עַל אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת הָעוֹמְדִים עָלַי עָזְרֵנִי, כִּי אַתָּה יְדַעְתָּנִי:


**But You, O Lord, have known me** Also, concerning the men of Anathoth You have aided me, for You have known me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:2)


**הַתִּיקֵם כְּצֹאן לְטִבְחָה.** נַתְּקֵם מִן הַחַיִּים לָמוּת, דִּישְׁטָאקָאלוּ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״עַד הַתִּיקְנוּ אוֹתָם מִן הָעִיר״ (יהושע ח:ו):


**Draw them out like sheep to the slaughter** Draw them out of life to death (detache les in French, separate them, cut them off). Comp. (Joshua 8:6) “until we have drawn (התיקנו) them from the city.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:3:3)


**וְהַקְדִּישֵׁם.** הַזְמִינֵם:


**and prepare them** Heb. הקדישם, prepare them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:1)


**עַד מָתַי תֶּאֱבַל הָאָרֶץ וְעֵשֶׂב כָּל הַשָּׂדֶה יִיבָשׁ.** עַד כָּאן דִּבְרֵי הַנָּבִיא, וְרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ מְשִׁיבָתוֹ: **מֵרָעַת יֹשְׁבֵי בָהּ סָפְתָה בְהֵמוֹת וָעוֹף**:


**How long will the land mourn and the grass of all the field dry out?** Up to here are the prophet’s words, and the Holy Spirit replies to him, “Because of the evil of its inhabitants, beasts and fowl are ended.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:2)


**תֶּאֱבַל.** תֶּחֱרַב:


**will...mourn** Be waste.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:3)


**סָפְתָה.** פוֹנִיד״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**ended** (finira in French, will terminate).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:4)


**כִּי אָמְרוּ.** יוֹשְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ הַזֹּאת:


**for they said** i.e., the inhabitants of this land have said.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:4:5)


**לֹא יִרְאֶה אֶת אַחֲרִיתֵנוּ.** אֵין גָּלוּי לְפָנָיו מַה יִהְיֶה בְּסוֹפֵינוּ:


**“He will not see our end.”** It is not revealed to Him what will be at our end.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:1)


**כִּי אֶת רַגְלִים רַצְתָּה וַיַּלְאוּךָ.** קְרוֹבֶיךָ, הַכֹּהֲנִים כְּמוֹתְךָ, וְאַנְשֵׁי עִירְךָ בָּאִים לְהָרְגֶךָ. **אֶת רַגְלִים.** עִם רַגְלִים, פִּיאוֹנִיד״ש בְּלַעַז:


**For if you have run with foot men and they wearied you** Your kin, the priests like you and the men of your city come to kill you. **with footmen** Heb. רגלים (pieton in O.F), pedestrian.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:2)


**וְאֵיךְ תִּתְחָרֶה אֶת הַסּוּסִים.** לָרוּץ אֶת הַסּוּסִים; כְּלוֹמַר: וּמָה קְרוֹבֶיךָ הַכֹּהֲנִים בָּאִים לְהָרְגְךָ, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁיָּקוּמוּ לְהוֹרְגְךָ שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה; וְזֶהוּ קִצְפִּי עֲלֵיהֶם, וְזוֹ הִיא הַצְלָחַת הָאוֹיֵב. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״דְּנָא תְּיוּבְתָּא וְגוֹ׳״. וּמִדְרַשׁ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סנהדרין צו.): אִם עַל שְׂכַר שָׁלֹשׁ פְּסִיעוֹת שֶׁרָץ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לִכְבוֹדִי בִּימֵי מְרֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן אַתָּה תָּמֵהַּ, שֶׁהִרְבֵּיתִי גְדֻלָּתוֹ כָּל כָּךְ, מָה תִּתְמַהּ עוֹד כְּשֶׁתִּרְאֶה שִׁלּוּם שָׂכָר שֶׁאֲשַׁלֵּם לַצַּדִּיקִים שֶׁרָצוּ לְפָנַי כַּסּוּסִים!


**how will you compete with horses** To run with the horses. That is to say, If even your brethren the priests come to kill you, surely the princes of Judah will rise up against you to kill you. This is the reason for My anger against them, and this is the reason for the success of the enemy. And so did Jonathan render: This is the reply, etc. And, according to the Midrash of our Rabbis (Sanhedrin 96a): If you are amazed about the reward for the three steps that Nebuchadnezzar ran in My honor in the days of Merodach-baladan, why I gave him so much greatness, how much more will you be amazed when you see the payment of reward that I will pay the righteous who ran before Me like horses!


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:3)


**תִּתְחָרֶה.** אַאֲטִיר״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**will you compete** (aastir in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:4)


**וּבְאֶרֶץ שָׁלוֹם.** אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה בּוֹטֵחַ שָׁם הִלְאוּךָ:


**and in the peaceful land that you trust** there they wearied you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:5:5)


**וְאֵיךְ תַּעֲשֶׂה בִּגְאוֹן.** שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה. וּגְאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן מָשָׁל הוּא, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם אֲרָיוֹת וּנְמֵרִים, וְכֵן לִמְּדָנוּ יוֹנָתָן בְּהַרְבֵּה מְקוֹמוֹת; וְכָאן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וְאַכְדֵּין אַתְּ מְדַמֵּי לְקָבֵל חֵיוַת בָּרָא דִּי בְּרוּם יַרְדְּנָא״, וְהִנֵּה ״כְּאַרְיֵה יַעֲלֶה מִגְּאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן״ (ירמיה מט:יט; נ:מד) תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן בִּשְׁתֵּי מְקוֹמוֹת: ״כְּאַרְיֵה דְּסָלִיק מֵרוּם יַרְדְּנָא״:


**how will you do in the heights** The princes of Judah. The pride of the Jordan is symbolic since it is a place of lions and tigers, and Jonathan taught us this in many places. And here he paraphrased. And how do you expect to do against the wild beasts that are on the height of the Jordan? “And behold, as a lion ascends from the height of the Jordan,” Jonathan renders in two places (infra 49:19, 50:44): like a lion that ascends from the height of the Jordan.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:6:1)


**קָרְאוּ אַחֲרֶיךָ מָלֵא.** קְבוּצַת אֲנָשִׁים:


**have called a gang after you** Heb. מלא, a group of men.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:7:1)


**יְדִידוּת נַפְשִׁי.** עַם סְגֻלָּתִי:


**My soul’s beloved** My treasured people.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:8:1)


**נָתְנָה עָלַי בְּקוֹלָהּ.** עָלְתָה לְפָנַי צַעֲקַת חֲמָסֶיהָ:


**she raised her voice against Me** The cry of her violent deeds has ascended before Me.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:1)


**הָעַיִט צָבוּעַ.** הַה״א תָּמוּהַּ, לְכָךְ הוּא נָקוּד פַּתָּ״ח: הֲכִי הֵם עוֹף צָבוּעַ, מְגֹאָל בְּדָם, שֶׁשְּׁאָר הָעוֹפוֹת נֶאֱסָפִים עָלָיו? דָּבָר אַחֵר: **הָעַיִט צָבוּעַ** עוֹף אֶחָד שֶׁהוּא צָבוּעַ וְכָל הָעוֹפוֹת נֶאֱסָפִים עָלָיו לְאוֹכְלוֹ שֶׁשּׂוֹנְאִים אוֹתוֹ:


**Is...a speckled bird of prey?** The ‘heh’ is that of the interrogative. Therefore, it is vowelized with a ‘pattah.’ Are they like a speckled bird of prey, stained with blood, around which other birds gather? Another explanation:


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:2)


**הָעַיִט סָבִיב עָלֶיהָ.** שֶׁמָּא עוֹף שָׁמַיִם הֵם אֵלּוּ הַחַיָּלוֹת, הַגְּדוּדִים הַקַּלִּים אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבֶיהָ?!


**Is...a speckled bird of prey** One bird is speckled, and all the birds gather around it to eat it because they hate it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:9:3)


**הֵתָיוּ.** בֹּאוּ:


**Are there birds of prey around her?** Are these armies fowl of the heavens, the swift troops that are around her?



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:1)


**רוֹעִים רַבִּים.** רָאשֵׁי גְיָסוֹת שֶׁל חֵיל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה ״אֵלֶיהָ יָבֹאוּ רוֹעִים וְעֶדְרֵיהֶם״ (ירמיה ו:ג):


**Many shepherds** The commanders of the troops of Nebuchadnezzar’s army, as it is stated above (6:3): “Shepherds and their flocks shall come to her.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:2)


**בֹּסְסוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מִרְמָס״ (ישעיה י:ו); וְכֵן ״וְהָיוּ כַגִּבּוֹרִים בּוֹסִים בְּטִיט חוּצוֹת״ (זכריה י:ה):


**they have trampled** Heb. בססו, an expression of trampling. Cf. (Zech. 10:5) “And they shall be like mighty men trampling (בוסים) the mire of the streets.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:10:3)


**חֶלְקַת.** קַנְפַנְיָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**My field** Heb. חלקתי, (campagne in French), countryside.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:11:1)


**אָבְלָה עָלַי.** לְפָנַי:


**the wasted one mourns before Me** Heb. עלי, lit. on Me.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:1)


**זָרְעוּ חִטִּים וְקֹצִים קָצָרוּ.** הִתְפַּלְלוּ וְלֹא נִתְקַבְּלָה תְּפִלָּתָם. לָמָּה? לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נָרוּ לָהֶם נִיר תְּשׁוּבָה וּמַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים, לְכָךְ זָרְעוּ אֶל קוֹצִים (ירמיה ד:ג). סֵפֶר שֶׁנָּקוּד בּוֹ זִרְעוּ, בְּחִירִ״ק הַזַּיִ״ן, שִׁבּוּשׁ הוּא:


**They sowed wheat, but they reaped thorns** They prayed, but their prayer was not accepted. Why? Because they did not plow for themselves a furrow of repentance and good deeds. Therefore, they sowed seeds resulting in thorns. A book in which זרעו is vowelized with a ‘chirik’ under the ‘zayin,’ is erroneous.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:2)


**נֶחְלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן חוֹלִי, נֶחְלוּ בְּמַכְאוֹבָם וְצָעֲקָתָם לְפָנַי, וְ**לֹא יוֹעִלוּ**:


**they have become ill** Heb. נחלו, an expression of illness. They have become ill with their pain and their cry before Me, but they did not avail.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:13:3)


**מִתְּבוּאֹתֵיכֶם.** מִמַּעֲשֵׂיכֶם:


**of your increase** Of your deeds.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:14:1)


**שְׁכֵנַי הָרָעִים.** מִצְרַיִם, עַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב וְצוֹר וְצִידוֹן, שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁכֵנֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמַזִּיקִין אוֹתָם:


**My wicked neighbors** Egypt, Ammon and Moab, Tyre and Sidon, who were neighbors of Eretz Israel and were inflicting harm upon them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:15:1)


**אָשׁוּב וְרִחַמְתִּים.** כֻּלָּן לָקוּ סָמוּךְ לְחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת, וּבְכֻלָּם נֶאֱמַר ״וְשַׁבְתִּי אֶת שְׁבוּת״ בְּסֵפֶר זֶה (ירמיה כט:יד; ל:ג, יח; לא:כב; לג:ז, יא, כו; מח:מז; מט:ו, לט):


**I will return and have pity on them** They all suffered close to the destruction of the Temple, and concerning each one it is stated in this Book, “I will return the exile of...”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:16:1)


**אִם לָמוֹד יִלְמְדוּ.** אִם יַאֲמִינוּ בַּה׳:


**if they learn** If they proselytize.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 12:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/12:16:2)


**וְנִבְנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן בִּנְיָן:


**they shall be built up** an expression of building.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:1:1)


**אֵזוֹר.** פּוֹרְצִינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a girdle** (pourceint in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:1:2)


**וּבַמַּיִם לֹא תְבִיאֵהוּ.** לְכַבְּסוֹ כְּשֶׁיְּהֵא מָלֵא זֵיעָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּמַהֵר לֵירָקֵב:


**and do not put it in water** to wash it when it will be soaked with sweat, in order that it hasten to decay.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:7:1)


**לֹא יִצְלַח לַכֹּל.** אֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִכְלוּם:


**it was of no use for anything** It was not fit for anything.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:10:1)


**וִיהִי כָּאֵזוֹר הַזֶּה.** גְּאוֹנָם יִהְיֶה כָּאֵזוֹר הַזֶּה הַנִּרְקָב:


**and it shall be like this girdle** Their pride shall be like this rotten girdle.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:12:1)


**נֵבֶל.** כְּלִי חֶרֶס הוּא, וְעָשׂוּי לְהַכְנִיס בּוֹ **יָיִן**:


**bottle** Heb. נבל. This is an earthenware vessel, and it is used to hold wine.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:14:1)


**וְנִפַּצְתִּים אִישׁ אֶל אָחִיו.** אַכֶּה זֶה עַל זֶה, עַד שֶׁיִּשָּׁבְרוּ חֲתִיכוֹת דַּקּוֹת; כְּמוֹ ״כִּכְלִי יוֹצֵר תְּנַפְּצֵם״ (תהלים ב:ט); ״וְנִפֵּץ אֶת עֹלָלַיִךְ״ (תהלים קלז:ט); וְכֵן כָּל נִפּוּץ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא:


**And I will dash them one against the other** I will dash one against the other until they break into small pieces. Cf (Psalms 2:9) “Like a potter’s vessel shall you shatter them (תנפצם)” (ibid. 137: 9). “And dash ונפץ your babes”; and so is every expression of נפוץ in Scripture.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:14:2)


**וְהָאָבוֹת וְהַבָּנִים.** אֲנַפֵּץ זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה:


**and the fathers and the sons** I will dash one against the other.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:1)


**יִתְנַגְּפוּ.** יִכָּשְׁלוּ, אצופי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and before your feet stumble** Heb. יתנגפו (achopper in French), to stumble.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:2)


**הָרֵי נָשֶׁף.** הָרֵי חֹשֶׁךְ, בְּלֶכְתָּם בַּגּוֹלָה:


**the dark mountains** Heb. נשף, the dark mountains when they go into exile.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:16:3)


**וְשִׁית לַעֲרָפֶל.** כְּמוֹ וְלָשִׁית אֶת הָאוֹר לַעֲרָפֶל:


**and making it into a thick cloud** Heb. ושית, like ולשית, and to make the light into a thick cloud.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:17:1)


**מִפְּנֵי גֵוָה.** מִפְּנֵי גְדוּלָּה שֶׁלָּכֶם שֶׁתִּתְבַּטֵּל. דָּבָר אַחֵר, מִפְּנֵי גַּאֲוָתָהּ שֶׁל מַלְכוּת שָׁמַיִם שֶׁתִּנָּתֵן לֶאֱלִילִים שֶׁל כַּשְׂדִּים (חגיגה ה:):


**because of your pride** Because of your greatness which will be curtailed. Another explanation: Because of the pride of the kingdom of Heaven that will be given over to the idols of Babylon.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:18:1)


**לַמֶּלֶךְ וְלַגְּבִירָה.** יְהוֹיָכִין וְאִמּוֹ:


**to the king and to the queen mother** Jehoiachin and his mother.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:18:2)


**יָרַד מְרַאֲשׁוֹתֵיכֶם.** וּושְׁטְרִי קָאיִ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, עֲלִיַּתְכֶם; כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיֵּצֵא אֵלָיו יְהוֹיָכִין הוּא וְאִמּוֹ וַעֲבָדָיו״ (מלכים ב כד:יב), וְכֻלָּם הוֹלִיכָם בַּגּוֹלָה:


**your greatness has come down** Heb. מראשתיכם (vostremijevec in O.F.), your ascent, as it is stated: (II Kings 24:12) “And Jehoiachin... came out to him (sic), he and his mother and his servants,” and he led all of them in exile.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:19:1)


**עָרֵי הַנֶּגֶב סֻגְּרוּ.** לִפְנֵי הַבָּאִין עֲלֵיהֶם מִן הַצָּפוֹן; עָרֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל סְגוּרוֹת מֵאֵימַת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**The cities of the South are shut up** before those coming upon them from the north [i.e.,] the cities of Eretz Israel are shut up because of fear of Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:19:2)


**הָגְלַת שְׁלוֹמִים.** לֹא יִלָּחֵם יְהוֹיָכִין, כִּי לְשָׁלוֹם יֵצֵא וְיַגְלֵהוּ (מלכים ב כד:יב). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **שְׁלוֹמִים** גרדונאמינ״ץ בְּלַעַז, כְּלוֹמַר: תַּשְׁלוּם עֲוֹנָם; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אִיתְקַבְּלוּ תּוֹשְׁלַמַת חוֹבֵיהוֹן״:


**she was exiled peacefully** Jehoiachin will not wage war, but he will come out peacefully, and he will exile them. Another interpretation. שלומים (gueredon in O.F.), reprisal. That is to say: she was exiled as the recompense for their iniquity. And Jonathan rendered in this manner: the recompense of their sins was received.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:20:1)


**שְׂאוּ עֵינֵיכֶם וּרְאוּ.** אֶת **הַבָּאִים** עֲלֵיכֶם **מִצָּפוֹן**:


**Lift your eyes and see** those coming upon you from the North.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:20:2)


**אַיֵּה הָעֵדֶר נִתַּן לָךְ.** אַחַר שֶׁיִּגְלוּ, יֹאמַר לִירוּשָׁלַיִם כֵּן:


**Where is the flock that was given you** After they are exiled, this will be said to Jerusalem.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:21:1)


**מַה תֹּאמְרִי כִּי יִפְקֹד.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא **עָלַיִךְ**:


**What will you say when He visits** i.e., when the Holy One, blessed be He, visits upon you?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:21:2)


**לִמַּדְתְּ אוֹתָם עָלַיִךְ אַלֻּפִים לְרֹאשׁ.** שְׁלַחְתֶּם אֲלֵיהֶם מַלְאָכִים לַכַּשְׂדִּיִּים לְהָבִיא מִשָּׁם אֱלִילִים שֶׁלָּהֶם לְעָבְדָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּיחֶזְקֵאל (יחזקאל כג:טז) ״וַתַּעְגְּבָה עֲלֵיהֶם לְמַרְאֵה עֵינֶיהָ וַתִּשְׁלַח מַלְאָכִים אֲלֵיהֶם כַּשְׂדִּימָה״. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **לִמַּדְתְּ אוֹתָם עָלַיִךְ אַלֻּפִים** – חִזְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁהֶרְאָה לִשְׁלוּחֵי מְרֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן אֶת כָּל בֵּית נְכָאתָה (ישעיהו לט:ב):


**when you accustomed them to be princes at your head** You sent emissaries to the Chaldees to bring their idols from there to worship them, as it is written in Ezekiel (23 16): “And she lusted for them to the sight of her eyes, and she sent emissaries to them, to the Chaldees.” Another explanation of “you accustomed them to be princes over you”: Hezekiah who showed the emissaries of Merodach-baladan his entire treasure house (Isaiah 39:2, II Kings 20:13).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:1)


**שׁוּלָיִךְ.** שִׁפּוּלֵי הַבְּגָדִים, טִי״שׁ אוֹרְלָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּשֶׁמַּפְשִׁיטִין אֶת הָאִשָּׁה דֶּרֶךְ בִּזָּיוֹן, מְהַפְּכִין חֲלוּקָהּ מִלְּמַטָּה לְמַעְלָה:


**your skirts** The skirts of the garments (tes ourlets in French), your hems. When a woman is stripped in a humiliating way, they turn her chemise upside down.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:2)


**נֶחְמְסוּ עֲקֵבָיִךְ.** נִגְזְרוּ דְּרָכַיִךְ. **נֶחְמְסוּ** כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּחְמֹס כַּגַּן שֻׂכּוֹ״ (איכה ב:ו):


**your steps cut off** Your ways are cut off. נחמסו is like (Lam. 2:6) “And He cut off (ויחמֹס) like a garden His dwelling.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:22:3)


**עֲקֵבָיִךְ.** טְרַצִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; פָּסְקוּ עוֹבְרֵי דְרָכִים:


**your steps** (traces in O.F.) Travelers have stopped.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:1)


**כּוּשִׁי.** שֵׁם אָדָם שָׁחוֹר, וְקוֹרִין מָאוֹר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a Cushite** Heb. כושי, the name of a black man, who is called Maure in French, a Moor.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:2)


**וְנָמֵר.** לִיאַפַּרְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**or a leopard** leopard in French.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:3)


**חֲבַרְבֻּרֹתָיו.** הַנָּמֵר מְנֻמָּר הוּא, וְהֵם הַחֲבַרְבֻּרוֹת, טָק״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיֵּהָפְכוּ:


**his spots** The leopard is spotted, and they are the spots (taches in French), and it is impossible for them to change.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:23:4)


**הָרֵעַ.** כְּמוֹ ״לְהָרֵעַ״ (תהלים לז:ח):


**to do evil** Heb. הרע, like להרע, to do evil.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:25:1)


**מְנַת מִדַּיִךְ.** חֵלֶק מִדּוֹתַיִךְ, מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה:


**the portion of your measures** The portion of your measures, a measure for a measure.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:26:1)


**חָשַׂפְתִּי שׁוּלַיִךְ.** גִּלִּיתִי שִׁפּוּלֵי בְגָדַיִךְ, לְהָפְכָם עַל פָּנָיִךְ:


**have uncovered your skirts** I have uncovered the hems of your garments upon your face.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:27:1)


**מִצְהֲלוֹתַיִךְ.** כְּצַהֲלַת סוּסִים נוֹאֲפִים (ירמיה ה:ח):


**your neighings** like the neighing of adulterous horses.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 13:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/13:27:2)


**אַחֲרֵי מָתַי.** תִּטְהֲרִי **עֹד**:


**After when** will you ever become purified?



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:1:1)


**דִּבְרֵי הַבַּצָּרוֹת.** שֶׁיָּבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם רָעָב. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן מִבְצָר, כְּמוֹ ״וּמִבְצַר מִשְׂגַּב חוֹמוֹתֶיךָ״ (ישעיהו כה:יב):


**concerning the droughts** that He would bring famine upon them, and Menahem (Machbereth p. 47) classified it as an expression of fortification (מִבְצָר) like (Isaiah 25:12) “And the fortress of (וּמִבְצַר) the strength of your walls.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:2:1)


**וּשְׁעָרֶיהָ.** עָרֶיהָ:


**and her cities** Heb. וּשְעָרֶיהָ, lit., her gates.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:2:2)


**וְצִוְחַת יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** צַעֲקַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, כְּמוֹ ״מֵרֹאשׁ הָרִים יִצְוָחוּ״ (ישעיה מב:יא):


**and the cry of Jerusalem** Heb. צִוְחַת, the cry of Jerusalem. Comp. (Isaiah 42:11) “from the mountain peaks they shall shout (יִִִִִצְוָחוּ).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:1)


**עַל גֵּבִים.** בּוֹרוֹת שֶׁהַמַּיִם מִתְכַּנְּסִים בָּהֶם:


**upon pits** Pits in which the water is usually gathered.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:2)


**שָׁבוּ כְלֵיהֶם רֵיקָם.** שֶׁלֹּא יָרַד מָטָר:


**their vessels have returned empty** for no rain has fallen.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:3:3)


**וְחָפוּ.** לְשׁוֹן כִּסּוּי:


**and have covered** an expression of covering.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:4:1)


**אִכָּרִים.** בּוֹבִיֵּיר״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**farmers** bubirs [?] in O.F.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:5:1)


**יָלְדָה וְעָזוֹב.** אֶת וְלָדָהּ, לְבַקֵּשׁ לָהּ מִרְעֶה:


**has borne and abandoned** her offspring, to hunt for pastureland for herself.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:6:1)


**עַל שְׁפָיִים.** יִבְלֵי מַיִם, רִיבֵיירְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**by the rivulets** rivulets of water (riveyres in O. F.).



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:7:1)


**עָנוּ בָנוּ.** הֵעִידוּ בָּנוּ:


**have testified against us** Heb. עָנוּ.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:7:2)


**עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ.** עֲשֵׂה מַה שֶּׁתַּעֲשֶׂה עִמָּנוּ לְמַעַן שֵׁם גָּדוֹל שֶׁיֵּצֵא לְךָ, שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹשֵׁל בַּכֹּל, וַאֲנַחְנוּ עַמְּךָ וְצֹאן מַרְעִיתֶךָ (תהלים עט:יג), וְלֹא נָאֶה שֶׁתִּתֵּן שֵׁם נִצְחוֹנְךָ לָאֱלִילִים. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה יֵשׁ (ירושלמי תענית ב:ו סה,ד): **לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ** וְכוּ׳:


**do for Your name’s sake** Do what You will do with us for the sake of the great name that has spread about You that You rule over all and that we are Your people and the flock of Your pasture, and it is not fitting that You give Your victorious name to the idols. And the Midrash Aggadah explains: for the sake of Your name that is inherent in our name.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:8:1)


**מִקְוֵה.** תּוֹחֶלֶת:


**hope** מִקְוֵה



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:1)


**נִדְהָם.** רַקְרוּ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, מוֹשֵׁךְ יָדוֹ מִן הַמִּלְחָמָה; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: רַקְרִיאַנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**overcome** (d’cru in O.F.) diminished, waned, who withdraws his hand from the battle, and some say (recroyant in O.F.), tiring out, resigned.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:2)


**וְשִׁמְךָ עָלֵינוּ נִקְרָא.** לְפִיכָךְ אֵין כְּבוֹדְךָ בְּכָךְ:


**and Your name is called upon us** Therefore, by doing this, it is no longer honor for you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:9:3)


**תַּנִּחֵנוּ.** פֵּירוּשׁוֹ כְּמוֹ ״תַּעַזְבֵנוּ״ (איכה ה:כ):


**forsake us** Its explanation is like תַעַזְבֵנוּ, forsake us.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:10:1)


**כֵּן אָהֲבוּ לָנוּעַ.** כֵּן, כְּמָה שֶׁגָּזַרְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם גּוֹלָה בְּמִצְרַיִם וּבָבֶל, אָהֲבוּ שָׁם לִהְיוֹת גּוֹלִים, לַעֲבֹד אֱלִילֵיהֶם הָרְחוֹקוֹת:


**So have they loved to wander** So, just as I have decreed upon them exile (to Egypt and Babylon - Warsaw edition), they loved it there to go into exile to worship distant idols.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:14:1)


**וֶאֱלִיל.** לְשׁוֹן אַל, דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ:


**a thing of naught** an expression of אַל, not, a thing which is not.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:17:1)


**נַחְלָה מְאֹד.** לְשׁוֹן ״חֳלִי״ (ירמיה ו:ז):


**very grievous** an expression of illness (חֹלִי).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:18:1)


**סָחֲרוּ.** סָבְבוּ לְבַקֵּשׁ הַצָּלָה:


**have gone around** They have gone around to seek rescue.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:18:2)


**אֶל אֶרֶץ וְלֹא יָדָעוּ.** אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת, וְלֹא הָיוּ יוֹדְעִין בָּהּ עַד הֵנָּה:


**to a land [which] they had not known** To another land about which they did not know until then.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:19:1)


**אִם בְּצִיּוֹן גָּעֲלָה נַפְשֶׁךָ.** עַל מְנָת שֶׁלֹּא לָשׁוּב עָלֶיהָ? כִּי אִם דַּעְתְּךָ לָשׁוּב, **מַדּוּעַ** הִכִּיתָ בְּ**אֵין מַרְפֵּא**? (פסיקתא רבתי לא)


**or has Your soul despised Zion** with the intention of never returning, for, if You have in mind to return, why have You smitten us without a cure?



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:21:1)


**לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ.** שֶׁנִּקְרֵאתָ ״רַחוּם״ (שמות לד:ו):


**for Your name’s sake** that you are called merciful.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:21:2)


**כִּסֵּא כְבוֹדֶךָ.** בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (פסחים ה.), וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, במדבר כב): יִשְׂרָאֵל הַחֲקוּקִים בְּכִסֵּא כְבוֹדֶךָ:


**the throne of Your glory** The Temple. And according to Midrash Aggadah, Israel who is engraved on the throne of Your glory.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 14:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/14:22:1)


**וְאִם הַשָּׁמַיִם יִתְּנוּ רְבִיבִים.** מֵאֲלֵיהֶם, אִם לֹא עַל פִּיךָ? בִּתְמִיהָּ. כָּל תְּמִיהָה הַכְּפוּלָה, הָרִאשׁוֹנָה בְּה״א וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה בְּאִם, כְּגוֹן: ״הַבְּמַחֲנִים אִם בְּמִבְצָרִים״ (במדבר יג:יט); ״הֲטוֹבָה הִיא אִם רָעָה״ (במדבר יג:יט); ״הֲמָאוֹס מָאַסְתָּ אִם בְּצִיּוֹן גָּעֲלָה נַפְשֶׁךָ״ (ירמיהו יד:יט). אַף כָּאן: **הֲיֵשׁ בְּהַבְלֵי הַגּוֹיִם... וְאִם הַשָּׁמַיִם**:


**Does the heaven give raindrops?** by itself, except by Your command? This is the interrogative form. In every double question, the first question is preceded by a ‘heh’ and the second one with אִם, like (Num. 13:19) “Are they in open cities (הַבְּמַחֲנִים) or in fortresses (אִם בְּמִבְצָרִים)?”(Ibid. 13:19) “It is good (הֲטוֹבָה) or bad (אִם רָעָה)?” (verse 19) “Have You indeed rejected (הֲמָאֹס) Judah, or has Your soul despised (אִם...גָּעֲלָה) Zion?” Here, too, “are there among the vanities of the nations those who give rain? Does the heaven...?”



## Chapter 15


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:1:1)


**מֹשֶׁה וּשְׁמוּאֵל.** שְׁנֵיהֶם נִזְקְקוּ לְבַקֵּשׁ רַחֲמִים עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּמִתְּחִלָּה הֶחֱזִירוּם לְמוּטָב, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִתְפַּלְלוּ עֲלֵיהֶם; וְלֹא עָלָה בְלִבָּם לְהָשִׁיב אַפִּי עַד שֶׁהֶחֱזִירוּם לְמוּטָב. מֹשֶׁה אָמַר: ״שִׂימוּ אִישׁ חַרְבּוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות לב:כז), ״וַיִּזֶר עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות לב:כ), וְאַחַר כָּךְ ״אֶעֱלֶה אֶל ה׳ אוּלַי אֲכַפְּרָה״ (שמות לב:ל); שְׁמוּאֵל בַּמִּצְפָּה: ״וַיָּסִירוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַבְּעָלִים וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל א ז:ד), וְאַחַר כָּךְ ״קִבְצוּ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמִּצְפָּתָה וְאֶתְפַּלֵּל עֲלֵיכֶם״ (שמואל א ז:ה). אַתָּה אֵינְךָ יָכוֹל לַהֲשִׁיבָם אֵלַי, לְכָךְ אַל תִּתְפַּלֵּל עֲלֵיהֶם (ירמיה ז:טז):


**Moses and Samuel** Both were needed to beg mercy for Israel, but first they induced them to repent, and afterwards they prayed on their behalf, but they had no idea of turning away My wrath until they had induced them to repent. Moses said (Exodus 32:27), “Let each man put his sword...” (v. 20) “and he scattered it on the surface of the water, etc.” And afterwards (v. 30), “I will ascend to the Lord, perhaps I will atone.” Samuel in Mizpah (I Samuel 7:4,5) “And the children of Israel removed the Baalim,” and afterwards, “‘Gather all Israel to Mizpah, and I shall pray to the Lord on your behalf (sic).’” But you cannot bring them back to Me, therefore do not pray for them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:1:2)


**נַפְשִׁי.** רְצוֹנִי:


**desire** Heb. נַפְֹשִי lit., My soul.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:2:1)


**אֲשֶׁר לַמָּוֶת וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל הַמְאֻחָר בַּפָּסוּק קָשֶׁה מֵחֲבֵירוֹ. **חֶרֶב** קָשֶׁה מִמָּוֶת, מִיתַת חֶרֶב מְנֻוֶּלֶת וּמִיתָה עַל מִטָּתוֹ אֵינָהּ מְנֻוֶּלֶת, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (תהלים קטז:טו) ״יָקָר בְּעֵינֵי ה׳ הַמָּוְתָה לַחֲסִידָיו״. **רָעָב** קָשֶׁה מֵחֶרֶב, שֶׁזֶּה מִצְטַעֵר וְזֶה אֵינוֹ מִצְטַעֵר, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״טוֹבִים הָיוּ חַלְלֵי חֶרֶב מֵחַלְלֵי רָעָב״ (איכה ד:ט). **שְׁבִי** כֻּלְּהוּ אִיתַנְהוּ בְּגַוֵּהּ (בבא בתרא ח:):


**Such as are for death, to death** Each calamity mentioned later in this verse is harsher than the one preceding it. The sword is harsher than death. Death by the sword is defacing, whereas death in bed is not defacing. And so he says (Psalms 116:15): “Precious in the eyes of the Lord is the death of His Saints.” Famine is harsher than the sword, for this one is agonizing whereas the other is not agonizing. And so Scripture states (Lam. 4:9): “Better are those who die from the sword than those who die from famine.” Captivity - all sorts of death are in it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:3:1)


**וּפָקַדְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן מִנּוּי וּפְקִידוּת:


**And I will appoint** an expression of appointment and designation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:3:2)


**לִסְחֹב.** לִגְרֹר, וְכֵן ״אִם לֹא יִסְחָבוּם״ (ירמיה מט:כ):


**to drag** to drag. Cf. (infra. 49: 20) “If the young of the sheep do not drag them (יִסְחָבוּם).”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:4:1)


**לְזַעֲוָה.** מִגִּזְרַת ״לֹא קָם וְלֹא זָע״ (אסתר ה:ט); וּכְמוֹהוּ גַּאֲוָה מִגִּזְרַת ״גֵּא מְאֹד״ (ישעיה טז:ו):


**a horror** from the same root as (Esther 5:9), “And he neither rose nor quaked (זָע).” Similarly, גַּאֲוָה pride, from the root (Isa. 16:6) “Have become very proud (גֵּא).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:4:2)


**לְכָל מַמְלְכוֹת הָאָרֶץ.** כָּל הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ רָעָה שֶׁבָּאָה עֲלֵיהֶם, יָזוּעַ:


**to all the kingdoms of the earth** Anyone who hears the evil that has befallen you will shudder.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:5:1)


**יָנוּד.** יְקוֹנֵן:


**will lament** Heb. יָנוּד.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:5:2)


**וּמִי יָסוּר.** אֵלֶיךָ, לִנְטוֹת מִדַּרְכּוֹ אֵלַיִךְ, **לִשְׁאֹל לָךְ לְשָׁלוֹם**:


**and who will turn aside** to you, to turn aside from his road to you, to ask of your welfare?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:6:1)


**נִלְאֵיתִי הִנָּחֵם.** כַּמָּה פְּעָמִים חָשַׁבְתִּי לְהַשְׁחִית וְנִחַמְתִּי עַל הָרָעָה; עַתָּה נִלְאֵיתִי הִנָּחֵם. **הִנָּחֵם** – רְפוֹרְפִינְשְׁ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**I am weary of repenting** Many times I planned to destroy but repented of the evil. Now I am weary of repenting. הִנָחֵם (Dporpnser in O.F.)



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:7:1)


**בַּמִּזְרֶה.** נָפָה שֶׁקּוֹרִין וו״ן בְּלַעַ״ז, כָּזֶה שֶׁהוּא זוֹרֶה שְׂעוֹרִין; שֶׁלֹּא יְהוּ כֻּלָּן גּוֹלִין לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


**with a sieve** A sieve called (van in French), like one who winnows barley, so that they should not all be exiled to one place.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:1)


**עָצְמוּ לִי.** רַבּוּ לְפָנַי **אַלְמְנוֹתָו**:


**are to Me more numerous** His widows are more to Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:2)


**עַל אֵם.** עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁהָיְתָה עִיר וָאֵם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל (שמואל ב כ:יט):


**upon a mother** Upon Jerusalem, which is a city and a mother in Israel.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:3)


**וּבַצָּהֳרָיִם.** הַכֹּל יְהֵא גָּלוּי לְפָנָיו, כָּל גִּנְזֵי מַטְמוֹנָם:


**at midday** Everything will be exposed before him, all their hidden treasures.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:8:4)


**הִפַּלְתִּי עָלֶיהָ פִּתְאֹם עִיר.** הִשְׁכַּנְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם פִּתְאֹם גְּיָסוֹת שֶׁל עִיר **וּבֶהָלוֹת** רַבּוֹת. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים **עִיר** – לְשׁוֹן שׂוֹנֵא, כְּמוֹ ״וַיְהִי עָרֶיךָ״ (שמואל א כח:טז); ״וּפִשְׁרֵיהּ לְעָרָךְ״ (דניאל ד:טז):


**I have cast upon her suddenly a city** I caused the troops of a city and many terrors to rest upon her. Some interpret עִיר as ‘an enemy.’ Cf. (I Sam. 28:16), “And has become [the supporter of] your adversary (עָרֶךָ)” (see also Dan. 4:16).



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:1)


**אֻמְלְלָה יוֹלֶדֶת הַשִּׁבְעָה.** שׁוֹמְרוֹן וּכְנֵסִיָּה שֶׁל עֲשֶׂרֶת שְׁבָטִים, שֶׁעָמְדוּ מֵהֶם שֶׁבַע מִשְׁפָּחוֹת (גיטין פח.), כְּבָר אֻמְלְלָה וְגָלְתָה:


**She who bore seven** Samaria and the nation of the Ten Tribes, from whom rose seven dynasties of wicked kings, has already been cut off and exiled.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:2)


**וּשְׁאֵרִיתָם.** אֵלּוּ יְהוּדָה וּקְצָת בִּנְיָמִין שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּיְּרוּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, **לַחֶרֶב אֶתֵּן**. אֵלּוּ הֵן שֶׁבַע מִשְׁפָּחוֹת: יָרָבְעָם בֶּן נְבָט וּבֵיתוֹ, בַּעְשָׁא וּבֵיתוֹ, עָמְרִי וּבֵיתוֹ, יֵהוּא וּבֵיתוֹ, מְנַחֵם וּבֵיתוֹ, פֶּקַח וּבֵיתוֹ, הוֹשֵׁעַ וּבֵיתוֹ:


**and their remnant** These are Judah and part of Benjamin, who remained in Israel. I shall deliver them to the sword. These are the seven dynasties: Jeroboam son of Nebat and his dynasty, Baasha and his dynasty, Omri and his dynasty, Jehu and his dynasty, Menahem and his dynasty, Pekah and his dynasty, Hoshea and his dynasty.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:3)


**נָפְחָה נַפְשָׁהּ.** לְשׁוֹן דָּאֲבָה נַפְשָׁהּ, מְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״וְדַאֲבוֹן נֶפֶשׁ״ (דברים כח:סה) ״וּמַפְּחָן נְפָשׁ״:


**her soul grieves** an expression of ‘her soul grieves.’ We translate (Deut. 28:65) “And grieving of the soul (וְדַאֲבוֹן נֶפֶֹש)” as וּמַפְּחַן נְפָֹש.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:4)


**בְּעוֹד יוֹמָם.** בְּלֹא זְמַנָּם מִהֲרוּ לִיפּוֹל. כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת גִּיטִּין (גיטין פח.). וּבְמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בובר ויצא יד) דָּרְשׁוּ כְּלַפֵּי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, וּמוֹנֶה שִׁבְעָה רְשָׁעִים: יְהוֹרָם, יְהוֹאָשׁ, אָחָז, מְנַשֶּׁה, אָמוֹן, יְהוֹיָקִים, צִדְקִיָּהוּ. אַךְ יֵשׁ לִמְנוֹת עוֹד: רְחַבְעָם, אֲבִיָּה, אֲמַצְיָה, אֲחַזְיָה:
