**when it is still day** Before its time, they hastened to fall. In this manner it is expounded in Tractate Gittin. [See Gittin 88a]. And in the Midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (Tanhuma Buber, Vayetze p. 153) it is expounded as referring to Jerusalem, and he counts seven wicked men: Jehoram, Jehoash, Ahaz, Manasseh, Amon, Jehoiakim, and Zedekiah. But there are yet to count Rehoboam, Abijah, Amaziah, and Ahaziah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:9:5)


**וְחָפֵרָה.** לְשׁוֹן בּוֹשֶׁת:


**and confounded** an expression of shame.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:1)


**אוֹי לִי אִמִּי.** יִרְמְיָה הָיָה מִתְאוֹנֵן עַל עַצְמוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ שׂוֹנְאִין אוֹתוֹ בְּנֵי עִירוֹ עַל דִּבְרֵי תוֹכְחוֹתָיו:


**Woe is to me, my mother** Jeremiah was complaining about himself, that his townsmen hated him because of his castigations.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:2)


**וְאִישׁ מָדוֹן.** אִישׁ מְרִיבָה, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא יָדוֹן רוּחִי״ (בראשית ו:ג):


**and a man of contention** a man of quarrel, Cf. (Gen. 6:3) “Let My spirit not contend (יָדוֹן).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:3)


**לֹא נָשִׁיתִי.** כְּמוֹ ״לֹא תִהְיֶה לוֹ כְּנֹשֶׁה״ (שמות כב:כד):


**I have not lent** Cf. (Exodus 22:24) “You shall not be to him as a creditor (כְּנֹֹֹשֶה) .”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:10:4)


**כֻּלֹּה מְקַלְלוֹנִי.** אֵינִי רָב עִמָּהֶם עַל עִסְקֵי מָמוֹן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִי עֲלֵיהֶם וְלֹא לָהֶם עָלַי, וַהֲרֵי כָּל הָעָם מְקַלְלַנִי:


**they curse me** I do not quarrel with them concerning matters of money that I claim from them, neither have they made any claim on me, yet all the people curse me.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:1)


**שֵׁרִיתִיךָ.** הִשְׁאַרְתִּיךָ, לְשׁוֹן ״שְׁאֵרִית״ (בראשית מה:ז). וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹ מְנַחֵם. אֲבָל דּוֹנָשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ לְשׁוֹן הַתָּרָה, וְשֵׁרִיתִיךָ כְּמוֹ ״מְשָׁרֵי קִטְרִין״ (דניאל ה:יב). וּפִתְרוֹן **שֵׁרִיתִיךָ** כֵּן הוּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הִנֵּה פְתַחְתִּיךָ הַיּוֹם מִן הָאֲזִקִּים״ (ירמיה מ:ד); כְּלוֹמַר: בַּיּוֹם אֲשֶׁר הֵקִים ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ נֶאֱמַר לוֹ: ״וְעַתָּה הִנֵּה פְתַחְתִּיךָ הַיּוֹם מִן הָאֲזִקִּים אֲשֶׁר עַל יָדֶיךָ, אִם טוֹב בְּעֵינֶיךָ לָבוֹא אִתִּי בָבֶלָה״ (ירמיה מ:ד). וְגַם יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּקְּשׁוּ מִמֶּנּוּ שֶׁיִּתְפַּלֵּל בִּשְׁבִילָם, כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר לוֹ: ״הִתְפַּלֵּל בַּעֲדֵנוּ אֶל ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ... כִּי נִשְׁאַרְנוּ מְעַט מֵהַרְבֵּה״ (ירמיה מב:ב):


**1 leave you over** I leave you over, an expression of a remnant. In this manner, Menahem (Machbereth p. 181) interpreted it, but Dunash (Teshuvoth Dunash p. 36) interpreted it as an expression of release. And שֵרִיתִיךָ is like (Dan. 5:12) “And untying (וּמְֹשָרֵא) knots,” and the interpretation of שֵרִיתִיךָ is so, as it is stated (below 40:4): “Behold I have released you from the chains that are on your hand.” That is to say, on that day that the Lord fulfilled His word, it was said to him, “And now, behold I have released you today from the chains that are on your hand, if it pleases you to come with me to Babylon.” And also, Israel begged him to pray for them, as it was said to him (below 42:4): “Pray on our behalf to the Lord our Cod, for we remain few from many.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:2)


**אִם לֹא.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעָה:


**Verily I will** lit., if not, an expression of an oath.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:11:3)


**הִפְגַּעְתִּי בְךָ בְּעֵת רָעָה.** אֶת הָאוֹיֵב, כְּשֶׁתָּבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הָרָעָה, יִפְגְּעוּ בְךָ שֶׁתְּבַקֵּשׁ רַחֲמִים עֲלֵיהֶם; כְּגוֹן צִדְקִיָּה, אָמַר לוֹ: ״דְּרָשׁ נָא בַּעֲדֵנוּ אֶת ה׳ וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה כא:ב). דָּבָר אַחֵר: נְבוּזַרְאֲדָן יְבַקֵּשׁ מֵאִתְּךָ: ״אִם טוֹב בְּעֵינֶיךָ לָבֹא אִתִּי בָּבֶלָה״ (ירמיה מ:ד):


**I will cause [them] to beg you in time of evil** with the enemy. When evil befalls them, they will beg you to supplicate God for mercy upon them, for example, Zedekiah said to him, (below 21:2) “Beseech God now on our behalf, etc.” Another explanation. Nebuzaradan will beg you (below 40:4), “If it pleases you to come with me to Babylon.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:12:1)


**הֲיָרֹעַ בַּרְזֶל בַּרְזֶל מִצָּפוֹן.** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי: בַּרְזֶל הַבָּא מִצָּפוֹן קָשֶׁה מִשְּׁאָר בַּרְזֶל. וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: הֵם נִמְשְׁלוּ לְבַרְזֶל: ״הוֹלְכֵי רָכִיל נְחֹשֶׁת וּבַרְזֶל״ (ירמיה ו:כח), וּבְיִרְמְיָה נֶאֱמַר: ״נְתַתִּיךָ לְעַמּוּד בַּרְזֶל וּלְחוֹמַת נְחֹשֶׁת״ (ירמיה א:יח) – וְשֶׁלּוֹ חָזָק מִשֶּׁלָּהֶם, כִּי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר מִצָּפוֹן יָבֹא כִּדְבָרָיו עֲלֵיהֶם:


**Will iron break iron from the north** I say that iron that comes from the north is harder than other iron, and this is its interpretation: They were compared to iron (above 6:28), “Going tale bearing like copper and iron.” And concerning Jeremiah it is stated (above 1:18): “I have made you... into an iron pillar and into copper walls,” and his is stronger than theirs, for Nebuchadnezzar will come upon them according to his words.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:12:2)


**הֲיָרֹעַ.** הֲיְרוֹצֵץ בַּרְזֶל שֶׁלָּהֶם אֶת שֶׁלְּךָ? דָּבָר אַחֵר: הֲיְרוֹצֵץ פַּרְעֹה הַבָּא בְּעֶזְרַתְכֶם, שֶׁהוּא קָשֶׁה כְּבַרְזֶל, אֶת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, שֶׁהוּא **בַּרְזֶל הַבָּא מִצָּפוֹן וּנְחֹשֶׁת**? וְתַרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן נוֹטֶה לְצַד זֶה:


**Will…break** Will their iron break yours? Another explanation: Will Pharaoh who comes in your aid, who is as hard as iron, break Nebuchadnezzar, who is iron that comes from the north and copper? Targum Jonathan is inclined toward this direction.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:1)


**חֵילְךָ.** לְשׁוֹן מָמוֹן (ראה דברים ח:יז):


**Your wealth** An expression of money.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:2)


**וְאוֹצְרוֹתֶיךָ.** לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא אוֹמֵר:


**and your treasures** He is addressing Israel.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:3)


**וּבְכָל חַטֹּאותֶיךָ.** וּלְמַעַן כָּל חַטֹּאותֶיךָ תְּהִי זֹאת לָךְ:


**and for all your sins** And because of all your sins, this will befall you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:13:4)


**וּבְכָל גְּבוּלֶיךָ.** אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱמַדְתָּ אֱלִילִים בְּכֻלָּן:


**and throughout all your boundaries** For you erected idols in all of them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:1)


**אַל לְאֶרֶךְ אַפְּךָ תִּקָּחֵנִי.** תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לָא תִּתֵּן אַרְכָּא לְעוֹלְבָנִי״ – אַל תִּקַּח אֶת רִיבִי לְהַנִּיחוֹ לְאֹרֶךְ אַפְּךָ, אֶלָּא מַהֵר וְהִנָּקֶם לִי:


**Take me not to Your long suffering** Jonathan renders. Do not grant a reprieve for my humiliation. Do not take my cause to leave it for Your long suffering, but hasten and avenge me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:2)


**שְׂאֵתִי.** כְּמוֹ סָבַלְתִּי:


**I bore** like סָבַלְתִּי bore.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:15:3)


**עָלֶיךָ.** בִּשְׁבִילְךָ:


**for Your sake** for You.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:16:1)


**נִמְצְאוּ דְבָרֶיךָ וָאֹכְלֵם.** לְתַאֲוָה מִתְּחִלָּה, וְהָיוּ **לְשָׂשׂוֹן**, כִּי דִּמִּיתִי שֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ אֵלַי עַתָּה:


**Your words were found and I ate them** eagerly at the beginning, and they were a joy for me, for I thought that now they would listen to me.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:17:1)


**מִפְּנֵי יָדְךָ.** מִפְּנֵי נְבוּאָה הַבָּאָה לִי מֵאִתְּךָ:


**because of Your hand** Because of the prophecy that came to me from You.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:17:2)


**יָשַׁבְתִּי.** יְחִידִי וּמִשְׁתּוֹמֵם בַּאֲבֵילוּת, **כִּי** נְבוּאַת **זַעַם מִלֵּאתָנִי**, בְּחֻרְבַּן בֵּיתֶךָ:


**I sat** alone and bewildered by mourning, for You filled me with a prophecy of fury concerning the destruction of Your House.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:1)


**אֲנוּשָׁה.** חוֹלָה; כְּמוֹ ״וַיֵּאָנַשׁ״ (שמואל ב יב:טו) – וַיֶּחֱלֶה; בִּבְנָהּ שֶׁל בַּת שֶׁבַע:


**grievous** sick. Comp. (II Sam. 12:15) “And he became mortally ill (וַיֵּאָנַֹש).” mentioned concerning Bath-sheba’s son.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:2)


**הָיוֹ תִהְיֶה לִי.** אַתָּה הוֹוֶה לִי **כְּמוֹ אַכְזָב.** כְּאָדָם שֶׁמִּבְטָחוֹ נִפְסָק מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁאַתָּה מַנִּיחֵנִי לְהִצְטַעֵר בְּיָדָם. **אַכְזָב** מָקוֹר פּוֹסֵק, פייליינ״ץ בְּלַעַז:


**You are to me** Heb. הָיוֹ תִהְיֶה. **as a failing spring** Like a man whose trust has been cut off from him, for you allow me to suffer at their hands. אַכְזָב is a spring that stops (faylayne in O. F:), failing.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:18:3)


**מַיִם לֹא נֶאֱמָנוּ.** לִסְמֹךְ עֲלֵיהֶם:


**water that is not faithful** to rely upon it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:1)


**לָכֵן.** בַּעֲבוּר שֶׁאֲנִי סוֹבֵל הַצָּרוֹת הָאֵלֶּה:


**Therefore** Because I suffer from these troubles.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:2)


**כֹּה אָמַר ה׳.** לִי:


**so said the Lord** to me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:3)


**אִם תָּשׁוּב.** אַתָּה יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל יְדֵי **וַאֲשִׁיבְךָ** אֲנִי אֵלָיו, אָמַר לִי: **לְפָנַי תַּעֲמֹד**:


**If you return** You, Israel, through me, and I will return you to Him, He said to Me, “You will stand before Me.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:4)


**וְאִם תּוֹצִיא יָקָר מִזּוֹלֵל.** אִם תּוֹצִיא אָדָם הָגוּן מֵאָדָם רָשָׁע, שֶׁתַּחֲזִירֶנּוּ לְמוּטָב:


**and if you take precious out of the vile** If you take a pious man from a wicked man, i.e., if you bring him to repentance.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:5)


**כְּפִי תִהְיֶה.** שֶׁאֲנִי גּוֹזֵר גְּזֵירָה וְאַתָּה מְבַטְּלָהּ (בבא מציעא פה.):


**You shall be as my mouth** For I make a decree and you annul it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 15:19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/15:19:6)


**יָשׁוּבוּ הֵמָּה אֵלֶיךָ וְאַתָּה לֹא תָשׁוּב אֲלֵיהֶם.** ״יְתוּבוּן אִינּוּן לְפִתְגָּמָךְ וְאַתְּ לָא תְּתוּב לְמִטְעֵי בַּתְרֵיהוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Let them return to you, but you shall not return to them** Let them return to your word, but you shall not return to stray after them.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:1)


**בֵּית מַרְזֵחַ.** מִשְׁתֶּה; בְּסִפְרֵי בְּפָרָשַׁת בֵּית פְּעוֹר(ספרי פיסקא קלא) רָאִיתִי: חָזְרוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהֶם מַרְזֵיחִין; אֲבָל רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (כתובות סט:) **מַרְזֵחַ** – אֵבֶל:


**house of mourning** feasting. In Sifrei, in the section dealing with Beth-peor (Balak), I saw. They returned to make feasts (מַרְזֵיחִין) for them, but our Rabbis explained מַרְזֵחַ as ‘a mourner.’


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:2)


**תָּנוּד.** תְּקוֹנֵן:


**you bemoan** you shall lament.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:3)


**כִּי אָסַפְתִּי אֶת שְׁלוֹמִי וגו׳.** כְּשֶׁהָיָה אַבְרָהָם אֲבִיהֶם עוֹשֶׂה צְדָקָה וּמִשְׁפָּט (בראשית יח:יט), נָתַתִּי לְזַרְעוֹ חֶסֶד וְרַחֲמִים: ״וְשָׁמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְךָ וגו׳״ (דברים ז:יב), ״וְנָתַן לְךָ רַחֲמִים״ (דברים יג:יח). הֵם הָפְכוּ לַלַּעֲנָה מִשְׁפָּט וּצְדָקָה לָאָרֶץ הִנִּיחוּ (עמוס ה:ז), אַף אֲנִי חָזַרְתִּי וְאָסַפְתִּי אֵלַי אֶת חַסְדִּי וְאֶת רַחֲמִי:


**for I have gathered in My peace, etc.** When their father Abraham performed charity and justice, I gave his children loving-kindness and mercy, as in Deut. 7:12: “And the Lord your God shall keep for you the covenant and the loving-kindness, etc.” Also (Deut. 13:18), “And He shall give you mercy.” They turned “justice into wormwood, and cast righteousness to the ground” (Amos 5:7). I, too, have returned and gathered in to Me My loving-kindness and My mercies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:5:4)


**אָסַפְתִּי.** אטואי״ש בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״אָסַף אֱלֹהִים אֶת חֶרְפָּתִי״ (בראשית ל:כג):


**I have gathered in** (ote in O.F.), taken off. Comp. (Gen. 30:23) “God has gathered (אסף) in my disgrace.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:6:1)


**וּמֵתוּ גְדוֹלִים וּקְטַנִּים.** וְאַחַר שֶׁהַכֹּל מֵתִים, מַה לְּךָ לִסְפּוֹד?


**And large and small shall die** And since everyone will die, why should you lament?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:6:2)


**וְלֹא יִתְגּוֹדֵד וְלֹא יִקָּרֵחַ לָהֶם.** דֶּרֶךְ הָאֲבֵלִים לְהִתְגּוֹדֵד וְלִשְׂרֹט בְּשָׂרָם וְלִקְרוֹחַ קָרְחָה בְּרֹאשָׁם; וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם, לְיִשְׂרָאֵל אָסוּר (ויקרא יט:כח; דברים יד:א), וְלֹא הִזְכִּירוֹ כָּאן אֶלָּא לְדוּגְמַת אֵבֶל:


**and they shall not cut their flesh nor shall they tear their hair for them** It is the custom of mourners to cut and tear their flesh and to tear their hair our of their head. In any case, it is forbidden for Jews, but he mentions it here only as an example of mourning.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:7:1)


**וְלֹא יִפְרְסוּ.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבִירָה, כְּמוֹ ״מְנֵא מְנֵא תְּקֵל וּפַרְסִין״ (דניאל ה:כה); שֶׁמַּבְרִין אֶת הָאֲבֵלִים בָּרְחָבָה וּפוֹרְסִין לָהֶם בִּרְכַּת הַמּוֹצִיא. ״כִּי הוּא יְבָרֵךְ הַזֶּבַח״ (שמואל א ט:יג) – תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֲרֵי הוּא פָּרִיס עַל מְזוֹנָא״:


**And they shall not break [bread] for them** an expression of breaking. Comp. (Dan. 5:25) “Mene Mene Tekel Upharsin, (divided).” This alludes to the practice of giving a meal to the mourners in the city square and they break for them the bread upon reciting the blessing of ‘hamotzi.’ (I Sam. 9: 13) “For he will bless the sacrifices,” Jonathan renders: He will break (פָרִיס) bread.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:7:2)


**וְלֹא יַשְׁקוּ אוֹתָם וְגוֹ׳.** הֲרֵי טַעַם לָמָּה שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי: **אַל תֵּלֵךְ לִסְפּוֹד**:


**neither will they give them to drink etc** This is the reason for what I said, “Neither go to lament.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:8:1)


**וּבֵית מִשְׁתֶּה.** אֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתִּי לְךָ לְמַעְלָה **לֹא תָבוֹא**, זֶה הַטַּעַם: **הִנְנִי מַשְׁבִּית מִן הַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה וְגוֹ׳**:


**And to a house of feasting** that I said to you above, “You shall not go,” this is the reason: Behold, I will cut off from this place, etc.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:13:1)


**וְעָבַדְתֶּם שָׁם אֶת אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְתִפְלְחוּן תַּמָּן לְעַמְמַיָּא פָּלְחֵי טָעֲוָותָא״:


**and you will serve there other gods** Jonathan paraphrases: And you will serve there nations that worship idols day and night.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:14:1)


**לָכֵן.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּגַדְתֶּם בִּי, שְׁבוּעָה הִיא בְּיָדִי שֶׁאֶגְאוֹל אֶתְכֶם:


**Therefore** An expression of an oath. Although you betrayed Me, I have an oath that I will redeem you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:14:2)


**וְלֹא יֵאָמֵר עוֹד וגו׳.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (ברכות יב:) לֹא שֶׁתֵּעָקֵר יְצִיאַת מִצְרַיִם מִמְּקוֹמָהּ, אֶלָּא גְּאֻלָּה אַחֲרוֹנָה עִקָּר וִיצִיאַת מִצְרַיִם טָפֵל לָהּ:


**and it shall no longer be said** Our Rabbis expounded: Not that the departure from Egypt shall be uprooted from its place, but the final redemption will be the most important and the departure from Egypt secondary to it.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:16:1)


**וְדִיגוּם.** מָה הַדַּיָּג שׁוֹלְהוּ מִתּוֹךְ מְקוֹם גִּדּוּלוֹ, אַף אֵלּוּ יִלְכְּדוּ אוֹתָם בְּתוֹךְ הָעִיר; וּמָה עִסְקוֹ שֶׁל דָּג, מִשֶּׁנִּלְכַּד הוּא מֵת, כָּךְ אָבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם הוֹרְגִים; **וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן אֶשְׁלַח צַיָּדִים** לַנִּשְׁאָרִים וְלַבּוֹרְחִים מִן הַחֶרֶב עַל הַהָרִים וְהַגְּבָעוֹת, **וְצָדוּם** – לְהַגְלוֹתָם בַּשֶּׁבִי:


**and they will fish them** Just as the fisher takes - it i.e., - the fish from its habitat, so will these capture them within the city, and just as the manner of a fish, that when he is caught he dies, so will I bring killers upon them, and afterwards I will send hunters for the survivors and for those who flee from the sword to the mountains and the hills, and they will hunt them to exile them in captivity.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:18:1)


**מִשְׁנֵה עֲוֹנָם.** אֶת שֶׁשָּׁנוּ עַל עֲוֹנָם לַעֲשׂוֹת עֲוֹנוֹת אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם כֵּן: ״וַאֲשִׁלֵּים לְתִנְיָינִין כְּקַדְמָאִין עַל חַד תְּרֵין״:


**the doubling of their iniquity** Because they repeated their iniquity to do the iniquities of their fathers. And Jonathan renders in this manner: And I will recompense the second ones like the first ones double.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:1)


**ה׳ עֻזִּי וּמָעֻזִּי וּמְנוּסִי.** אַנְטִיפוֹיְימַאנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן פֶּלֶא, כְּמוֹ ״נָתַתָּ לִּירֵאֶיךָ נֵּס לְהִתְנוֹסֵס״ (תהלים ס:ו), וְכֵן ״ה׳ נִסִּי״ (שמות יז:טו). וְדוֹנָשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ לְשׁוֹן מָנוֹס מַמָּשׁ:


**and my refuge** (mon vantement in French), my boast. And Menahem (Machbereth p. 123) classified it as an expression of a wonder. Cf. (Psalms 60:6) “You have given those who fear You a wonder to be wondrous (נֵס לְהִתְנוֹסֵס).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:2)


**אֵלֶיךָ גּוֹיִם יָבֹאוּ.** סוֹף הָאֻמּוֹת לָשׁוּב אֵלֶיךָ וּלְעָבְדְּךָ שְׁכֶם אֶחָד (צפניה ג:ט); וְעַמְּךָ, אֵיךְ עָשׂוּ לָהֶם **אֱלֹהִים וְהֵמָּה לֹא אֱלֹהִים**?!


**to You nations will come** Eventually, the heathens will come to you and worship You of one accord, and they will say, “How did they make gods for themselves, and they are no gods?”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:3)


**מֵאַפְסֵי.** כְּמוֹ ״מִקְּצֵה״ (ירמיה יב:יב):


**from the ends of** Like מִקְצֵה, from the end of.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:4)


**נָחֲלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן נַחֲלָה:


**handed down** an expression of an inheritance.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:19:5)


**מוֹעִיל.** לְשׁוֹן תּוֹעֶלֶת:


**avail** an expression of use (תּוֹעֶלֶת).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/16:21:1)


**כִּי שְׁמִי ה׳.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: מוֹשֵׁל וְשַׁלִּיט לְקַיֵּם גְּזֵרָתִי (ויקרא רבה כד, א):


**that My name is the Lord** As its apparent meaning, a ruler and a sovereign to fulfill My decree.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:1)


**בְּעֵט בַּרְזֶל.** מָשָׁל הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: חֲקוּקָה בְּשִׁקּוּעַ וְאֵין נוֹחָה לִמְחוֹק. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה **בְּעֵט בַּרְזֶל בְּצִפֹּרֶן שָׁמִיר** עַל יְדֵי יִרְמְיָה שֶׁנִּקְרָא עַמּוּד בַּרְזֶל, וְעַל יְדֵי יְחֶזְקֵאל שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לוֹ״ כַּשָּׁמִיר חָזָק מִצּוּר נָתַתִּי מִצְחֶךָ״ (יחזקאל ג:ט):


**with a pen of iron** This is an allegory. That is to say that it is deeply engraved, and cannot be erased. And the Midrash Aggadah explains: with a pen of iron, with a diamond point, preserved by Jeremiah, called ‘an iron wall,’ and by Ezekiel, to whom it was said (3:9): “As a diamond, harder than flint have I made your forehead.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:2)


**חֲרוּשָׁה.** חֲקוּקָה, כְּזֶה שֶׁהוּא חוֹרֵשׁ בָּעֹמֶק:


**engraved** Engraved like one who plows deeply.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:1:3)


**עַל לוּחַ לִבָּם.** שֶׁאֵינָם שׁוֹכְחִין עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**on the tablet of their heart** that they do not forget their idolatry.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:2:1)


**כִּזְכֹּר בְּנֵיהֶם מִזְבְּחוֹתָם.** כִּזְכִירַת בְּנֵיהֶם כֵּן הָיְתָה לָהֶם זְכִירַת מִזְבְּחוֹתָם; כְּאָדָם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ גִּעְגּוּעִין עַל בְּנוֹ (סנהדרין סג:):


**As they remember their children, [so do they remember] their altars** Like the remembrance of their children, so was the remembrance of their altars to them, like a man who longs for his son.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:2:2)


**וַאֲשֵׁרֵיהֶם.** אֲשֶׁר **עַל עֵץ רַעֲנָן**. **עַל עֵץ** – כְּמוֹ אֵצֶל, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״תְּחוֹת כָּל אִילָן עֲבוּף״:


**and their asherim** which are by the green trees. **by...trees** like אֵצֶּל, by. And Jonathan renders in this manner. under every green tree.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:1)


**הֲרָרִי בַּשָּׂדֶה.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת בָּהָר הַמִּישׁוֹר (ירמיה כא:יג), כָּל סְבִיב הָעִיר מִישׁוֹר, שָׂדֶה. וְלָשׁוֹן **הֲרָרִי** – כְּמוֹ ״עֲרָבִי״ (ישעיה יג:כ), עַל שֵׁם שֶׁיּוֹשֵׁב בָּעֲרָבָה; מוֹנְטִינְיֵיר״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, יוֹשְׁבֵי הָהָר (ספרים ארחים יוֹשֶׁבֶת הָהָר):


**You, who sit upon the mountain in the field** Jerusalem that is situated on the mountain of the plain. All around the city is a fielded plain. The structure of הֲרָרִי is like עֲרָבִי, called so because he dwells in the plain (עֲרָבָה) (montanyers in O.F), mountain dwellers (other versions: a mountain dweller).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:2)


**חֵילְךָ.** כְּמוֹ מָמוֹנְךָ (ראה דברים ח:יז):


**your substance** like מָמוֹנְךָ, your money.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:3:3)


**בָּמוֹתֶיךָ בְּחַטָּאת.** בָּמוֹתֶיךָ עֲשׂוּיוֹת בְּחֵטְא לְשֵׁם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה **בְּכָל גְּבוּלֶיךָ**:


**Your high places are made for sin** Your high places are made in sin for the purpose of idolatry within all your borders.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:1)


**וְשָׁמַטְתָּה.** עַל כָּרְחֲךָ תִּשְׁמַט אַרְצְךָ אֶת אֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׁבְתָה בְּשַׁבְּתוֹתֵיכֶם (ויקרא כו:לה), שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לָכֶם ״וְשָׁבְתָה הָאָרֶץ״ (ויקרא כה:ב):


**And you shall release** Perforce you will release your land for the time that it did not rest on your Sabbaths, which I said to you (Lev. 25:2), “And the land shall rest a Sabbath for the Lord.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:2)


**וּבְךָ.** אֶנָּקֵם מֵחֲמַת **נַחֲלָתְךָ אֲשֶׁר נָתַתִּי לָךְ** שֶׁלֹּא עָשִׂיתָ רְצוֹנִי לְהַשְׁמִיטָהּ, דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וּבְךָ מִנַּחֲלָתְךָ** – וּבְךָ אֶנָּקֵם מִנַּחֲלָתְךָ, שֶׁאַגְלֶה אוֹתְךָ מִמֶּנָּה:


**of you** I will avenge Myself of you because of your heritage that I gave you, that you did not perform My will to release it. Another explanation: even of you, from your heritage; I will avenge Myself of you from your heritage, that I will exile you from it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:4:3)


**כִּי אֵשׁ קְדַחְתֶּם בְּאַפִּי.** יְקַדְתֶּם בִּנְחִירַי:


**for you have kindled fire in My nostrils** Heb. קְדַכְתֶּם.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יִבְטַח בָּאָדָם.** בַּחֲרִישׁוֹ וּקְצִירוֹ, לוֹמַר: אֶזְרַע בַּשְּׁבִיעִית וְאֹכַל:


**who trusts in man** In his plowing and his harvest, saying, “I will sow during the seventh year and I will eat.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:2)


**זְרוֹעוֹ.** כְּמוֹ עֶזְרוֹ, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״רוּחְצָנֵיהּ״:


**his arm** Like ‘his help,’ and so did Jonathan render: his trust.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:5:3)


**וּמִן ה׳ יָסוּר לִבּוֹ.** שֶׁהִבְטִיחוֹ: ״וְצִוִּיתִי אֶת בִּרְכָתִי לָכֶם״ (ויקרא כה:כא). כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**and whose heart turns away from the Lord** Who promised him (Lev. 25:21), “And I will command My blessing to you.” This is what I heard.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:1)


**כְּעַרְעָר.** כְּעֵץ יְחִידִי; לְשׁוֹן ״עֲרִירִי״ (בראשית טו:ב). וְיֵשׁ אוֹמֵר: דְּגַאשְׁטִי״הּ בְּלַעַ״ז. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ: שֵׁם עֵץ מֵעֲצֵי הַיַּעַר וְ**עַרְעָר** שְׁמוֹ; וְכֵן פֵּירֵשׁ ״כְּעַרְעָר בַּמִּדְבָּר״ (ירמיה מח:ו): עֵץ אֶחָד מֵעֲצֵי הַיַּעַר:


**like a lonely tree** like a lonely tree, an expression of (Gen. 15:2) “Childless (עֲרִירִי),” and some say (degat in O.F) laid waste, and Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 137) explained: The name of a tree of the forest trees, and its name is ar’ar, and this is the explanation of (infra 48.6) “Like an ‘aroer’ in the desert,” one of the trees of the forest.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:2)


**בָּעֲרָבָה.** לְשׁוֹן מִישׁוֹר:


**in the plain** an expression of a plain.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:3)


**חֲרֵרִים.** יֹבֶשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״חָרָה מִנִּי חֹרֶב״ (איוב ל:ל):


**parched land** aridness. Comp. (Job 30:30) “Became dry (חָרָה) from heat.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:6:4)


**אֶרֶץ מְלֵחָה.** אֶרֶץ שֶׁהִיא מְלֵחָה וְלֹא תְהֵא מְיֻשֶּׁבֶת:


**salt-sodden soil** Land that is salty and will not be settled.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:1)


**וְלֹא יִרְאֶה.** הָעֵץ **כִּי יָבֹא חֹם**:


**and will not see** The tree will not see when heat comes.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:2)


**בַּצֹּרֶת.** לְשׁוֹן רָעָב פאמצ״א בְּלַעַ״ז,.לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **בַּצֹּרֶת** לְשׁוֹן מִבְצָר, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**drought** an expression of hunger, (famine in French). Another interpretation: בַּצֹרֶת is an expression of fortification (מִבְצָר), and in this manner, Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 47) classified it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:8:3)


**לֹא יִדְאָג.** לֹא יִפְחַד:


**will not be anxious** will not be afraid.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:1)


**עָקֹב הַלֵּב.** מָלֵא תּוֹאֲנָה וַעֲקֻבָּה **מִכֹּל** רָעָה:


**The heart is deceitful** Full of pretext and deceit of all evil.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:2)


**וְאָנוּשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן חֹלִי, וְחָלְיוֹ **הוּא** זֶה:


**and when it is sick** an expression of sickness, and this is its sickness.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:9:3)


**מִי יֵדָעֶנּוּ.** הוּא סָבוּר: מִי יֵדָעֶנּוּ. אֲנִי ה׳ חוֹקְרוֹ:


**who recognizes it?** He thinks, “Who recognizes it?” I, the Lord, search it.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:1)


**קוֹרֵא דָגַר.** קוּקוּ״וּ גְלוּנְצְנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The cuckoo calls** (Coucou glousse in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:2)


**וְלֹא יָלָד.** הַקּוֹרֵא הַזֶּה מוֹשֵׁךְ אַחֲרָיו אֶפְרוֹחִים שֶׁלֹּא יָלָד. **דָּגַר.** צִפְצוּף שֶׁמְּצַפְצֵף הָעוֹף בְּקוֹלוֹ לִמְשׁוֹךְ אֶפְרוֹחִין אַחֲרָיו, וְאֵלּוּ שֶׁהַקּוֹרֵא דָּגַר לֹא יֵלְכוּ אַחֲרָיו מִשֶּׁיִּגְדְּלוּ שֶׁאֵינָם מִינוֹ, כָּךְ **עֹשֶׂה עֹשֶׁר וְלֹא בְמִשְׁפָּט**:


**but has not laid** This cuckoo draws after it chicks that it did not lay. דָגָר - This is the chirping which the bird chirps with its voice to draw the chicks after it, but those whom the cuckoo called will not follow it when they grow up for they are not of its kind. So is one who gathers riches but not by right.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:11:3)


**יִהְיֶה נָבָל.** ״מִתְקְרֵי רְשִׁיעָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**he stands dishonored** He is called wicked.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:12:1)


**כִּסֵּא כָבוֹד וגו׳.** לְפִי שֶׁבָּא לוֹמַר **כָּל עֹזְבֶיךָ יֵבֹשׁוּ**, מַתְחִיל בְּקִלּוּסוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם וּמְדַבֵּר לְפָנָיו: אַתָּה, שֶׁ**כִּסֵּא כָבוֹד** וּ**מָרוֹם** מֵרֵאשִׁית בְּרִיאַת עוֹלָם וּמְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד **מִקְדָּשֵׁנוּ**:


**As a Throne of Glory** Since he wishes to state: All that forsake You shall be shamed, he commences with the praise of the Omnipresent, and says before Him, “You, Whose Throne of Glory is exalted from the beginning of the Creation of the world and is directed opposite our Sanctuary.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:13:1)


**מִקְוֵה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** ה׳ יוֹשֵׁב עָלָיו, לְפִיכָךְ **כָּל עֹזְבֶיךָ יֵבֹשׁוּ**:


**The Lord Who is the source of the hopes of Israel** sits upon it. Therefore, all that forsake You shall be shamed.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:13:2)


**וְסוּרַי בָּאָרֶץ יִכָּתֵבוּ.** אוֹתָם שֶׁסָּרִים מִדְּבָרַי, שֶׁאֵינָם שׁוֹמְעִים שְׁלִיחוּתִי, בְּקִבְרוֹת תַּחְתִּיּוֹת אֶרֶץ יִכָּתֵבוּ:


**and they that turn away from me shall be marked on the earth** Those who turn away from my words, who do not hearken to my message, will be marked out in the nether graves of the earth.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:14:1)


**כִּי תְהִלָּתִי אָתָּה.** בְּךָ אֲנִי מִתְהַלֵּל וּמִתְפָּאֵר לוֹמַר שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹשִׁיעִי:


**for You are my praise** I praise myself and boast with You, saying that You are my Savior.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:14:2)


**תְּהִלָּתִי.** וונטנצ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**my praise** (vantance in O.F.), boast.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:15:1)


**אַיֵּה דְּבַר ה׳.** הַפּוּרְעָנִיּוֹת שֶׁאַתָּה מִתְנַבֵּא:


**“Where is the word of the Lord?** The retribution which you prophesy.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:1)


**וַאֲנִי לֹא אַצְתִּי.** לֹא מִהַרְתִּי לְזָרֶזְךָ לַהֲבִיאָם, מִהְיוֹת **רֹעֶה** טוֹב מְחַזֵּר **אַחֲרֶיךָ** לְבַקֵּשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם רַחֲמִים:


**But I did not hasten** I did not hasten to urge You to bring them because I am a good shepherd, who goes after You to beg mercy for them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:2)


**וְיוֹם אָנוּשׁ.** חֹלִי שֶׁל פֻּרְעָנוּת **לֹא הִתְאַוֵּיתִי**. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וַאֲנָא לָא עַנְבִּית עַל מֵימְרָךְ מִלְּהִתְנַבָּאָה עֲלֵיהוֹן לַאֲתָבוּתְהוֹן לְדַחַלְתָּךְ״. וּלְפִי הַתַּרְגּוּם יִהְיֶה **אַצְתִּי** – לְשׁוֹן עִכּוּב, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא עִכַּבְתִּי מִלּוֹמַר לָהֶם שְׁלִיחוּתֶיךָ וּלְהָשִׁיבָם אֵלֶיךָ, אִם הָיוּ שׁוֹמְעִים אֵלַי:


**and...the woeful day** The illness of their retribution I did not desire. Jonathan renders: And I did not hamper Your word from prophesying concerning them to return them to fear You. According to the Targum, אַצְתִּי is an expression of hampering. That is to say, I did not hesitate from telling them Your message and to return them to you if they would hearken to me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:16:3)


**מוֹצָא שְׂפָתַי נֹכַח פָּנֶיךָ הָיָה.** לְהָשִׁיב אֶת חֲמָתְךָ מֵהֶם:


**What came out of my lips was before Your face** to return Your wrath from them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:17:1)


**אַל תִּהְיֵה לִי לִמְחִתָּה.** אַל יְהִי שְׁלִיחוּתְךָ לִמְחִתָּה:


**Let it not be to me for a ruin** Let not Your mission be to me for a ruin.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:18:1)


**יֵבֹשׁוּ רֹדְפַי.** אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת לְבַדָּם:


**May my pursuers be shamed** i.e., the men of Anathoth alone.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:23:1)


**וְלֹא שָׁמְעוּ. אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם** לְמַה שֶּׁצִּוִּיתִים:


**But they did not hearken** i.e., your forefathers did not hearken to what I commanded them.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 17:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/17:27:1)


**וּבֹא.** כְּמוֹ וְלָבֹא:


**and to come** like וְלָבֹא.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:3:1)


**עַל הָאָבְנִים.** ״עַל סַדְנָא״ (תרגום יונתן). פורמ״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**on the wheels** Jonathan renders: on the wheel. (Formes in French.)



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:4:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הוּא עוֹשֶׂה בַּחֹמֶר.** אֲשֶׁר הוּא עוֹשֶׂה מִן הַחֹמֶר **נִשְׁחַת בְּיַד הַיּוֹצֵר**, בְּעוֹדוֹ לַח; וּמִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא:


**that he was making of clay** That he was making of the clay was spoiled in the potter’s hand while it was still wet, and this is an inverted verse.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:8:1)


**מֵרָעָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתִּי עָלָיו.** מֵעֲבֵירוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, אֲשֶׁר בִּשְׁבִילוֹ דִּבַּרְתִּי עָלָיו ״לִנְתוֹשׁ וְלִנְתוֹץ״ (ירמיה א:י), אִירְפוֹרְקִ״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**of its evil for which I spoke concerning it** Of its sins for which I spoke concerning it. **to uproot and to demolish** (depayser [?] in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:8:2)


**וְנִחַמְתִּי.** וְחָשַׁבְתִּי מַחֲשָׁבָה אַחֶרֶת:


**I will repent** And I will think another thought.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:12:1)


**וְאָמְרוּ נוֹאָשׁ.** וְיָדַעְתִּי שֶׁהֵם יֹאמְרוּ לְךָ עַל דְּבָרֶיךָ, שֶׁאֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לָהֶם:


**But they shall say, We despair** I know that they will say to you regarding your words that they do not heed them.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:13:1)


**שַׁעֲרֻרִת.** דְּבָר גְּנַאי:


**disgraceful thing** Heb. שַעֲרֻרִת.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:1)


**הֲיַעֲזֹב מִצּוּר שָׂדַי שֶׁלֶג לְבָנוֹן.** הֲיַעֲזֹב אָדָם הַצָּרִיךְ לִשְׁתּוֹת מַיִם הַ**נּוֹזְלִים** מִצּוּר שֶׁבַּשָּׂדוֹת, הַבָּאִים מִשֶּׁלֶג הַלְּבָנוֹן שֶׁהוּא נָקִי?!


**Shall one abandon from rocks of the field the snow of Lebanon** Shall a man who needs to drink abandon water that flows from the rock in the fields that comes from the snow of the Lebanon, which is clean?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:2)


**אִם יִנָּתְשׁוּ מַיִם זָרִים.** אוֹ אִם יַעַזְבוּ מַיִם חַיִּים נוֹבְעִים, שֶׁהֵם זָרִים עַד הֵנָּה מִכָּל אָדָם, וְהֵם **קָרִים. זָרִים** הֵם נוֹבְעִים, וְזֶה יוֹכִיחַ: ״אֲנִי קַרְתִּי וְשָׁתִיתִי מַיִם זָרִים״ (מלכים ב יט:כד). וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ זָרִים עִם ״לֹא זֹרוּ וְלֹא חֻבָּשׁוּ״ (ישעיהו א:ו) – לְשׁוֹן מֵימֵי תְרוּפָה. וְנָתַן טַעַם לִדְבָרָיו וְאָמַר, כִּי אִם בִּקֵּשׁ לְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן זֶרֶם, הָיָה מַכְפִּיל אֶת הַמֵּ״ם וְהָיָה מוֹסִיף יוֹ״ד מֵ״ם, וְהָיָה אוֹמֵר זְרוּמִים, לְרַבּוֹת הַמַּיִם וְלִהְיוֹתָם זְרָמִים:


**Shall strange cold... water be abandoned?** Or shall flowing spring water, which was strange to all people until now, and it is cold, be abandoned? זָרִים means flowing, and this proves it (2 Kings 19:24): “I dug and drank strange water (זָרִים).” Menahem, however, classified with (Isaiah 1:6) “they have not been sprinkled (זֹרוּ), neither have they been bandaged,” an expression of curative water, and he gave a reason for his statement, and he said that if he wished to speak with an expression of זֶרֶם, a stream, he would double the ‘mem,’ and he would add ‘yud’ ‘mem,’ and he would say זְרוּמִים because of the plural number of מַיִם water, and because they are streams (זְרָמִים).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:3)


**שָׂדַי.** כְּמוֹ שָׂדֶה:


**the field** like שָדֶה, a field


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:4)


**קָרִים.** פְּרִיוְודָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**cold** (froids in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:5)


**נוֹזְלִים.** קוֹרַאנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ לְפָתְרוֹ: **אִם יִנָּתְשׁוּ** לִהְיוֹת **מַיִם זָרִים** נִמְאָסִים, הַמַּיִם שֶׁהֵם **קָרִים נוֹזְלִים**:


**flowing** (courants in French). And we can also interpret it: “Shall it be abandoned to be strange rejected water, the water that is cold running water?”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:14:6)


(זָרִים לְלָשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן הוּא לְשׁוֹן זֶרֶם; לְלָשׁוֹן שֵׁנִי הוּא לָשׁוֹן זָרוֹת, כְּלוֹמַר מַיִם מְאוּסִים):


**Heb.** זרים According to the first interpretation, it is an expression of a stream [זֶרֶם]. According to the second interpretation, it is an expression of strangeness, i.e., rejected water.)



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:15:1)


**כִּי.** כֵּן עָשׂוּ **עַמִּי** אֲשֶׁר **שְׁכֵחֻנִי** וְ**לַשָּׁוְא יְקַטֵּרוּ**:


**For** so have My people done, that they have forgotten Me and burnt incense to vanity.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:15:2)


**שְׁבִילֵי עוֹלָם.** שְׁבִילִים הַמְתֻקָּנִים אָמְרוּ שֶׁהֵם מִכְשׁוֹל, כְּדֵי **לָלֶכֶת דֶּרֶךְ לֹא סְלוּלָה** לֹא כְבוּשָׁה:


**ancient paths** The proper paths they said that they are a stumbling block, in order to go...**[on] a way that is not trodden down** Heb. סְלוּלָה.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:16:1)


**שְׁרִיקוֹת.** הָעוֹבֵר עַל חֳרָבוֹת שֶׁרָאָה כְּבָר בְּבִנְיָנָם, טוֹבִים, רָגִיל לִשְׁרֹק:


**hissing** One who passes by ruins that he already saw with good buildings is accustomed to whistle.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:16:2)


**יִשּׁוֹם.** יִתְמַהּ:


**will be astonished** will wonder.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:17:1)


**עֹרֶף וְלֹא פָנִים.** כְּשֶׁיִּבְרְחוּ מִפְּנֵי הָאוֹיֵב:


**upon their back and not their face** when they flee from before the enemy.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:17:2)


**אֶרְאֵם.** וְלֹא אוֹשִׁיעֵם:


**I will look** but I will not save them.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ לְכוּ וְגוֹ׳.** יִרְמְיָה הָיָה קוֹבֵל עַל אַנְשֵׁי עֲנָתוֹת וְאוֹמֵר שֶׁאוֹמְרִים כֵּן:


**And they said, “Come, etc.”** Jeremiah was complaining about the men of Anathoth and saying that they say this.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:2)


**תּוֹרָה מִכֹּהֵן.** שֶׁעַל הַכֹּהֲנִים לְהוֹרוֹת תּוֹרָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״יוֹרוּ מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לְיַעֲקֹב״ (דברים לג:י):


**Torah...from the priest** For it is incumbent upon the priests to teach the Torah, as it is stated (Deut. 33:10): “They shall teach Your ordinances to Jacob.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:3)


**וְדָבָר מִנָּבִיא.** דִּבְרֵי נְבוּאָה:


**not speech from the prophet** I. e., words of prophecy.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:18:4)


**וְנַכֵּהוּ בַלָּשׁוֹן.** ״וּנְסַהֲדִינֵיהּ סַהֲדוּתָא דְּשִׁקְרָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Come, let us strike him with the tongue** Let us testify falsely against him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:1)


**וְהַגִּרֵם.** (הוֹרִידֵם **עַל יְדֵי חֶרֶב**) לְשׁוֹן ״עֵינִי נִגְּרָה״ (איכה ג:מט), ״כַּמַּיִם הַמּוּגָרִים״ (מיכה א:ד):


**and spill their blood** (Bring them down by the sword,) an expression of (Lam. 3:49) “My eye runs (נִגְרָה)”; (Micah 1:4) “Like water that runs (מֻגָרִים).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:2)


**עַל יְדֵי חֶרֶב.** עַל יְדֵי גְּיָסוֹת:


**by the sword** by troops.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:21:3)


**וְאַנְשֵׁיהֶם.** שֶׁל הָאַלְמָנוֹת, **יִהְיוּ הֲרֻגֵי** מַלְאַךְ הַ**מָּוֶת**:


**and their husbands** i.e., the husbands of the widows shall be slain by the angel of death.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:22:1)


**כִּי כָרוּ שׁוּחָה.** שֶׁחֲשָׁדוּהוּ מֵאֵשֶׁת אִישׁ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כִּי שׁוּחָה עֲמֻקָּה זוֹנָה״ (משלי כג:כז):


**for they dug a pit to seize me** that they suspected him of intimacy with a married woman, as it is stated (Prov. 23:27): “For a harlot is a deep pit (ֹשוּחָה).”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 18:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/18:23:1)


**אַל תִּמְחִי.** אַל תְּהִי מְחוּיָה. וּלְפִי שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה נָתַן בּוֹ יוֹ״ד, וְאַף הִיא יְתֵרָה, כְּיוֹ״ד שֶׁל ״וַתִּזְנִי בָם״ (יחזקאל טז:יז):


**let not be erased** let it not be erased. And because it is a feminine gender, he placed a ‘yud’ therein. This too is superfluous, as the ‘yud’ of (Ezekiel 16:17) “And you played the harlot (וַתִּזְנִי) with them.”



## Chapter 19


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:1)


**בַּקְבּוּק.** צְלוֹחִית:


**a jug** Heb. בַּקְבֻּק.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:2)


**יוֹצֵר חָרֶשׂ.** כְּלוֹמַר: שֶׁל יוֹצֵר, שֶׁהוּא שֶׁל חָרֶשׂ:


**potter’s earthen** That is to say, of a potter, which is of earthenware.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:1:3)


**וּמִזִּקְנֵי הָעָם.** הָלוֹךְ עִמָּךְ; וּמִזִּקְנֵי הָעָם מוּסָב עַל **הָלוֹךְ** :**וּמִזִּקְנֵי הָעָם** עִמָּךְ:


**and some of the elders of the people** take along with you. “And of the elders of the people” refers back to ‘Go,’ and some of the elders of the people with you.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:2:1)


**שַׁעַר הַחַרְסִית.** שַׁעַר הָאַשְׁפּוֹת (נחמיה ב:יג), שֶׁשָּׁם זוֹרְקִין חֲרָסִים הַנִּשְׁבָּרִים; וְכֵן תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן: ״תְּרַע קִילְקִלְתָּא״:


**the Harsith Gate** The Dung Gate, where broken pottery is cast, and Jonathan rendered in this manner תְרַע קִלְקַלְתָּא.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:3:1)


**תִּצַּלְנָה.** טִינְטִינִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; לְשׁוֹן ״מְצִלְתַּיִם״ (נחמיה יב:כז); ״בְּצִלְצְלֵי שָׁמַע״ (תהלים קנ:ה):


**his ears will tingle** (tinter in French), an expression similar to “cymbals” (מְצִלְתַּיִם) (Neh. 12: 27), “with loud sounding cymbals (בְּצִלְצְלֵי ֹשָמַע)” (Psalms 150:5).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:4:1)


**וַיְנַכְּרוּ.** עֲשָׂאוּהוּ נָכְרִי לְפָנַי:


**and they estranged** They made it strange before Me.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:7:1)


**וּבַקּוֹתִי.** וְרוֹקַנְתִּי, לְשׁוֹן ״בּוּקָה וּמְבוּקָה״ (נחום ב:יא). וְעַל שֵׁם **בַּקְבּוּק** שֶׁהוּא מְדַבֵּר בּוֹ, לָקַח לְשׁוֹנוֹ כֵּן:


**And I will frustrate** and I will empty, an expression similar to (Nahum 2:11), “She is empty and void (בּוּקָה וּמְבוּקָה),” and because of the jug (בַּקְבֻּק) about which he is speaking, he adopted this expression.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:9:1)


**וּבְמָצוֹק.** לְשׁוֹן צָרָה, כְּמוֹ ״צוּקָה״ (ישעיה ל:ו):


**the straits** An expression of pain, like צוקה.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:11:1)


(**אֲשֶׁר לֹא יוּכַל לְהֵרָפֵה.** אַ**הָעָם** קָאֵי, אֲבָל **הָעִיר** אֶעֱשֶׂה כְּ**תֹפֶת** וַאֲטַהֵר לֶעָתִיד):


(**which can no longer be repaired** This refers to the people, but the city I will make like the Topheth and purify it in the future.)



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/19:12:1)


**הָעִיר הַזֹּאת כְּתֹפֶת.** כֻּלָּהּ מְלֵאָה הֲרוּגִים, כְּמוֹ הַתֹּפֶת שֶׁהוֹרְגִין שָׁם אֶת הַיְלָדִים לַמֹּלֶךְ (מלכים ב כג:י):


**this city like the Topheth** All of it will be full of the slain just as the Topheth, where they slay the children for Molech.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:2:1)


**הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת.** שֵׁם מְקוֹם הָאֲסוּרִים, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״לְכִיפְתָּא״, אַרְוְלוֹ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and placed him in the prison** the name of a place of a prison, and Jonathan rendered: לכיפתא (arvolud in O.F.), a vault.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:3:1)


**מָגוֹר.** יִרְאָה:


**Magor** Terror.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:3:2)


**לֹא פַשְׁחוּר קָרָא ה׳ שְׁמֶךָ וגו׳.** לֹא נִקְרָא שִׁמְךָ פַּשְׁחוּר אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִיל דָּבָר זֶה. כְּלוֹמַר פָּשַׁח שָׁחוֹר כְּמוֹ אִילָן שֶׁנִּפְשַׁח (משנה שביעית ד, ו), וְכֵן ״וַיְפַשְּׁחֵנִי״ (איכה ג, יא), וְכֵן אָמַר לוֹ לֹא פַשְׁחוּר קָרָא שְׁמֶךָ שֶׁלְּשׁוֹן פַּשְׁחוּר מַשְׁמַע אָדָם גָּדוֹל וּבֶן חוֹרִין, פָּשׁ חוּר, אֶלָּא לְכָךְ נִקְרָא שִׁמְךָ פַּשְׁחוּר – פָּשַׁח שָׁחוֹר. דָּבָר אַחֵר: פָּשׁ סְחוֹר, רַבִּים סְבִיבוֹתֶיךָ לַהֲרֹג, וְלַלָּשׁוֹן זֶה **מָגוֹר** הוּא לְשׁוֹן אֲסִיפָה, כְּמוֹ ״מְגוּרֵי אֶל חָרֶב״ (יחזקאל כא:יז):


**Not Passhur did the Lord call your name, etc.** Your name was not called Pashhur except for this matter, that is to say: פָֹשַח ֹשָחוֹר, cut off and black like “a tree that was cut off (נִפְֹשַח)” (Shevi’ith 4:6, Orlah 1:5). Also (Lam. 3:11), “And He cut me off (וַיִפַֹשְחֵנִי).” And so he said to him, Not Pashhur did He call your name, for the expression of Pashhur means a great man and the son of nobles, Pashhur. ‘Pash’ denotes expansion and ‘hur’ nobility. But for this reason your name was called Pashhur, Pashah shahor. ‘Pashah’ means ‘cut off,’ and ‘shahor’ means ‘black.’ Another explanation. ‘Pashah sehor.’ Many will be around you to slay you. Now according to this explanation, מָגוֹר is an expression of gathering, like (Ezekiel 21:17) “Gathered (מְגוּרֵי) to the sword.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:5:1)


**חֹסֶן.** מָמוֹן:


**the wealth** money.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:1)


**פִּתִּיתַנִי.** לָלֶכֶת בִּשְׁלִיחוּתְךָ:


**You enticed me** to go on your mission.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:2)


**חֲזַקְתַּנִי.** הִכְבַּדְתָּ עָלַי יָדְךָ הַחֲזָקָה, לָלֶכֶת עַל כָּרְחִי:


**You overcame me** You made Your strong hand heavy upon me to go against my will.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:3)


**וַתּוּכָל.** וְנִצַּחְתָּ:


**and You prevailed** And You were victorious.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:7:4)


**כֻּלֹּה.** כָּל הָעָם **לֹעֵג לִי**:


**everyone** All the people mock me.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:1)


**מִדֵּי אֲדַבֵּר.** בְּכָל עֵת שֶׁאֲדַבֵּר אֲלֵיהֶם, אֲנִי צָרִיךְ לִזְעֹק וּלְהָרִים קוֹל; וְאֵינִי קוֹרֵא אֲלֵיהֶם טוֹבָה כִּי אִם נְבוּאוֹת **חָמָס וָשֹׁד**:


**Whenever I speak** Every time I speak to them, I must cry out and raise my voice, and I do not proclaim good for them but prophecies of violence and spoil.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:2)


**כִּי הָיָה דְבַר ה׳ לִי.** לְפִיכָךְ דִּבְּרוּ לִי **חֶרְפָּה**, שֶׁהֵם מְחָרְפִים אוֹתִי:


**for the word of the Lord has been...for me** Therefore, they spoke to me with scorn, for they scorn me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:8:3)


**וְלִקְלֶס.** וּלְדִבּוּר, כְּמוֹ ״וְקַלָּסָה״ (יחזקאל כב:ד):


**and mockery** for speech. Comp. (Ezekiel 22:4) “and mockery (וְקַלָּסָה).”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:1)


**וְאָמַרְתִּי לֹא אֶזְכְּרֶנּוּ.** וְאִם אָמַר לִי לִבִּי שֶׁלֹּא לֵילֵךְ בִּשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ עוֹד:


**Should I say: I will not mention Him** If my heart said to me not to go on Your mission anymore.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:2)


**וְהָיָה בְלִבִּי.** הַנְּבוּאָה **כְּאֵשׁ בֹּעֶרֶת**, וְעַל כָּרְחִי אֲנִי צָרִיךְ לֵילֵךְ:


**it would be in my heart** i.e., the prophecy, **like a burning fire** And against my will I must go.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:3)


**עָצוּר בְּעַצְמוֹתָי.** וְכָנוּס בְּעַצְמוֹתַי הָ**אֵשׁ**:


**confined in my bones** And as if the fire were confined in my bones.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:9:4)


**כַּלְכֵּל.** לִסְבּוֹל הָ**אֵשׁ**:


**to contain** To endure the fire.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:1)


**כִּי שָׁמַעְתִּי.** אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי שֶׁלֹּא אֶזְכְּרֶנּוּ לְפִי שֶׁשָּׁמַעְתִּי **דִּבַּת רַבִּים**, אֵלּוּ שֶׁמִּתְלַחֲשִׁים עָלַי:


**For I heard** I said that I would not mention Him because I heard the speech of many, those who whisper about me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:2)


**מָגוֹר.** לְשׁוֹן אֲסִיפָה, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**a gathering** an expression of gathering, and so did Jonathan render it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:3)


**הַגִּידוּ.** עָלָיו עֵדוּת שֶׁקֶר:


**tell** false testimony against him.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:4)


**וְנַגִּידֶנּוּ.** וְנָעִידֶנּוּ. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ **הַגִּידוּ וְנַגִּידֶנּוּ** כְּמוֹ ״לֹא תִתְגּוֹדְדוּ״ (דברים יד:א), וְכֵן חִבֵּר ״לְחֵלֶק יַגִּיד רֵעִים״ (איוב יז:ה) לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁחָתָה, כְּמוֹ ״גֹּדּוּ אִילָנָא״ (דניאל ד:יא). וְדוּנָשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ לְשׁוֹן הַגָּדָה מַמָּשׁ, וְכֹה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: **הַגִּידוּ** עָלָיו דִּבְרֵי שֶׁקֶר **וְנַגִּידֶנּוּ** לַמֶּלֶךְ. וְיֵשׁ לָנוּ כְּמוֹ נַגִּידֶנּוּ דְּבָרִים רַבִּים בַּמִּקְרָא, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִכּוֹן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ כִּי אִם עַד אֲשֶׁר יְחַלְּקוּן לִשְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת אוֹ לְשָׁלֹשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״וְלֹא יָכְלוּ דַּבְּרוֹ לְשָׁלוֹם״ (בראשית לז:ד); ״בָּנַי יְצָאֻנִי״ (ירמיה י:כ):


**and let us tell about him** and let us testify against him. And Menahem (Machbereth p. 53) explained ונגידֶנוּ like (Deut. 14:1) “You shall not cut yourselves (לא תתגודדוּ).” He also joined (Job 17:5) “For the sake of flattering, he destroys (יגיד) his friends.” This is an expression of destruction. Comp. (Dan. 4:11) “Cut down (גֹדוּ) the trees.” But Dunash (Teshuvoth p. 54) interpreted it as an actual expression of telling, and this is its interpretation: “Tell false things about him and we will tell about him to the king.” And we have like נַגִידֶנוּ, we will tell about him, many words in the Scriptures that cannot be interpreted unless they are divided into two words or three. Comp. (Gen. 37: 4) “And they could not speak with him (דַבְּרוֹ) peacefully.” Also (supra 10:20), “My children have left me (יצאני).”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:5)


**שׁוֹמְרֵי צַלְעִי.** מְצַפִּים שִׁבְרִי:


**await my destruction** Heb. צַלְעֵי


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:10:6)


**אוּלַי יְפֻתֶּה.** לִשְׁמֹעַ לָנוּ וְלִטְעוֹת אַחֲרֵינוּ:


**Perhaps he will be enticed.** to listen to us and to stray after us. and we will prevail against him to bring him to commit idolatry.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:11:1)


**כִּי לֹא הִשְׂכִּילוּ.** לֹא הִצְלִיחוּ בַּעֲצָתָם:


**for they did not succeed** They did not succeed with their plan.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:14:1)


**אָרוּר הַיּוֹם אֲשֶׁר יֻלַּדְתִּי בּוֹ.** פוא״י אינייניר״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, הִיא שְׁעַת הֵרָיוֹן. וְעַל יְדֵי אֹנֶס שִׁמֵּשׁ חִלְקִיָּהוּ אָבִיו מִטָּתוֹ בַּיּוֹם, שֶׁהָיָה בּוֹרֵחַ מִפְּנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה שֶׁהָיָה הוֹרֵג אֶת הַנְּבִיאִים; וְשִׁמֵּשׁ מִטָּתוֹ וּבָרַח (בראשית רבה סד, ה):


**Cursed be the day in which I was begotten** (engendrer or engenrer in O.F.). That is the time of conception, and through compulsion Hilkiah his father performed the marriage act by day, for he was fleeing from before Manasseh, who was slaying the prophets, and he performed the marriage act and fled.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:15:1)


**שַׂמֵּחַ.** מ״ם דְּגוּשָׁה, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן תְּשַׂמַּח אֲחֵרִים; שֶׁשִּׂמַּח אֶת אָבִיו בִּבְשׂוֹרָתוֹ:


**making him glad** The ‘mem’ is punctuated with a ‘dagesh,’ which is an expression of making others glad, for he made his father glad with his news.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:16:1)


**כֶּעָרִים אֲשֶׁר הָפַךְ.** סְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה (בראשית יט:כד-כה):


**like the cities that...overturned** Namely Sodom and Gemorrah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:16:2)


**וְלֹא נִחָם.** מִשֶּׁגָּזַר עֲלֵיהֶם חוּרְבָּן, לֹא חָשַׁב מַחֲשָׁבָה אַחֶרֶת לְבַטֵּל גְּזֵרָתוֹ (בראשית יח:כ-לב):


**and did not repent** Since He decreed destruction upon them, he did not think another thought to rescind His decree.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:17:1)


**אֲשֶׁר לֹא מוֹתְתַנִי** מַלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת **מֵרָחֶם**. וַהֲרֵי זֶה מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת קִצְרֵי דִּבּוּר:


**That he did not put me to death** I.e., that the angel of death did not put me to death from the womb, and this is one of the verses with brief language.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:17:2)


**הֲרַת עוֹלָם.** הֵרָיוֹן שֶׁל עוֹלָמִית:


**a perpetual pregnancy** a pregnancy of eternity.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/20:18:1)


**עָמָל וְיָגוֹן.** חֻרְבַּן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**toil and grief** The destruction of the Temple.



## Chapter 21


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:4:1)


**הִנְנִי מֵסֵב אֶת כְּלֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה.** יֵשׁ בָּכֶם הַיּוֹדְעִים לְהַשְׁבִּיעַ אֶת הַשָּׂרִים שֶׁל מַעְלָה הַמְמֻנִּים עַל הַפֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת, לְהַצִּיל אִישׁ אִישׁ מִן הָרָעָה שֶׁהוּא מְמֻנֶּה עָלֶיהָ, וּבְכָךְ אַתֶּם בּוֹטְחִים; הִנְנִי מֵסֵב אֶת הַשְּׂרָרָה שֶׁל זֶה לָזֶה, וְלֹא תֵדְעוּ עַל מִי תַּשְׁבִּיעוּ:


**Behold I turn back the weapons of war** There are some among you who know how to adjure the heavenly princes in charge of retribution, that each one save them from the evil he is in charge of, and you rely on that. Behold, I will transfer one’s authority to another one, and you will not know whom to adjure.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:4:2)


**וְאָסַפְתִּי אוֹתָם.** כְּלֵי מִלְחָמָה **אֶל תּוֹךְ הָעִיר הַזֹּאת**, שֶׁאַשְׁחִית אֶתְכֶם בְּדֶבֶר עַל יְדֵי מַלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת, שֶׁהוּא מִן הַשָּׂרִים הָעֶלְיוֹנִים; וְכֵן מְפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן: **בְּדֶבֶר גָּדוֹל יָמוּתוּ**:


**and I will gather them** i.e., the weapons of war, into this city, that I will destroy you with pestilence through the angel of death, who is one of the heavenly princes. And in this manner it is explained in the chapter (v. 6): “with a great pestilence they shall die.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:9:1)


**וְהָיְתָה לוֹ נַפְשׁוֹ לְשָׁלָל.** כְּאָדָם הַשּׁוֹלֵל שָׁלָל וְחוֹטְפוֹ וְהוֹלֵךְ לוֹ, כֵּן תִּהְיֶה לוֹ נַפְשׁוֹ שְׁלוּלָה מִן הַמִּיתָה, מִן הַהוֹרְגִים:


**and he will have his life as a prey** As a man who plunders booty, and he seizes and goes away, so will his life be plundered from death by the slayers.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:10:1)


**פָּנַי.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״רוּגְזִי״:


**My anger** as the Targum, רוּגְזִי My anger.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:12:1)


**מִיַּד עוֹשֵׁק.** טַעֲמוֹ לְמַטָּה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֵׁם דָּבָר אֶלָּא שֵׁם הַגַּזְלָן הָעוֹשֵׁק אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


**from the hand of the robber** Its accent is below for it is not a noun but a noun denoting the robber who robs others.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:12:2)


**וְאֵין מְכַבֶּה.** שֶׁלֹּא יִמָּלֵט לָכֶם שָׂרִיד:


**with no one to extinguish [it]** that no one of you will escape as a survivor.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:1)


**יוֹשֶׁבֶת הָעֵמֶק.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הִיא בְּתוֹךְ עֵמֶק. דָּבָר אַחֵר: שֶׁהֶעֱמִיק הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עֵצָה עָלֶיךָ לְהַחֲרִיבֶךָ:


**O dweller of the valley** Jerusalem is in the midst of a valley. Another explanation. That the Holy One, blessed be He, devised a deep plot against you to destroy you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:2)


**צוּר הַמִּישׁוֹר.** הַר שֶׁכָּל סְבִיבָיו מִישׁוֹר:


**rock of the plain** Mountain, entirely surrounded by plain.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 21:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/21:13:3)


**מִי יֵחַת עָלֵינוּ.** מִי יַחֲנֶה עָלֵינוּ, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי שָׁם אֲרָם נְחִתִּים״ (מלכים ב ו:ט):


**Who will encamp upon us** who will encamp upon us. Comp. (II Kings 6: 9) “For there the Arameans are encamped (נְחִתִּים).”



## Chapter 22


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:3:1)


**אַל תּוֹנוּ.** זוֹ אוֹנָאַת דְּבָרִים (תורת כהנים בהר פרשתא ג, ד):


**do no wrong** This means taunting with words.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:6:1)


**גִּלְעָד אַתָּה לִי וגו׳.** ״אִלּוּ אַתְּ חֲבִיב קֳדָמַי כְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא דְּרָם בְּרֵישׁ טוּרַיָּא, אִם לֹא אֲשַׁוִּינָךְ מִדְבְּרָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**You are [as] Gilead to Me, etc.** Even if you are as dear to Me as the Temple, which is high on the mountain peaks, I swear that I will make you a desert.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:6:2)


**גִּלְעָד אַתָּה.** הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (מכילתא בשלח עמלק ב), שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ צֳרִי וּרְפוּאָה לְכָל הָעוֹלָם (פסיקתא רבתי לג):


**You are [as] Gilead** The Temple from which balm and healing emanate to the whole world.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:7:1)


**וְקִדַּשְׁתִּי.** וְזִמַּנְתִּי:


**And I will prepare** and I will prepare.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:7:2)


**וְכָרְתוּ מִבְחַר אֲרָזֶיךָ.** לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּהוּ לַ**לְּבָנוֹן**, נוֹפֵל כָּאן הַלָּשׁוֹן הַזֶּה:


**and they will cut your choice cedars** Since he compares him to the Lebanon, this expression is appropriate.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:10:1)


**אַל תִּבְכּוּ לַמֵּת.** לִיהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁיָּמוּת לִפְנֵי הַשַּׁעַר כְּשֶׁיִּסְחָבוּהוּ לְהַגְלוֹתוֹ (ירמיה כב:יט):


**Weep not for the dead** Namely, for Jehoiakim, who will die before the gate, when they drag him into exile.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:10:2)


**בְּכוּ לַהֹלֵךְ.** לִיהוֹיָכִין וְלִצִדְקִיָּהוּ. אֱמוֹר מֵעַתָּה: חֲבִיבָה מִיתָתוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ ״וְנִבְלָתוֹ תִּהְיֶה מֻשְׁלֶכֶת לַחֹרֶב בַּיּוֹם וְלַקֶּרַח בַּלָּיְלָה״ (ירמיה לו:ל), מֵחַיָּיו שֶׁל יְהוֹיָכִין, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ ״וַאֲרֻחָתוֹ אֲרֻחַת תָּמִיד וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה נב:לד):


**weep for him who goes** For Jehoiachin and for Zedekiah. Deduce from here that Jehoiakim’s death, concerning which it is stated (infra 36:30): “And his carcass shall be cast to the heat by day and to the cold at night,” is preferable to the life of Jehoiachin, about whom it is stated (52:34): “And his meals, regular meals, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:11:1)


**אֶל שַׁלּוּם.** הוּא צִדְקִיָּהוּ, וְכָךְ נִקְרָא ״וְהָרְבִיעִי שַׁלּוּם״ (דברי הימים א ג:טו). וְקְרָאוֹ רְבִיעִי לַמַּלְכוּת בִּבְנֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ, שֶׁבַּתְּחִלָּה מָלַךְ יְהוֹאָחָז וְאַחַר כָּךְ יְהוֹיָקִים וְאַחֲרָיו יְהוֹיָכִין וְאַחֲרָיו צִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כג-כה; הוריות יא:):


**concerning Shallum** That is Zedekiah, and that is what he was called in I Chron. (3:14): “the fourth Shallum.” And he called him the Fourth, meaning the fourth to assume the kingship of Josiah’s sons, for first Jehoaha, reigned, then Jehoiakim, and after him Jehoiachin, and after him Zedekiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:11:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יָצָא.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יָצָא עֲדַיִן, מִתְנַבֵּא עָלָיו כְּאִלּוּ יָצָא:


**who left** Even though he hadn’t left yet, he prophesied as if he had already left.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:13:1)


**הוֹי בּוֹנֶה בֵיתוֹ.** עַל יְהוֹיָקִים הוּא אוֹמֵר, שֶׁהוּא הָיָה רָשָׁע. וְסוֹף הָעִנְיָן מוֹכִיחַ: **לָכֵן כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ אֶל יְהוֹיָקִים וְגוֹ׳**:


**Woe to him who builds his house** He is saying this about Jehoiakim, who was a wicked man, and the end of the section proves this: “Therefore, so said the Lord about Jehoiakim.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:1)


**בֵּית מִדּוֹת.** בַּיִת גָּדוֹל, כְּמוֹ ״אַנְשֵׁי מִדּוֹת״ (במדבר יג:לב) שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן מַה לִמְדֹּד:


**a wide house** a large house. Cf. (Num. 13:32) “Men of great stature (אַנְֹשֵי מִדּוֹת),” who have what to measure.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:2)


**וְקָרַע לוֹ.** וְהִרְחִיב לוֹ. אֲחֵרִים הִגִּיהוּ **וְקָרַע לוֹ** וּפָתַח לוֹ, וְכֵן ״וַתִּקְרְעִי בַּפּוּךְ״ (ירמיה ד:ל), ״קָרַעְתָּ שָׁמַיִם יָרַדְתָּ״ (ישעיהו סג:יט) – כֻּלָּן לְשׁוֹן פְּתִיחָה:


**and he cuts out...for himself** And he widens for himself (Some emend: And he opens for himself) Comp. (supra 4:30) “that you enlarge (וַתִּקְרְעִי)...with paint.” Also (Isaiah 63:19), “Had You rent (קָרַעְתְּ) the heavens, had You descended.” They are all an expression of opening.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:3)


**וְסָפוּן בָּאָרֶז.** מְסַכֵּךְ הַגַּג בַּאֲרָזִים:


**and ceiled with cedar** He covers the roof with cedars.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:14:4)


**וּמָשׁוּחַ בַּשָּׁשַׁר.** ״וּמְצֻיָּר בְּסַמְמָנִים״, תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. **בַּשָּׁשַׁר** צֶבַע הוּא מִמִּין הַצִּיּוּרִים. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **וּמָשׁוּחַ בַּשָּׁשַׁר** כְּמוֹ חוּט הַמִּשְׁחָה (כלים יז,יג) אֵיינַאנְ״ט אַנְשִׁינוֹפְלָ״א בְּלַעַז:


**and painted with vermilion** painted with dyes, so does Jonathan render. בּשָּׁשַּׁר is a color of a type used for painting. Another explanation: וּמָשּׁוֹחַ בִּשָּׁשַּׁר is like a cord used for measuring (חוּט הַמִּשְּׁחָה) ([a] lignant en sinople in O.F.), drawing green lines, delineate with a green color.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:15:1)


**הֲתִמְלֹךְ.** הַתַּאֲרִיךְ יָמִים עַל שֶׁאַתָּה **מְתַחֲרֶה** לְהַכְבִּיד עַל מֶמְשַׁלְתְּךָ לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁאַתָּה מֶלֶךְ?!


**Shall you reign** Will you live long that you contend to make your rule oppressive to let all know that you are a king?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:15:2)


**אָבִיךָ.** יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ, שֶׁהִתְנַהֵג בַּעֲנָוָה, **הֲלוֹא אָכַל וְשָׁתָה** וְרָאָה **טוֹב** כָּל יְמֵי חַיָּיו:


**your father** Josiah, who demeaned himself with humility, did he not eat and drink and enjoy pleasure all the days of his life?



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:17:1)


**עַל בִּצְעֶךָ.** לִגְזֹל מָמוֹן:


**on your gain** to steal money.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:17:2
