[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:17:2)


**הַמְּרוּצָה.** רִצּוּץ דַּלִּים; וְ**עֹשֶׁק** יוֹכִיחַ עָלָיו, כִּי עָשׁוּק וְרָצוּץ תָּמִיד סְמוּכִין בִּלְשׁוֹן מִקְרָא (דברים כח:לג; עמוס ד:א), וְכֵן ״אֶת מִי עָשַׁקְתִּי וְאֶת מִי רַצּוֹתִי״ (שמואל א יב:ג):


**the crushing** the crushing of the poor, and עֹשֶק, oppression proves it, for ‘oppressed’ and ‘crushed’ are always juxtaposed in the language of the Scriptures. Cf. (I Sam. 12: 3) “Whom did I oppress or whom did I crush?”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:18:1)


**וְהוֹי הֹדֹה.** הוֹי עַל תִּפְאַרְתּוֹ:


**Hoe, his glory!** Woe for his glory.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:19:1)


**קְבוּרַת חֲמוֹר יִקָּבֵר.** ״כְּמָה דִמְשַׁגְּרִין יָת נִבְלַת חֲמָרָא כֵּן יְשַׁגְּרוּן יָת נְבִילְתֵיהּ״ (תרגום יונתן). יְשַׁגְּרוּן – יִסְחֲבוּן:


**A donkey’s burial** As they drag the carcass of a donkey, so will they drag his carcass. The word יְֹשַגְּרוּן used by Targum Jonathan means, they will drag.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:1)


**עֲלִי הַלְּבָנוֹן וּצְעָקִי.** ״סַקִי לְבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא וְצַוְּחִי״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Ascend the Lebanon and cry** Go up to the Temple and cry.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:2)


**וּבַבָּשָׁן תְּנִי קוֹלֵךְ.** עַל הַבָּשָׁן שֶׁיֶּחֱרַב. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וּבְתַרְעֵי טוּר בֵּיתָא״; וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ לְפָרֵשׁ הָאֵיךְ נוֹפֵל לְשׁוֹן בָּשָׁן בְּשַׁעֲרֵי הַר הַבַּיִת, אִם לֹא בִּשְׁבִיל הַדְּלָתוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂין מֵאַלּוֹנֵי הַבָּשָׁן, וְהוּא שֵׁם מָקוֹם. אוֹ לְשׁוֹן **בָּשָׁן** – מְקוֹם בִּקְעָה רְחָבָה, כֵּן הֶקֵּף הַר הַבַּיִת מִגְרָשׁ רֶוַח לְבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and on Bashan give forth your voice** On Bashan which will be destroyed. And Jonathan paraphrased: And in the gates of the Temple Mount. And I do not know how to explain how the expression ‘Bashan’ fits to the gates of the Temple Mount, unless it is because of the doors which they make from the terebinths of Bashan, and that is the name of a place, or the expression, ‘Bashan’ denotes a place of a wide valley. So is the area of the Temple Mount an open area around the Temple.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:20:3)


**וְצַעֲקִי מֵעֲבָרִים.** ״וְצַוְּחִי עַל מְגִיזָתָא״ (תרגום יונתן), וְהֵם לְשׁוֹן מָקוֹם:


**and cry for Avarim** And cry for Megizatha. They are place names.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:21:1)


**בְּשַׁלְוֺתַיִךְ.** כְּשֶׁהָיִיתָ בְּשַׁלְוָה:


**in your peaceful times** when you lived in peace.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:22:1)


**כָּל רֹעַיִךְ.** כָּל מְלָכַיִךְ:


**All your shepherds** All your kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:22:2)


**תִּרְעֶה רוּחַ.** רוּחַ קָדִים תְּרוֹצְצֵם:


**shall be broken by the wind** The east wind shall break them.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:23:1)


**מְקֻנַּנְתְּ בָּאֲרָזִים.** שֶׁשַּׂמְתְּ קִנֵּךְ בְּמִגְדְּלוֹת בָּתֵּי אֲרָזִים, לִבְנוֹת בָּתֵּי מִדּוֹת:


**who nest in the cedars** For you placed your nest in the towers of the cedar houses to build wide houses.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:23:2)


**מַה נֶּחַנְתְּ.** מַה מָּצָאת חֵן בִּשְׁבִיל גֹּבַהּ מִגְדָּלַיִךְ בְּעֵינֵי שׁוֹדְדַיִךְ, **בְּבֹא לָךְ חֲבָלִים** שֶׁל לֵידָה. וּמְנַחֵם פָּתַר **נֶּחַנְתְּ** לְשׁוֹן חֲנִיָּה; אֲבָל דּוּנַשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ לְשׁוֹן חֵן:


**how gracious shall you be** What grace have you found because of the height of your towers, in the eyes of your plunderers, when birth pangs come upon you. And Menahem interpreted נֵחַנְתְּ as an expression of camping, but Dunash interpreted it as an expression of grace.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:24:1)


**חוֹתָם עַל יַד יְמִינִי.** חָקוּק וְחָתוּם בְּתוֹךְ בְּשַׂר זְרוֹעִי:


**a signet** Engraved and sealed in the flesh of My arm.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:24:2)


**אֶתְּקֶנְךָּ.** אֶנְתְּקֶךָ, לְשׁוֹן ״הַתִּיקֵם כַּצֹּאן לְטִבְחָה״ (ירמיה יב:ג); ״עַד הִתִּיקוּנוֹ אוֹתָם״ (יהושע ח:ו); וְהַנּוּ״ן יְתֵרָה בּוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (פסיקתא דרב כהנא כד:יא): בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁנִּתַּק – נִתְקַן; שֶׁעָשָׂה תְּשׁוּבָה בְּבָבֶל, וְנִשְׁאַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּע **כִּתְבוּ אֶת הָאִישׁ הַזֶּה עֲרִירִי**, וְנוֹלַד לוֹ זְרֻבָּבֶל בְּבָבֶל, וְנֶאֱמַר לוֹ עַל פִּי הַנָּבִיא: ״בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא אֶקָּחֲךָ זְרֻבָּבֶל בֶּן שְׁאַלְתִּיאֵל עַבְדִּי וְשַׂמְתִּיךָ כַּחוֹתָם״ (חגי ב:כג); כְּלַפֵּי שֶׁאָמַר לְאָבִיו: **אִם יִהְיֶה חוֹתָם עַל יַד יְמִינִי**, אֶנְתְּקֶנּוּ:


**I will remove you** Cf. (supra 12:3) “Draw them out (התִקֵם) like sheep to the slaughter” (Jos. 8:6) “Until we have drawn (התִקֵינוּ) them.” And the ‘nun’ is superfluous. And, according to the Midrash Aggadah (Pesikta d’Rav Kahana, p. 163a): In the place to which he was removed (נִתַּק), there he was rectified (נִתְקַן), for he repented in Babylon, and the Holy One, blessed be He, applied for absolution of the oath He had sworn, “Inscribe this man childless.” and Zerubbabel was born to him in Babylon, and it was said to him through the prophet (Haggai 2:23), “On that day...I will take you, Zerubbabel, and I will make you as a signet,” directed toward what He said to his father, “Though...be a signet on My right arm,...I will remove him (sic).”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:26:1)


**וְהֵטַלְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁלָכָה:


**And I will cast** an expression of throwing.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:27:1)


**מְנַשְּׂאִים אֶת נַפְשָׁם.** לְשׁוֹן תַּנְחוּמִים; שֶׁנַּפְשָׁם מִתְנַשְּׂאָה לְהִתְנַחֵם וְלוֹמַר: עוֹד נָשׁוּב לְאַרְצֵנוּ:


**they long** This is an expression of consolation, that their soul lifts itself to console itself and to say, “We will again return to our land.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:1)


**הַעֶצֶב נִבְזֶה.** תְּמִיהָּ הוּא: שֶׁמָּא צוּרָה נִשְׁבָּר וְנִבְזֶה **הָאִישׁ הַזֶּה** שֶׁהֻשְׁלַךְ מֵעַל פָּנַי כְּמִי שֶׁ**אֵין חֵפֶץ בּוֹ**?!


**Is...a despised... image** It is a rhetorical question. Is this man a broken and despised image, that he was cast away from My face like a vessel in which there is no use?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:2)


**נָפוּץ.** נִשְׁבָּר, כְּמוֹ ״כִּכְלִי יוֹצֵר תְּנַפְּצֵם״ (תהלים ב:ט). וַאֲחֵרִים הִגִּיהוּ **הַעֶצֶב** כְּמוֹ הָעֲצַבִּים, ״מְעַצְּבִין אֶת הַקָּטָן״ בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת (קמז.), כְּלוֹמַר: הַאַתָּה נִבְזֶה וְנָפוּץ בְּעֵינֶיךָ כְּעוֹלֵל קָטָן שֶׁמְּעַצְּבִין אוֹתוֹ? מילויילי״ר בְּלַעַז. אֵין זֶה כְּדַי:


**shattered** broken. Cf. (Psalms 2: 8) “Like a potter’s vessel you shall shatter them (תְּנַפְּצֵם).” [Others annotated: הַעֶצֶב is like the idols (הָעֲצַבִּים). “We may swathe (מְעַצְּבִין) an infant” in Tractate Shabbath 22:6. That is to say, “Are you despised and broken in your eyes like a small infant who is being swathed (emmailloter in French)? This one is not worthy.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:28:3)


**הוּטְלוּ.** הֻשְׁלְכוּ, לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁלָכָה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ תְּקוּמָה, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יִפֹּל לֹא יוּטָל״ (תהלים לז:כד):


**cast away** thrown, an expression of throwing that has no restoration. Comp. (Psalms 37:24), “If he falls, he will not be cast off (יוּטָל).”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:29:1)


**אֶרֶץ אֶרֶץ אָרֶץ.** מֵאַרְצוֹ גָּלָה אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת, וְאַתְּ, אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, **שִׁמְעִי דְּבַר ה׳**. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **אֶרֶץ אֶרֶץ, אֶרֶץ** שֶׁהוּא **אֶרֶץ** שֶׁבָּאֲרָצוֹת, שֶׁהָיְתָה חֲשׁוּבָה שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן. דָּבָר אַחֵר (בבא בתרא לח.): **אֶרֶץ** יִשְׂרָאֵל שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת הָיוּ בָּהּ: יְהוּדָה וְעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן וְהַגָּלִיל:


**O land, land, land** A land that is a land among lands, the most esteemed of all of them. Another explanation: Eretz Israel had three lands in it. Judea, Transjordania, and Galilee.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 22:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/22:30:1)


**גֶּבֶר.** אֲשֶׁר **לֹא יִצְלַח בְּיָמָיו** כִּתְבוּהוּ:


**a man who will not prosper in his days** Inscribe him.



## Chapter 23


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:1:1)


**הוֹי רוֹעִים.** מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה:


**Woe to the shepherds** The kings of Judah.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:2:1)


**לֹא פְקַדְתֶּם אוֹתָם.** לֹא נִזְהַרְתֶּם לְשָׁמְרָם:


**and have not taken care of them** You were not careful to watch them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:4:1)


**וְלֹא יִפָּקֵדוּ.** וְלֹא יֶחְסְרוּ, לָשׁוֹן ״וְלֹא נִפְקַד מִמֶּנּוּ אִישׁ״ (במדבר לא:מט):


**nor shall [any of them] be missing** and they shall not be missing. (Cf. Num. 31:49) “And no one of us was missing (נפקד).”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:5:1)


**וְהִשְׂכִּיל.** וְיַצְלִיחַ:


**and prosper** Heb. והשכּיל.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:6:1)


**ה׳ צִדְקֵנוּ.** ה׳ מַצְדִּיק אוֹתָנוּ בְּיָמָיו שֶׁל זֶה:


**The Lord is our righteousness** The Lord will vindicate us during this one’s days.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:9:1)


**לַנְּבִיאִים נִשְׁבַּר לִבִּי בְקִרְבִּי.** לְדִבְרֵי הַנְּבִיאִים, הָאוֹמְרִים **שָׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לָכֶם**, **נִשְׁבַּר לִבִּי**. **רָחֲפוּ** – הוֹלְכִים וְנָעִים, כְּכַנְפֵי צִפּוֹר הַמְרַחֵף עַל קִנּוֹ; טְרַנְבְּלִיר״ט בְּלַעַז:


**Because of the prophets my heart is broken within me** Because of the words of the prophets who say, “You shall have peace,” my heart is broken. **shake** sway like the wings of a bird who hovers over his nest, (trenblent in O.F.) tremble.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:9:2)


**מִפְּנֵי ה׳.** שֶׁאֲנִי שׁוֹמֵעַ מִמֶּנּוּ גְּזֵרָה רָעָה זֹאת:


**because of the Lord** For I heard from Him this evil decree.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:1)


**מִפְּנֵי אָלָה.** שְׁבוּעַת שֶׁקֶר:


**because of oaths** False oaths.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:2)


**אָבְלָה.** חֲרֵבָה:


**mourns** i.e., is destroyed.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:3)


**מְרוּצָתָם.** קוֹרְשָׁ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם לְשׁוֹן רָצוֹן:


**their eagerness** (course in French,) their course, their running. But Jonathan renders it as an expression of desire.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:10:4)


**לֹא כֵן.** לֹא בַדִּין:


**not right** Not according to the law.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:11:1)


**גַּם נָבִיא גַּם כֹּהֵן.** אֲפִילּוּ אֵלּוּ, שֶׁהַכֹּל לְמֵדִין מֵהֶם:


**both prophet and priest** Even these, from whom everyone learns.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:11:2)


**גַּם בְּבֵיתִי מָצָאתִי רָעָתָם.** שֶׁהֶעֱמִידוּ דְּמוּת בַּהֵיכָל:


**even in My house have I found their evil** For they placed an image in the Temple.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:12:1)


**כַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת.** אֶרֶץ חֲלָקָה, שֶׁהָרֶגֶל מַחְלֶקֶת בָּהּ וְהָאָדָם נוֹפֵל:


**like slippery places** Smooth ground upon which the foot slips and the person falls.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:12:2)


**כַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת בָּאֲפֵלָה.** כִּשָּׁלוֹן עַל כִּשָּׁלוֹן: חֲלַקְלַקּוֹת בָּאוֹר יֵשׁ כִּשָּׁלוֹן, אֲפֵלָה בְּלֹא חֲלַקְלַקּוֹת יֵשׁ כִּשָּׁלוֹן:


**like slippery places in the dark** Stumbling upon stumbling. On slippery places in the light there is stumbling. In the dark without slippery places, there is stumbling.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:13:1)


**וּבִנְבִיאֵי שֹׁמְרוֹן רָאִיתִי תִפְלָה.** קֹדֶם שֶׁגָּלוּ:


**And in the prophets of Samaria I saw unseemliness** before they were exiled.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:1)


**וּבִנְבִיאֵי יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** אֲנִי רוֹאֶה עַתָּה **שַׁעֲרוּרָה**, דָּבָר מְגֻנֶּה:


**And in the prophets of Jerusalem** I now see. **a horrible thing** a shameful thing.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:2)


**וְחִזְּקוּ יְדֵי מְרֵעִים.** אוֹמְרִים לָרְשָׁעִים: **שָׁלוֹם יִהְיֶה לָכֶם**:


**and they encouraged evil doers** They say to the wicked, “You shall have peace.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:14:3)


**לְבִלְתִּי שָׁבוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יָשׁוּבוּ הַ**מְּרֵעִים אִישׁ מֵרָעָתוֹ**:


**that none returns** So that the evildoers would not return each one from his evil.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:17:1)


**וְכֹל הוֹלֵךְ בִּשְׁרִירוּת לִבּוֹ וְגוֹ׳.** וּלְכָל הוֹלֵךְ בִּשְׁרִירוּת לִבּוֹ וְגוֹ׳:


**and everyone who follows the view of his heart** And to everyone who follows the view of his heart, etc.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:18:1)


**מִי עָמַד בְּסוֹד ה׳.** וְיִשְׁמַע אֶת הַנִּגְזָר מִלְּפָנָיו? אוֹתוֹ שֶׁהִקְשִׁיב דְּבַר תּוֹרָתוֹ **וְיִשְׁמַע** לְמִצְוֹתָיו, וְלֹא הָרְשָׁעִים הָאֵלֶּה! שְׁמִיעָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא זֶה – שְׁמִיעָה מַמָּשׁ; שְׁמִיעָה הָאַחֲרוֹנָה – קַבָּלָה:


**For who stood in the council of the Lord** and will hear what is decreed by Him? He who hearkened to the word of His Torah and listened to His commandments, but not these wicked men. The first expression of ֹשְמִיעָה in the verse is actual hearing; the latter expression of ֹשְמִיעָה is an expression of acceptance.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:19:1)


**מִתְחוֹלֵל.** מִשְׁתַּכֵּן, מְחַזֵּר לִשְׁכּוֹן; וְ**עַל רֹאשׁ רְשָׁעִים** יִשְׁכּוֹן:


**a whirling** A resting storm, a storm which seeks to rest, and it shall rest on the heads of the wicked.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:20:1)


**מְזִמּוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן מַחֲשָׁבָה:


**the plans of** an expression of thought.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:22:1)


**וְאִם עָמְדוּ בְּסוֹדִי וְיַשְׁמִיעוּ דְבָרַי וגו׳.** הָיָה לָהֶם לְהַשְׁמִיעַ דְּבָרַי וְתוֹרָתִי **אֶת עַמִּי**, וְלֹא לְהַטְעוֹתָם בָּהּ מֵעָלַי:


**And if they stood in My council, they should have let My people hear My word, etc.** They should have let My people hear My words and My Torah, rather than cause them to stray away from Me.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:23:1)


**הַאֱלֹהֵי מִקָּרֹב.** שֶׁאֵינִי רוֹאֶה אֶלָּא בְּסָמוּךְ לִי, וְאֵין בִּי כֹּחַ לִשְׁפּוֹט אֶת הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים שֶׁהֵם רְחוֹקִים מִמֶּנִּי, וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם?!


**Am I a God from near** that I do not see but what is close to Me and have I no power to judge the lower creatures that are far from Me and do I not know of their deeds?



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:24:1)


**אִם יִסָּתֵר אִישׁ.** זֶהוּ כֶּפֶל תְּמִיהָה **הַאֱלֹהֵי מִקָּרֹב**, כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל תְּמִיהוֹת הַכְּפוּלוֹת:


**Can a man hide in secret places** This is a repetition of the question, ‘Am I a God from near,’ according to the manner of all double questions.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:26:1)


**עַד מָתַי.** יִנָּבְאוּ שֶׁקֶר? וְכִי יֵשׁ בְּלִבָּם שֶׁל הַנְּבִיאִים הַלָּלוּ, הַנִּבְּאִים **שָׁקֶר**, לְקַיֵּם מַחְשְׁבוֹתָם שֶׁהֵם חוֹשְׁבִים **לְהַשְׁכִּיחַ אֶת עַמִּי שְׁמִי וְגוֹ׳**?!


**How long [will this be]?** That they will prophesy falsely? Do these prophets who prophesy falsely have in their heart to execute their thoughts that they are thinking to cause My people to forget My name, etc.?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:26:2)


**תַּרְמִית.** לְשׁוֹן מִרְמָה:


**deceit** an expression of מִרְמָה, deceit.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:1)


**יְסַפֵּר חֲלוֹם.** יְסַפְּרֶנּוּ לְשׁוֹן הֲבַאי כִּשְׁאָר חֲלוֹמוֹת, וְלֹא יְחַזְּקֶנּוּ בְּדִבְרֵי נְבוּאָה:


**let him tell a dream** Let him tell it as vain talk like other dreams, and let him not presume it as words of prophecy.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:2)


**מַה לַתֶּבֶן אֶת הַבָּר.** מַה עִנְיַן שֶׁקֶר אֵצֶל אֱמֶת?


**What has the straw to do with the wheat?** What has falsehood to do with truth?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:28:3)


**אֶת הַבָּר.** עִם הַבָּר:


**with the wheat** like עִם-הַבָּר.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:29:1)


**הֲלֹא כֹה דְבָרִי כָּאֵשׁ.** לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם לִטְעוֹת וּלְדַמּוֹת חֲלוֹמוֹת לוֹמַר שֶׁהֵם נְבוּאָה, כִּי דְּבַר נְבוּאָה כְּשֶׁבָּאָה בְּפִי הַנָּבִיא בִּגְבוּרָה הִיא בָאָה בּוֹ כְּאֵשׁ בּוֹעֶרֶת, כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַתְּהִי בְלִבִּי כְּאֵשׁ בֹּעֶרֶת״ (ירמיה כ:ט), וְאוֹמֵר ״וְיַד ה׳ עָלַי חֲזָקָה״ (יחזקאל ג:יד):


**Is not My word so like fire?** They should not have erred to compare their dreams to say that they are prophecy, for the prophetic word, when it comes to a prophet, it comes into him with force like a burning fire, as the matter is stated (supra 20:9): “And it was in my heart like a burning fire,” and (Ezekiel 3:14): “And the hand of the Lord became strong upon me.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:29:2)


**וּכְפַטִּישׁ.** פִּי״ק בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיֵשׁ אוֹמֵר מַרְטִיי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and as a hammer** (pic in French), a pick. And some say (marteau in French), a hammer.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:30:1)


**מְגַנְּבֵי דְבָרַי אִישׁ מֵאֵת רֵעֵהוּ.** אִישׁ מֵאֵת רֵעֵהוּ יֵשׁ לָהֶם מְרַגְּלִים אֵצֶל נְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת, וְשׁוֹמְעִין סִגְנוֹן שֶׁלָּהֶם שֶׁהֵם מִתְנַבְּאִים בּוֹ, וְאוֹמְרִין נְבוּאַת שֶׁקֶר בְּאוֹתוֹ לָשׁוֹן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן עַזּוּר: שָׁמַע אֶת יִרְמְיָה מִתְנַבֵּא בַּשּׁוּק הָעֶלְיוֹן ״הִנְנִי שׁוֹבֵר אֶת קֶשֶׁת עֵילָם״ (ירמיה מט:לה), וְהוּא נִיבָּא בַּשּׁוּק הַתַּחְתּוֹן ״שָׁבַרְתִּי אֶת עוֹל מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל״ (ירמיה כח:ב), כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (פט.):


**those who steal My wards from one another** They have spies who spy upon the true prophets, who listen to their expression with which they prophesy and say false prophecies with that same expression, as Hananiah son of Azzur did. He heard Jeremiah prophesying in the upper marketplace, “Behold I break the bow of Elam,” and he prophesied in the lower marketplace, “I have broken the yoke of the king of Babylon” (28:2), as our Rabbis wrote in Sanhedrin 89a.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:31:1)


**הַלּוֹקְחִים לְשׁוֹנָם.** הַמְּלַמְּדִים לְשׁוֹנָם לוֹמַר שִׁקְרֵיהֶם בְּלָשׁוֹן **נְאֻם ה׳**, כִּנְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת, לְמַעַן יַאֲמִינוּ בָם:


**those who train their tongues** who teach their tongues to say their falsehoods with the expression, “Says the Lord,” like the true prophets, so that they should believe them.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:32:1)


**וּבְפַחֲזוּתָם.** לְשׁוֹן תִּמָּהוֹן, אִישְׁטוֹרְדִיזוֹ״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with their bewilderment** an expression of wonder (estordisoun in O. F.).



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:1)


**מַה מַשָּׂא ה׳.** לְשׁוֹן שְׂחוֹק, לְפִי שֶׁנְּבוּאָתוֹ לָהֶם לְמַשָּׂא:


**“What is the burden of the Lord?”** This is an expression of derision, for his prophecy is a burden to them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:2)


**וְאָמַרְתָּ אֲלֵיהֶם אֶת מַה מַשָּׂא וְנָטַשְׁתִּי וגו׳.** אוֹמְרִים לָכֶם מָה הַמַּשָּׂא, אַתֶּם לְמַשָּׂא לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וְהוּא אָמַר שֶׁיִּטּוֹשׁ וְיַשְׁלִיךְ אֶתְכֶם מֵעַל זְרוֹעוֹתָיו מִשָּׁמַיִם אֶרֶץ (איכה ב:א), כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**you shall say to them, “What burden? And I will forsake you, etc.”** They say to you (sic), “What is the burden?” You are the burden of the Holy One, blessed be He, and He says that He will forsake you and cast you off His arms from heaven to earth. So I heard.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:33:3)


**וְנָטַשְׁתִּי.** וְעָזַבְתִּי:


**and I will forsake** Heb. וְעָזַבְתִּי.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:36:1)


**כִּי הַמַּשָּׂא יִהְיֶה לְאִישׁ דְּבָרוֹ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאַתֶּם מְהַפְּכִין אֶת הַמַּשָּׂא לִגְנַאי, מַשָּׂא דְּבַר ה׳ לְאִישׁ דְּבָרוֹ וּלְאִישׁ עֲצָתוֹ הוּא מְגַלֵּהוּ (ישעיהו מ:יג), וְאַתֶּם מְהַפְּכִין אוֹתוֹ לִלְשׁוֹן מַשָּׂאוֹי. זוֹ לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי כֵּן, וְהִיא עִקָּר; אֲבָל כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי: כִּי הַמַּשָּׂאוֹי יִהְיֶה מַשָּׂאוֹי שֶׁל פֻּרְעָנוּת לְמִי שֶׁאוֹמְרוֹ, הַשּׁוֹאֵל **מַה מַּשָּׂא**. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: **מַשָּׂא ה׳**. לְשׁוֹן נְבוּאָה הוּא; וְהָיָה הַנָּבִיא הַשֶּׁקֶר שׁוֹאֵל לַנָּבִיא הָאֱמֶת לֵאמֹר לוֹ אֶת דְּבַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּלָשׁוֹן שֶׁבָּא הַסִּיגְנוֹן אֵלָיו, וְהַסִּיגְנוֹן לֹא הָיָה מְפָרֵשׁ הַדִּבּוּר כָּל כָּךְ, וְהָיָה נְבִיא הַשֶּׁקֶר מְהַפֵּךְ אֶת הַפֵּרוּשׁ לְצַד אַחֵר, וְהָיָה מְדַבֵּר בְּדוּגְמַת נְבִיא הָאֱמֶת כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּאֲמִינוּ בּוֹ; וְכֵן עָשָׂה חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן עַזּוּר. לָכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר: אַל תִּשְׁאֲלוּ **מַשָּׂא ה׳** – אֶת לְשׁוֹן הַסִּיגְנוֹן, אֶלָּא **מַה דִּבֵּר** וּמַה עָנָה – אֶת פֵּרוּשׁ הַדָּבָר; שַׁאֲלוּ לַנָּבִיא הָאֱמֶת לֵאמֹר לָהֶם מַה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר וּלְהֵיכָן נוֹטִין אֶת דְּבָרָיו:


**For the burden shall be to the man of His word** Although you pervert the massa into a pejorative, the prophecy (משא) of the word of the Lord shall be to the man of His word, and to the man of His counsel He reveals it, but you pervert it to an expression of a burden. This interpretation I did not hear, but it is the main interpretation. But this is how I heard it, namely that the burden shall be a burden of retribution for the one who says it, who asks, “What is the burden?” Another interpretation: משא is an expression of prophecy, and the false prophet would ask the true prophet to tell him the word of the Holy One, blessed be He, in the language in which the expression came to him, and the expression did not explain the speech adequately. Therefore, the false prophet would pervert it to another meaning and would speak after the fashion of the true prophet in order that they should believe him. And so did Hananiah son of Azzur do. Therefore, he says, “Do not ask the prophecy of the Lord,” the language of the expression, but “What did He speak?” or “What did He reply?” The explanation of the words you shall ask the true prophet to tell you what the Holy One, blessed be He, says, and to what His words are inclined.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:36:2)


**כִּי הַמַּשָּׂא יִהְיֶה לְאִישׁ דְּבָרוֹ.** סִיגְנוֹן סְתָם; הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲמָרוֹ סְתָם לְאִישׁ עֲצָתוֹ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁוּד לְהוֹפְכוֹ, וְאַתֶּם שׁוֹאֲלִים אוֹתוֹ כְּדֵי לְהוֹפְכוֹ. **וַהֲפַכְתֶּם אֶת דִּבְרֵי** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה **מִגַּנְּבֵי דְבָרַי אִישׁ מֵאֵת רֵעֵהוּ**, גַּם זֶה לָשׁוֹן מְיֻתָּר:


**for the prophecy shall be for the man of His word** The unexplained expression the Holy One, blessed be He, said unexplained to the man of His counsel, who is not suspected of perverting it, but you ask it in order to pervert it, and you pervert the words of the Holy One, blessed be He, and that is what is stated above (v. 30): “those who steal My words from one another.” This too is a correct explanation.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:37:1)


**כֹּה תֹאמַר אֶל הַנָּבִיא.** כְּשֶׁאַתֶּם בָּאִים לִשְׁאֹל דְּבָרַי מֵאֵת הַנָּבִיא:


**So shall you say to the prophet** when you come to ask My words of the prophet.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 23:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/23:39:1)


**וְנָשִׁיתִי.** לְשׁוֹן ״גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה״ (בראשית לב:לג), וְלָשׁוֹן ״נָשְׁתָה גְבוּרָתָם״ (ירמיה נא:ל); קוֹפֵץ וּמְטַלְטֵל מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם:


**and I will exile** an expression of “the sinew that was dislocated (גִיד הַנָֹּשֶה)” (Gen. 32:33), and an expression of “their might has failed (נָֹשְתָה),” it jumps and moves from place to place.



## Chapter 24


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:1)


**דּוּדָאֵי.** קְדֵרוֹת:


**pots** Heb. דּוּדָאֵי.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:2)


**מוּעָדִים.** מוּכָנִים:


**prepared** Heb. מוּעדים.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:1:3)


**הֶחָרָשׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר.** תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים שֶׁגָּלוּ עִם יְכָנְיָה (סדר עולם רבה כה), וּכְבָר פֵּירַשְׁתִּיו בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים (מלכים ב כד:טז) הֶחָרָשׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר לימייטראה ליפורטי״ר בְּלַעַז. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **הֶחָרָשׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר** – הֶחָרָשׁ לְשׁוֹן חֶרֶשׁ, הַמַּסְגֵּר לְשׁוֹן סָגוּר, כְּלוֹמַר: חֲכָמִים גְּדוֹלִים בַּתּוֹרָה, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהָיָה אֶחָד מֵהֶם מְדַבֵּר, הַכֹּל שׁוֹתְקִים. **מַסְגֵּר** שֶׁהָיוּ סוֹגְרִים וְאֵין פּוֹתֵחַ (ישעיה כב:כב; סנהדרין לח.):


**the craftsmen and the sentries of the gates** Torah scholars who were exiled with Jeconiah, and I already explained in the Book of Kings (II 24:14) הֶחָרָש וְהַמַּסְגֵּר (les maitres, les portiers in French) the masters, the sentries of the gates. Another explanation: הֶחָרָש וְהַמַּסְגֵּר: הֶחָרָש is an expression of silence, וְהַמַּסְגֵּר is an expression of closing. That is to say, great sages in Torah, to the extent that when one of them would speak, everyone would remain silent. מַסְגֵּר means that when they would close the discussion, no one would reopen it.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:2:1)


**כִּתְאֵנֵי הַבַּכֻּרוֹת.** שֶׁבִּשְּׁלוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן בִּתְחִלַּת בִּכּוּרֵי תְאֵנִים, שֶׁהֵם חֲבִיבוֹת עַל מוֹצְאֵיהֶם בְּרֵאשִׁיתָם (ישעיה כח:ד):


**like the first ripe figs** that have ripened completely in the beginning of the ripening of the figs which are dear to all those who find them in their beginning.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:5:1)


**כֵּן אַכִּיר אֶת גָּלוּת וגו׳.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס: **כֵּן אַכִּיר לְטוֹבָה אֶת גָּלוּת יְהוּדָה** שֶׁהִגְלֵיתִי כְּבָר, יְכָנְיָה וְגָלוּתוֹ:


**so will I recognize the exile of, etc.** This is an inverted verse: So will I recognize for good the exile of Judah which I have already exiled, viz., Jeconiah and his exile.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:6:1)


**אֶתּוֹשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן עֲקִירָה:


**and not pluck them** an expression of uprooting.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:9:1)


**לְזַעֲוָה לְרָעָה לְכָל מַמְלְכוֹת הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ הָרָעָה הַבָּאָה עֲלֵיהֶם וְיָזוּעוּ:


**for a horror [and] for evil for all the kingdoms of the earth** For they will hear the evil that came upon them and quake.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 24:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/24:9:2)


**וְלִשְׁנִינָה.** שֶׁיִּשְׁנְנוּ וִידַבְּרוּ הַכֹּל בְּפֻרְעָנוּתָם:


**and for a conversation piece** That everyone will repeat and discuss their retribution.



## Chapter 25


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:1:1)


**בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית.** שָׁנָה שֶׁנִּתְחַתֵּם גְּזַר דִּינָם שֶׁיִּגְלוּ וְשֶׁיִּשְׁתּוּ כּוֹס הַחֵמָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן: **קַח אֶת כּוֹס וְגוֹ׳**. וְקוֹדֶם גְּזַר דִּין אָמַר לַנָּבִיא לְהוֹכִיחָם, אוּלַי יָשׁוּבוּ וְלֹא יִתְחַתֵּם גְּזַר דִּינָם:


**in the fourth year** The year that their decree was sealed that they be exiled and that they drink the cup of wrath, as he says in this section (v. 15): “Take the cup, etc.,” and before the decree He commanded the prophet to reprove them, perhaps they would repent, and their decree would not be sealed.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:3:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים.** יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ מָלַךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאַחַת (מלכים ב כב:א), צֵא מֵהֶם שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, נִשְׁאֲרוּ תְּשַׁע עֶשְׂרֵה, וְאַרְבַּע שֶׁל בְּנוֹ (ירמיה א:ג), הֲרֵי **שָׁלֹשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים**:


**twenty-three years** Josiah reigned thirty-one years (II Kings 22:1). Subtract twelve years from them (Jer. 1:2). This leaves nineteen, and four of his son amount to twenty-three.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:3:2)


**אַשְׁכִּים וְדַבֵּר.** לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה: הָיִיתִי מַשְׁכִּים וּמְדַבֵּר תָּמִיד:


**arising early** This is a present tense. I would arise early and speak constantly.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:10:1)


**קוֹל רֵחַיִם.** סִימָן הוּא לִסְעוּדַת בְּרִית מִילָה, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁטּוֹחֲנִין וְשׁוֹחֲקִין לָהּ סַמְמָנִין לִרְפוּאָה:


**the sound of a mill** That is an indication of a circumcision feast, since they would grind and crush ingredients for healing the wound.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:10:2)


**אוֹר נֵר.** סִימַן מִשְׁתֶּה. כָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין לב:):


**the light of a candle** An indication of a feast. So we learned in Sanhedrin (32b).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:13:1)


**אֶת כָּל הַכָּתוּב.** עַל בָּבֶל:


**all that is written** concerning Babylon.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:13:2)


**בַּסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה.** בְּסוֹפוֹ:


**in this Book** at its end.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:14:1)


**כִּי עָבְדוּ בָם.** כְּמוֹ כִּי יַעַבְדוּ בָם, בִּבְנֵי בָבֶל:


**For... will enslave them** Like ‘For... shall enslave them,’ i.e., shall enslave the people of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:14:2)


**גַּם הֵמָּה גּוֹיִם רַבִּים.** מָדַי וּפָרָס:


**many nations...too** Viz. Media and Persia.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:15:1)


**כּוֹס הַיַּיִן הַחֵמָה.** נְבוּאַת הַפֻּרְעָנוּת:


**cup of the wine of fury** The prophecy of retribution


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:15:2)


**וְהִשְׁקִיתָה אוֹתוֹ.** הִנָּבֵא לָהֶם שֶׁלֹּא תָשׁוּב עוֹד בְּשׁוּם תְּשׁוּבָה:


**and you shall give...to drink it** Prophesy to them that it will no longer be retracted for any repentance.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:16:1)


**וְהִתְגֹּעֲשׁוּ.** ״וְיִשְׁתַּגְּעוּן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and reel to and fro** Jonathan renders: and they shall be confused.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:16:2)


**וְהִתְהוֹלָלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״הוֹלֵלוּת״ (קהלת י:יג), יִשְׁתַּטּוּ:


**and be like madmen** an expression of madness, they shall be mad.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:18:1)


**כַּיּוֹם הַזֶּה.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הֵם הַיּוֹם; לְאַחַר הַחֻרְבָּן כָּתַב יִרְמְיָה סִפְרוֹ:


**as of this day** As they are today. Jeremiah wrote his Book after the destruction.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:20:1)


**וְאֵת כָּל הָעֶרֶב.** ״כָּל סוּמְכְוָתָא״ (תרגום יונתן) אֲשֶׁר עֲרֻבַּת פַּרְעֹה וּמִשְׁעַנְתּוֹ עֲלֵיהֶם לְעֶזְרָה. **הָעֶרֶב** גרנטיא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And all the allies** All the supports (סוּמְכְוָתָא) which are Pharaoh’s guarantee, and he leans upon them for aid. **the allies** Garantie in French.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:22:1)


**וְאֵת מַלְכֵי הָאִי.** כָּל אֵלּוּ שֶׁמָּנָה עַד כָּאן, שְׁכֵינֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵם:


**and the kings of the isle** All these that he counts (ֹשֶמוֹנֶה) until here are neighbors of Eretz Israel.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:23:1)


**קְצוּצֵי פֵאָה.** הַמּוּקְצִים בִּקְצֵה אוֹתָהּ הָרוּחַ:


**and all those separated in that corner** those separated at the end of that corner.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:24:1)


**מַלְכֵי עֲרָב.** עֲרַבְיָא (כתובות סז.):


**the kings of Arabia** Heb. עֲרָב, Arabia.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:24:2)


**וְאֵת כָּל מַלְכֵי הָעֶרֶב.** ״סוּמְכְוָותָא״ שֶׁל עַרְבִיִּים:


**and all the kings of the allies** The allies of the Arabs.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:26:1)


**שֵׁשַׁךְ.** הוּא בָּבֶל בְּא״ת ב״ש (תנחומא, קרח יב):


**Sheshach** That is ‘Babylon’ in א"תב"ש.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:27:1)


**וּקְיוּ.** כְּאָדָם שֶׁשּׁוֹתֶה יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי, שֶׁמֵּקִיא:


**and vomit** Like a person who drinks too much, who vomits.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:27:2)


**הַחֶרֶב.** הַתִּגָּר שֶׁל הַמִּלְחָמָה:


**the sword** the strife of war.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:29:1)


**מֵחֵל**. מַתְחִיל:


**commence** מֵחֵל.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:1)


**עַל נָוֵהוּ.** מִתְאַבֵּל עַל חֻרְבַּן בֵּיתוֹ:


**over His habitation** He mourns over the destruction of His house.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:2)


**הֵידָד.** לָשׁוֹן הוּא שֶׁצּוֹעֲקִין עוֹשֵׂי מְלָאכָה כְּבֵדָה בְּקוֹל רָם, לְזָרֵז זֶה אֶת זֶה לְהִתְחַזֵּק, וְדוֹרְכֵי גִּתּוֹת רְגִילִין בָּהּ:


**“Hedad!”** A cry of “Ha!” that those engaged in hard work cry out aloud to urge each other on to strengthen themselves, and those who tread the winepresses are accustomed to it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:30:3)


**יַעֲנֶה.** כָּל לְשׁוֹן עֲנִיָּה צְעָקַת קוֹל רָם הִיא:


**He shall call out** Every expression of עֲנִיָה, is an expression of a cry with a loud voice.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:31:1)


**נִשְׁפָּט.** מִתְוַכֵּחַ בַּדִּין:


**He contends** He contends in judgment.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:32:1)


**מִגּוֹי אֶל גּוֹי.** שֶׁאֲנִי מְסַכְסְכָן זֶה בָּזֶה:


**from nation to nation** whom I incite against one another.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:32:2)


**מִיַּרְכְּתֵי.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״מִסְּיָפֵי״:


**from the ends** as the Targum renders it: מִסְיָפֵי, from the ends of.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:1)


**הֵילִילוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״יְלָלָה״ (צפניה א:י):


**Howl** an expression of יְלָלָה, howling.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:2)


**וְהִתְפַּלְּשׁוּ.** הִתְגַּלְגְּלוּ בֶּעָפָר, וֹוטְרְלִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and roll** i.e., roll in the dust (se vautrer in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:3)


**וּתְפוּצוֹתֵיכֶם.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא קָצָר, כְּלוֹמַר: וּתְפוּצוֹתֵיכֶם תִּקְרַבְנָה לָבֹא:


**and your scatterings** This is a short verse; i.e., to say: and your scatterings shall come near.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:34:4)


**כִּכְלִי חֶמְדָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר: אִם תֹּאמְרוּ חֲשׁוּבִים אֲנַחְנוּ, הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ כְּלֵי חֶמְדָּה שֶׁל זְכוּכִית, נוֹפְלִים וּמִשְׁתַּבְּרִים וְאוֹבְדִים:


**like a precious vessel** That is to say: If you say, “We are important,” there are many precious glass vessels that fall, break, and are lost.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:37:1)


**וְנָדַמּוּ.** לְשׁוֹן חֻרְבָּה, לְשׁוֹן ״הֲדִמָּנוּ״, ״וְנִדְּמָה שָׁם״ (ירמיה ח:יד):


**shall be destroyed** an expression of destruction, an expression related to ‘has silenced us (הֲדִמָּנוּ) .’ ‘and there let us sit in silence (וְנִדְּמָה).’



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:38:1)


**עָזַב כַּכְּפִיר וְגוֹ׳.** ״גָּלָה מַלְכָּא מִכַּרְכֵּיהּ״ (תרגום יונתן), כַּכְּפִיר הָעוֹזֵב אֶת **סֻכּוֹ**:


**He has left his hut like a young lion** The king has gone in exile from his city like a young lion that leaves his hut.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 25:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/25:38:2)


**הַיּוֹנָה.** מִשְׁתַּכֶּרֶת בְּיַיִן, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן אוֹנָאָה:


**because of the wrath of the oppressing sword** drunk from wine. So did Jonathan translate. Another explanation: It is an expression of oppression (אוֹנָאָה).



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:1:1)


**בְּרֵאשִׁית מַמְלְכוּת.** פָּרָשָׁה זוֹ תַּחְתּוֹנָה קָדְמָה לָעֶלְיוֹנָה אַרְבַּע שָׁנִים (ירמיה כה:א):


**In the beginning of the reign** This chapter below preceded the above chapter by four years.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:10:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמְעוּ שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה.** שֶׁתָּפְשׂוּ נְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר וְ**הַכֹּהֲנִים** אֶת יִרְמְיָה:


**And the princes of Judah heard** that the false prophets and the priests had seized Jeremiah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:10:2)


**בְּפֶתַח שַׁעַר ה׳ הֶחָדָשׁ.** אֲחֵרִים הִגִּיהוּ: כְּשֶׁגָּלָה יְהוֹיָכִין וְקְצָת כְּלֵי בֵּית אֱלֹהֵינוּ עִמּוֹ, שָׁבְרוּ חֵילוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שַׁעַר הַמִּזְרָחִי, וְחָזַר צִדְקִיָּהוּ וְתִקְּנוֹ וְחִדְּשׁוֹ; לְכָךְ נִקְרָא חָדָשׁ:


**at the entrance of the new gate of the Lord** Others annotated: When Jehoiachin went in exile and some of the vessels of the house of our God went with him, Nebuchadnezzar’s army breached the eastern gate, and Zedekiah subsequently repaired it and renewed it. It is therefore called ‘new.’



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:18:1)


**מִיכָה הַמּוֹרַשְׁתִּי.** ״דְּמִן מָרֵישָׁא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Micah the Morashtite** Who was from Maresha.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:18:2)


**עִיִּים.** גַּלִּים:


**heaps** עִיִּים, heaps.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:19:1)


**וַיְחַל.** לְשׁוֹן תְּפִלָּה:


**and he entreated** Heb. וַיְחַל, an expression of prayer.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:19:2)


**וַאֲנַחְנוּ עֹשִׂים רָעָה גְדוֹלָה.** אִם נַהֲרֹג אוֹתוֹ:


**but we are doing great harm to ourselves** if we kill him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/26:20:1)


**וְגַם אִישׁ הָיָה מִתְנַבֵּא.** מִי שֶׁאָמַר זוֹ לֹא אָמַר זוֹ; עַד כָּאן דִּבְרֵי צַדִּיקִים, וְעָמְדוּ רְשָׁעִים שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם וְאָמְרוּ: גַּם אִישׁ מִתְנַבֵּא וְגוֹ׳, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאוּרִיָּה נֶהֱרַג, יֵהָרֵג יִרְמְיָה; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי (ספרי במדבר פיסקא פח):


**Also a man was prophesying** The one who said this did not say this. I.e., those who related the incident of Micah did not relate the incident concerning Uriah. Up to here are the words of the righteous. Now the wicked who were there arose and said, “Also a man was prophesying, etc.” Just as Uriah was killed, so shall Jeremiah be killed. In this way it is explained in Sifrei (Num. 11:7).



## Chapter 27


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:1:1)


**בְּרֵאשִׁית מַמְלֶכֶת יְהוֹיָקִם.** שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי שֶׁמָּלַךְ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (ירמיה כה:א), נִתְנַבֵּא עָלָיו שֶׁיִּמְלֹךְ:


**In the beginning of the reign of Jehoiakim** Three years prior to Nebuchadnezzar’s ascent to the throne, he prophesied about him that he would reign.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:2:1)


**מוֹסֵרוֹת.** הֵם רְצוּעוֹת הָעוֹל, שֶׁקּוֹרִין קוֹיוּנְגְמְ״שׁ:


**thongs** They are the thongs of the yoke that are called kongoijli (cojongles).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:3:1)


**וְשִׁלַּחְתָּם אֶל מֶלֶךְ אֱדוֹם.** בִּימֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית לְמָלְכוֹ (ירמיה כח:א); שֶׁעָשָׂהוּ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שַׁלִּיט עַל חֲמִשָּׁה מְלָכִים:


**And you shall send them to the king of Edom** In the days of Zedekiah in the fourth year of his reign, when Nebuchadnezzar made him ruler over five kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:3:2)


**מֶלֶךְ אֱדוֹם וּמֶלֶךְ מוֹאָב וּמֶלֶךְ בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן וְגוֹ׳.** אֵלּוּ חֲמִשָּׁה מְלָכִים נָתַן נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר תַּחַת יָדוֹ שֶׁל צִדְקִיָּהוּ (איכה רבה ב, יד). חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה הָיוּ עַל צַוָּארוֹ, מִשָּׁנָה אַחַת לִיהוֹיָקִים עַד הַשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ, שֶׁשְּׁבָרָם הֵימֶנּוּ חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן עַזּוּר (ירמיה כח:י):


**the king of Edom, the king of Moab, the king of the children of Ammon, etc.** These five kings Nebuchadnezzar placed under the authority of Zedekiah. Fifteen years they (the bars and thongs) were on his neck, from the first year of Jehoiakim until the fourth year of Zedekiah, when Hananiah son of Azzur broke them off him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:6:1)


**עַבְדִּי.** שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה רְצוֹנִי לִפָּרַע מִשּׂוֹנְאַי:


**My servant** Who will perform My will to requite My enemies.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:1)


**וְאֶת בְּנוֹ.** אֱוִיל מְרֹדַךְ:


**and his son** Evil-Merodach.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:2)


**וְאֶת בֶּן בְּנוֹ.** בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר:


**and his son’s son** Belshazzar.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:7:3)


**עַד בֹּא עֵת אַרְצוֹ.** שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הַמָּדִי וְכוֹרֶשׁ וְיַהַרְגוּ אֶת בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר:


**until the time of his own land come** When Darius the Mede and Cyrus come and slay Belshazzar.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:12:1)


**וְאֶל צִדְקִיָּהוּ מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה דִּבַּרְתִּי וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל זֶה אָמַר יִרְמְיָהוּ אֶל שְׁלוּחֵי הַמְּלָכִים:


**And to Zedekiah the king of Judah have I spoken, etc.** All this Jeremiah said to the ambassadors of the kings.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:16:1)


**הִנֵּה כְלֵי בֵית ה׳.** שֶׁגָּלוּ עִם יְכָנְיָה (להלן כ):


**“Behold the vessels of the house of the Lord”** That were exiled with Jeconiah.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:19:1)


**הַמְּכוֹנוֹת.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בְּסִיסַיָּא״, כְּמוֹ ״אֶת הַכִּיּוֹר וְאֶת כַּנּוֹ״ (שמות לה:טז):


**the bases** Jonathan renders: בְּסִיסַיָּא Cf. “the washbasin and its stand” (Exodus 35:16).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:20:1)


**בַּגְלוֹתוֹ.** כְּמוֹ בְּהַגְלוֹתוֹ:


**when he exiled** Heb. בַּגְּלוֹתוֹ like בְּהַגְלוֹתוֹ.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:20:2)


**חֹרֵי.** כְּמוֹ ״שָׂרֵי״ (ירמיה כד:א):


**the nobles of** חֹרֵי, like שָֹרֵי the princes of.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 27:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/27:22:1)


**עַד יוֹם פָּקְדִי אוֹתָם.** בִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפוֹרָשׁ בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (עזרא א:ז-יא):


**until the day I remember them** In the days of Cyrus, as is explained in the Book of Ezra (1:7 11).



## Chapter 28


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:1)


**וַיְהִי בַּשָּׁנָה הַהִיא.** כְּשֶׁהִגִּיעַ אוֹתוֹ הַזְּמַן שֶׁהִשְׁלִיט נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ עַל חֲמֵשֶׁת מְלָכִים, שֶׁהָיָה יִרְמְיָה אוֹמֵר שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ וּמְשַׁלֵּחַ הַמּוֹסֵרוֹת וְהַמּוֹטוֹת אֶל הַמְּלָכִים בְּיַד מַלְאָכֵיהֶם (ירמיה כז:ב-ג):


**And it came to pass in that year** When that time arrived that Nebuchadnezzar gave Zedekiah dominion over the five kings to whom Jeremiah stated his message and sent the thongs and the bars to the kings by the hand of their ambassadors.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:2)


**בְּרֵאשִׁית מַמְלֶכֶת צִדְקִיָּה.** בִּתְחִלָּה שֶׁהִשְׁלִיטוֹ עַל חֲמֵשֶׁת הַמְּלָכִים, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר בִּתְחִלַּת מַלְכוּתוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר **בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית**. אוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה יָרַד לְהַקְבִּיל אֶת פְּנֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּבָבֶל, וְשָׂרָיָה שַׂר הַמְּנוּחָה עִמּוֹ, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (ירמיה נא:נט), וְהִשְׁלִיטוֹ עַל הַמְּלָכִים:


**in the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah** In the beginning of his dominion over the five kings. But it is impossible to say that it means at the beginning of his reign, since it says, “in the fourth year.” In that year, he went down to greet Nebuchadnezzar in Babylon, and Seraiah the receptionist was with him, as is stated at the end of the Book (51:59), and he gave him dominion over the kings.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:1:3)


**אָמַר אֵלַי וגו׳.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סדר עולם רבה כה): מִי הִטְעָה אֶת חֲנַנְיָה? שָׁמַע שֶׁהָיָה יִרְמְיָה מִתְנַבֵּא בַּשּׁוּק הָעֶלְיוֹן: ״הִנְנִי שֹׁבֵר אֶת קֶשֶׁת עֵילָם״ (ירמיה מט:לה), וּנְבוּאָה זוֹ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר נֶאֶמְרָה, **בְּרֵאשִׁית מַמְלֶכֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ שָׁם (ירמיה מט:לד); וּלְפִי שֶׁעֵילָם סְמוּכָה לְבָבֶל, עָמַד הוּא בַּשּׁוּק הַתַּחְתּוֹן וְדָרַשׁ וְדָן קַל וָחֹמֶר: וּמָה עֵילָם, שֶׁלֹּא בָּאָה אֶלָּא לַעֲזֹר אֶת הַכַּשְׂדִּים וְכוּ׳; כְּמָה שֶׁהִיא בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (פט.):


**said to me** Our Sages state: Who misled Hananiah? He heard Jeremiah prophesying in the upper street, “Behold I am breaking the bow of Elam” (49:35). And this prophecy was said in the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah, as is explained there, and since Elam is adjacent to Babylon, he stood in the lower street, and expounded. And he derived a major case from a minor one. If Elam, who came only to aid the Chaldeans, the Holy One, blessed be He, said, “Behold I am breaking the bow of Elam,” how much more is this so with the Chaldeans themselves? He came into the lower street and said: So said the Lord: I have broken the yoke of the king of Babylon, as it is in Sanhedrin (89a).



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:6:1)


**יַעֲשֶׂה.** פַאצָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**may...do** Fasse in French.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:6:2)


**יקם.** לְשׁוֹן תְּפִלָּה (של״ש שטי״ן אלו ע״ד והביא״ו לבית הכל״א לא סדר״ן הר״ב המחב״ר) (**אָמֵן כֵּן יַעֲשֶׂה וְגוֹ׳.** בַּעֲלֵי אַגָּדָה אוֹמְרִים: כָּךְ אָמַר לוֹ הַלְוַאי וְיִתְקַיְּמוּ דְּבָרֶיךָ אֲנִי מִשְׁתַּכֵּר וְאַתָּה נִפְסַד, אֲנִי כֹּהֵן וְאוֹכֵל קָדְשֵׁי שָׁמַיִם אִם יַעֲמֹד הַבַּיִת בְּבִנְיָנוֹ, וְאַתָּה גִּבְעוֹנִי כְּדָכָתִיב כָּאן) אֲשֶׁר מִגִּבְעוֹן, וְתִהְיֶה לִי עֶבֶד חוֹטֵב עֵצִים וְשׁוֹאֵב מַיִם (וּמוֹרֵה צֶדֶק שֶׁלֹּא פֵּרֵשׁ נָקִיט לֵהּ טַעַם רַבּוֹתֵינוּ, שֶׁלָּמְדוּ מִכָּאן: כָּל הַמַּחֲנִיף לָרָשָׁע סוֹף נוֹפֵל בְּיָדוֹ אוֹ בְּיַד בְּנוֹ אוֹ בְּיַד בֶּן בְּנוֹ, שֶׁכֵּן יִרְאִיָּיה בֶּן שֶׁלֶמְיָה בֶּן חֲנַנְיָה תָּפְשׂוּ לְבַסּוֹף וְהֵבִיאוּ לְבֵית הַכֶּלֶא):


**May...fulfill** An expression of prayer. (These three lines up to “and he brought him to the prison” were not arranged by the author.) **Amen! So may the Lord do, etc.**Amen! The scholars of Aggadah say: So did he say to him: If only your words were fulfilled! I gain and you lose. I am a priest, and I eat the food hallowed in the name of Heaven if the Temple remains standing. But you are a Gibeonite, as it is written [here], “who was from Gibeon.” And you will be a slave to me, chopping wood and drawing water (Pesikta Rabbathi 27:4, Yalkut Shimoni ad loc.). But Moreh Zedek who did not explain, adopted the reason of our Sages (Sotah 41b) who learned from here that whoever flatters a wicked man will ultimately fall into his hands or into the hands of his son or into the hands of his son’s son, for Irijah, the son of Shelemiah, the son of Hananiah seized him and brought him to prison.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:7:1)


**אַךְ שְׁמַע נָא וְגוֹ׳.** עַד **הַנָּבִיא אֲשֶׁר יִנָּבֵא לְשָׁלוֹם**, אָמַר: אֲנִי מִתְנַבֵּא פּוּרְעָנִיּוֹת; אִם לֹא יָבֹא, אֵינִי שַׁקְרָן, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא ״נִחָם עַל הָרָעָה״ (יואל ב:יג), אֲבָל **הַנָּבִיא אֲשֶׁר יִנָּבֵא לְשָׁלוֹם, בְּבֹא דְּבַר הַנָּבִיא יִוָּדַע וְגוֹ׳**, אֲבָל אִם לֹא יָבֹא דְּבָרוֹ – שַׁקְרָן הוּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״לֹא אִישׁ אֵל וִיכַזֵּב וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר כג:יט). כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, וירא יג):


**But, hearken now... The prophet who would prophesy for peace.** He said: I prophesy retribution. If it does not come, I am not a liar, for the Holy One, blessed be He, renounces the evil, but the prophet who prophesies for peace, etc., when the word of the prophet comes, etc., but if his word does not come, he is a liar, as it is said, “God is not a man that He should lie, etc.” (Num. 23:19). In this manner Rabbi Tanhuma (Vayera 13) expounded.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:14:1)


**אֶת חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה נָתַתִּי לוֹ.** שֶׁרָכַב עַל אֲרִי וְקָשַׁר תַּנִּין בְּרֹאשׁוֹ (שבת קמט:):


**the beasts of the field have I given to him** He rode on a lion and tied a serpent on its head.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:16:1)


**הַשָּׁנָה אַתָּה מֵת.** וּבַשָּׁנָה הַבָּאָה תִּקָּבֵר:


**this year you shall die** And next year you shall be buried.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/28:17:1)


**בַּשָּׁנָה הַהִיא בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי.** וַהֲלֹא אֵין חֹדֶשׁ שְׁבִיעִי מִן הַשָּׁנָה? אֶלָּא מֵת עֶרֶב רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה, וְצִוָּה לְבָנָיו שֶׁיִּקְבְּרוּהוּ לְאַחַר רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה וְלֹא קֹדֶם, וְיַעֲלִימוּ אֶת מִיתָתוֹ אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם, כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת נְבוּאַת יִרְמְיָה שֶׁקֶר (ירושלמי סנהדרין יא,ה):


**in that year, in the seventh month** Is it not so that the seventh month is not of the year? Rather, he died on the day preceding Rosh Hashanah and commanded his children to hurry him after Rosh Hashanah, but not before, and they should conceal his death on that day in order to make Jeremiah’s prophecy appear false.



## Chapter 29


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:2:1)


**וְהַגְּבִירָה.** אֵם הַמֶּלֶךְ (מלכים א טו:יג):


**and the queen mother** The mother of the king.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:8:1)


**יַשִּׁיאוּ.** לְשׁוֹן הֲסָתָה, כְּמוֹ ״הַנָּחָשׁ הִשִּׁיאַנִי״ (בראשית ג:יג):


**mislead** An expression of enticing. Cf: “The snake misled me (הִֹשִיאַנִי)” (Gen. 3:13).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:12:1)


**וּקְרָאתֶם אֹתִי וְהֲלַכְתֶּם.** בִּדְרָכַי; וַהֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא קָצָר:


**And you shall call Me and go** In My ways. This is an ellipsis.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:16:1)


**אֶל הַמֶּלֶךְ.** צִדְקִיָּהוּ, אֲשֶׁר לֹא גָּלָה:


**concerning the king** Zedekiah, who has not been exiled.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:17:1)


**כַּתְּאֵנִים הַשֹּׁעָרִים.** לְשׁוֹן ״שַׁעֲרוּרִיָּה״ (הושע ו:י):


**as the loathsome figs** Heb. הַֹשֹּעָרִים, an expression of, ֹשַעֲרוּרִיָּה, “a disgraceful thing” (Hos. 6:10).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:18:1)


**וְלִחֶרְפָּה.** שֶׁיְּחָרְפוּ אוֹתָם, דִּישְׁטְרוּוִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; וְכֵן כָּל חֶרְפָּה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא:


**and for a reproach** That they will reproach them, and so is every expression of ירפה in Scripture.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:22:1)


**אֲשֶׁר קָלָם.** לְשׁוֹן ״קָלוּי בָּאֵשׁ״ (ויקרא ב:יד):


**whom...burnt** an expression of “roasted with fire” (Lev. 2:14).



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:23:1)


**וַיְנַאֲפוּ אֶת נְשֵׁי רֵעֵיהֶם.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין צג.): כֹּה אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: שִׁכְבִי עִם חֲבֵרִי:


**and they committed adultery with the wives of their neighbors** As is explained in Sanhedrin (93a), “So said the Holy One, blessed be He, ‘Lie with my friend.’”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:23:2)


**וָעֵד.** לְשׁוֹן עֵדוּת:


**and a witness** an expression of testimony (עֵדוּת).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:24:1)


**הַנֶּחֱלָמִי.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**the Nehelamite** A place name.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:1)


**נְתָנְךָ כֹהֵן.** אֶל צְפַנְיָה כָּתַב כֵּן (ירמיה כא:א):


**made you a priest** He wrote this to Zephaniah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:2)


**פְּקִידִים.** מְמֻנִּים:


**officers** appointees.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:3)


**מַהְפֶּכֶת.** כִּיפָּה:


**the prison** כִּפָה


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:26:4)


**הַצִּינוֹק.** סַדָּן, צִיפְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the stocks** a tree trunk (ceps in French).



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:28:1)


**כִּי עַל כֵּן שָׁלַח.** כִּי יֵשׁ לְךָ לִגְעֹר בּוֹ עַל אֲשֶׁר **שָׁלַח אֵלֵינוּ בָּבֶל לֵאמֹר אֲרֻכָּה הִיא** גָּלוּתְכֶם שָׁם:


**Forasmuch as** For you should rebuke him since he sent to us to Babylon saying, “Your exile there is long.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 29:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/29:28:2)


**עַל כֵּן.** כְּמוֹ עַל אֲשֶׁר:


**Forasmuch as** like עַל-אֲֹשֶר because of which.



## Chapter 30


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:3:1)


**כִּי הִנֵּה יָמִים בָּאִים.** וְלֹא תֹאמַר לָמָּה אֶכְתְּבֶנּוּ כִּי יֹאבְדוּ כֻּלָּם וְאֵין קוֹרֵא בּוֹ וְאֵין זוֹכְרוֹ עוֹד כִּי הִנֵּה יָמִים בָּאִים וְגוֹ׳:


**For, behold, days are coming** And you shall not say, Why should I write it, since they all will perish, and no one will read it, neither will anyone remember it anymore, for, “behold, days are coming, etc.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:5:1)


**קוֹל חֲרָדָה שָׁמָעְנוּ.** יֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין עַל שְׁמוּעַת בָּבֶל שֶׁהָיוּ חֲרֵדִים מִמֶּנָּה אוֹתָם שֶׁגָּלוּ שָׁם, וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה עַל מִלְחֶמֶת גּוֹג וּמָגוֹג:


**A sound of quaking we have heard** Some interpret this as alluding to the tidings of Babylon, from which those exiled there quaked. But the Midrash Aggadah explains it as an allusion to the war of Gog and Magog.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:6:1)


**אִם יוֹלֵד זָכָר.** אִם דֶּרֶךְ לִזְכָרִים לָלֶדֶת שֶׁיֹּאחֲזוּם חֲבָלִים **כַּיּוֹלֵדָה**:


**whether a male gives birth** Whether it is customary for males to give birth, so that labor pains should seize them like a woman in confinement.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:6:2)


**חֲלָצָיו.** כְּמוֹ מָתְנָיו:


**his loins** like מָתְנָיו.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:7:1)


**הַיּוֹם הַהוּא.** יוֹם הֲרִיגַת בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר וּמַפֶּלֶת בָּבֶל, עִנְיָן אַחֵר יוֹם מַפֶּלֶת גּוֹג:


**that day** The day of the assassination of Belshazzar and the downfall of Babylon. Another explanation: the day of the downfall of Gog.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:8:1)


**אֶשְׁבֹּר עֻלּוֹ.** יָסוּר עוֹל הָאֻמּוֹת מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**I will break his yoke** The yoke of the heathens will be removed from Israel.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:10:1)


**וְאַתָּה אַל תִּירָא.** מִכָּל הָרָעוֹת אֲשֶׁר תִּרְאֶה בָּאָה עַל בָּבֶל אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה בְתוֹכָהּ:


**And you, fear not** Because of all the evils that you will see befalling Babylon in whose midst you are.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:10:2)


**וְשַׁאֲנַן.** כְּמוֹ **וְשָׁקַט**:


**and at ease** like וְֹשָקֵט.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:11:1)


**וְיִסַּרְתִּיךָ לַמִּשְׁפָּט.** בְּדִין חָסוּךְ:


**but I will chasten you in measure** according to the law of the Torah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:11:2)


**וְנַקֵּה לֹא אֲנַקֶּךָ.** לְשׁוֹן טִיאוּט, הַשְׁמֵד:


**and I will not completely destroy you** an expression of a sweeping destruction.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:12:1)


**אָנוּשׁ לְשִׁבְרֵךְ נַחְלָה.** שִׁבְרֵךְ אנפר״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Your injury is painful, your wound grievous** (Enfers in Old French).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:13:1)


**לְמָזוֹר.** רְפוּאָה, אֵין אָדָם סָבוּר שֶׁתְּהֵא לָךְ תְּשׁוּעָה:


**to be healed** cure. No one thinks that you will have salvation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:13:2)


**תְּעָלָה.** רְפוּאָה, לְשׁוֹן הוֹעִיל:


**healing** a cure, an expression of availing.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:14:1)


**מוּסַר אַכְזָרִי.** יִסּוּרֵי אַכְזָרִיּוּת. הַמּוּסַר אַכְזָרִי הוּא:


**cruel chastisement** chastisements of cruelty. The chastisement is cruel.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:16:1)


**לָכֵן.** שְׁבוּעָה:


**Therefore** This is an expression denoting an oath.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:16:2)


**שֹׁאסַיִךְ לִמְשִׁסָּה.** לְשׁוֹן בִּיזָּה, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וִיהוֹן עָדַיִךְ לַעֲדִי״:


**those who plunder you** an expression of spoil, and so did Jonathan render it: וִיהוֹן עָדַיִךְ לַעֲדִי.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:17:1

**אֲרֻכָה.** רְפוּאָה:


**healing** Heb. ארכה.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:18:1)


**וְאַרְמוֹן.** הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and the palace** The Temple.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:19:1)


**וְהִכְבַּדְתִּים.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עַם כָּבֵד:


**and I will increase them** I will make them a numerous nation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:19:2)


**וְלֹא יִצְעָרוּ.** כְּמוֹ יִמְעָטוּ:


**and they shall not become few in number** like יִמְעָטוּ.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:1)


**אַדִּירוֹ.** שַׂר שֶׁלּוֹ:


**their prince** lit., his mighty one.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:2)


**עָרַב אֶת לִבּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן עֵרָבוֹן, פרוויל״א הטינ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**pledged his heart** an expression of a pledge (fermaille, aatine in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:21:3)


**לָגֶשֶׁת אֵלַי.** לַמִּלְחָמָה, לִמְחוֹת בְּיָדִי:


**to approach Me** to war, to stop Me.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:23:1)


**מִתְגּוֹרֵר.** חוֹנֶה וְדָר, לְשׁוֹן ״אֶרֶץ מְגוּרֵי״ (בראשית לז:א). וְדוּגְמָתוֹ ״וְסַעַר מִתְחוֹלֵל״ (ירמיה כג:יט) הָאָמוּר בַּמִּקְרָא הָאַחֵר, הוּא זֶה, שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם לְשׁוֹן חֲנִיָּה:


**settling storm** camping and dwelling an expression of “the land of the sojournings of (מְגוּרֵי)” (Gen. 37:1), and a similar case is “and a whirling (מִתְחוֹלֵל) storm” (supra 23:19) stated in the other verse is the same as this for both of them are expressions of camping.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 30:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/30:23:2)


**עַל רֹאשׁ רְשָׁעִים.** עַל רֹאשׁ הַגּוֹיִים:


**on the head[s] of the wicked** On the heads of the heathens.



## Chapter 31


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:1)


**מָצָא חֵן.** בְּעֵינֵי דּוֹר הַמִּדְבָּר:


**found favor** The generation of the wilderness found favor in My eyes.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:2)


**שְׂרִידֵי חָרֶב.** מִצְרַיִם וְהָעֲמָלֵקִי וְהַכְּנַעֲנִי:


**who had escaped the sword** of the Egyptians the Amalekites and the Canaanites.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:2:3)


**הָלוֹךְ לְהַרְגִּיעוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** בְּהוֹלִיכָם לִנְחוֹל אֶרֶץ מַרְגּוֹעָם:


**He [therefore] went to give Israel their resting place** When He led them to take possession of the land of their rest.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:3:1)


**מֵרָחוֹק.** בִּזְכוּת אָבוֹת (בראשית יז:ז):


**From long ago** In the merit of the Patriarchs.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:3:2)


**נִרְאָה לִי.** אֲנִי הַנָּבִיא, וְאָמַר לִי לֵאמֹר לִכְנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל **אַהֲבַת עוֹלָם אֲהַבְתִּיךְ**:


**appeared to me** I, the prophet, and told me to say to the congregation of Israel, “With everlasting love have I loved you.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:4:1)


**עוֹד אֶבְנֵךְ וְנִבְנֵית.** בִּנְיָנִים שְׁנַיִם הָיוּ לָךְ עַל יְדֵי אָדָם, לְכָךְ חָרְבוּ. **עוֹד אֶבְנֵךְ** אֲנִי בְּעַצְמִי בִּנְיָן שְׁלִישִׁי, **וְנִבְנֵית** לְעוֹלָם. **תַּעְדִּי** תִּתְקַשְּׁטִי:


**Yet again will I rebuild you, then you shall be built** You had two buildings made by men. Therefore, they were destroyed. Yet again will I rebuild you, I by Myself, a third building, and you shall be built forever. **shall you be adorned** Heb. תַּעְדִּי.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:5:1)


**וְחִלֵּלוּ.** בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית יְחַלְּלוּ אֶת פִּרְיָם לִפְדּוֹתוֹ בְּכֶסֶף (ויקרא יט:כד):


**and redeem [them]** in the fourth year, they shall profane their produce by redeeming it with money.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:6:1)
