**נוֹצְרִים.** צוֹפִים בְּרָאשֵׁי מִגְדָּלִים גְּבוֹהִים לְהַכְרִיז קוֹל נִשְׁמָע לְמֵרָחוֹק. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **נוֹצְרִים** כְּמוֹ ״נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לָאֲלָפִים״ (שמות לד:ז), לְשׁוֹן שְׁמִירָה. כְּלוֹמַר, **יֶשׁ יוֹם** שֶׁיִּקְרְאוּ אוֹתָם שֶׁשָּׁמְרוּ הַתּוֹרָה וְיֹאמְרוּ **קוּמוּ**. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֲרֵי אִית אֲרִיכוּת יוֹמַיָּא וְסַגִּי טוּבָא דְּעָתִיד לְמֵיתֵי לְצַדִּיקַיָּא דְּנַטְרוּ אוֹרַיְתִי מִלְּקַדְמִין״:


**the watchers shall call** The lookouts at the tops of the lofty towers to announce with a voice heard a distance away. Another explanation: נֹצְרִים is like “He keeps (נֹצֵר) lovingkindness” (Exod. 34:7), an expression of keeping. That is to say: There is a day when they will call those who kept the Torah and say, “Rise...” And so did Jonathan render: There are many days and much goodness that is destined to come to the righteous who kept My Torah from days of old.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:7:1)


**וְצַהֲלוּ בְּרֹאשׁ הַגּוֹיִם.** בְּמִגְדָּלוֹת גְּבוֹהִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ לְמֵרָחוֹק:


**and shout at the head of the nations** On the lofty towers, so that they should hear from afar.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:8:1)


**בָּם עִוֵּר וּפִסֵּחַ.** אֲפִילּוּ הַנִּכְשָׁלִים שֶׁבָּהֶם לֹא אֶמְאַס:


**the blind and the lame amongst them** Even the staggering ones among them I will not reject.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:9:1)


**בִּבְכִי יָבֹאוּ.** עַל יְדֵי תְּפִלָּה וּתְשׁוּבָה:


**With weeping will they come** Through prayer and repentance.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:10:1)


**מְזָרֶה.** אֶסְפְּרֵיינְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**“He Who scattered”** (separant in French, separating.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:12:1)


**וְנָהֲרוּ.** יֵאָסְפוּ כְּמוֹ נָהָר:


**and they will stream** They will gather like a river.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:14:1)


**דָּשֶׁן.** שָׁמֵן:


**fat** Heb. דָשֶן.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:15:1)


**קוֹל בְּרָמָה נִשְׁמָע.** ״קָלָא בְּרוּם עָלְמָא אִישְׁתְּמַע, קָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דְּבָכְיָן עַל יִרְמְיָה נְבִיָּיא כַּד שְׁלַח יָתֵיהּ נְבוּזַרְאֲדָן מֵרָמָתָא״. לָשׁוּב מֵאַחַר הַגּוֹלִים אֶל גְּדַלְיָה בֶּן אֲחִיקָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר בְּסֵפֶר זֶה (ירמיה מ:א):


**A voice is heard on high** A voice is heard in the height of the world, the voice of the house of Israel weeping over Jeremiah the prophet when Nebuzaradan sent him from Ramah to return after the exiles to Gedaliah son of Ahikam, as is stated in this Book (below ch. 39).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:15:2)


**רָחֵל מְבַכָּה עַל בָּנֶיהָ.** מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה אָמַר שֶׁהָלְכוּ אָבוֹת וְאִמָּהוֹת לְפַיֵּס אֶת הַהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל שֶׁהֶעֱמִיד מְנַשֶּׁה דְּמוּת בַּהֵיכָל, וְלֹא נִתְפַּיֵּס. נִכְנְסָה רָחֵל, אָמְרָה לְפָנָיו: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם רַחֲמֵי מִי מְרֻבִּים רַחֲמֶיךָ אוֹ רַחֲמֵי בָּשׂר וָדָם, הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר רַחֲמֶיךָ מְרֻבִּים. וַהֲלֹא אֲנִי הִכְנַסְתִּי צָרָתִי בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתִי, שֶׁכָּל עֲבוֹדָה שֶׁעָבַד יַעֲקֹב אֶת אָבִי לֹא עָבַד אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִילִי; כְּשֶׁבָּאתִי לִיכָּנֵס לַחֻפָּה הִכְנִיסוּ אֶת אֲחוֹתִי, וְלֹא דַי שֶׁשָּׁתַקְתִּי אֶלָּא שֶׁמָּסַרְתִּי לָהּ סִימָנִי. אַף אַתָּה, אִם הִכְנִיסוּ בָנֶיךָ צָרָתְךָ בְּבֵיתְךָ, שְׁתוֹק לָהֶם. אָמַר לָהּ: יָפֶה לִמַּדְתְּ סַנֵּגוֹרְיָא, יֵשׁ שָׂכָר לִפְעֻלָּתֵךְ וּלְצִדְקָתֵךְ שֶׁמָּסַרְתְּ סִימָנֵךְ לַאֲחוֹתֵךְ:


**Rachel weeping for her children** The Midrash Aggadah states (see Lam. Rabbah Proem 24) that the Patriarchs and the Matriarchs went to appease the Holy One blessed be He concerning the sin of Manasseh who placed an image in the Temple but He was not appeased. Rachel entered and stated before Him “O Lord of the Universe, whose mercy is greater, Your mercy or the mercy of a flesh and blood person? You must admit that Your mercy is greater. Now did I not bring my rival into my house? For all the work that Jacob worked for my father he worked only for me. When I came to enter the nuptial canopy, they brought my sister, and it was not enough that I kept my silence, but I gave her my password. You, too, if Your children have brought Your rival into Your house, keep Your silence for them.” He said to her, “You have defended them well. There is reward for your deed and for your righteousness, that you gave over your password to your sister.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:1)


**מִתְנוֹדֵד.** קוֹמְפְּלֵיְינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן ״מִי יָנוּד לָךְ״ (ירמיה טו:ה):


**complaining** (Konpljjet in O.F.), an expression of “who will lament for you (יָנוּד)?” (above 15:5).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:2)


**יִסַּרְתַּנִי.** בְּיִסּוּרִין אַתָּה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**“You have chastised me”** With pains, You, the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:18:3)


**כְּעֵגֶל לֹא לוּמָּד.** פוּשָׁאגוּיֵילוֹנִ״י בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן ״מַלְמַד הַבָּקָר״ (שופטים ג:לא). הָיִיתִי בַּתְּחִלָּה, לְכָךְ חָטָאתִי לְךָ:


**like an ungoaded calf** (fut aiguillones in O.F.), was goaded, an expression of “an ox-goad (מַלְמַד הַבָּקָר)” (Judges 3:31). I was like an ungoaded calf in the beginning. Therefore, I sinned against You.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:1)


**נִחַמְתִּי.** אֲנִי מִתְחָרֵט עַל תּוֹעֲבוֹתַי:


**I have completely changed my mind** I regret my abominations.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:2)


**הִוָּדְעִי.** שֶׁהִתְבּוֹנַנְתִּי בְּעַצְמִי שֶׁמָּעַלְתִּי, אפרינו״יי בְּלַעַ״ז:


**I had been brought to know myself** That I thought of myself that I was treacherous, (aparsoujjge in O. F.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:19:3)


**חֶרְפַּת נְעוּרָי.** שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים אוֹיְבַי לְחָרְפֵנִי עַל חַטֹּאת נְעוּרָי:


**the reproach of my youth** That my enemies can reproach me for the sins of my youth.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:20:1)


**הֲבֵן יַקִּיר לִי אֶפְרַיִם.** הֵן דִּבְרֵי שְׁכִינָה, כְּלוֹמַר הוּא מִתְנוֹדֵד וַאֲנִי לִבִּי נֶהְפַּךְ עָלַי לְרַחֵם, שֶׁמָּא בְּנִי יַקִּירִי הוּא, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּן אֶחָד שֶׁהוּא עוֹשֶׂה לוֹ כָּל רְצוֹנוֹ. **שַׁעֲשׁוּעִים** אישבניימני״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**“Is Ephraim a son who is dear to me?”** These are the words of the Shechinah. That is to say, He is complaining, “And as for Me, I have a change of heart to have pity. Is he My dear son (as one who has but one son, whose every wish he grants him)?” **dandled** (Esbaniement in O.F.)


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:20:2)


**מִדֵּי דַבְּרִי בּוֹ.** בְּכָל עֵת שֶׁאֲנִי מְדַבֵּר בּוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ וַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה (ויקרא רבה ב,ג): דַּי דִּבּוּרִי שֶׁנָּתַתִּי בּוֹ, שֶׁלִּמַּדְתִּיו תּוֹרָתִי, לְרַחֵם עָלָיו:


**whenever I speak of him** Every time that I speak of him. And the Midrash Leviticus Rabbah (2:3) explains: It is enough My speech (דַּי דִבּוּרִי) with which I endowed him, that I taught him My Torah, for Me to have mercy on him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:1)


**הַצִּיבִי לָךְ צִיֻּנִים.** סִימָנִים בְּדַרְכֵי אֲבוֹתַיִךְ הַטּוֹבִים:


**Set up markers for yourself** Signs in the good ways of your ancestors.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:2)


**תַּמְרוּרִים.** תְּמָרִים קְטַנִּים נְטוּעִים לְסִימָן בַּ**דֶּרֶךְ**. וּפְשׁוּטוֹ שֶׁל מִקְרָא, עֲשִׂי סִימָנִים לָדַעַת הַ**דֶּרֶךְ** שֶׁ**הָלַכְתְּ** בָּהּ מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבָבֶל, שֶׁתַּחְזִירִי בְּאוֹתוֹ **דֶּרֶךְ**. כְּלוֹמַר, כִּי וַדַּאי תָּשׁוּבִי מִשָּׁם:


**small palms** small palms (תְּמָרִים) planted for a sign on the way. And the simple interpretation of the verse is. Make signs to know the way you traversed from Eretz Israel to Babylon, so that you will return by that road; that is to say that you will surely return from there.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:21:3)


**דֶּרֶךְ הָלָכְתְּ.** הָלַכְתִּי כְּתִיב, וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהָלַכְתְּ הָלַכְתִּי אֲנִי עִמָּךְ. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **שִׂימִי לָךְ תַּמְרוּרִים** ״הִתְפַּלֵּל בְּמָרַר נַפְשֵׁךְ״ (וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ תַּמְרוּרִין עִנְיַן מוֹעֵל, וְכֵן פֵּרֵשׁ ״וְתִימֲרוֹת עָשָׁן״ (יואל ג:ג) סא״א):


**the road upon which you went** The “kethib” is הָלָכְתִּי, “I went,” and everywhere that you went I went with you Jonathan, however, renders. place “tamrurim” for yourself: Pray with embittered spirit, deriving תַּמְרוּרִים from מַר bitter. (And Menahem [Machbereth p. 119] explained תַּמְרוּרִים as an expression of raising up, and so he explained וְתִמְרוֹת “raising of smoke” (Joel 3:3). In some editions, this is missing.)



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:22:1)


**תִּתְחַמָּקִין.** תִּסְתְּרִי מִמֶּנִּי, שֶׁאַתְּ בּוֹשָׁה לָשׁוּב אֱלֵי מִפְּנֵי דַּרְכֵּךְ. הִנֵּה חֲדָשָׁה נִבְרֵאת בָּאָרֶץ, שֶׁהַ**נְּקֵבָה** תַּחֲזֹר אַחַר הַזָּכָר לְבַקְּשׁוֹ שֶׁיִּשָּׂאֶנָּה, לְשׁוֹן ״וַאֲסוֹבְבָהּ בָּעִיר אֲבַקְּשָׁהּ וְגוֹ׳״ (שִׁיר הַשִּׁירִים ג:ב); וּמִשּׁוּם רַבִּי יְהוּדָה הַדַּרְשָׁן שָׁמַעְתִּי: **נְקֵבָה תְּסוֹבֵב** לִהְיוֹת **גָּבֶר**, אֲנִי נְתַתִּיךְ כְּבַת הַנּוֹטֶלֶת עִשּׂוּר נְכָסִים שִׁבְעָה אֻמּוֹת מִשִּׁבְעִים, סוֹף שֶׁתִּטְּלִי הַכֹּל כְּבֶן זָכָר שֶׁהוּא יוֹרֵשׁ הַכֹּל:


**will you hide** will you hide from Me, that you are ashamed to return to Me because of your way? Behold, something new has been created on earth, that a woman shall go after a man to seek him that he should marry her. This is an expression of “and I will go around (וַאֲסוֹבְבָה) in the city; I will seek, etc.” (Song 3:2). And in the name of Rabbi Judah the Preacher I heard: A woman shall turn around to become a man. I gave you like a daughter, who receives a tenth of the property of her father, seven nations out of seventy. Ultimately, you shall take everything like a son, a male, who inherits everything.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:26:1)


**עַל זֹאת הֱקִיצוֹתִי.** כָּךְ אָמַר יִרְמְיָה, כָּל מַה שֶּׁהָיִיתִי יָשֵׁן וְנִרְדָּם בִּנְבוּאַת פֻּרְעָנוּת, בַּנְּבוּאָה הַזֹּאת הֱקִיצוֹתִי מִתַּרְדֵּמָתִי:


**Thereupon, I awoke** So said Jeremiah, “All this time I was sleeping deeply while perceiving the prophecy of retribution, but with this prophecy I awoke from my deep sleep.”


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:26:2)


**עֲרֵבָה.** נְעֵמָה:


**was pleasant** Heb. עָרְבָה .



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:27:1)


**זֶרַע אָדָם וְזֶרַע בְּהֵמָה.** הַטּוֹבִים וְהַסְּכָלִים שֶׁבָּהֶם, אֲנִי אֶזְרָעֵם כֻּלָּם לִהְיוֹת זֶרַע שֶׁלִּי. **זֶרַע אָדָם וְזֶרַע בְּהֵמָה** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֲקִימִינוּן כִּבְנֵי אֱנָשָׁא וְאַצְלְחִינוּן כִּבְעִירָא״, שֶׁאֵין עֲוֹנוֹתֶיהָ נִפְקָדִין עָלֶיהָ:


**seed of man and seed of beasts** The good and the foolish of them, I will sow all of them to be My seed. Seed of man and seed of beasts. Jonathan renders. I will set them up (our edition: I will increase them) like people and I will make them succeed like a beast, whose iniquities are not visited upon her.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:29:1)


**בֹּסֶר.** פְּרִי שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמַר, וְהוּא מַקְהֶה אֶת הַשִּׁנַּיִם:


**unripe grapes** A fruit that is not yet ripe, and it sets the teeth on edge.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:29:2)


**וְשִׁנֵּי בָנִים תִּקְהֶינָה.** הַבָּנִים יִלְקוּ בַּעֲוֹן **אָבוֹת**:


**And the teeth of the children shall be set on edge...** The children for the fathers’ iniquity.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:35:1)


**לְאוֹר.** אֵיאַקְלַארְזִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״לְאַנְהָרָא״:


**to illuminate** (esclarzir in Prov. or eclaircir in O.F., eclairer in Modern French), to brighten, and so did Jonathan render: לְאַנְהָרָא, to illuminate.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:35:2)


**רוֹגַע הַיָּם.** מְנִידוֹ וּמַרְתִּיחוֹ וְנַעֲשֶׂה קְמָטִים קְמָטִים, כְּמוֹ ״עוֹרִי רָגַע״ (איוב ז:ה):


**Who stirs up the sea** He breaks the sea, moves it and “boils” it, and it becomes wrinkles. Cf. “my skin became wrinkled (רָגַע)” (Job 7:5).



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:36:1)


**יָמֻשׁוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״לֹא יָמִישׁ״ (שמות יג:כב):


**depart** “He would not move away (יָמִיש)” (Exod. 13:22).



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:37:1)


**אִם יִמַּדּוּ שָׁמַיִם.** כַּמָּה גָּבְהָם:


**If the heavens... will be measured** How much is their altitude.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:37:2)


**גַּם אֲנִי אֶמְאַס.** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהֵם לֹא יִמַּדּוּ וְלֹא יֵחָקְרוּ, כָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא יִמָּאֲסוּ **בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר** חָטְאוּ:


**I too will reject...Israel** That is to say that just as they can neither be measured nor fathomed so will Israel not be rejected because of all that they sinned.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:1)


**הָעֵמֶק הַפְּגָרִים.** עֵמֶק שֶׁנָּפְלוּ שָׁם פִּגְרֵי מַחֲנֵה סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יט:לה; ישעיה לז:לו):


**valley of the dead bodies** The valley where the bodies of the camp of Sennacherib fell.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:2)


**וְהַדֶּשֶׁן.** מְקוֹם שֶׁפֶךְ הַדֶּשֶׁן שֶׁהָיָה חוּץ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, יוֹסִיפוּ עַל הָעִיר וְיַכְנִיסוּ כָּל זֶה לְתוֹךְ חוֹמוֹתֶיהָ. וּנְבוּאָה זוֹ עֲתִידָה לָבֹא:


**and the ash** The place where the ashes are poured out, which was outside Jerusalem, they will add to the city and include all this within its walls. Now this prophecy relates to the future, to the final redemption, since it did not take place in the time of the Second Temple.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 31:40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/31:40:3)


**הַשְּׁדֵמוֹת.** קַנְפַנְיָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the fields** (Kanpanjje in O.F.) (campagne in Modern French, open country.



## Chapter 32


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:2:1)


**צָרִים.** לְשׁוֹן ״מָצוֹר״ (דברים כ:יט):


**was besieging** an expression of a siege (מָצוֹר).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:2:2)


**בַּחֲצַר הַמַּטָּרָה.** לְשׁוֹן בֵּית הַמִּשְׁמָר:


**in the prison yard** an expression of “the prison” (בֵּית הַמִֹּשְמָר).



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:5:1)


**עַד פָּקְדִי אֹתוֹ.** פְּקֻדַּת כָּל הָאָדָם הִיא הַמִּיתָה (במדבר טז:כט):


**until I remember him** The remembrance of all mankind. That is death.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:6:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר יִרְמְיָהוּ וגו׳.** לֹא לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ אָמַר כֵּן, אֶלָּא פֵּירֵשׁ הַכָּתוּב לָמָּה כְּלָאוֹ, וְחוֹזֵר לְדִבּוּרוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן לְפָרֵשׁ מַה **הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר הָיָה אֶל יִרְמְיָהוּ**:


**And Jeremiah said, etc.** He did not say this to Zedekiah but Scripture explained why he imprisoned him and then returned to its first statement to explain what the word was that came to Jeremiah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:7:1)


**בָּא אֵלֶיךָ.** עַל פִּי הַדִּבּוּר:


**is coming** By Divine command.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:1)


**אֶת סֵפֶר הַמִּקְנָה.** שְׁטַר הַמְּכִירָה:


**the deed of the purchase** The deed of the sale.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:2)


**אֶת הֶחָתוּם וְגוֹ׳.** עִם חֲתִימָתוֹ, כְּשֶׁהוּא חָתוּם כְּדָת וְחֹק:


**that which was signed, etc.** With its signature as it was signed according so the law and the statute.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:11:3)


**וְאֶת הַגָּלוּי.** שֶׁהוּחְזַק בְּבֵית דִּין עַל פִּי עֵדָיו, וְכָתְבוּ וְחָתְמוּ הַדַּיָּנִין אַשְׁרְתָּא דְדַיָּנֵי, הוּא קִיּוּם הַשְּׁטָרוֹת (כתובות כא:), לַעֲשׂוֹת הַדָּבָר גָּלוּי וּמְפֻרְסָם, פֶּן יָמוּתוּ הָעֵדִים. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ לְעִנְיַן גֵּט פָּשׁוּט וּמְקֻשָּׁר בְּבָבָא בַּתְרָא (קס.-קס:):


**and that which was open** Which was confirmed in the court by word of its witnesses, and the judges wrote and signed a judicial confirmation. This is the confirmation of the deeds to make the matter open and publicized lest the witnesses die, and our Rabbis explained this concerning a plain, open document and a folded one in Baba Bathra (160b).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:12:1)


**לְעֵינֵי חֲנַמְאֵל דּוֹדִי.** וְעַד עַכְשָׁו קְרָאוֹ בֶּן דּוֹדִי, שֶׁמָּא שְׁנַיִם הָיוּ, וְאִם אֵין זֹאת לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַהוּ. וְיֵשׁ נוֹטִים לוֹמַר דּוֹדוֹ וּבֶן דּוֹדוֹ הָיָה חֲנַמְאֵל לְיִרְמְיָה, כְּגוֹן רְאוּבֵן נָשָׂא אֶת בַּת שִׁמְעוֹן אָחִיו וְיָלְדָה לוֹ נְמוּאֵל, וּכְבָר הוֹלִיד שִׁמְעוֹן יָמִין, הֲרֵי יָמִין דּוֹדוֹ לִנְמוּאֵל, שֶׁבֶּן אֲחִי אִמּוֹ הוּא וְגַם בֶּן דּוֹדוֹ, שֶׁהוּא בֶּן שִׁמְעוֹן אֲחִי אָבִיו שֶׁל נְמוּאֵל. וְאֵינָהּ הִיא הַמִּדָּה, שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ בְּכָל הַמִּקְרָא אָח הָאֵם קָרוּי דּוֹד:


**in the presence of Hanamel my uncle** And until now, he calls him “my uncle’s son.” Perhaps there were two. Otherwise, I do not know what it means. Some are inclined to say that Hanamel was both Jeremiah’s uncle and his uncle’s son; e.g. Reuben married the daughter of his brother Simon, and she bore him Jemuel, and Simon had already begotten Jamin. Hence, Jamin is Jemuel’s uncle, since he is his mother’s brother, and also his uncle’s son, since he is the son of Simon, Jemuel’s father’s brother. This is, however, not the method of expression, for we do not find anywhere in the Bible, that the mother’s brother is called an uncle.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:14:1)


**כֹּה אָמַר וְגוֹ׳.** אַל תֹּאמַר: לְחִנָּם אֲנִי מַצְנִיעָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַכֹּל גּוֹלִים:


**So said, etc.** Do not say that I am concealing them for nothing since everyone is going into exile.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:15:1)


**כִּי כֹה אָמַר ה׳.** סוֹפָן לִקְנוֹת בָּתִּים:


**For so says the Lord** They will ultimately purchase houses.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:17:1)


**לֹא יִפָּלֵא.** לֹא יִכָּחֵד; וְיוֹדֵעַ אַתָּה שֶׁסּוֹפֵנוּ לִגְלוֹת, וְלָמָּה אָמַרְתָּ לִי לִקְנוֹת שָׂדֶה?


**nothing is hidden from You** Nothing is hidden; hence You know that we are destined to be exiled, so why did You tell me to buy a field?



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:18:1)


**וּמְשַׁלֵּם עֲוֹן אָבוֹת אֶל חֵיק.** הַדּוֹר הַזֶּה, הַהוֹלְכִים אַחֲרֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם בְּדֶרֶךְ הָרָעָה:


**and requites the iniquity of the fathers in the bosom** Of this generation who follow their ancestors in their evil way.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:18:2)


**הַגָּדוֹל הַגִּבּוֹר.** וְלֹא אָמַר וְהַנּוֹרָא, אָמַר: עוֹבְדֵי אֱלִילִים מְרַקְּדִין בְּהֵיכָלוֹ, אַיֵּה נוֹרְאוֹתָיו? בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא (יומא סט:):


**Great and Mighty** But he did not say, “and awesome.” He said. Heathens are dancing in His Temple. Where then are His awesome deeds? In Tractate Yoma (69b).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:20:1)


**וּבָאָדָם.** בִּשְׁאָר אֲנָשִׁים. דָּבָר אַחֵר: אוֹתוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁקוּלִין לְיִשְׂרָאֵל וּלְמִצְרַיִם, כֵּיצַד? ״וַיְהִי הֶעָנָן וְהַחֹשֶׁךְ וַיָּאֶר הַלַּיְלָה״ (שמות יד:כ) – אוֹתוֹ מַזָּל שֶׁשִּׁמֵּשׁ חֹשֶׁךְ לְמִצְרַיִם, שִׁמֵּשׁ אוֹר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל; מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה:


**and in mankind** And in other people. Another explanation: Signs that were equal for Israel and for the Egyptians. How so? “And there was the cloud and the darkness, and it illuminated the night” (Exodus 14:20). That guardian angel that served darkness for the Egyptians, served light for the Israelites Midrash Aggadah (source unknown). And Jonathan renders: And for Israel You performed wonders among people.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:23:1)


**וַתַּקְרֵא אוֹתָם.** הִקְרֵיתָ אוֹתָם; לְשׁוֹן ״מִקְרֶה״ (שמואל א ו:ט):


**then You caused...to come upon them** You caused to happen to them, an expression of a happening (מִקְרֶה).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:24:1)


**וַאֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתָּ.** רָעָה עָלֶיהָ, **הָיָה** וּבָא:


**and what You have proclaimed** Evil upon it has come to pass (lit., has been and has come).



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:25:1)


**וְהָעִיר נִתְּנָה.** וְלָמָּה לִי שָׂדֶה?


**Yet the city is given** Of what avail is the field to me?



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:27:1)


**הֲמִמֶּנִּי יִפָּלֵא.** וְכִי הָעֲתִידוֹת עֲלוּמוֹת מִמֶּנִּי?


**Is...concealed from Me?** Are future events concealed from Me?



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:31:1)


**לְמִן הַיּוֹם אֲשֶׁר בָּנוּ אוֹתָהּ.** בְּיוֹם שֶׁהוּסַד הַבַּיִת, נָשָׂא שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת בַּת פַּרְעֹה (ויקרא רבה יב, ה):


**since the day they built it** On the day that the Temple was founded, Solomon married Pharaoh’s daughter.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:33:1)


**וְלַמֵּד אוֹתָם.** לְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל, אפרננ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**I taught them** lit., and teaching them. This is a participle, (apprenant in French).



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 32:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/32:37:1)


**הִנְנִי מְקַבְּצָם.** בְּרַחֲמִים, **מִכָּל אֲשֶׁר הִדַּחְתִּים שָׁם בְּאַפִּי**:


**Behold I will gather them** with mercy from wherever I have driven them with My anger.



## Chapter 33


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:1:1)


**עָצוּר.** בְּבֵית הַכֶּלֶא:


**confined** in the prison.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:2:1)


**כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ עֹשָׂהּ.** הָעוֹשֶׂה זֹאת:


**So said the Lord, the Maker thereof** Who makes this.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:3:1)


**קְרָא אֵלַי וְאֶעֱנֶךָּ.** לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ הָעֲתִידוֹת:


**Call Me and I will answer you** to inform you of future events.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:3:2)


**וּבְצוּרוֹת.** שְׁמוּרוֹת בְּלִבִּי לַעֲשׂוֹתָם:


**and strong things** laid up in My heart to perform them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:4:1)


**הַנְּתֻצִים אֶל הַסֹּלְלוֹת.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶם סֹלְלוֹת, תִּלִּים שֶׁצְּבוּרִים לְמַעְלָה, אֲשֶׁר בּוֹנִים בְּנֵי הָעִיר לַעֲלוֹת אֶל הַסֹּלְלוֹת, לְהִלָּחֵם מֵעֲלֵיהֶם אֶל הַכַּשְׂדִּים:


**that are demolished for the mounds** To make mounds of them, mounds piled up high which the city dwellers build to ascend the mounds to wage war from upon them with the Chaldeans.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:4:2)


**וְאֶל הֶחָרֶב.** ״לְתַקְּפָא שׁוּרָא מִן קֳדָם דְּקָטְלִין בְּחַרְבָּא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and for the sword** To strengthen the wall against those who slay with the sword.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:5:1)


**וּלְמַלְּאָם אֶת פִּגְרֵי הָאָדָם.** וְאֶת הַבָּתִּים אֲשֶׁר נָתְצוּ עוֹשִׂים קְבָרוֹת לַפְּגָרִים שֶׁנֶּהֱרָגִים בְּתוֹךְ הָעִיר:


**and to fill them with the corpses of the men** And the houses that they demolished they make into graves for the corpses of those killed within the city.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:5:2)


**וַאֲשֶׁר הִסְתַּרְתִּי וְגוֹ׳. כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ הִנְנִי מַעֲלֶה לָהּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**and that which I have hidden, etc.** So said the Lord: Behold, I will bring it, etc.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:6:1)


**עֲתֶרֶת שָׁלוֹם.** בִּרְכַּת שָׁלוֹם; שֶׁלוּ״ד בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a greeting of peace** a blessing (salud in O.F., salut in modern French), a greeting.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:12:1)


**נְוֵה רוֹעִים.** מְדוֹר רוֹעִים:


**a dwelling of shepherds** Heb. נְוֵה רֹעִים.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:13:1)


**עוֹד תַּעֲבֹרְנָה הַצֹּאן וגו׳.** יֵצְאוּ וְיָבֹאוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל יְדֵי מַנְהִיג הוֹלֵךְ בְּרֹאשָׁם (מיכה ב:יג):


**flocks shall again pass, etc.** Israel will come and go under the hands of a king who walks at their head.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:17:1)


**לֹא יִכָּרֵת.** כְּרִיתוּת עוֹלָם, אֶלָּא אִם יִפְסוֹק, סוֹפוֹ לַחֲזוֹר:


**There shall not be cut off** permanently, but if it is discontinued, it will ultimately be reinstated.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:18:1)


**כָּל הַיָּמִים.** לֹא יִכָּרֵת לָהֶם זֶרַע הָרָאוּי לְהַעֲלוֹת **עוֹלָה** וּלְהַקְטִיר **מִנְחָה**:


**for all time** There shall not be cut off from them seed fit to offer up a burnt-offering or to burn a meal-offering.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:20:1)


**אִם תָּפֵרוּ אֶת בְּרִיתִי הַיּוֹם.** אִם תּוּכְלוּ לְהָפֵר אֶת בְּרִיתִי אֲשֶׁר כָּרַתִּי אֶת הַיּוֹם וְאֶת **הַלַּיְלָה** לִהְיוֹתָם **בְּעִתָּם**, אֲשֶׁר כָּרַתִּי לְנֹחַ וּבָנָיו ״וְיוֹם וָלַיְלָה לֹא יִשְׁבֹּתוּ״ (בראשית ח:כב):


**If you break My covenant with the day** If you can break My covenant that I formed with the day and with the night to be in their time, which I formed with Noah and his sons, “And day and night shall not cease” (Gen. 8:22).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:1)


**שְׁתֵּי הַמִּשְׁפָּחוֹת.** הַמַּלְכוּת וְהַכְּהֻנָּה (תנחומא, לך כ):


**The two families** Viz. royalty and the priesthood.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:2)


**וְאֶת עַמִּי יִנְאָצוּן.** וּבַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה הֵם גּוֹרְמִים לְעַמִּי לְנָאֵץ **מִהְיוֹת גּוֹי** לִי:


**and they make My people despise** And with these words, they cause My people to despise being a nation to Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:24:3)


**לִפְנֵיהֶם.** לִפְנֵי דִּבְרֵיהֶם אֵלֶּה, שֶׁמְּלַמְּדִין אוֹתָןלוֹמַר: לֹא יָשׁוּב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵחֲרוֹנוֹ עוֹד, וְלֹא תוֹעִיל לָכֶם תְּשׁוּבָה:


**before them** According to these words of theirs, that they teach them to say that the Holy One, blessed be He, will no longer return from His anger, and repentance will not avail them.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 33:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/33:25:1)


**אִם לֹא בְרִיתִי וְגוֹ׳.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא יִתְקַיֵּם הַבְּרִית שֶׁכָּרַתִּי לְיוֹם **וָלַיְלָה** לִהְיוֹת **בְּעִתָּם**, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְ**חֻקּוֹת שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ** לִיבָּטֵל כְּאִלּוּ **לֹא** שַׂמְתִּים, ״גַּם זֶרַע יַעֲקֹב וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיהו לג:כו). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ (נדרים לב.) לְעִנְיַן בְּרִית הַתּוֹרָה, לִלְמֹד מִכָּאן שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל הַתּוֹרָה נִבְרְאוּ שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ; אַךְ אֵין הַמִּדְרָשׁ מְיֻשָּׁב עַל סֵדֶר הַמִּקְרָאוֹת:


**If not My covenant, etc.** If it is possible that the covenant that I formed with day and night to be in their time should be abolished, and if it is possible for the statutes of heaven and earth to be abolished as though I had not placed them, also the seed of Jacob, etc. Our Sages, however, expounded it in regard to the covenant of the Torah and circumcision, to derive from here that because of them heaven and earth were created. But this Midrash does not fit the sequence of the verses.



## Chapter 34


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:5:1)


**בְּשָׁלוֹם תָּמוּת.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (מועד קטן כח:), שֶׁמֵּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּיָמָיו. שֶׁכָּל יְמֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לֹא יָצְאוּ אֲסִירָיו מֵאֲסִירֵיהֶם, וּכְשֶׁמֵּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר יָצָא צִדְקִיָּהוּ מִבֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים, וּלְמָחֳרָת מֵת וְקָבְרוּהוּ בְּכָבוֹד:


**You shall die in peace** Our Sages stated that Nebuchadnezzar died during his lifetime (Moed Katan 28b), for all the days of Nebuchadnezzar his prisoners were not freed from their imprisonment, and when Nebuchadnezzar died, Zedekiah emerged from the prison. He died the next day and was buried with pomp.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:5:2)


**וּבְמִשְׂרְפוֹת אֲבוֹתֶיךָ.** כָּךְ הָיָה דַּרְכָּם, לִשְׂרֹף עַל הַמְּלָכִים מִטָּתָן וּכְלֵי תַשְׁמִישָׁן (דברי הימים ב טז:יד; עבודה זרה יא.):


**and with the burnings of your forefathers** So was their custom, to burn for the kings their bier and the utensils they used.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:8:1)


**אַחֲרֵי כְּרוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁבִיעִית לְמָלְכוֹ. כָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו): ״וַיְהִי בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁבִיעִית... בָּאוּ אֲנָשִׁים מִזִּקְנֵי יְהוּדָה לִדְרֹשׁ אֶת ה׳״ (יחזקאל כ:א). אָמְרוּ לִיחֶזְקֵאל: עֶבֶד כֹּהֵן שֶׁמְּכָרוֹ רַבּוֹ, מַהוּ שֶׁיֹּאכַל בִּתְרוּמָה? כְּלוֹמַר, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ רוֹצִין לוֹמַר: לֹא נֶעֱנַשׁ עַל הַמִּצְוֹת, עַל שֶׁהֶחֱזִירוּ אֶת הָעֲבָדִים:


**after King Zedekiah had made a covenant** In the seventh year of his reign. So we learned it in Seder Olam (ch. 26): “And it came to pass in the seventh year...men came of the elders of Judah (sic) to inquire of the Lord” (Ezekiel 20:1). They said to Ezekiel, ‘The slave of a priest whom his owner sold what is the ruling regarding whether he may eat of the priest’s due?’ That is to say, because they wanted to say that they should not be punished for infracting the commandment by returning the slaves.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:17:1)


**הִנְנִי קֹרֵא לָכֶם דְּרוֹר.** מֵאִתִּי, שֶׁאֵינִי אָדוֹן לָכֶם לְהַצִּיל, וְתִהְיוּ הֶפְקֵר אֶל הַחֶרֶב וְאֶל הָרָעָב:


**behold I proclaim freedom to you** from Me, that I am not your master to save you, and you shall be free to the sword and to the famine.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:18:1)


**הָעֵגֶל אֲשֶׁר כָּרְתוּ לִשְׁנַיִם.** כְּשֶׁחָזְרוּ וְכָבְשׁוּם, כָּרְתוּ כֻּלָּם בְּרִית לִמְרוֹד בַּמָּקוֹם, וְכָרְתוּ הָעֵגֶל לִשְׁנַיִם וְעָבְרוּ **בֵּין בְּתָרָיו**, לִמְרוֹד בּוֹ. וְהִיא הָיְתָה בְּרִית חֲזָקָה וּגְמוּרָה, לֵאמֹר: כָּכָה יִבָּתֵר וְיֵחָלֵק הָעוֹבֵר עָלֶיהָ (בראשית טו:ט-יז):


**when they cut the calf in two** Whey they returned and forced them to be slaves, they all made a covenant to rebel against the Omnipresent and cut a calf in two and passed between its parts to rebel against Him, and that was a strong covenant and a final one, saying, So shall the one who transgresses be cut and divided.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:21:1)


**הָעוֹלִים מֵעֲלֵיכֶם.** מִפְּנֵי חֵיל פַּרְעֹה שֶׁיָּצָא מִמִּצְרַיִם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵפֶר זֶה (ירמיה לז:ה):


**who have gone up away from you** because of the army of Pharaoh that came out of Egypt, as is explained in this Book (37:5).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 34:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/34:22:1)


**הִנְנִי מְצַוֶּה.** וְשָׁב חֵיל פַּרְעֹה לְאַרְצוֹ, וְהַכַּשְׂדִּים יָשׁוּבוּ עֲלֵיכֶם (ירמיה לז:ה-ח):


**Behold I command** that Pharaoh’s army return to his land, and the Chaldeans return upon you.



## Chapter 35


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 35:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:2:1)


**אֶל בֵּית הָרֵכָבִים.** בְּנֵי יוֹנָדָב בֶּן רֵכָב (ירמיה לה:ו), וּמִבְּנֵי יִתְרוֹ הָיוּ (מכילתא יתרו עמלק ב):


**to the house of the Rechabites** The sons of Jonadab the son of Rechab, and they were of the sons of Jethro.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 35:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:4:1)


**אִישׁ הָאֱלֹהִים.** ״נָבִיא דַּה׳״ (תרגום יונתן):


**the man of God** The prophet of the Lord.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 35:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:4:2)


**שׁוֹמֵר הַסַּף.** ״אֲמַרְכְּלָא״ (תרגום יונתן), טְרֵשׁוֹרִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the keeper of the vessels** Heb. שמר הסף. Jonathan renders: אמרכּלא (tresorier in French,) a treasurer.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 35:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:7:1)


**בָּאֳהָלִים תֵּשְׁבוּ.** בַּמִּדְבָּרִיּוֹת. **אֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם גָּרִים שָׁם.** לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם חֵלֶק בָּאָרֶץ, שֶׁבְּנֵי גֵרִים הָיוּ (ספרי במדבר עח):


**but you shall dwell in tents** in the deserts where you dwell. They had no share in the land since they were the children of proselytes.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 35:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/35:11:1)


**וַיְהִי בַּעֲלוֹת נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּר.** עַל כָּרְחֵנוּ בָּאנוּ הָעִיר, לְכָךְ עַכְשָׁיו אָנוּ **יוֹשְׁבִים** בַּבָּתִּים:


**And it came to pass when Nebuchadrezzar...came up** Perforce we came to the city. Therefore, we are now dwelling in houses.



## Chapter 36


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:5:1)


**עָצוּר.** בְּבֵית הַכֶּלֶא:


**imprisoned** in the jail.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:10:1)


**פֶּתַח שַׁעַר בֵּית ה׳ הֶחָדָשׁ.** הוּא שַׁעַר הַמִּזְרָחִי, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**the new gate** That is the Eastern Gate; so did Jonathan translate.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:16:1)


**הַגֵּיד נַגִּיד לַמֶּלֶךְ.** אוּלַי יִשְׁמַע:


**“We will surely tell the king”** Perhaps he will hear.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:20:1)


**חָצֵרָה.** אֶל הֶחָצֵר. כָּל תֵּיבָה שֶׁצְּרִיכָה לָמֶ״ד בִּתְחִלָּתָהּ, הִטִּיל לָהּ הֵ״א בְּסוֹפָהּ (יבמות יג:):


**to the court** Heb. חצרה, equivalent to אל החצר. Every word that requires a ‘lammed’ in the beginning Scripture placed a ‘heh’ at its end.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:22:1)


**וְאֶת הָאָח.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (שבת כ.): עֵצִים; עַל שֵׁם שֶׁנִּדְלָקִים בְּאַחְוָה (כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁנִּדְלָקִים זֶה מִזֶּה כְּאַחִים שֶׁמְּהַנִּים זֶה לָזֶה סא״א). וּלְפִי פְשׁוּטוֹ מַשְׁמַע שֶׁהוּא כְּלִי שֶׁמְּבַעֲרִין עָלָיו אוּר לִפְנֵי הַשָּׂרִים:


**and the brazier** Heb. האח. Our Rabbis (Shabbath 21a) defined it as wood, called so because they burn with brotherhood. (That is to say that they are ignited one from the other, like brothers, who benefit each other. Omitted in other editions.) And according to its simple meaning, it appears that it is a vessel upon which they kindle fire before the princes.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:23:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ דְּלָתוֹת.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (מועד קטן כו.): מְגִלַּת קִינוֹת הָיְתָה, קָרְאוּ לְפָנָיו ״אֵיכָה״, ״בָּכֹה תִבְכֶּה״, ״גָּלְתָה יְהוּדָה״, ״דַּרְכֵי צִיּוֹן אֲבֵלוֹת״ (איכה א:א-ד) – בְּכָל זֶה לֹא חָשׁ, אָמַר: אֲנִי מֶלֶךְ עַל הַנּוֹתָרִים; כֵּיוָן שֶׁקָּרָא ״הָיוּ צָרֶיהָ לְרֹאשׁ״ (איכה א:ה), אָמַר: מֵעַכְשָׁיו אֵינִי מֶלֶךְ; מִיָּד וַ**יִּקְרָעֶהָ בְּתַעַר הַסֹּפֵר**:


**three...verses** Our Rabbis (Moed Katan 26a) stated. This was the Scroll of Lamentations. They read before him, (1:1) “How...(:2) She weeps...(ibid. 3) Judah has been exiled...(ibid. 4) The roads of Zion are mournful.” Despite all this, he was not troubled. He said, “I am the king over the survivors.” As soon as he read (ibid. 5), Her adversaries have become the head,” he said, “From now on, I am [no longer] the king.” Immediately, he rent it with a scribe’s razor.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:23:2)


**יִקְרָעֶהָ.** כְּמוֹ קְרָעָהּ:


**he rent** Heb. יקרעה, like קְרָעָה.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:25:1)


**הִפְגִּיעוּ.** הִתְחַנְּנוּ:


**entreated** Heb. הפגעו, supplicated.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 36:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/36:32:1)


**דְּבָרִים רַבִּים כָּהֵמָּה.** מִתְּחִלָּה הָיוּ שָׁלֹשׁ אַלְפָא בֵּיתִין: ״אֵיכָה יָשְׁבָה״ (איכה א:א), ״אֵיכָה יָעִיב״ (איכה ב:א), ״אֵיכָה יוּעַם״ (איכה ד:א), וְהוֹסִיף עָלֶיהָ ״אֲנִי הַגֶּבֶר״ (איכה ג:א), שֶׁכָּל אוֹת מְשֻׁלֶּשֶׁת בָּהּ (איכה רבה ג:א):


**many words like those** At first, there were three alphabetical acrostics: (Lam. ch. 1) “O how... remained,” (2) “How...brought darkness,” (4) “How dim...has become.” And he added to it, (3) “I am the man,” in which every letter is tripled.



## Chapter 37


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:1:1)


**וַיִּמְלֹךְ מֶלֶךְ.** לְפִי שֶׁמֵּעַתָּה בָּא לְסַפֵּר וְלוֹמַר שֶׁנִּתְקַיְּמוּ הַפּוּרְעָנִיּוֹת שֶׁנִּבָּא עַד הֵנָּה, הִתְחִיל לוֹמַר כֵּן: וּבְבֹא עֵת הַפְּקֻדָּה, שֶׁקָּרְבוּ הַיָּמִים לְהִתְקַיֵּם הַדְּבָרִים, וַיִּמְלֹךְ מֶלֶךְ אַחֵר עַל יְהוּדָה **תַּחַת כָּנְיָהוּ בֶּן יְהוֹיָקִים וְגוֹ׳**:


**reigned as king** Since from now on, i.e., from this point, he comes to tell and to say that the retributions that he prophesied until now were fulfilled, he started to tell in this manner, that when the time of the visitation, when the days drew near that the words should be fulfilled, another king reigned over Judah instead of Coniah the son of Jehoiakim, etc.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:4:1)


**וְיִרְמְיָהוּ בָּא וְיֹצֵא בְּתוֹךְ הָעָם.** כְּשֶׁמָּלַךְ צִדְקִיָּהוּ, צִוָּה וְהוֹצִיאוּהוּ מִבֵּית הַכֶּלֶא שֶׁכְּלָאוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים. שֶׁצִּדְקִיָּה הָיָה צַדִּיק וְדוֹרוֹ רְשָׁעִים, וִיהוֹיָקִים הָיָה רָשָׁע וְדוֹרוֹ צַדִּיקִים (סנהדרין קג.):


**And Jeremiah was coming and going among the people** When Zedekiah assumed the throne, he gave an order and they freed him from the jail where Jehoiakim had imprisoned him, for Zedekiah was a righteous man and the people of his generation were wicked, whereas Jehoiakim was a wicked man and the people of his generation were righteous.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:5:1)


**וְחֵיל פַּרְעֹה יָצָא מִמִּצְרָיִם.** לְעֶזְרָה לִיהוּדָה:


**Now Pharaoh’s army had left Egypt** to aid Judah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:7:1)


**שָׁב לְאַרְצוֹ מִצְרָיִם.** בִּסְפִינוֹת הָיוּ בָאִים, וְרָמַז הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַיָּם, וְהֵצִיף עָלָיו כְּמוֹ נוֹדוֹת וְאוֹבוֹת (כְּלוֹמַר נְפוּחוֹת) דּוֹמוֹת לְעוֹרוֹת בְּנֵי אָדָם. אָמְרוּ זֶה לָזֶה: מַה זֹּאת? אָמְרוּ: אֵלּוּ אֲבוֹתֵינוּ, שֶׁטִּבְּעוּם אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל אֵלּוּ שֶׁאָנוּ הוֹלְכִים לְעֶזְרָתָם בַּיָּם. מִיָּד שָׁבוּ לְאַרְצָם (איכה רבה ד, כ):


**is returning to his land Egypt** They were coming in ships, and the Holy One, blessed be He, signaled to the sea and caused a type of bottles and flasks (i.e., inflated) resembling human skins to float on it. They said to each other, What is this? They replied, These are our forefathers, whom the forefathers of these whom we are going to aid, drowned. They immediately returned to their land.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:12:1)


**לַחֲלִק מִשָּׁם בְּתוֹךְ הָעָם.** ״לְפַלְגָא אַחֲסַנְתָּא דִילֵיהּ בְּגוֹ עַמָּא״ (תרגום יונתן). וּבְעוֹד הַכַּשְׂדִּים צָרִים עָלֶיהָ לֹא הָיָה יוֹצֵא מִמֶּנָּה, שֶׁמִּתְפַּלֵּל עַל הָעִיר שֶׁלֹּא תִלָּכֵד:


**to share [land] from there among the people** Jonathan [renders]: To share his inheritance among the people, and as long as the Chaldeans were besieging it, he did not leave it, for he prayed that it should not be taken.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:13:1)


**וְשָׁם בַּעַל פְּקִדֻּוֹת.** מְמֻנֶּה לִשְׁמֹר שֶׁלֹּא יֵצֵא אִישׁ מִן הָעִיר לְהַשְׁלִים **אֶל הַכַּשְׂדִּים**:


**and there was the captain of the guard** Appointed to guard so that no man should leave the city to make peace with the Chaldeans.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:13:2)


**בֶּן שֶׁלֶמְיָה בֶּן חֲנַנְיָה.** הוּא חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן עַזּוּר (ירמיה כח:א). בֶּן בְּנוֹ הָיָה זֶה, וּלְכָךְ יִחֲסוֹ הַכָּתוּב, לְלַמֵּד שֶׁכָּל הַמַּחֲנִיף לָרָשָׁע סוֹף נוֹפֵל בְּיָדוֹ (סוטה מא:) אוֹ בְּיַד בְּנוֹ אוֹ בְּיַד בֶּן בְּנוֹ; וְיִרְמְיָה הֶחֱנִיף לוֹ, שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ: ״אָמֵן יָקֵם ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרֶיךָ״ (ירמיה כח:ו). וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: שֶׁצִּוָּה חֲנַנְיָה אֶת בְּנוֹ: רְאֵה שֶׁאֲנִי מֵת עַל יְדֵי יִרְמְיָה, וְהֶעֱמִידַנִי שַׁקְרָן בִּנְבוּאָתִי; אִם תּוּכַל לְהַכְשִׁילוֹ, הַכְשִׁילֵהוּ. וּבְנוֹ צִוָּה גַּם הוּא לְבָנָיו כָּךְ, וּכְשֶׁהִגִּיעַ שָׁעָה, הִכְשִׁילוֹ:


**the son of Shelemiah the son of Hananiah** That is Hananiah the son of Azzur. He was his grandson, and for the following reason Scripture traced his lineage, [viz.] to teach that whoever flatters a wicked man eventually falls into his hands or into the hands of his son or into the hands of his grandson, and Jeremiah flattered him, for he said to him, “Amen! (28:6) [So may the Lord do.] May the Lord fulfil your words” (Sotah 41b). And [according to] the Midrash Aggadah, Hananiah commanded his son, “See that I am dying because of Jeremiah, and he made me a liar in my prophecy. If you can cause him to stumble, cause him to stumble. And his son commanded his son also in that manner, and when the time came, he caused him to stumble.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:16:1)


**וְאֶל הַחֲנֻיּוֹת.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לְגַיו מִן חֲנָוָאתָא״; חֲנֻיּוֹת הָיוּ לִפְנֵי אוֹתוֹ בֵּית הַכֶּלֶא:


**and to the cells** Jonathan renders: within the cells. There were cells before that prison.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 37:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/37:21:1)


**מֵחוּץ הָאֹפִים.** מִשּׁוּק נַחְתּוֹמִים, כְּמוֹ ״וְחֻצוֹת תָּשִׂים לְךָ בְדַמֶּשֶׂק״ (מלכים א כ:לד):


**from the street of the bakers** Heb. מחוץ, from the street of the bakers. Cf: “And you shall make streets (וחצות) for yourself in Damascus” (I Kings 20:34).



## Chapter 38


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:2:1)


**וְהָיְתָה לוֹ נַפְשׁוֹ לְשָׁלָל.** וְהָיוּ לוֹ חַיָּיו הַצָּלָה, כְּאִלּוּ שָׁלַל נַפְשׁוֹ; כְּאָדָם הַשּׁוֹלֵל שָׁלָל וּמִשְׂתַּכֵּר, כָּךְ יִשְׂתַּכֵּר זֶה נַפְשׁוֹ:


**and he shall have his life as prey** His life shall be saved, as if had “plundered” his soul; just as a man profits from plundering booty, so this person will profit [by saving] his life.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:4:1)


**עַל כֵּן הוּא מְרַפֵּא.** אֶלַאקְנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, לְשׁוֹן רִפְיוֹן:


**since he weakens** (a lacher in O.F.), an expression of weakness.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:5:1)


**יוּכַל.** כְּמוֹ יָכוֹל, עַל אֲשֶׁר הוּא **מְרַפֵּא אֶת וְגוֹ׳**:


**cannot** Heb. יוּכַל, like יָכוֹל, since he is weakening the hands of, etc.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:6:1)


**הַבּוֹר מַלְכִּיָּהוּ.** שֶׁל מַלְכִּיָּהוּ:


**the pit of Malchiah** lit. the pit Malchiah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:1)


**עֶבֶד מֶלֶךְ.** בָּרוּךְ בֶּן נֵרִיָּה:


**Ebed-melech** Baruch the son of Neriah.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:2)


**הַכּוּשִׁי.** צִדְקִיָּהוּ: מָה כּוּשִׁי מְשֻׁנֶּה בְּעוֹרוֹ, אַף צִדְקִיָּהוּ מְשֻׁנֶּה בְּצִדְקוֹ מִכָּל דּוֹרוֹ (מועד קטן טז:). וְיֵשׁ מִדְרָשׁ דּוֹרֵשׁ **הַכּוּשִׁי** עַל בָּרוּךְ (ספרי במדבר צט):


**the Cushite** Zedekiah. Just as a Cushite is different in his skin, so was Zedekiah different in his righteousness, from all his generation (Moed Katan 16b). And there is a Midrash that expounds “the Cushite,” as referring to Baruch (Sifrei ibid.).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:7:3)


**אִישׁ סָרִיס.** גֶּבֶר רַב:


**an officer** Heb. סריס, a great man.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:9:1)


**וַיָּמָת תַּחְתָּיו וגו׳.** אִלּוּ הִנִּיחוּ אוֹתוֹ בִּמְקוֹמוֹ הָיָה מֵת מֵאֵלָיו בָּרָעָב, וְהֵם הֵרֵעוּ לְקָרֵב מִיתָתוֹ:


**He would have died in his place, etc.** If they had left him in his place, he would have died by himself in the famine, but they have done harm to bring his death closer.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:10:1)


**שְׁלֹשִׁים אֲנָשִׁים.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁכֹּחָן תַּשׁ בָּרָעָב, נִצְרְכוּ כֻּלָּן:


**thirty men** Since they were weakened by the famine, all of them were required.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:11:1)


**בְּלוֹיֵי סְחָבוֹת.** בְּלוּאֵי בְגָדִים הַנִּסְחָבִים:


**worn clouts** Heb. בלוי סחבות, worn out garments, that are dragged.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:1)


**וְהַמְּלָחִים.** לְשׁוֹן רִקָּבוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״כֶּעָשָׁן נִמְלָחוּ״ (ישעיה נא:ו):


**and the rags** Heb. והמלחים, an expression of decay. Cf. “shall vanish (נמלחוּ) like smoke” (Isa. 51: 6).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:2)


**תַּחַת אֲצִילוֹת יָדֶיךָ.** שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיקוּךָ הַחֲבָלִים:


**under your armpits** So that the ropes should not harm you.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:12:3)


**אַצִּילוֹת.** אֵיישִׁילָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**armpits** Heb. אצילות, aisseles in French.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:14:1)


**מָבוֹא הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי הֵיכָן הוּא. וְשֶׁמָּא בְּעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, לוֹמַר שֶׁהַחֵיל וְעֶזְרַת נָשִׁים לְפָנֶיהָ (משנה סכה ה,ד):


**the third entrance** I do not know where it is. Perhaps it is in the Israelites’ Court. The appellation “third” is to say that the hel [the wall surrounding the Temple Court], and the Women’s Court are before it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:1)


**דּוֹאֵג.** יָרֵא:


**“I fear”** Heb. דֹאֵג.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:2)


**פֶּן יִתְּנוּ אוֹתִי. הַכַּשְׂדִּים** בְּיַד **הַיְּהוּדִים**, לוֹמַר: מַלְכְּכֶם יְהִי עַבְדְּכֶם עַל שֶׁקְּדַמְתֶּם לְהַשְׁלִים עִמָּנוּ:


**lest they deliver me** [Lest] the Chaldeans [deliver me] into the hands of the Jews, saying, Your king shall be your slave since you have made peace with us first.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:19:3)


**וְהִתְעַלְּלוּ בִי.** הַיְּהוּדִים. אִיגְבְּרוֹנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and mock me** [i. e.,] the Jews [will mock me] (engregier in O.F.), complain.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:22:1)


**אַנְשֵׁי שְׁלוֹמֶךָ.** נְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר, שֶׁאָמְרוּ לְךָ: לֹא תִלָּכֵד:


**“Your cohorts”** [lit. the men of your peace,] the false prophets, who told you that you would not be caught.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:22:2)


**בַּבֹּץ.** טִיט, לְשׁוֹן ״הֲיִגְאֶה גֹּמֶא בְּלֹא בִצָּה״ (איוב ח:יא):


**into the mire** Heb. בבץ, mire, an expression as in (Job 8:11): “Can the rush shoot up without mire (בצה)?”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 38:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/38:23:1)


**וְאֶת הָעִיר הַזֹּאת תִּשְׂרֹף בָּאֵשׁ.** הֲרֵי אַתָּה כְּאִלּוּ שׂוֹרְפָהּ בְּיָדַיִם, שֶׁאַתָּה גּוֹרֵם לָהּ:


**and this city you shall burn with fire** It is as though you are burning it with your hands for you are causing it.



## Chapter 39


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:3:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ כָּל שָׂרֵי מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל וַיֵּשְׁבוּ בְּשַׁעַר הַתָּוֶךְ וְגוֹ׳.** מַגִּיד שֶׁנִּתְקַיְּמָה כָּאן נְבוּאָה עֶלְיוֹנָה: ״וּבָאוּ וְנָתְנוּ אִישׁ כִּסְאוֹ פֶּתַח שַׁעֲרֵי יְרוּשָׁלִַם וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה א:טו):


**And all the officers of the king of Babylon came and sat in the middle gate, etc.** [This] tells [us] that here the above prophecy, “And they will come and place, each one his throne at the entrance of the gates of Jerusalem, etc.” (1:15) was fulfilled.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:3:2)


**בְּשַׁעַר הַתָּוֶךְ.** ״בְּתַרְעָא מְצִיעָתָא״ (תרגום יונתן); שַׁעַר הָעֲזָרָה, שַׁעַר נִיקָנוֹר הַמִּזְרָחִי, וְהוּא בֵּין שַׁעַר עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים וּבֵין שַׁעַר הַהֵיכָל. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ **שַׁעַר הַתָּוֶךְ** שַׁעַר שֶׁמְּחַתְּכִין בּוֹ הֲלָכוֹת, עֲלֵיהֶם הוּא אוֹמֵר ״מְקוֹם הַמִּשְׁפָּט שָׁמָּה הָרֶשַׁע״ (קהלת ג:טז):


**in the middle gate** Heb. בשער התוך. Jonathan renders: in the middle gate, the gate of the Temple court, the Nicanor Gate, the Eastern Gate, and it is between the gate of the Women’s Court and the gate of the Temple proper. And our Rabbis (Sanh. 103a) said: The gate where the halachoth were decided (מחתכים). Concerning them, Scripture says. “The place of judgment there is wickedness” (Ecc. 3:16).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:4:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ גַּן הַמֶּלֶךְ.** מְעָרָה הָיְתָה לוֹ מִבֵּיתוֹ עַד עַרְבוֹת יְרֵחוֹ, וַיֵּצְאוּ לֵילֵךְ דֶּרֶךְ הַמְּעָרָה (פסיקתא רבתי כו); מָה עָשָׂה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לְקַיֵּם ״וּפָרַשְׂתִּי אֶת רִשְׁתִּי עָלָיו״ (יחזקאל יב:יג)? זִמֵּן צְבִי לִפְנֵי הַכַּשְׂדִּים מְהַלֵּךְ עַל גַּג הַמְּעָרָה חוּץ לָעִיר, וְרָצוּ אַחֲרָיו לְתָפְשׂוֹ וְהוּא רָץ עַד פֶּתַח הַמְּעָרָה, וְרָאוּ אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ יוֹצֵא מִן הַמְּעָרָה:


**by way of the king’s garden** He had a cave from his house to the plains of Jericho, and he went out at night by way of the cave. What did the Holy One, blessed be He, do to fulfill “And I will spread My net over him” (Ezek. 12:13)? He prepared a deer before the Chaldeans walking on the roof the cave outside the city, and they ran after it to seize it, and it ran up to the entrance of the cave, and they saw Zedekiah going out of the cave.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:5:1)


**וַיְדַבֵּר אִתּוֹ מִשְׁפָּטִים.** לָמָּה עָבַרְתָּ עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה?!


**he called him to account** Why did you transgress the oath?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:6:1)


**וְאֵת כָּל חוֹרֵי יְהוּדָה.** אֵלּוּ הַסַּנְהֶדְרִין, עַל שֶׁהִתִּירוּ לוֹ אֶת שְׁבוּעָתוֹ (תנחומא, ואתחנן א). הַחֵטְא הָיָה בְּעֵינֵי הָאֱלֹהִים שֶׁהִתִּירוּ הַשְּׁבוּעָה:


**and...all the nobles of Judah** These are the Sanhedrin, because they had annulled the oath.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:8:1)


**וְאֶת בֵּית הָעָם.** בָּתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת (שבת לב.):


**the houses of the people** The synagogues.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:10:1)


**כְּרָמִים וִיגֵבִים.** שָׂדוֹת לְעָבְדָם. כָּל יְגִיבָה לְשׁוֹן חֲפִירָה, אַפּוֹשִׁי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**vineyards and fields** Heb. ויגבים, fields to till. Every יגיבה is an expression of digging (a fosser in French), to dig.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:13:1)


**רַבֵּי.** כְּמוֹ **שָׂרֵי**:


**the officers of** Heb. רבי, like שָּׂרֵי.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:14:1)


**אֶל גְּדַלְיָהוּ בֶן אֲחִיקָם.** שֶׁנָּפַל אֶל הַכַּשְׂדִּים עַל פִּי יִרְמְיָה, וּכְשֶׁנִּלְכְּדָה הָעִיר מִנָּהוּ שַׁלִּיט בַּנִּשְׁאָרִים שָׁם:


**to Gedaliah the son of Ahikam** who defected to the Chaldeans in compliance with Jeremiah’s orders, and when the city was taken, he appointed him governor over those remaining there.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:16:1)


**וְהָיוּ לְפָנֶיךָ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים לְפָנֶיךָ, שֶׁתִּרְאֶה אוֹתָם מִתְקַיְּמִים:


**and they shall be before you on that day** And the words shall be before you, for you shall see them fulfilled.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 39:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/39:17:1)


**בְּיַד הָאֲנָשִׁים.** בְּיַד הַכַּשְׂדִּים:


**into the hand[s] of the men** into the hand[s] of the Chaldeans.



## Chapter 40


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:1)


**הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר הָיָה.** מַה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסוֹף הָעִנְיָן: ״וַיְהִי מִקֵּץ עֲשֶׂרֶת יָמִים וַיְהִי דְבַר ה׳ וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה מב:ז), אֶלָּא שֶׁכָּתַב תְּחִלָּה עַל מָה נֶאֱמַר לוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה בְּאֵיכָה רַבָּתִי (איכה רבה פתיחתא לד): מַה הָיָה הַדָּבָר? – **וְשׁוּבָה אֶל גְּדַלְיָהוּ**. אָמַר לוֹ: אוֹ אַתְּ נָחִית עִמְּהוֹן וַאֲנָא מְסוֹבֵר הָכָא, אוֹ אַתְּ סוֹבֵר הָכָא וַאֲנָא נָחִית עִמְּהוֹן; אָמַר: אִין אֲנָא נָחִית עִמְּהוֹן, מָה אֲנָא מְהַנֵּי לְהוֹן? אֶלָּא יֵיחוֹת בָּרְיֵיהוֹן עִמְּהוֹן, דְּסַגִּין הוּא מְהַנֵּי לְהוֹן:


**The word that came** What is stated at the end of the section (42:7): “And it was at the end of ten days that the word of the Lord came etc.,” but he first wrote on account of what it was said to him. And the Midrash Aggadah in Lamentations Rabbah (Proem 34): What was the word? “And return to Gedaliah.” He said to him: Either you go down with them and I will remain (?) here, or you remain here and I will go down with them. He replied: If I go down with them, what will I help them? Rather, let their Creator go down with them, for He will help them exceedingly.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:2)


**מִן הָרָמָה.** שֶׁהָיָה גּוֹלֶה מֵעַצְמוֹ עִמָּהֶן; הָיָה רוֹאֶה כַּת שְׁלוּלִים בְּקוֹלָרִין, הָיָה מַכְנִיס צַוָּארוֹ בֵּינֵיהֶם, וְכוּ׳; כִּדְאִיתָא בִּפְסִיקְתָּא (פסיקתא דרב כהנא יג, ט) דְּאַפְטַרְתָּא ״דִּבְרֵי יִרְמְיָהוּ״ (ירמיה א:א). שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ נְבוּזַרְאֲדָן **בָּאזִקִּים**, שֶׁהֲרֵי צִוָּה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר: ״אַל תַּעַשׂ לוֹ מְאוּמָה רָע״ (ירמיה לט:יב):


**from Ramah** Where he went into exile by himself with them. He would see a group [of youths] joined by a collar and would put his neck between them, etc., as is stated in Pesikta of the Haftarah Divrei Yirmiyahu (p. 113). For it is impossible to say that Nebuzaradan put him in chains since Nebuchadnezzar commanded him, “Do him no harm” (above 39:12).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:1:3)


**בָּאֲזִיקִים.** בְּזִיאוֹ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**in fetters** Heb. באזקים, ceps in French, fetters.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:2:1)


**ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ דִּבֶּר וְגוֹ׳.** לָמָּה אַתָּה מִצְטַעֵר עֲלֵיהֶם? וַהֲלֹא אַתָּה נִבֵּאתָ לָהֶם וְלֹא שָׁמְעוּ; כִּדְאִיתָא בַּפְּסִיקְתָּא (פסיקתא דרב כהנא יג, ט):


**“The Lord your God spoke”** Why are you troubled about them? Did you not prophesy to them and they did not hearken? As it is stated in Pesikta d’Rav Kahana (p. 113a).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:4:1)


**פִתַּחְתִּיךָ.** לְשׁוֹן ״וַיְפַתַּח הַגְּמַלִּים״ (בראשית כד:לב), וְכַן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּכְעַן הָא שָׁרִיתָךְ״. וּמְנַחֵם פָּתַר כֻּלָּם לְשׁוֹן פְּתִיחָה מַמָּשׁ:


**I have released you** Heb. פתחתיך, an expression related to “And he untied (ויפתח) the camels” (Gen. 24.32). And so did Jonathan render: Behold, now I have untied you. But Menahem (p. 147) interpreted all of them as an expression of actual opening (פתיחה).



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:5:1)


**וְעוֹדֶנּוּ לֹא יָשׁוּב.** בְּכָל זֹאת לֹא הָיָה חָפֵץ לָשׁוּב; אָמַר לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: **וְשֻׁבָה אֶל גְּדַלְיָה**, אֲשֶׁר הֻפְקַד עַל הָעֲנִיִּים הַנִּשְׁאָרִים; גַּם הֵם צְרִיכִין לְךָ, כִּשְׁאָר הַגּוֹלִים:


**But he would still not return** Despite this, he did not wish to return. The Holy One, blessed be He, said to him, “And return to Gedaliah,” who was appointed over the remaining poor people. They, too, need you, like the rest of the exiles.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:5:2)


**אֲרוּחָה וּמַשְׂאֵת.** ״מַתְּנָן וּנְבִזְבְּזָן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**an allowance and a present** Heb. ארחה ומשאת. Jonathan renders: מתנן וּנבזבן.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 40:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/40:10:1)


**וְקַיִץ.** דְּבֵלָה:


**and dried figs** Heb. וקיץ, dried figs.



## Chapter 41


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:1:1)


**וְרַבֵּי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** וְשָׂרֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ. נִתְקַנְּאוּ בּוֹ שֶׁנָּטַל גְּדֻלָּה:


**and officers of the king** They were jealous of him that he took a high position.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:5:1)


**לְהָבִיא בֵּית ה׳.** כְּשֶׁנָּסְעוּ מִבֵּיתָם לֹא הָיוּ יוֹדְעִים בְּחֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת, וּבַדֶּרֶךְ שָׁמְעוּ וְקָרְעוּ בִּגְדֵיהֶם וְגִלְּחוּ זְקָנָם (מועד קטן כו.):


**to bring to the house of the Lord** When they left home, they were unaware of the destruction of the Temple, and en route, they heard of it, and rent their clothing and shaved their beards.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:5:2)


**וּמִתְגּוֹדְדִים.** לְשׁוֹן שְׂרִיטָה:


**and having cut themselves** An expression [denoting] laceration.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:9:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הִכָּה בְּיַד גְּדַלְיָהוּ.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לָחוּשׁ לַעֲצַת יוֹחָנָן בֶּן קָרֵחַ וְלֹא חָשׁ (ירמיה מ:יג-טז), הֶעֱלָה עָלָיו כְּאִלּוּ נֶהֶרְגוּ עַל יָדוֹ (נדה סא.):


**through Gedaliah** Heb. ביד, [lit. by the hand of Gedaliah]. Since he should have heeded the counsel of Johanan the son of Kareah, and he did not heed, Scripture accounts it for him as though they had been slain by him.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:12:1)


**אֶל מַיִם רַבִּים.** ״עַל בְּרֵכַת מַיִּין סַגִּיאִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**by the great waters** [By the pool of great waters.]



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 41:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/41:17:1)


**בְּגֵרוּת כִּמְהָם.** ״בְּגֵרוּת דִּיהַב דָּוִד לְכִמְהָם בַּר בַּרְזִלַּי גִּלְעָדָאָה״ (תרגום יונתן), כְּשֶׁשָּׁב מִמִּלְחֶמֶת אַבְשָׁלוֹם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר שְׁמוּאֵל ״אִתִּי יַעֲבֹר כִּמְהָם וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל ב יט:לט):


**in Geruth Chimham** In the sojourning place that David gave to Chimham the son of Barzilai the Gileadite when he returned from the war of Absalom, as it is stated in the Book of Samuel (II 19: 39): “Chimham shall go over with me.”



## Chapter 42


### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 42:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/42:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/42:14:1)


**וְקוֹל שׁוֹפָר.** שֶׁתּוֹקְעִין הַצּוֹפִים לְהַזְהִיר אֶת הָעָם:


**the sound of the horn** which the lookouts sound to warn the people.



## Chapter 43


### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 43:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:9:1)


**וְטָמַנְתָּם בַּמֶּלֶט.** מ״ם זוֹ יְסוֹד נוֹפֵל הִיא בַּתֵּיבָה, כְּמוֹ מָקוֹם, מַעֲמָד, מַעֲשֶׂה; וִיסוֹדוֹ לָט, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּלֶט פָּנָיו״ (מלכים א יט:יג): לְאַט אוֹתָהּ בְּתוֹךְ הַטִּיט **בַּמַּלְבֵּן**. **בַּמֶּלֶט** אַנְוְלוֹפְמַנְטוֹ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and hide them in the mortar** Heb. במלט. This ‘mem’ is a defective radical in the word, like מקום, a place, מעמד, a stand, מעשה, a deed, and its root is לט, cf. “he wrapped (וילט) his face” (I Kings 19:13). Envelop them in the mortar in the framework. **in the mortar** (Envolopemant in O.F)



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 43:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:10:1)


**שַׁפְרִירוֹ.** אָהֳלוֹ, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ: ״בְּרוּחוֹ שָׁמַיִם שִׁפְרָה״ (איוב כו:יג):


**his pavilion** Heb. שפרירו, his tent, and a similar case is [found] in Job 26:13: “By His breath, He made the heavens a tent (שפרה).”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 43:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:12:1)


**וְעָטָה אֶת אֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם.** יִכְרוֹךְ כָּל שְׁלָלָהּ וְיֵצֵא, **כַּאֲשֶׁר** יִכְרוֹךְ וְיִגְלוֹל **הָרעֶה אֶת בִּגְדּוֹ** וְיַשְׁלִיכוֹ מֵעַל כְּתֵפָיו כְּשֶׁהוּא מוֹלִיךְ צֹאנוֹ לְפָנָיו:


**and he shall wrap up the land of Egypt** He shall wrap up all its plunder and leave, as a shepherd wraps up and rolls his garment and casts it over his shoulder when he leads his flock before him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 43:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/43:13:1)


**אֶת מַצְּבוֹת בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ.** מִזְבְּחוֹת שֶׁעָשׂוּ שָׁם אוֹתָם שֶׁנִּתְגַּיְּרוּ בִּימֵי חִזְקִיָּהוּ, שְׁבוּיֵי סַנְחֵרִיב שֶׁהוֹלִיךְ מִכּוּשׁ וּמִמִּצְרַיִם לִפְנֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בְּיוֹם מַפַּלְתּוֹ; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר (ישעיהו יט:יח): ״בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִהְיוּ חָמֵשׁ עָרִים בְּאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם מְדַבְּרוֹת שְׂפַת כְּנַעַן וְנִשְׁבָּעוֹת לַה׳ צְבָאוֹת עִיר הַהֶרֶס יֵאָמֵר לְאַחַת״, וְתִרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״עִיר קַרְתָּא בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ דַּעֲתִידָא לְמֵיחֲרַב יִתְאַמַּר דְּהִיא חֲדָא מִנְּהוֹן״:


**the monuments of Beth-shemesh** The altars that those who proselytized in the days of Hezekiah had made there Sennacherib’s captives whom he brought from Cush and from Egypt before Jerusalem on the day of his downfall, as it is stated “On that day there shall be five cities in the land of Egypt speaking the language of Canaan and swearing to the Lord of Hosts one of which will be called ‘the city of Heres’” (Isa. 19.18). Jonathan renders: The city of Beth-shemesh, which is destined to be destroyed, will be said to be one of them.



## Chapter 44


### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:10:1)


**לֹא דֻכְּאוּ.** לִבָּם מֵרָעָתָם:


**They were not crushed** Their hearts were not crushed because of their evil.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:14:1)


**כִּי אִם פְּלִיטִים.** יִרְמְיָה וּבָרוּךְ חָזְרוּ לְשָׁם, שֶׁהִגְלָה נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּר כְּשֶׁנִּתְּנָה אֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם בְּיָדוֹ בְּעֶשְׂרִים וְשֶׁבַע לְמָלְכוֹ (יחזקאל כט:יז-כ). כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו):


**only fugitives** Jeremiah and Baruch returned from there, for Nebuchadnezzar exiled them when the land of Egypt was delivered into his hand in the twenty-seventh year of his reign (Ezek. 29:17). So we learned in Seder Olam (ch. 26).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:19:1)


**הֲמִבַּלְעֲדֵי אֲנָשֵׁינוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעְתָּם:


**without our husbands** Without their knowledge.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:19:2)


**כַּוָּנִים לְהַעֲצִיבָהּ.** דְּפוּסִים כִּדְמוּת עֲצַבִּים (עֲצַבִּים דְּמוּת עַלְמָה כְּמוֹ לְעֵיל כב):


**cakes in her image** Heb. כונים להעצבה, molds in the shape of idols (idols in the shape of a young lady as above ch. 22:28).



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:22:1)


**וְלֹא יוּכַל ה׳ עוֹד לָשֵׂאת.** כְּמוֹ וְלֹא יָכוֹל:


**And He could no longer bear** Heb. ולא יוּכַל, like ולא יָכוֹל, and He could not.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 44:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/44:30:1)


**פַּרְעֹה חָפְרַע.** ״פַּרְעֹה תְּבִירָא״ (תרגום יונתן); וְחִי״ת זוֹ מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בִּמְקוֹם הֵ״א:


**Pharaoh Hophra** Heb. פרעה חפרע, rendered by Jonathan as פרעֹה תבירה, the broken Pharaoh, and this ‘heth’ serves instead of a ‘heh.’



## Chapter 45


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 45:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:3:1)


**וּמְנוּחָה לֹא מָצָאתִי.** לֹא שָׁרְתָה עָלַי שְׁכִינָה לְהִנָּבֵא; כְּמוֹ ״וַתָּנַח עֲלֵיהֶם הָרוּחַ״ (במדבר יא:כו). מָה נִשְׁתַּנֵּיתִי מִתַּלְמִידֵי הַנְּבִיאִים: יְהוֹשֻׁעַ שִׁמֵּשׁ אֶת מֹשֶׁה – נָחָה עָלָיו רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ; אֱלִישָׁע שִׁמֵּשׁ אֶת אֵלִיָּהוּ – נָחָה עָלָיו רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ; אֲנִי – מָה נִשְׁתַּנֵּיתִי? (מכילתא בא פסחא א)


**but I have found no rest** Heb. ומנוחה. The Shechinah has not rested upon me to prophesy. Cf. “And the spirit rested (ותנח) upon them” (Num. 12:26). “Why am I different from the disciples of the prophets? Joshua served Moses and the Holy Spirit rested upon him. Elisha served Elijah and the Holy Spirit rested upon him. Why am I different?”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 45:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:4:1)


**וְאֶת כָּל הָאָרֶץ.** הִיא הַנִּדּוֹנֶת בִּנְתִישָׁה וַהֲרִיסָה זוֹ:


**and it is all the land** that is being judged with this uprooting and tearing down.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 45:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/45:5:1)
