**תְּבַקֵּשׁ לְךָ גְדֹלוֹת.** נְבוּאוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״סַפְּרָה נָא לִי אֵת כָּל הַגְּדֹלוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב ח:ד), ״קְרָא אֵלַי וְאַגִּידָה לְךָ גְּדֹלוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיה לג:ג). אָמַר לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: אִם אֵין כֶּרֶם אֵין סְיָג, וְאִם אֵין צֹאן אֵין רוֹעֶה; אֵינִי נִגְלֶה עַל הַנְּבִיאִים אֶלָּא בִּזְכוּתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל. כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בַּמְּכִילְתָּא בִּתְחִלָּתָהּ (מכילתא בא פסחא א):


**And you seek great things for yourself?** [“Great things” means] prophecies. Cf. “Please tell me all the great things etc.” (II Kings 8:4). “Call to me...and I will tell you great, etc,” (above 33:3). The Holy One, blessed be He, said to him: If there is no vineyard, there is no fence, and if there are no sheep, there is no shepherd. I reveal Myself to the prophets only in the merit of Israel. We learned this in the Mechilta, in the beginning.



## Chapter 46


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:3:1)


**מָגֵן.** שֶׁל עוֹר הוּא, וְהוּא שָׁלוּק; וּכְשֶׁיּוֹצֵא **לַמִּלְחָמָה** מוֹשְׁחוֹ בְּשֶׁמֶן:


**shield** Heb. מגן. It is made of leather, and it is boiled, and when he goes forth to battle, he anoints it with oil.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:3:2)


**צִנָּה.** שֶׁל עֵץ, דְּמוּת תְּרִיסִין שֶׁלָּנוּ:


**and buckler** Heb. וצנה, [made] of wood, in the likeness of our shields.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:4:1)


**מִרְקוּ הָרְמָחִים.** כְּמוֹ ״וּמֹרַק וְשֻׁטַּף״ (ויקרא ו:כא); פורביר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**polish the spears** Heb. מרקו. Cf. “It shall be scoured (וּמֹרַק) and rinsed” (Lev. 6:21), (forbir in O.F., to furbish).



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:5:1)


**הֵמָּה חַתִּים.** מִצְרַיִם:


**them breaking** The Egyptians.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:5:2)


**וְלֹא הִפְנוּ.** עוֹד לַעֲמוֹד בַּמִּלְחָמָה, לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם פְּנַאי:


**and were not freed?** [They were] no longer freed to stand in battle; they had no free time.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:7:1)


**כַּיְאֹר יַעֲלֶה.** שֶׁהָיָה רָגִיל לְהַעֲלוֹת גְּיָסוֹת סְבִיבָיו כְּמוֹ נָהָר, לִשְׁטֹף סְבִיבָיו:


**Who...would come up like the Nile** Who was wont to bring up troops around him like the waters of a river, to flood its environs.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:7:2)


**יִתְגָּעֲשׁוּ מֵימָיו.** ״דְּמִטָּרְפִין מוֹהִי״ (תרגום יונתן):



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:8:1)


**כַּיְאוֹר יַעֲלֶה.** הָיָה רָגִיל לַעֲלוֹת:


**would come up like the Nile** Was accustomed to coming up.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:8:2)


**אוֹבִידָה.** כְּמוֹ אֲאַבֵּד:


**I will destroy** Heb. אֹבִידָה, like אֲאַבֵּד.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:9:1)


**וְהִתְהוֹלְלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן סִכְלוּת:


**and rush madly** Heb. והתהללו, an expression of folly.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:10:1)


**וְרָוְתָה.** לְשׁוֹן שֹׂבַע. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן שִׁכָּרוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״שֵׁכָר״ (במדבר ו:ג) דִּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״מְרַוֵי״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**and its thirst shall be quenched** Heb. ורותה, an expression of satiety. [Another interpretation: It] is an expression of drunkenness; cf. שֵּׁכַר, strong drink, which we translate מְרַוֵי.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:10:2)


**בְּאֶרֶץ צָפוֹן.** שֶׁהָלַךְ פַּרְעֹה עַל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּכַרְכְּמִישׁ, וְגַם נָפַל וְנֶהֶרְגוּ גִּבּוֹרָיו (ירמיה מו:ב-ו), וְהוּא בָּרַח מִן הַמִּלְחָמָה:


**in the north land** For Pharaoh marched on Nebuchadnezzar in Carchemish, and there he fell and his mighty men were slain, and he fled from the battle.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:12:1)


**גִּבּוֹר בְּגִבּוֹר כָּשָׁלוּ.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹרְחִים הָיוּ נִכְשָׁלִים וְנִתְקָלִים זֶה בָּזֶה:


**a mighty man has stumbled upon a mighty man** When they were fleeing they would stumble over each other.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:13:1)


**לָבוֹא נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּר.** זוֹ מַכָּה שְׁנִיָּה, שֶׁהֶחֱרִיב נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר אֶת מִצְרַיִם בִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וְשֶׁבַע לְמָלְכוֹ (יחזקאל כט:יז), כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו):


**concerning the coming of Nebuchadnezzar** This was a second blow, [viz.] that Nebuchadnezzar laid Egypt waste in the twenty-seventh year of his reign (Ezek. 29:17), as we learned in Seder Olam (ch. 26).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:14:1)


**הִתְיַצֵּב וְהָכֵן לָךְ.** כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּלְחָמָה, **וְהָכֵן** וְאַתְקִין:


**“Stand fast and prepare yourself”** for the war. והכן means “and prepare.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:15:1)


**נִסְחַף.** נִשְׁטְפוּ וְהֶחֱלִיקוּ גִבּוֹרֶיךָ, כְּמוֹ ״מָטָר סֹחֵף״ (משלי כח:ג), נִסְתַּחֲפָה שָׂדֵהוּ בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (כתבות ז,ח). וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן כִּלָּיוֹן, וְכֵן חִבֵּר ״מָטָר סֹחֵף״. וְדוֹנָשׁ פָּתַר ״מָטָר סֹחֵף״ כְּמוֹ מָטָר סוֹעֵף, כְּמוֹ ״מְסָעֵף פֻּארָה בְּמַעֲרָצָה״ (ישעיהו י:לג):


**been swept away?** Heb. נסחף. Your mighty men have been swept away and have slid. Cf. “sweeping (סֹחֵף) rain” (Prov. 28:3); “His field has been inundated (נסתחפה)” (Kethuboth 1:6). Menahem (Machbereth p. 126) classified it as an expression of complete destruction, and so did he classify מטר סחף. Dunash, however, interprets מטר סחף like מטר סועף, cutting rain. Cf. “Lops off (מְסָעֵף) the branches with a saw” (Isa. 10:33).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:1)


**הִרְבָּה כּוֹשֵׁל.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהֲדָפוֹ, וְהִרְבָּה לוֹ מִכְשׁוֹל:


**He made many stumbling blocks** The Holy One, blessed be He, Who pushed him down, made many stumbling blocks for him.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:2)


**גַּם נָפַל.** נִתְקַבְּצוּ **אִישׁ אֶל רֵעֵהוּ** לֵאמֹר נָקוּמָה **וְנָשׁוּבָה אֶל עַמֵּנוּ**, מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁיָּצָאנוּ מִשָּׁם לְהִלָּחֵם:


**yea, they joined** Heb. נפל [lit. fell]. They joined one another, saying, Arise and let us go back to our own people, whence we came to wage war.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:16:3)


**חֶרֶב הַיּוֹנָה.** ״חַרְבָּא דְּסָנְאָה דְּהִיא כְּחַמְרָא מְרַוְיָא״ (תרגום יונתן); **הַיּוֹנָה** לְשׁוֹן יַיִן. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן אוֹנָאָה:


**the oppressing sword** Heb. חרב. Jonathan renders: The sword of the enemy, which is like intoxicating wine. היונה. An expression of wine (יין). Another interpretation: It is an expression of oppression (אונאה).



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:1)


**קָרְאוּ שָׁם.** בַּמִּלְחָמָה הִכְרִיזוּ מָשָׁל שֶׁל חֶרְפָּה:


**There they called out** In the battle, they proclaimed publicly a reproachful byword.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:2)


**פַּרְעֹה מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם שָׁאוֹן.** שֶׁהָיָה מַגְבִּיהַּ קוֹל שָׁאוֹנוֹ תְּשׁוּאוֹת, לְהִתְהַלֵּל בְּחֵילוֹתָיו:


**“Pharaoh, king of Egypt, who made a lot of noise”** Who would raise the sound of his noise, the noises to boast with his armies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:17:3)


**הֶעֱבִיר הַמּוֹעֵד.** שֶׁיִּעֵד הַיּוֹם מִלְחָמָה קָבוּעַ לָצֵאת וּלְהִלָּחֵם וְלֹא יָצָא, וְעָבַר יוֹם זְמַן הַמּוֹעֵד (מועד קטן טז.):


**has allowed the appointed time to pass by** For he had appointed a set time to go forth and wage war, and he did not go forth, and the day of the appointed time passed by.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:18:1)


**כִּי כְתָבוֹר בֶּהָרִים.** כִּי כַּאֲשֶׁר דְּבַר אֱמֶת הוּא שֶׁתָּבוֹר קָבוּעַ בֶּהָרִים וְהַר הַכַּרְמֶל עַל הַיָּם, כֵּן בֶּאֱמֶת **יָבוֹא** הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה עַל מִצְרַיִם (מכילתא יתרו בחדש ה):


**that as sure as Tabor is among the mountains** Just as it is a true fact that Tabor is fast among the mountains and Mount Carmel is by the sea, so is it true that this thing shall come upon Egypt.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:19:1)


**כְּלֵי גוֹלָה.** אָדָם יוֹצֵא לַדֶּרֶךְ, מְתַקֵּן לוֹ חֵמֶת וּמְקַדָּה שֶׁל חֶרֶס לִשְׁתּוֹת בָּהּ:


**equipment for exile** When a person embarks on a journey, he prepares for himself a flask and an earthenware cup with which to drink.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:19:2)


**וְנִצְּתָה.** לְשׁוֹן ״צִיָּה״ (ישעיה מא:יח):


**desolate** Heb. ונצחה, an expression of desolation (ציה).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:1)


**עֶגְלָה יְפֵה־פִיָּה.** ״מַלְכוּתָא יָאיָיא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**a fair heifer** A beautiful kingdom.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:2)


**קֶרֶץ.** ״עַמְּמִין קְטוּלִין״ (תרגום יונתן); **קֶרֶץ** לְשׁוֹן חִתּוּךְ, וְכֵן ״מֵחֹמֶר קֹרַצְתִּי״ (איוב לג:ו):


**destruction** peoples that kill [acc. to Jonathan]. קֶרֶץ is an expression of cutting. Cf. “I...was cut (קֹרצתי) out of clay” (Job 33:6).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:20:3)


**מִצָּפוֹן.** מִבָּבֶל:


**from the north** From Babylon.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:1)


**שְׂכִירֶיהָ.** שָׂרֶיהָ, כְּמוֹ ״בְּתַעַר הַשְּׂכִירָה״ (ישעיה ז:כ):


**Also her princes** Heb. שְׂכִרֶיהָ, her princes. Cf. “with the great razor (הַשְׂכִירָה)” (Isa. 7:20).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:2)


**מַרְבֵּק.** קוֹפְלָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**fattened** (kopla in O.F., couple in modern French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:21:3)


**הִפְנוּ.** פָּנוּ עֹרֶף לְבַקֵּשׁ לָנוּס:


**turn around** Turned their back to seek to flee.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:22:1)


**קוֹלָהּ כַּנָּחָשׁ יֵלֵךְ.** הֲרֵי הַנָּחָשׁ בָּא לְלַמֵּד עַל מִצְרַיִם וְנִמְצָא לָמֵד: לָמַדְנוּ מִכָּאן, שֶׁכְּשֶׁאָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַנָּחָשׁ ״עַל גְּחֹנְךָ תֵלֵךְ״ (בראשית ג:יד), וְקִטֵּעַ אֶת רַגְלָיו וְהָלַךְ קוֹלוֹ בְּסוֹף הָעוֹלָם; כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בִּשְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם מִדּוֹת דְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּנוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי (מידה יב):


**Its voice shall go like [that of] the snake** The “snake” comes to teach us about Egypt but ends by learning. For we learn from here that when the Holy One, blessed be He, said to the serpent, “You shall walk on your belly” (Gen. 3:14), He severed his feet, and his voice went to the end of the world. So we learned in the ‘Thirty-two Methods of Rabbi Eliezer the son of Rabbi Jose the Galilean’ (Method 12).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:22:2)


**כִּי בְחַיִל יֵלֵכוּ.** אֵלֶיהָ:


**for they will march with an army** against her.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:23:1)


**כָּרְתוּ יַעְרָהּ.** כֹּחַ יֵשׁ בָּהֶן לִכְרוֹת כָּל יַעְרָהּ:


**They will cut down her forest** They have power to cut down her forest.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:23:2)


**כִּי לֹא יֵחָקֵר. מִסְפַּר** צְבָאָם:


**for they are innumerable** [lit. it shall not be fathomed, i.e.,] the number of their host.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:25:1)


**אֶל אָמוֹן.** עַל אָמוֹן, עַל הַשַּׂר אָמוֹן. יֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן גְּדֻלָּה, כְּמוֹ ״הֲתֵיטְבִי מִנֹּא אָמוֹן״ (נחום ג:ח):


**upon Amon** Heb. אל אמון, [lit. to Amon,] upon Amon, upon the prince Amon. There are some that are an expression of greatness. Cf. “Are you greater than the greatness of No (מִנֹא אָמוֹן)?” (Nahum 3: 8).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:25:2)


**מִנֹּא.** אֲלֶכְּסַנְדְּרִיָּה רַבְּתָא (פסיקתא רבתי לג), לָאשִׁינְיוֹרִיאָ״ה דְּלִישַׁנְדְּרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**of No** The great Alexandria (les seigneurs d’Alexandria in O.F.).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:26:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן.** מִקֵּץ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר יְחֶזְקֵאל (יחזקאל כט:יג):


**and after that** At the end of forty years, as Ezekiel stated (29:1 3ff.).



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:27:1)


**וְאַתָּה אַל תִּירָא.** צַדִּיקִים שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ מִצְרַיִם, שֶׁגָּלוּ שָׁם עַל כָּרְחָם:


**You fear not** The righteous men who were in Egypt, who were exiled there against their will.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:28:1)


**לַמִּשְׁפָּט.** ״בְּדִין חָסוּךְ״ (תרגום יונתן), מְעַט מְעַט:


**justly** With sparing justice, little by little.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 46:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/46:28:2)


**וְנַקֵּה.** לְשׁוֹן טִיאוּט וְהַשְׁמֵד; וְכֵן תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן לְשׁוֹן כְּלָיָה:


**completely destroy** Heb. ונקה [lit. clean,] an expression of sweeping and destroying, and Jonathan too rendered it an expression of destroying.



## Chapter 47


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:1:1)


**אֶל פְּלִשְׁתִּים.** עַל פְּלִשְׁתִּים:


**concerning the Philistines** Heb. אל פלישתים [lit.] to the Philistines.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:1:2)


**בְּטֶרֶם יַכֶּה פַרְעֹה אֶת עַזָּה.** כְּשֶׁהָיָה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר צָר עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בַּשָּׁנָה הָעֲשִׂירִית לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ, יָצָא חֵיל פַּרְעֹה מִמִּצְרַיִם וַיַּעֲלוּ הַכַּשְׂדִּים מֵעַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם (ירמיה לז:ה); שָׁמַע פַּרְעֹה, וְשָׁטַף אֶת עַזָּה וְשָׁב לְאַרְצוֹ. בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (פרק כו):


**before Pharaoh struck Gaza** When Nebuchadnezzar besieged Jerusalem in the tenth year of Zedekiah, Pharaoh’s army came forth from Egypt, and the Chaldeans withdrew from Jerusalem. Pharaoh heard, invaded Gaza and returned to his land. In Seder Olam (ch. 26).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:1)


**שַׁעֲטַת.** אֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגְּמוֹ: ״מִפְּסוֹעִית״:


**the stamping** Heb. שעטת. There is no similar [word in Scripture]. And Jonathan rendered it: from the steps.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:2)


**מֵרַעַשׁ לְרִכְבּוֹ.** מֵרַעַשׁ אֲשֶׁר לְרִכְבּוֹ:


**from the noise of his chariots** Heb. מרעש לרכבו [lit.] from the noise to his chariots.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:3)


**גַּלְגִּלָּיו.** אוֹפַנֵּי מַרְכְּבוֹתָיו:


**his wheels** The wheels of his chariots.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:3:4)


**לֹא הִפְנוּ אָבוֹת אֶל בָּנִים.** לְהַצִּילָם:


**fathers did not turn to sons** to save them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:4:1)


**לְהַכְרִית לְצוֹר וְלְצִידוֹן.** פְּלִשְׁתִּים הָיוּ עוֹזְרִים אוֹתָם מֵאוֹיְבֵיהֶם:


**to cut off from Tyre and Zidon** The Philistines were aiding them against their enemies.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:4:2)


**אִי כַפְתּוֹר.** אִיזְלָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the island of Caphtor** (Isls in O.F.)



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:5:1)


**נִדְמְתָה.** נֶחְרְבָה:


**has become a waste** Heb. נדמתה.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:5:2)


**תִּתְגּוֹדָדִי.** תְּשָׂרְטִי בְּאֵבֶל וּבְבֶכִי:


**will you tear your flesh** Will you tear your flesh in mourning and in weeping?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:6:1)


**חֶרֶב לַה׳.** שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵבִיא לָעוֹלָם:


**sword of the Lord** Which the Holy One, Blessed be He, brings to the world.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 47:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/47:6:2)


**הֵרָגְעִי.** לְשׁוֹן ״מַרְגּוֹעַ״ (ירמיה ו:טז):


**rest** Heb. הרגעי, an expression of מרגועַ rest.



## Chapter 48


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:1:1)


**הַמִּשְׂגָּב.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**Misgab** A place name.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:2:1)


**חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** וּ**מַדְמֵן** – שֵׁם מָקוֹם. וּלְפִי שְׁמוֹתָם הוּא מַזְכִּיר פֻּרְעָנוּתָם:


**in Heshbon...Madmen** These are place names, and according to their names he mentions their retribution.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:2:2)


**תִדֹּמִּי.** תֶּחֱרָבִי:


**you shall be a waste** Heb. תִּדֹּמִי.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:4:1)


**צְעִירֶיהָ.** ״שָׁלְטוֹנְהָא״ (תרגום יונתן) הַצְּעִירִים מִן הַמְּלָכִים:


**her youths** Jonathan [renders]: Her governors, younger than the kings.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:5:1)


**מַעֲלֵה הַלּוּחִית.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם הוּא מַעֲלוֹת הַר:


**the ascent of Luhith** It is a place name, the ascent of a mountain.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:6:1)


**כַּעֲרוֹעֵר.** מִגְדַּל עֲרוֹעֵר עָשׂוּי בַּמִּדְבָּר, וְאֵין סְבִיבָיו יִשּׁוּב כִּי אִם שׁוֹכְנֵי אֹהָלִים. וּמִגְדָּל הָעוֹמֵד בְּאֵין יִשּׁוּב נִרְאֶה כְּחוּרְבָה; כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ **כַּעֲרוֹעֵר** – שֵׁם עֵץ מֵעֲצֵי הַיַּעַר:


**like Aro’er** The tower of Aro’er that is made in the desert, and there is no civilization around it except tent dwellers, and a tower standing without an inhabited region appears as ruins. So did Jonathan render it. (See Isaiah 17:2, Commentary Digest.) But Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 137) explained כּערוער as the name of a tree of the forest trees.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:9:1)


**תְּנוּ צִיץ לְמוֹאָב.** תְּנוּ כָנָף לְמוֹאָב. כָּל דְּבָר תְּלַאי הַתּוֹלֶה וּבוֹלֵט מִן הָאָדָם וּמִן הַבְּהֵמָה קָרוּי צִיץ, כְּמוֹ ״וְהָיָה לָכֶם לְצִיצִת״ (במדבר טו:לט), ״וַיִּקָּחֵנִי בְּצִיצִת רֹאשִׁי״ (יחזקאל ח:ג):


**Give wings to Moab** Heb. ציץ. Give wings to Moab. Every appendage that hangs or protrudes from man or beast is called ציץ. Cf. “And it shall be to you for fringes (לציצית)” (Num. 15:39); “And He took me by the locks (בציצית) of my head” (Ezek. 8:3).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:9:2)


**כִּי נֵצֵא תֵּצֵא.** כִּי בִּמְעוֹפָהּ וְחִפָּזוֹן תֵּצְאוּ בַּגּוֹלָה. וְיִהְיֶה נֵצֵא מִגְּזֵרַת נִצָּהּ, כֵּן חָבְרוּ מְנַחֵם, וְכֵן ״כִּי נָצוּ גַּם נָעוּ״ (איכה ד:טו), וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם ״אַעֲדוֹ כִּתְרָא מִמּוֹאָב״, **תְּנוּ** לַאֲחֵרִים **צִיץ** הַנֵּזֶר אֲשֶׁר **לְמוֹאָב**:


**for she shall fly away** Heb. נצא תצא. For with flight and haste you shall go forth into exile, and נָצֹא will be of the conjugation of נוֹצָה, a feather. So did Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 151) classify it, and so “Yea, they fled away (נָצוּ) and wandered” (Lam. 4:15). Jonathan renders: Remove the crown from Moab. Give others the crown of Moab.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:10:1)


**מְלֶאכֶת ה׳.** מְלֶאכֶת הַשְׁחָתַת מוֹאָב, שֶׁהִיא שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם:


**the Lord’s work** The work of the destruction of Moab, which is the mission of the Omnipresent.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:11:1)


**לֹא נָמָר.** לֹא נֶחְלַף:


**did not change** Heb. נָמָר.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:1)


**צוֹעִים.** מְרִיקִים, וְכֵן ״מִהַר צֹעֶה לְהִפָּתֵחַ״ (ישעיהו נא:יד) – הוּא הִלּוּךְ הַמֵּעַיִם בְּשִׁלְשׁוּל:


**men to pour** Heb. ציעים, those who pour. Cf. “What must be poured out (צֹעֶה) hastened to be opened” (Isa. 51:14). That is the moving of the bowels by pouring.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:2)


**וְנִבְלֵיהֶם.** חֲבִיּוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל מוֹאָבִים:


**his jars** Heb. נבליהם [lit. their jars], the Moabites’ flasks.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:12:3)


**וְנַפֵּצוּ.** יְשַׁבְּרוּ, כְּמוֹ ״אֵיכָה נֶחְשְׁבוּ לְנִבְלֵי חֶרֶשׂ״ (איכה ד:ב); ״כָּל נֵבֶל יִמָּלֵא יָיִן״ (ירמיה יג:יב):


**smash** Heb. ינפצו, they shall break. Cf. “How were they regarded as earthen jars (לְנִבְלֵי) (Lam. 4:2). Also, “Every bottle (נֵבֶל) will be filled with wine” (supra 13.12).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:13:1)


**מִכְּמוֹשׁ.** אֲשֶׁר בָּטַח בּוֹ:


**of Chemosh** in whom they trusted.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:13:2)


**מִבֵּית אֵל.** עֵגֶל הַזָּהָב (מלכים א יב:לב):


**of Beth-el** The golden calf.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:17:1)


**נֻדוּ לוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן קִינָה, כְּמוֹ ״אַל תָּנֻדוּ לוֹ״ (ירמיה כב:י):


**Lament for them** Heb. נדו, an expression of lamentation. Cf. “and do not lament (תָּנוּד) for him.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:19:1)


**שַׁאֲלִי נָס וְנִמְלָטָה.** שַׁאֲלִי מִן הַנָּסִים וְהַנִּמְלָטִים מִמּוֹאָב **מַה נִּהְיָתָה** אִם נִמְלַט כְּלוּם מֵהַשְׁחִית:


**ask those who have fled and escaped** Ask those who flee and escape from Moab, what transpired, if anyone escaped from being destroyed



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:21:1)


**וּמִשְׁפָּט בָּא.** וּפוּרְעָנוּת בָּא:


**and judgment has come** Retribution has come.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:26:1)


**הַשְׁכִּירוּהוּ.** הַשְׁקוּהוּ תַּרְעֵלָה (ישעיהו נא:יז) עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּכֵּר:


**Make him drunk** Give him to drink [the cup of] weakness until he is drunk.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:26:2)


**גַּם הוּא.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה שְׂחוֹק לוֹ:


**he too** Just as Israel was derided by him [i.e., Moab].



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:27:1)


**תִּתְנוֹדָד.** הָיִיתָ מֵנִיד בְּרֹאשְׁךָ וּמִתְלוֹצֵץ עַל חֻרְבָּנוֹ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **כִּי מִדֵּי דְבָרֶיךָ בּוֹ** – שֶׁהָיִיתָ מִתְרוֹנֵן עַל חָרְבָּנוֹ, אַתָּה **תִּתְנוֹדָד** – לָצֵאת בַּגּוֹלָה. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״עַל דְּאַסְגִּיתוֹן עֲלֵיהוֹן פִּתְגָּמִין בְּכֵן תִּיטַלְטְלוּן״:


**you shake your head** Heb. תתנודד. You would shake your head and scoff at their destruction. Another interpretation: For because of your words about him, that you would sing about his destruction, you are going to wander (מתנודד) to go out into exile. And so did Jonathan render: Because you spoke many words about them, therefore you shall wander.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:28:1)


**בְּעֶבְרֵי פִי פָחַת.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַמַּיִם כּוֹרִין תַּחַת גִּדּוּדֵי שְׂפַת הַנַּחַל הַגְּבוֹהִים, וּכְשֶׁהַנַּחַל מִתְמַעֵט נִמְצֵאת הַגּוּמָא רֵיקָנִית, וְהַיּוֹנָה מְקַנֶּנֶת בְּאַחַת מֵעֶבְרֵי הַגּוּמָא, וּכְשֶׁנַּחַל רַבָּה שׁוֹטֵף אֶת הַקֵּן וְהִיא נוֹדֶדֶת מִשָּׁם; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי. וְיוֹנָתָן לֹא תִרְגֵּם כֵּן: ״וְהֲוָה כְּיוֹנָא דְּשַׁבְקָא פּוּם שׁוּבְכָהּ וְנָחֲתָא וְשָׁרְיָא בְּאַרְעִית גּוּבָא״:


**in the sides of the mouth of a pit** The place where the water passes under the high embankments of the bank of a wadi, and when the wadi is diminished, the pit is found to be empty, and the dove nests in one of the sides of the pit. And when the wadi increases, it inundates the nest and she wanders from there. So I heard, but Jonathan did not render in that manner, [but:] Be like a dove, that leaves the opening of the dovecote and goes down and rests at the bottom of a pit.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:1)


**עֶבְרָתוֹ וְלֹא כֵן.** שִׂנְאָתוֹ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא בֶּאֱמֶת וְלֹא בְמִשְׁפָּט:


**his wrath, and it is not so** His hatred of Israel is not in truth or in justice.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:2)


**בַּדָּיו.** גִּבּוֹרָיו:


**his mighty men** Heb. בדיו [lit.] his branches.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:30:3)


**לֹא כֵן עָשׂוּ.** לֹא כַּגְּמוּל הֵשִׁיבוּ לְזֶרַע אַבְרָהָם, שֶׁנִּלְחַם עִם הַמְּלָכִים וְהִצִּיל אֶת לוֹט אֲבִיהֶם (בראשית יד:יד-טז):


**have not dealt properly** They did not recompense justly the seed of Abraham, who fought with the kings and saved Lot, their ancestor.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:31:1)


**קִיר חֶרֶשׂ.** עִיר מֵאֶרֶץ **מוֹאָב**:


**Kir-heres** A city of the land of Moab.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:31:2)


**יֶהְגֶּה.** גּוֹנֵחַ כְּיוֹנִים הוֹגִים:


**they shall moan** Moaning like moaning doves.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:1)


**מִבְּכִי יַעְזֵר אֶבְכֶּה לָּךְ.** כְּשֶׁנֶּאֶמְרָה נְבוּאָה זוֹ כְּבָר חָרְבָה יַעְזֵר, וְהִיא סְמוּכָה לְמוֹאָב:


**From the weeping of Jazar I will weep for you** When this prophecy was said, Jazer had already been destroyed, and that was near Moab.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:2)


**אֶבְכֶּה לָּךְ.** אֶבְכֶּה בִּשְׁבִילֵךְ דּוּגְמַת אוֹתוֹ בֶּכִי, כִּי גַם אַתְּ תֶּחֱרְבִי כְּמוֹהוּ:


**I will weep for you** I will weep for you, similar to that other weeping [i.e., for Jazer], for you will be destroyed just like [Jazer].


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:3)


**הַגֶּפֶן שִׂבְמָה.** אֶרֶץ כְּרָמִים הָיְתָה:


**O grape vine of Sibmah** It was a land of vineyards.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:4)


**נְטִישֹׁתַיִךְ.** לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה אוֹתָהּ לְגֶפֶן, דִּמָּה אֶת גִּבּוֹרֶיהָ לִנְטִישׁוֹת הַגֶּפֶן:


**your tendrils** Since he compared them to a grape vine, he compared their mighty men to the tendrils of the vine.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:5)


**עָבְרוּ יָם.** הָלְכוּ בַּגּוֹלָה:


**crossed the sea** i.e., they went into exile.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:32:6)


**קֵיצֵךְ.** לְשׁוֹן ״קַיִץ״ (שמואל ב טז:א) – תְּאֵנִים; וְ״קִצֵּךְ״ (ירמיה נא:יג), שֶׁהִיא לְשׁוֹן קֵץ – אֵין בּוֹ יוֹ״ד וְהַצָּדִ״י דְּגוּשָׁה:


**your dried figs** Heb. קיצך, an expression of dried figs, but קִצֵךְ (51:13), an expression of (קץ), an end, has no ‘yud’ and its ‘tzaddi’ has a ‘dagesh.’



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:1)


**וְנֶאֶסְפָה.** וְכָלְתָה, שֶׁלֹּא תֵרָאֶה:


**shall be taken away** Heb. ונאספה, shall be ended that it shall not be seen.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:2)


**לֹא יִדְרֹךְ.** עֲנָבִים בְּקוֹל עֲנוֹת **הֵידָד**, בְּשִׂמְחָה, כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים:


**one shall not tread** grapes with a voice of shouting, “Hedad!” with joy as they were wont to do.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:33:3)


**הֵידָד לֹא הֵידָד.** שֶׁהֵידָד יִצְעֲקוּ לֹא יְהִי הֵידָד הָרִאשׁוֹן, כִּי אִם הֵידָד שֶׁל מְנוּסַת חֶרֶב:


**The shouting shall be no shouting** The shouting that they will cry will not be the first shouting, but the shouting of fleeing the sword.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:34:1)


**עֶגְלַת שְׁלִשִׁיָּה.** מְדִינָה גַּבְרְתָּנִית:


**O third born heifer** Heb. עגלת שלישיה, mighty province.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:36:1)


**עַל כֵּן יִתְרַת עָשָׂה.** עַל אֲשֶׁר גַּאֲוָה עָשָׂה, לְכָךְ **אָבָדוּ**:


**because they dealt haughtily** יתרת עשה. Because they dealt haughtily, they perished.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:37:1)


**גְּדֻדוֹת.** שְׂרִיטוֹת:


**scratches** Heb. גדדת.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:39:1)


**אֵיךְ חַתָּה הֵילִילוּ.** הֵילִילוּ עָלֶיהָ וְאִמְרוּ אֵיךְ חַתָּה:


**Wail, “How was she dismayed?”** Wail about her and say, “How was she dismayed?”



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:40:1)


**כַּנֶּשֶׁר יִדְאֶה.** הָאוֹיֵב **אֶל מוֹאָב**:


**like an eagle he shall soar** [i.e.,] the enemy [shall swoop] upon Moab.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:40:2)


**יִדְאֶה.** יָעוּף:


**shall soar** Heb. ידאה, shall fly.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:41:1)


**הַקְּרִיּוֹת.** שֵׁם הָעִיר:


**Kerioth** Heb. הקריות, the name of the city.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:43:1)


**פַּחַד וָפַחַת.** אֵלּוּ שְׁנֵי דְבָרִים קָשִׁים זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה, שֶׁהַפַּחַד גּוֹרֵם לוֹ לָנוּס, וְהַפַּחַת מוּכָן לִיפּוֹל בּוֹ:


**Fright and a pit** These are two hard things, one next to the other, for fright causes him to flee, and the pit is ready to fall into.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:43:2)


**פַּחַת.** פוֹשָׁ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


פַּחַת is (fosse in French) a pit.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:1)


**בְּצֵל חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** שֶׁהִיא גְבוֹהָה וְיֵשׁ לָהּ צֵל:


**In the shadow of Heshbon** which is high and has a shadow.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:2)


**עָמְדוּ.** הַנָּסִים לָנוּחַ בַּצֵּל, אֲשֶׁר לֹא הָיָה בָּהֶן עוֹד **כֹּחַ** לָנוּס:


**stood** Those who were fleeing [stood] to rest in the shadow since they had no more strength to flee.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:3)


**מִבֵּן סִיחוֹן.** מֵעִיר סִיחוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״בֶּן רְאוּבֵן״ בִּיהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע טו:ו):


**from the city of Sihon** Heb. מבין, from the city of Sihon. Cf. “the city of (בֶּן) Reuben” in Joshua (15:6).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:4)


**וְקָדְקֹד.** אַטְרִיפִירְצְדָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן מָקוֹר, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וִיקִירֵי״, לְשׁוֹן יְקָר:


**and the crown of the head** Heb. וקדקד, (estrit in Provencal or estrif in O.F. [to fight]) and Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 158) classified it as an expression of a spring (מָקוֹר) and Jonathan rendered: יַקִּירֵי the esteemed ones, an expression of יְקָר, esteem.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 48:45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/48:45:5)


**בְּנֵי שָׁאוֹן.** אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ מַגְבִּיהִים קוֹל תִּפְאָרֶת:


**the tumultuous ones** Heb. בני שאון, those who would raise their voice with boasting.



## Chapter 49


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:1:1)


**הֲבָנִים אֵין לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** בֵּי״ת רָפָה וְהֵ״א חֲטַף פַּתָּח, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָה:


**Has Israel no sons?** The ‘beth’ is weak [without a ‘dagesh’], and the ‘heh’ is vowelized with a ‘hataph-pattah,’ which is an expression of wonder [interrogative].


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:1:2)


**מַלְכָּם.** עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁל בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן מַלְכָּם שְׁמָהּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וּלְמִלְכֹּם תּוֹעֲבַת בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן״ (מלכים ב כג:יג):


**Malcam** The deity of the children of Ammon is named Malcam, as it is stated: “And to Milcom, the abomination of the children of Ammon” (II Kings 23:13), an expression of מֶלֶךְ, a king.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:1)


**כִּי שֻׁדְּדָה עַי.** הַסְּמוּכָה לָהּ, וּמֵאֶרֶץ עַמּוֹן הִיא:


**for Ai has been spoiled** Ai, that is near it, [has been spoiled,] and it is of the land of Ammon.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:2)


**בְּנוֹת רַבָּה.** רַבַּת בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן:


**O villages of Rabbah** Rabbah of the children of Ammon.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:3:3)


**וְהִתְשׁוֹטַטְנָה בַּגְּדֵרוֹת.** תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן: ״וְאִתְהַמְּמוֹן בְּסִיעָן״ – הִתְגּוֹדְדוּ בַּחֲיָלוֹת; פָּתַר וְהִתְשׁוֹטַטְנָה – לְשׁוֹן חֲיָלוֹת מְשׁוֹטְטִים, וּגְדֵרוֹת – לְשׁוֹן ״גִּדְרוֹת צֹאן״ (במדבר לב:טז) הַמְּלֵאִים עֲדָרִים. וְיֵשׁ לִפְתֹּר: **וְהִתְשׁוֹטַטְנָה** – וְהִתְעוֹלַלְנָה בְּזֶבֶל **גִּדְרוֹת** צֹאן, לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן; ״וַיִּבֶז עֵשָׂו״ (בראשית כה:לד): ״וְשָׁט עֵשָׂו״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**and mobilize in companies** Heb. והתשוטטנה בגדרות. Jonathan renders: ואתהמכוּן בסיעָן, mobilize in companies. He interprets והתשוטטנה as an expression of armies that run to and fro (משוטטים) ; and גדרות is an expression of sheepcotes full of flocks, and can be interpreted as “and roll in the dung of the sheepcotes,” an expression of contempt. [Onkelos translates:] “And Esau despised” (Gen. 25:34), ושט עשו.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:1)


**תִּתְהַלְלִי.** תִּתְפָּאֲרִי, וַונְטִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**You boast** Heb. תתהללי (vanter in French).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:2)


**בָּעֲמָקִים.** שֶׁאַרְצֵךְ אֶרֶץ עֲמָקִים וְאֵין עֲצִירַת גְּשָׁמִים קָשָׁה לָהּ, שֶׁאֵין חַמָּה שׁוֹלֶטֶת בָּהּ:


**about the valleys?** That your land is a land of valleys and lack of rainfall is not harmful to it since the sun does not penetrate into it.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:4:3)


**זָב עִמְקֵךְ.** מַכַּת רֹב גְּשָׁמִים בָּאָה, עַד שֶׁזָּבוּ הַמַּיִם בָּעֵמֶק וְשָׁטְפוּ שָׁרְשֵׁי הַתְּבוּאָה; כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**Your valley is flowing** A misfortune of an overabundance of rain shall come upon it until the water will flow in the valley and inundate the roots of the grain So did Menahem [Machbereth Menahem p. 77] explain it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:5:1)


**אִישׁ לְפָנָיו.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר יוּכַל לְמַהֵר וְלָנוּס:


**each man forward** Anyone who can hurry and flee.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:5:2)


**לַנֹּדֵד.** לְשׁוֹן ״נָע וָנָד״ (בראשית ד:יב):


**the wanderer** The term [לנדד] derives from נע ונד, “a wanderer and an exile” (Gen. 4:14).



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:1)


**בְּתֵימָן.** אֱדוֹם בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְהָיָה לָכֶם פְּאַת נֶגֶב מִמִּדְבַּר צִן עַל יְדֵי אֱדוֹם״ (במדבר לד:ג):


**in the south?** Edom is in the south of Eretz Israel, as it is stated. “And your south side shall be from the desert of Zin close by the side of Edom” (Num. 34:3).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:2)


**מִבָּנִים.** מִדּוֹר זֶה:


**from the sons** Heb. מבנים, from this generation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:7:3)


**נִסְרְחָה.** ״אִתְקַלְקְלַת״ (תרגום יונתן):


**has spoiled** Heb. נסרחה.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:8:1)


**הָפְנוּ.** הִתְבַּעֲרוּ מִן הַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה, לְשׁוֹן ״וּפִנּוּ אֶת הַבַּיִת״ (ויקרא יד:לו):


**Clear out!** Heb. הפנו. Vacate this place. This is an expression of “And they shall clear out (וּפְּנוּ) the house” (Lev. 14:36).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:8:2)


**הֶעְמִיקוּ לָשֶׁבֶת.** לְהֵחָבֵא בְּעִמְקֵי מִסְתָּרִים. **נֻסוּ הָפְנוּ הֶעְמִיקוּ.** כֻּלָּם לְשׁוֹן צִוּוּי הֵם:


**Dwell deep** to hide in the secret valleys. נֻסוּ הָפְנוּ הֶעְמִקוּ are all in the imperative form.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:9:1)


**אִם בּוֹצְרִים בָּאוּ לָךְ חָשַׂפְתִּי אֶת וְגוֹ׳ שְׁמוּעָה שָׁמַעְתִּי וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל כְּעֵין דּוּגְמָא זוֹ נִבָּא עוֹבַדְיָה עַל אֱדוֹם (עובדיה א:א-ה), וּלְפִי שֶׁסִּיגְנוֹן אֶחָד עוֹלֶה לְכַמָּה נְבִיאִים וְאֵין שְׁנֵי נְבִיאִים מִתְנַבְּאִים בְּסִיגְנוֹן אֶחָד (סנהדרין פט.), בְּמִקְצָת הַדְּבָרִים מִשְׁתַּנִּין:


**If vintagers have come upon you, etc.** I have exposed Esau, etc. I have heard tidings, etc. On this order, Obadiah prophesied concerning Edom, but since one watchword is passed to many prophets, but no two prophets prophesy under the same watchword, they differ in several words.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:9:2)


**לֹא יַשְׁאִירוּ עוֹלֵלוֹת.** בִּתְמִיהָּ: וְהַשּׁוֹדְדִים שֶׁבָּאוּ עָלֶיךָ, לֹא הִשְׁאִירוּ לְךָ כְּלוּם!


**will they not leave over gleaning grapes?** This is the interrogative. Yet the spoilers who came upon you left over nothing for you.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:10:1)


**חָשַׂפְתִּי.** גִּלֵּיתִי, כְּמוֹ ״מַחְשֹׂף הַלָּבָן״ (בראשית ל:לז). וְאַתָּה הִסְגַּרְתָּ שְׂרִידֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמַעַן לֹא יִשָּׁאֵר מֵהֶם אִישׁ; כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אַל תַּסְגֵּר שְׂרִידָיו״ (עובדיה א:יד):


**I exposed** Heb. חשפתי, uncovered. Cf. “the exposure (מַחְשֹף) of the white” (Gen. 30:37). And you delivered the survivors of Israel in order that no one remain of them, as it is stated: “Neither should you have delivered his survivors” (Obadiah 1:14).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:10:2)


**וְנֶחְבָּה לֹא יוּכָל.** וִיבַקֵּשׁ לְהֵחָבֵא וְלֹא יוּכָל:


**and he could not hide** He will seek to hide but will be unable.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:11:1)


**עָזְבָה יְתֹמֶיךָ.** שֶׁיִּתַּמְתָּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Leave your orphans** whom you have orphaned of Israel.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:12:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אֵין מִשְׁפָּטָם לִשְׁתּוֹת.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ אַחִים לָהֶם, וְאִם הֵרֵעוּ לָהֶם, אֵין לָהֶם מִשְׁפַּט מָוֶת כְּמוֹתְךָ:


**those who do not deserve to drink** For they were not brothers to Israel, and if they harmed them, they do not deserve the death sentence like you.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:13:1)


**לְחֶרְפָּה וְלִקְלָלָה תִּהְיֶה בָצְרָה.** בָּצְרָה מֵעָרֵי מוֹאָב, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי הָעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״יוֹבָב בֶּן זֶרַח מִבָּצְרָה״ (בראשית לו:לג), הִיא לוֹקָה עִמּוֹ:


**that Bozrah shall become desolation, reproach, waste, and a curse** Bozrah was one of the cities of Moab, but because it raised up a king for Edom, as the matter is stated, “And Jobab the son of Zerah from Bozrah” (Gen. 36:33), it will suffer with them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:15:1)


**קָטֹן נְתַתִּיךָ.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם מֶלֶךְ בֶּן מֶלֶךְ; לֹא הָיָה דַּרְכָּם בְּרוֹמִי לְהַעֲמִיד מֶלֶךְ בֶּן מֶלֶךְ (עבודה זרה י.):


**I have made you small** For they have no king who is the son of a king. It was not their custom to set up a king the son of a king.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:1)


**תִּפְלַצְתְּךָ.** לְשׁוֹן בֶּהָלָה; שֶׁהָיִיתָ מְמַהֵר וְנִבְהָל לְעוֹרֵר שְׂטִימָתְךָ לְאָחִיךָ. וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִין לְשׁוֹן ״פַּלָּצוּת״ (ישעיה כא:ד); וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִין לְשׁוֹן ״מִפְלֶצֶת״ (מלכים א טו:יג):


**Your hastiness** Heb. תפלצתך, an expression of haste, for you hurried and were hasty to arouse your hatred for your brother. And there are some who translate this as an expression of fright (פַּלָצוּת), and there are some who translate it as an expression of an image (מפלצת).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:2)


**הִשִּׁיא אוֹתָךְ.** לְהָרַע לָהֶם:


**has enticed you** to harm them.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:3)


**שׁוֹכְנִי.** שׁוֹכֵן:


**you who dwell** Heb. שכני, like שׁוֹכֵן.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:16:4)


**תּוֹפְשִׂי.** כְּמוֹ ״תּוֹפֵשׂ״ (ירמיה נ:טז), וְהַיּוֹ״ד יְתֵרָה בּוֹ:


**who hold** תפשי, like תּופֵשׂ, and the ‘yud’ is superfluous in it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:1)


**כְּאַרְיֵה יַעֲלֶה מִגְּאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן.** שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם אֲרָיוֹת, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן, כֵּן יַעֲלֶה הָאוֹיֵב **אֶל נְוֵה אֵיתָן**:


**as a lion** that ascends from the heights of the Jordan, which is a place frequented by lions, as Jonathan renders, so shall the enemy ascend upon the strong dwelling place.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:2)


**אַרְגִּיעָה.** אַקְרִיב אֶת הָרֶגַע:


**for I will bring the moment** Heb. ארגיעה.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:3)


**אֲרִיצֶנּוּ מֵעָלֶיהָ.** פִּתְאוֹם וּבְהַצְלָחָה יַחֲרִיבֶנָּה, וְיָשׁוּב לוֹ בְּרֶגַע קָטָן אֶל מְקוֹמוֹ. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: **אֲרִיצֶנּוּ מֵעָלֶיהָ** מִמַּעַל לָהּ, שֶׁיְּהֵא מִתְגַּבֵּר עָלֶיהָ וְעֶלְיוֹן לָהּ; וְכֵן ״מִמַּעַל לָרָקִיעַ״ (יחזקאל א:כו); וְכֵן ״מִמַּעַל לוֹ״ (ישעיה ו:ב); ״מֵעַל לְמַדָּיו״ (שמואל א יז:לט):


**I will hasten him away from her** Suddenly and successfully he shall destroy her and return in a brief movement to his place. But I say that אריצנוּ מעליה means: I will hasten him over her, that he shall overpower her and be over her. Cf. “Above (ממעל) the expanse” (Gen. 1: 7); “Above (ממעל) for Him” (Isa. 6:2); “Over (מֵעַל) his garments” (I Sam. 17:39).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:4)


**וּמִי בָחוּר.** אוֹתוֹ שֶׁבָּחַרְתִּי לִנְקוֹם נִקְמָתִי עָלֶיהָ **אֶפְקֹד**:


**and he who is chosen** He whom I have chosen to wreak My vengeance, I will appoint over her.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:5)


**וּמִי יוֹעִידֵנִי.** לַמִּלְחָמָה, לְהִלָּחֵם אִתִּי:


**and who will appoint Me a time** For bartle, to wage war with Me?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:19:6)


**רוֹעֶה.** מֶלֶךְ:


**a shepherd** A king.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:1)


**אִם לֹא.** לְשׁוֹן בֶּאֱמֶת:


**if...not** An expression of ‘indeed.’


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:2)


**יִסְחָבוּם.** יִגְרְרוּם:


**drag them** Heb. יסחבום.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:3)


**צְעִירֵי הַצֹּאן.** בְּזוּיֵי הָאֻמּוֹת. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (יומא י.): פָּרַס, שֶׁהוּא הַצָּעִיר בִּבְנֵי יֶפֶת (בראשית י:ב):


**the young of the flock**. The despised of the nations, and our Rabbis [Yoma 10] expounded that it refers to Persia, who was the youngest of Japheth’s sons.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:20:4)


**יַשִּׁים.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁמָמָה:


**make desolate** Heb. ישים, an expression of desolation (שממה).



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:23:1)


**בּוֹשָׁה חֲמָת וְאַרְפָּד.** עָרֵי אֲרָם, **כִּי שָׁמְעוּ שְׁמֻעָה רָעָה** עַל דַּמֶּשֶׂק, שֶׁהִיא רֹאשׁ אֲרָם, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כִּי רֹאשׁ אֲרָם דַּמֶּשֶׂק״ (ישעיהו ז:ח); לְכָךְ **נָמֹגוּ**:


**Hamath and Arpad are shamed** [Hamath and Arpad,] cities of Aram, [are shamed,] for they heard bad news concerning Damascus, which is the head of Aram, as it is stated. “For the head of Aram is Damascus” (Isa. 7:8). Therefore, they melted.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:23:2)


**בְּיָם דְּאָגָה.** דַּאֲגָתָהּ דּוֹמָה לְדַאֲגַת הַיָּם **הַשְׁקֵט לֹא יוּכָל**. כָּל דְּאָגָה לְשׁוֹן יִרְאָה הוּא:


**there is worry in the sea** He compares her worry to the worry of the sea, which cannot be quiet. Every instance of דאגה, worry, is an expression of fear.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:24:1)


**רָפְתָה דַמֶּשֶׂק.** רָפוּ יָדֶיהָ מֵהִלָּחֵם:


**Damascus become feeble** Her hands have become too feeble to wage war.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:24:2)


**וְרֶטֶט.** זִיעַ:


**trembling** Heb. רֶטֶט.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:25:1)


**אֵיךְ לֹא עֻזְּבָה.** לֹא נִתְמַלֵּאת עָפָר סְבִיב חוֹמָתָהּ לְחַזֵּק הַחוֹמָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּעַזְבוּ אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַד הַחוֹמָה״ (נחמיה ג:ח). וְיֵשׁ עוֹד לִפְתֹּר: לָמָּה לֹא נֶעֶזְבָה מִן הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת, שֶׁלֹּא תָבֹא הָרָעָה עָלֶיהָ?


**“How was...not fortified”** It was not filled with earth around its wall to strengthen the wall. Cf. “And they fortified (ויעזבוּ) Jerusalem up to the wall” (Neh. 3:8). It can also be interpreted: Why was it not forsaken by this evil, that the evil should not come upon it?


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:25:2)


**קִרְיַת מְשׂוֹשִׂי.** כָּךְ יִתְאַבֵּל מַלְכָּהּ עָלֶיהָ:


**the city of my joy** So shall her king mourn over her.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:26:1)


**לָכֵן.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעָה:


**Therefore** This is an expression of an oath.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:26:2)


**יִדַּמּוּ.** יֶחֶרָבוּ, וְאֵינוֹ זָז מִלְּשׁוֹן דְּמָמָה, שֶׁכָּל עִיר חֲרֵבָה אֵין קוֹל נִשְׁמָע בָּהּ:


**shall be destroyed** Heb. ידמו, [lit. shall be silent,] shall be destroyed, but it does not deviate from being an expression of silence, for in every ruined city, no voice is heard.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:28:1)


**קֵדָר.** שׁוֹכְנֵי מִדְבָּרוֹת בָּאֳהָלִים הֵם, אֵין חוֹמָה וְאֵין עִיר:


**Concerning Kedar** They were desert dwellers, with neither wall nor city.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:29:1)


**מָגוֹר.** אֵימַאשְׁמְנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן יִרְאָה:


**terror** Heb. מגור (amassment in French). Another explanation: an expression of fear.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:30:1)


**כִּי יָעַץ עֲלֵיכֶם וגו׳.** וּמָה הִיא הָעֵצָה? **קוּמוּ עֲלוּ אֶל גּוֹי שָׁלֵיו**:


**has taken counsel against you** And what was that counsel? “Rise and go up to a peaceful nation.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:31:1)


**בָּדָד יִשְׁכֹּנוּ.** בֶּטַח בִּיחִידוּת; אֵין צְרִיכִין לְהִתְאַסֵּף וְלֵישֵׁב בְּרֹב אָדָם, שֶׁאֵין יְרֵאִים שֶׁיָּבֹא אוֹיֵב עֲלֵיהֶם:


**they dwell alone** Confidently alone. They need not gather and dwell among people, for they fear not that an enemy will come upon them.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:32:1)


**קְצוּצֵי פֵאָה.** מוּקְצִים מִקָּצֶה אֶל קָצֶה הָרוּחוֹת:


**cast to the ends** Separated from one end to the other end of the directions.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:35:1)


**אֶת קֶשֶׁת עֵילָם.** אֶת תִּקְוַת עֵילָם (ס״א חָזְקַת עֵילָם):


**the bow of Elam** The might of Elam (other editions: the strength of Elam).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:35:2)


**רֵאשִׁית גְּבוּרָתָם.** מֵיטַב גְּבוּרָתָם:


**the chief of their might** The best of their might.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 49:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/49:38:1)


**וְשַׂמְתִּי כִסְאִי.** מָצוֹר שֶׁלִּי:


**And I will place My throne** My siege (מָצוֹר).



## Chapter 50


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:2:1)


**וּשְׂאוּ נֵס.** פּוֹרְקָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, סִימַן אֲסִיפָה הוּא:


**and raise a standard** (Perche in French.) That is a signal for gathering.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:2:2)


**בֵּל.** וּ**מְרֹדָךְ** – שְׁמוֹת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁל בָּבֶל:


**Bel...Merodach** The names of the Babylonian deities.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:3:1)


**גּוֹי מִצָּפוֹן.** מָדַי וּפָרָס:


**a nation...from the north** [viz.] Persia and Media.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:5:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ הֵנָּה.** דֶּרֶךְ הַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה, יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, יָסֵבּוּ פְנֵיהֶם, כְּשֶׁתֶּחֱרַב בָּבֶל וְיֵצְאוּ מִתּוֹכָהּ בִּרְשִׁיּוֹן כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס:


**are directed hitherward** Toward this place, Jerusalem, they shall direct their faces when Babylon will be destroyed and they will leave it with the sanction of Cyrus, king of Persia.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:6:1)


**רוֹעֵיהֶם הִתְעוּם.** גְּדוֹלֵיהֶם הִתְעוּם מֵעָלַי:


**their shepherds caused them to stray** Their kings caused them to stray away from Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:6:2)


**הָרִים שׁוֹבְבוּם.** אֶל הֶהָרִים, אֶל עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁבֶּהָרִים. שׁוֹבְבוּם אַנְוִויזַאנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, שׁוֹבְבוּם מַלְכֵיהֶם. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **הָרִים שׁוֹבְבוּם** ״שַׁלְטוֹנִיָּא בְּזוֹנוּן״:


**mountains...led them astray** To the mountains, to the idol worship that was on the mountains. שׁוֹבְבוּם (esvayent in O.F.), led astray, their kings led them astray. And Jonathan rendered: their governors plundered them.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:7:1)


**וּמִקְוֵה אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם.** ״וְסִבּוּר אֲבָהָתְהוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and the hope of their forefathers** Jonathan renders: וְסִבּוּר אֲבָהָתְהוֹן, and the hope of their forefathers.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:8:1)


**נֻדוּ מִתּוֹךְ בָּבֶל.** מֵעַתָּה צְאוּ מִשָּׁם:


**Wander out of Babylon** Accordingly, go out of there.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:8:2)


**כְּעַתּוּדִים לִפְנֵי צֹאן.** דֶּרֶךְ הָעַתּוּדִים לָלֶכֶת לִפְנֵי הָעֵדֶר בְּרֹאשׁ הָעִזִּים:


**like the he goats before the flocks** It is customary for the he-goats to go before the flock, at the head of the she-goats.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:9:1)


**וְעָרְכוּ לָהּ.** ״וִיסַדְּרוּן עֲלַהּ קְרָב״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and they shall set themselves in array against her** They shall array themselves in battle against her.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:1)


**שׁוֹסֵי נַחֲלָתִי.** בּוֹזְזֵי נַחֲלָתִי:


**O spoilers of My heritage** Heb. שֹּׁסֵי.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:2)


**כִּי תָפוּשׁוּ.** לְשׁוֹן שׁוּמָן:


**as you become fat** Heb. תפושו, an expression of fat.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:11:3)


**כְּעֶגְלָה דָשָׁה.** הַדָּשָׁה בַּתְּבוּאָה וְאוֹכֶלֶת תָּמִיד:


**a threshing heifer** Heb. דשא, a heifer that threshes the grain and eats constantly.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:12:1)


**אִמְּכֶם.** כְּנֵסִיַּתְכֶם:


**Your mother** Your nation.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:12:2)


**אַחֲרִית.** אַתֶּם, הַכַּשְׂדִּים, אִם רֵאשִׁיתְכֶם גָּדוֹל, אַחֲרִיתְכֶם יִהְיוּ **מִדְבָּר**:


**the end of the nations** Although your beginning was great, your end will be a desert.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:14:1)


**יְדוּ אֵלֶיהָ.** זִרְקוּ לָהּ אֲבָנִים, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּדּוּ אֶבֶן בִּי״ (איכה ג:נג):


**shoot at her** Heb. ידו, cast stones at her. Cf. “And they cast (וידוּ) stones at me” (Lam. 3:53).



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:1)


**וְנָתְנָה יָדָהּ.** כְּאָדָם שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כֹּחַ וּמוֹשִׁיט יָד לְכָל מִי שֶׁרוֹאֶה, לְבַקֵּשׁ רַחֲמִים וְעֶזְרָה; וְכֵן ״מִצְרַיִם נָתְנוּ יָד״ (איכה ה:ו) – הוֹשַׁטְנוּ יָד לְמִצְרַיִם לְבַקֵּשׁ מֵהֶם עֶזְרָה:


**she gave her hand** like a person who has no strength and stretches forth his hand to whomever he sees, to beg mercy and aid. Cf. We gave our hand to Egypt” (Lam. 5:6).


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:2)


**אָשְׁיוֹתֶיהָ.** חֹזֶק יְסוֹדוֹתֶיהָ, וְכֵן ״וְאֻשַּׁיָּא יְחִיטוּ״ (עזרא ד:יב). וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (בבא קמא נ.): כְּגוֹן אֵלּוּ הַחוֹפְרִים לְאוּשִׁין; וְכֵן ״וְהִתְאוֹשָׁשׁוּ״ (ישעיה מו:ח) – וְהִתְחַזְּקוּ:


**her foundations have fallen** Heb. אשיותיה. The strength of her foundations. Cf. “And the foundations (וְאֻשַּׁיָא) shall be fastened” (Ezra 4:12). And in the language of the Mishnah: Those who dig for foundations (לְאֻשִׁין) [Baba Kamma 50a]. Cf. “And strengthen yourselves (והתאוששוּ)” (Isa. 46:8), equivalent to והתחזקוּ, and strengthen yourselves.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:15:3)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשְׂתָה.** לְעִיר ה׳:


**as she did** to the city of the Lord.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:16:1)


**כִּרְתוּ זוֹרֵעַ.** ״שַׁיְצוּ מַלְכָּא מִבָּבֶל וַאֲחִידוּ חַרְבָּא בְּעִידָּן קְטוֹל״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Cut off a sower** Destroy a king from Babylon and one who grasps a sword in time of slaughter.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:17:1)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן אֲכָלוֹ.** שֶׁהִגְלָה עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**First...devoured him** For he exiled the Ten Tribes.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:17:2)


**עִצְּמוֹ.** גֵּרְמוֹ, גִּירֵם עַצְמוֹתָיו, שֶׁגָּמַר הַשְּׁיָרִים: יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין. **עִצְּמוֹ** דִּישׁוֹשָׁ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**broke his bones** He broke his bones, for he finished the leftovers, Judah and Benjamin. עִצְמוֹ (desossa in French), [broke the bones].



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:19:1)


**וְרָעָה הַכַּרְמֶל.** לְשׁוֹן ״מִרְעֶה״ (בראשית מז:ד):


**and he shall pasture in the Carmel** An expression of pasture.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:1)


**מְרָתַיִם.** שֶׁהִמְרַתַּנִי וְהִרְגַּזְתַּנִי:


**the rebellious land** Heb. מרתים, which rebelled against Me and provoked Me.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:2)


**פְּקוֹד.** שֵׁם מְדִינָה, וְכֵן ״פְּקוֹד וְשׁוֹעַ וְקוֹעַ״ (יחזקאל כג:כג) תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן שֵׁם מְדִינוֹת:


**Pekod** The name of a province. Cf. “Pekod and Shoa and Koa” (Ezekiel 23:23). Jonathan renders [them as] names of provinces.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:21:3)


**וַעֲשֵׂה כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִיךָ.** אַתָּה, כּוֹרֶשׁ, בְּיַד יְשַׁעְיָה הַנָּבִיא (ישעיה מה):


**and do according to all that I have commanded you** You, Cyrus, through Isaiah the prophet.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:23:1)


**פַּטִּישׁ כָּל הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁהָיָה מְפוֹצֵץ וּמְכַתֵּת אֶת הַכֹּל:


**the hammer of the whole earth** which would shatter and crush everyone.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:24:1)


**יָקֹשְׁתִּי לָךְ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מוֹקֵשׁ״ (דברים ז:טז):


**I laid a snare for you** Heb. יקשתי, an expression of a snare (מוֹקֵש).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:1)


**בֹּאוּ לָהּ מִקֵּץ.** מִסּוֹף הָעִיר הִתְחִילוּ בָהּ, שֶׁלֹּא תֵּדַע וְתִתָּפֵשׂ פִּתְאוֹם:


**Come against her from her border** From the end of the city, commence upon her, that she not know, and she will be taken suddenly.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:2)


**פִּתְחוּ מַאֲבֻסֶיהָ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אִבּוּלָהָא״ – שַׁעֲרֵיהָּ; וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן אֵבוּס:


**open her gates** Jonathan renders: אִבּוּלָהָא, her gates, but it has no similar instance [in Scripture]. Menahem (Machbereth p. 14) classifies it as an expression of a manger.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:3)


**סָלּוּהָ.** דּוּשׁוּ אוֹתָהּ:


**thresh her** Heb. סלוה.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:26:4)


**כְּמוֹ עֲרֵמִים.** גְּרָנוֹת:


**stacks** Granaries.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:27:1)


**כָּל פָּרֶיהָ.** שָׂרֶיהָ. כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הַנְּבִיאִים לְהַמְשִׁיל הַשָּׂרִים לְפָרִים (ישעיה לד:ז), אַבִּירִים (ישעיה לד:ז), וְלַעֲתוּדִים (ישעיה יד:ט):


**all her bulls** Her princes. So is the custom of the prophets to compare the princes to mighty bulls and to he-goats.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:29:1)


**הַשְׁמִיעוּ אֶל בָּבֶל רַבִּים.** לְשׁוֹן ״וַיַּשְׁמַע שָׁאוּל אֶת הָעָם״ (שמואל א טו:ד): הַזְעִיקוּ לְהֵאָסֵף אֶל בָּבֶל מוֹרִים בְּחִצִּים; כְּמוֹ ״יָסֹבּוּ עָלַי רַבָּיו״ (איוב טז:יג):


**Gather archers against Babylon** Heb. השמיעו, an expression similar to: “And Saul called the people together (וַיְשַׁמַע)” (I Sam. 15:4). Call out that archers assemble against Babylon. Cf. “His archers (רַבָּיו) surround me” (Job 16:13).



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:30:1)


**לָכֵן.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁבוּעָה:


**Therefore** An expression of an oath.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:34:1)


**הִרְגִּיעַ אֶת הָאָרֶץ.** כְּשֶׁתִּפּוֹל בָּבֶל יְהִי מַרְגּוֹעַ וְהַנָּחָה לַכֹּל:


**in order to give rest to the earth** When Babylon falls, there will be rest and peace for all.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:36:1)


**אֶל הַבַּדִּים.** אֶל הַקּוֹסְמִים שֶׁלָּהּ:


**upon the imposters** Heb. הבדים, to her diviners.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:36:2)


**וְנֹאָלוּ.** בְּקִסְמֵיהֶם. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ: ״מֵפֵר אוֹתוֹת בַּדִּים״ (ישעיה מד:כה), וְכֵן תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן; עַל שֵׁם שֶׁבּוֹדִים וְאוֹמְרִים דִּבְרֵי כָּזָב:


**and they shall become foolish** with their divining. Similar to this is: “Who frustrates the signs of imposters” (Isa. 44:25), and so did Jonathan render. They are called בּדים because they fabricate (בּוֹדִים) and say false things.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:37:1)


**הָעֶרֶב.** ״סוּמְכְוָותָא״ (תרגום יונתן); גְּרַנְטִיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the allies** Heb. הערב. Jonathan renders: סוּמְכותא (garantie in French).



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:38:1)


**וּבָאֵימִים יִתְהֹלָלוּ.** יִשְׁתַּטּוּ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: פורוונטרונט בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״מִשְׁתַּבְּחִין״ – בֵּאלֹהֵי הָעֲנָקִים שֶׁהָיוּ לָהֶם:


**and they are mad upon things of horror** Heb. יתהללו, they shall become mad. Another interpretation: (Pour vanter in French) in order to boast. And so did Jonathan render: משתבּחין, they boast, [i.e.,] with the gods of the giants that they had.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:39:1)


**צִיִּים אֶת אִיִּים.** ״יְעָרְעוּן תַּמְוָן בַּחֲתוּלִין״ (תרגום יונתן), מִינֵי חַיּוֹת הַמְּצוּיִין בְּחוּרְבָּה:


**martens...with cats** Jonathan [renders]: And martens shall meet cats. [These are] species of animals found in ruins.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:1)


**הִנֵּה כְּאַרְיֵה.** אֲשֶׁר **יַעֲלֶה מִגְּאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן** כֵּן יָבֹא הַשּׁוֹדֵד **אֶל נְוֵה אֵיתָן**. כָּךְ תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״הָא מֶלֶךְ בְּמַשְׁרִיָּתֵיהּ יִיסַק עֲלֵיהוֹן כְּאַרְיָא דְּסָלִיק מֵרוּם יַרְדְּנָא״; לָמַדְנוּ, שֶׁגְּאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן מְקוֹם אֲרָיוֹת הוּא. וְאַף כָּךְ תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן ״וְאֵיךְ תַּעֲשֶׂה בִּגְאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן״ (ירמיה יב:ה): ״לְקָבֵיל חֵיוָא בָּרָא בְּרוּם יַרְדְּנָא״:


**Behold, as a lion** that ascends from the heights of the Jordan, so will the spoiler come to the strong dwelling. So did Jonathan paraphrase: Behold a king with his camp shall ascend upon them like a lion that ascends from the height of the Jordan. We learn that the height of the Jordan is a place of lions. Also, in this manner Jonathan renders: “How will you do in the heights of the Jordan” (12:5): Against the wild beasts of the heights of the Jordan.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:2)


**כִּי אַרְגִּיעָה.** כְּשֶׁאָבִיא הַשָּׁעָה:


**when I bring the moment** Heb. ארגיעה, when I bring the time.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:3)


**מֵעָלֶיהָ.** מִמַּעַל לָהּ:


**over her** Heb. מעליה.


###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:44:4)


**וּמִי יוֹעִידֵנִי.** לַמִּלְחָמָה:


**and who will appoint Me a time** for war?



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 50:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/50:45:1)


**צְעִירֵי הַצֹּאן.** פָּרַס, צָעִיר לִבְנֵי יֶפֶת (בראשית י:ב; יומא י.):


**the young of the flock** Persia, the youngest of the sons of Japheth.



## Chapter 51


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Jeremiah 51:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/51:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Jeremiah/r/51:1:1)


**לֵב קָמָי.** כַּשְׂדִּים בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא דְּא״ת ב״ש, **רוּחַ מַשְׁחִית** אֶת רוּחַ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס וּמָדַי שֶׁהוּא מַשְׁחִית. **רוּחַ** טַלְנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:
